Lab'ban bakinta ne yafara motsawa kafin ubangiji yabata ikon bud'e idanunta da suka cika da kasala da kuma gajiyar baccin  data dauki tsayin awanni biyu tanayi wanda yasama mata sauki akan halin datake ciki...


Agogon dake bedside dake kan locker ta duba karfe 3:36pm ta nunamata ...
Lumshe idanu ta kumayi ,ta kara lumewa ajikin lallausan blanket din da ta dukunkune gangar jikinta aciki
 Tadauki tsayin dak'ik'a  biyar kafin ta yakice blanket din tare da yunkurawa ahankali ,hannunta na rike da kanta tana  cije lebenta tafara kokarin raba jikinta daga rantsattsen gadon da yataimaka wurin sake fito da kyan bedroom din ,da kallo daya zaka kirashi da aljannar duniya ...
Yanayinta kad'ai
 Ya isa ya nuna bawata cikakkiyar lafiyace ta cika taba ,duk da karfin halin danne hakan da takeyi, Cikin sanyin jiki ta ziro kafafunta daga kan gadon ta zira slipas tanufi bayi  tamkar wacce kwai ya fashewa aciki
Koda ta isa bayi toothpaste ta matsa akan brush dinta ,tagoge bakinta ,fuskanta kawai ta wanke tafito hannunta dauke da dan karamin towel tana tsane ruwan dake fuskanta kai tsaye a kayataccen dressing mirror dinta tawuce saidai bata iya aiwatar da komaiba illah kafe kanta da idanu tayi a mirror   cike da wata irin damuwa da take tausayawa rayuwarta ....

Matashiyar macece da shekarunta baza su 29 ba ,kyan da allah yabata daidai misali da kyan madaidacin hasken fatarta  kadai zai nuna maka yadda hutu yasamu muhalli ajikinta ..kyakkyawace dazai yi wuya lokaci daya ka gano aibunta ..

  Runtse idanunta tayi   tare da malalowar kwallar datake kokarin hanata zubowa akan kuncinta ...duk kokarinta nayin hakan saida zuciyarta ta ida tsinkewa ta fasa kukan da bazaka iyo jiyo sautinsa ba ....
Hanmu tasaka ta shafi siffarta da madubin ya bayyana ,cikin rawar murya tasoma magana
"Ina godemaka allah daka aramin wannan ruhin dake gangar jikina ,ka ja da nisan kwanana zuwa yadda kaso ,duk abinda avinda ka aiwatar akaina shine mafi dai dai agareni ,imma na rayu ko akasin haka ,wannan yana rubuce akuddin qaddarata...."

Kukan yakuma tsinkemata tare da toshe bakinta da tafin hannunta ta runtse idanunta tana girgiza kai,da dukkan alamu yaki take da zuciyarta domin karamata karfi..
 
Komawa tayi bedside tazauna tare da rike kanta da lokaci zuwa lokaci takeji yana sara mata tacigaba da rero kukan ta...
"Allah ka azurtani da karfin zuciya ,nayi imani da kullu nafsin za iqatul mout ,kabamu juriyar rabuwa da juna ...kasanya wa yarana hakurin rashina aduk sanda suka wayi gari babu ni arayuwarsu,allah karka bari na muzgunawa mijina da sauran rayuwar da taragemin ..koda da kallon da baimasa dadi ba ...kabashi karfin guiwa da na zciya akan rabuwa dani...."

Vibrating din da wayarta ya shigayi ne ya sanyata makale sauran zancen datake amayarwa daga kasan zuciyarta..
Amaimakon tayi zummar daga wayar ,sai kawai taci gaba da kukanta ...

Saida ta tsinke ta kuma tsinkewa wani kiran yakuma shigow kafin talalubo wayar akarkashin pillow ,Ganin sunan da ke shimfide a screen din wayar yasa ta saurin goge hawayenta da hannunta alokaci daya tadan samawa kanta nutsuwa kafin ta daga wayar..
Tun kafin tayi magana dayan banganren yafara magantuwa "jalila badai jikinba ina fatan lafiya dai..."
Sauke ajiyar zuciya tayi "Am..junaidiyya...nadan... ina  bacci ne .."
Cike dason gamsar da wanda takira ,jalila tabata amsar ...

Sauke ajiyar zuciya wacce aka kira junaidiyya itama tayi "nagode allah ,hankalina ya dan tashi da naji bakiyi picking call dina ba ,nadauka jikinne duk da nasa. May be kina bacci ne Sakamakon maganin dakikasha" ..

" karki damu Dr naji sauki sosai banjima da Tashiba.."
Jalila ta katse junaidiyya tare da gyara  zamanta agefen gadon ...

"Ina fatan ,kuma kan ya daina ciwo ?"..

"Uhmm "
Kawai jalila ta bata amsa..

"Good kinsamu isasshen bacci kuwa"...

Shima a takaice jalila takuma bata amsa da "uhmmm"badan komaiba  sai yadda zuciyarta ke kokarin tsinkemata da batason kawartata tasani...
Yadda suke da junansu kadai zai gamsar dakai cewa abokaine koma ince aminai
Shiru na gajeran lokaci ya shiga tsakaninsu kafin Dr junaidiyya ta magantu  
"Jalila kin isa ki canja abinda allah ya ke rubutawa akan bayinsa ..Meyasa kikeso ki cutar da kanki ki sakawa kanki damuwa muma ki saka mana ,rayuwa da mutuwa duka ahannun allah suke ,dan allah karki ruguza farincikin iyalanki dana maigidanki ,da har yanxu kinki yarda yasan halin dakike ciki ..wlh ajikina naji har yanxu adamuwa kike ....

Da bayan hannunta tashare kwallar idanunta .cikin son kawar da zancen ,tace "shknan junaidaiyya nadaina bazan sake ba duk abinda allah yayi akaina maikyaune ,saidai dan allah koda yallabai yadawo banyarda kisanar dashi halin da nake ciki ba,koda kuwa kunyi waya ,duk da bana samunsa awaya nima koda nakirasa nasan yadda yake fama d. Aiki ..."
.ta idar da zancen cikin damuwa


"Shknan nayi maki alkawari jalila ,amma nima saikinmin alkawari bazan sake ji ko ganin damuwa shimfide afuskarkiba..."

Dasauri jalila ta jinjina kai ,tamkr junaidiyya nagninta...

Sauke ajiyar zuciya junaidiyya tayi "bari nabarki haka ki huta ,idan na dawo daga office zan biyo nasake ganin jikinki"...

Hakan aminan junan suka gama waya kowa zuciyar sa a dagule ...
Jalila kuwa tana ajiye wayar ...komawa tayi takwanta saidai baccin yki zuwa dole ta nufi kitshen dan yadda takejin yunwa na addabarta ,ita kanta ta manta rabon da takai abinci abakin ta ,dakyar ta iya saukowa daga matattakalar benen zuwa  kayataccen parlourn da mai kallo zai iya shagalta da kallonsa ,its all weste of time domin kissima yadda parlourn yagaji da haduwa ,saidai ace masha allah domin kuwa naira ta na kokawa aparlourn ...

Tun fitowar jalila women securities din dake akowace kofa da aka tsaga aparlourn suka shiga rissinawa suna gaisheta ....
Ta hanyar jinjina masu kai kawai ta amsa domin yadda take danjin jikin nata
 Jiri yadan kwashe ta ,dole ta dafe bango.. 

Hakan yasa wasu daga cikin ss din sukayo kanta tare da riko hannayenta ...
"Madam are uh okey?"
.cike da kulawa suke tambayarta  yayinda suke kokarin  zaunar da ita adaya daga cikin kayatattun kujerun dake falon ...

"Im ok ,zanje kitchen ne ,dakaina nakeso naje ,inaso nadan motsa jikina ne ...."


Tafada tare da mikewa tsaye ahankali kuma bataso suci gba da takuramata...
Atake suka rissina tare da komawa bakin aikinsu...
Daya daga cikin ss da ke kula da kai ko mo na gidan ta shigo ,cike da gaisuwar girmamawa ta soma magana "now it half fast 4:pm ,driver yatafi yadauko kids a skull,sir yana hanyar dawowa ma!..."


Wani irin farinciki yasauka azuciyar jalila har saida fuskarta ta nuna hakan...ahankali tamayar da dubanta akan tafkeken enlargement din hotonsa dake manne aparlourn sanye cikin kakin sa na 'yan sanda ,da suka kara fito da haiba dakwarjinin da allah yayimasa uwa uba kyau,daran daran fararen idanunsa takafe da kallo ,_sam bana gajiya ko kosawa idan ina kallon wannan kyakkyawar fuskar ta Abban kids_
Azuciyar ta tayi zancen tare da kafe sunan dake jikin photon da idanu wato *INSPECTOR GENERAL ABDULMAJEED USMAN*
Atake tanemi kaso 40 na daga damuwarta tarasa ,sosai takejin kewar mijinta ,duk da itama tasan duk inda hankalinsa yana agunta ...
Tunda tayi trying number dinsa taji akashe ta tabbatar yana busy ..daya samu kansa firstluv dinsa zaifara kira sannan ita ...hakan yasa bata kuma kokarin kara kiran layinsa ba ..

Coffee kawai ta sha ..suka soma hada wa yallabai abincin dinner ,ita da kukus din gidan,duk da bata jin dadin jikinta but atleast tasamu farinciki da karfin guiwa ...
Saida suka kammala sannan tabarsu suna saving food din a manya manyan flasks ...

Wanka tayi ,tare da simple makeup kamar yadda tasaba ..wani lallausan tsadadden austrilian lace ta saka ,dinkin riga da skit da suka zauna ajikinta daram ta feshe jikinta da perfumes masu tsada da daukar hankali ...
Duk wannan abun acikin karfin hali takeyinsa ,tasan halin ogannata kamar yana karanto abinda ke zuci ne ....

Zata fito babban falo kenan ,yaran suka bude kofa da gudu sukayo kanta hakan yasa ta ware hannayenta suka fado ajikinta ,
Ta dubi sultan dazaiyi 9 to 10 yrs ta shafa kansa haka ta dubi iftahalda zatayi 7ys  ta manna mata sumba a goshi tagaza cewa komai kawai saita  rungumesu tare da lumshe idanunta wata irin kaunar yaranta ke ratsa ta ,zasu shiga damuwa da wata irin rayuwa matukar ta fita daga rayuwarsu ...kulafucin sone ko wakana awannan ahalin
Cije lebenta tayi ..alokacin sultan yadago daga jikinta "ammie kinyi kyau sosai "
Ya fada tare da sumbatar kumatunta ...
Ware idanunta tayi da suka soma sauya kala kafin tayi magana ,cikin maganar kuruciya irin ta yara iftahal tace "uhmmmm wai dan ya ji  abie na fada ,kuma idan mukaje gidan ummie nikadai za'a bari banda kai ...tafada ta na masa gwalo....

Murmishi ya subucewa jalila yadda kyawawan yarannata   keta shirmen kuruciyarsu yasanya ta gaza cewa komai sai binsu da idanu takeyi cike da sonsu da tausayin su ...
Musamman iftahal ma ,matukar son datakewa yarinyar ya ta allaka tsantsar kama da takeyi da kanwarta da har yau take mugun so duk da bata tare da ita ,takanji rayuwar babu dadi idan ta tuna inda kanwar tata take rayuwa ,an raba ta da kanwarta da itakadai take da aduniya ...
An raba su alokacin da batada yadda zatayi ..
Ba irin wannan rayuwar takeso kanwarta tayiba ,gata adaula gudan jininta ,na a inda babu wuta bare tsabtataccen bare kuma ingantacciyar makaranta...tana da qudiri akan kanwarta ,amma tana shakkar tarar yallabai da wannan bukatar,tasani babu abinda zata nema tarasa afadin duniyar nan agunsa tarasa ,amma bataso ta takurasa ,kowani abu makamancin hakan ..hakan yasa take dakon wannan damuwar tsayin shekaru goma kenan,kafin numfashinta ya tsinke aduniya tanaso ta taimakawa rayuwar gudan jininta....

Bata ankara ba sai jin hannun sultan tayi akumcinta yana share hawayenta ,arazane
Tadawo hayyacinta adogon tunanin da ta nutsa aciki...

"Ammie waye ya snyaki kuka"...
Sultan yayi tambayar ,bakidaya yanayin yaran suma ya canja ...

Murmishi takakaro tare da rike hannunsa ... ta rufe idanunta da dayan hannunta,"wani abune yafada min a ido tun dazu nakeso na fitar dashi......

Jiniyar motocine ya katsesu wanda ya karade gidan tun kafin gatekeeper ya bude gate din ..

Daidaita nutsuwarta jalila tashigayi kafin ta yaye labulen window din, murmishi yacigaba da wanzuwa afuskarta adai-dai lokacin dataga  gatekeeper ya gama wangale Katafaren gate din wa  jerin motocin su *_IG_* Rantattsun motocine jere reras guda biyar ke shigowa harabar gidan ahankali,wanda bakidayansu daga airpot suke ..tun kafin motocin su ida isa a packing area wasu daga cikin jami'an tsaron suka fito dasauri saida suka tabbatar da daidaituwar parking din motocin kafin su bude kofar daya daga cikin motocin ..
Ya kwashi tsayin dakika shidda kafin yafara fito Da kafafunsa ,sanye yake cikin uniform dinsa dasuke dad'a  fito da haibarsa ,da kyan hallitarsa da ubangiji yayi masa,kyakkyawan matashi kenan ,shekarunsa bazasu gazawa 37 aduniya ba ,IG kenan yana tafiya da hankalin duk wanda yayi tozali dashi ..pink lip dinsa yadan cija tare da kallon wrist watch din dake daure a tsintsiyar hannunsa ,data nuna masa karfe  6:13pm ..
Cikin kazarniyarsa irinta yan maza ,da takunsa dake nuna tsantsar jarumta ajikinsa yake tafiya ...yayinda sauran jami'an tsaron sukayiwa kansu masauki afarfajiyar gidan tamkar yadda suka saba...

Sai alokacin jalila tasauke labulen dakin tare da sauke ajiyar zuciya ...ta lumshe idanunta tana yiwa ubangiji tasbihi ,tawannan hanyar ce kadai zata iya godemasa akan baiwar azurta ta da miji da yayi irin IG ABDULMAJEED da kowace mace ke mafarkin samu arayuwarta .. 
Ada ta yarda da maganar malam bahaushe dayake cewa ruwa ba saan kwando bane ,kawai wannan zancene ,gashi tasamu mijin dako kusa ko amafarkinta bata taba tsammanin samun saba,yakuma jarrabeshi da masifar sonta ,a irin rayuwarta da tayi mai kama da rashin yanci...

"Alhmdlllh" 
Jalila ta Furta ta nufi babban falon da take tsammanin IG da can ya nufa ...

Tun tana downstairs ta hangosa shida yara zaune a kujera suna ta masa shiririta ,cike da soyayya da kewar yarannasa yake biyemusu ...
Cike da burgewa jalila tayi tsaye bata ida karasowaba ta tsaresu da idanu ..duk da yadanyi rama kadan ,amma hakan bai hanata kara ganin kyansa da tayi ba ..

Ajikinsa yaji ana kallonsa ,kamar yadda yayi tsammani haka yattabata ,jalilace...
Kwayar idanunta ta shiga cikin tasa ..lokaci daya fara'ar dake fuskarsa takuma fadada ...
Da fararen idanunsa  yayimata alama data karaso ,tare da jifarta da kallon _anya kinyi kewata?_
Cikin farin ciki jalila ta ida karasowa alokacin IG ya ware hannayensa tare dayimata nuni da faffadan kirjinsa ....
Duk da ba sabon abu bane kawai taji Yayimata Kwarjinin da hakan. Kawai tanajin wani irin yanayi daba. Atare da Shi yau Musamman yadda yakansance agaban yarane ,
 Hakan da yayi yatabbatar mata da yadda yayi kewarta ...
Dan basar dashi tayi da cewar "sannu da zuwa yallabaina"

Harde hannayensa yayi akan faffadan kirjinsa ,tare da jifanta da  kallon data gaza fassara ma'anarsa

Murmishi tadnyi domin kuwa sai alokacin ta gano abinda yake nufi ...

"Zanshiga ciki ranki yadade idan angama sarautar ...."
Ya idar da sauran zancen da ido ...kamar yadda yasaba ..

Da murmushi kawai taraka bayansa har ya haye upstairs cikin hanzarinsa...
Anan tabar su sultan bayan ta sauyamusu chennel zuwa cartoon...

Kai tsaye sashinsa ta nufa daya gaji da haduwa ,komai na falon green ne ,hatta kayatattun kujerun ..a bedroom kuwa bata  lokacine wajen zayyana kyansa saidai funitures din dakin da komai golden ne..

Murda handle din kofar tayi .tare da sallama ..
Tsaye abakin mirror ta iskesa daga shi boxer da singlet yana cire wristwatch dinsa ..
Ta mirror yakafeta da idanu har ta karaso dab da shi ...
Saida ya cire agogon kafin ya juyo ..

"Ina ankare dake ,nema kike kimayar da kanki miskila ta karfi da yaji ..da yake ba halinki bane sam dabiar batama yimiki kyau ba ..."
Yafada tare da dan yamutsa fuska ya daga kafadunsa ...

Ta kamo hannayensa "allah yatsareni abban kids kaidin zanyiwa miskilanci ,bazan fara ba ...."ta fada tana girgiza kai tare da kallon kwayar idanunsa dake kanta..
"Kawai dai kaga yara wurin...

Girgiza kai yayi tare da katseta da fadar "jalila kin cika ahalinnan da soyayya tun shekaru goma ta yadda bamajin kunyar nunawa junanmu soyayya domin kuwa soyayya gaskiya ce...
Yadanyi shiru ,tare da kara rike hannayenta gam cikin nasa ,hakan ya tsanantawa jalila faduwargaba ,tana fatn damuwarta batayi tasirin da IG zai iya karanto ta afuskarta ba...
Kalamansa suka dawo da ita hankalinta ...

"Imma dai kin zama miskila ,ko kuma akwai abinda kike boyemin jalila  .."

Cike da son basar dashi tayi dariya "yallabaina kenan ,kanada abin dariya wani lokacin ...
Kaga muje nataimaka ma kayi wanka ,and then food is ready...
Tafada tare da riko hannayensa ..

Ya fahimci akwai abinda take boyemasa ..ya bita ayadda takeso ne yaga inda gudun ruwan nata zai tsaya...

Sosai jalila ta taimaka masa ya shirya,saida sukayi sallar magrib da issha sannan suka hallar akayaticciyar harabar cin abincin (dining table area)

Cike dasoyayya suke dinner sosai IG yaji dadin hakan koba komai idan yana tare da iyalinsa yakan gaza musulta farincikin dayake tsintar kansa aciki,gakuma kewar jalila data kids da ta damesa ,musamman fisrt luv dinsa dayakeji bazai iya kwana b. Batare da ya sakata a idnunsa ba
 Haka kuwa akayi suna gama dinner ,yafara zancen gidan ammi ,aikuwa su sultn suka fara murna dole dasu zaa tafi..kasancewar weekend ne yasanya ya amince da tafiya da su ..

Jalila ma taso tabisu ,IG ya jefeta da wani irin kallo na tuhuma ,"kibari saikin warware kafin nan  kijiramu agida..."

Shagwabe fuska jalila tayi ..tare da binsu da kallo har suka fice ...

Da mota daya suka fita ,still drivern dake driving dinsu jami i ne ...baki daya zuciyar IG adagule take ,akwai abinda ke damun jalila ,so why take boyemasa ,ko kuwa zata fara raina masa hankaline ta hanyar nuna damuwarta itakadai ta dama...
Furzan da zazzafar iska yayi dayakejin zuciyarsa tafara dagulemasa ...
  A sajuma eatry dake nan birnin dikko ,yayiwa drivern umurni da ya tsaya ,saida akayiwa su iftahal shopping da tsarabar kamar yadda suka bukata ,kafin su kama hanyar zuwa gidan ammi ...
****Hadadde kuma tsararren gida rantsatssiyar motarsu IG ta dosa gidan... 
Horn daya Drivern yayi baikaiga na biyunba Gatekeeper  ya wangalemasa katafaren gate din gidan... ganin kowaye agidan yasanya Gatekeeper  yadinga kwasar gaisuwa  da zuba kirari wa IG

Fuskarsa dauke da murmishin dake kuma fito da cikar zatinsa da haibarsa IG yake dagawa matashin gatekeeper din hannu alamar yana amsa gaisuwarSa  Har ya driver iso parking area  yayi parking din motar tare da fitowa dasauri ya budewa IG ,da kids dinsa... 

Tun kafin tsayuwar motar mai gadin Gidan kebin motar har ta tsaya agun da aka tanada 

Dasauri yakuma matsawa yana  kwasar gaisuwa wurin IG abdulmajid... Sam IG bai cikason girmamasa da b ma aikatan gidan keyi kamar zasuyi masa bauta,musamman saboda maula.. 
. Dattijon dake baiwa fulawoyi ruwa shima yayo wurin da rawar jiki yana gaida  sa .. saida IG yayimusu alkhairi  kamar yadda yasaba kafin ya kutsa kansa acikin gidan 
Godiya suka dingayi sosai tare da tsokanar su sultan ,driver ne yayimusu umurni subude boot akwai kaya su shigo dasu ...

Saida jami'in ya budewa IG kofar dazata kaishi babban falo na gidan ya sara masa tare da kamewa a bakin kofar yatsa ya yana kai dakawowa
 

A tamfatsetsen falonta suka isketa atsaye cikin shigarta ta alfarma.. Hannunta daya sha adon zabba na zinari dauke yake  da glass cup na ruwa.. kykkyawar datijjuwar matar dashekarunta zasu haurama 55 aduniya ..tamkar tayi kaki ta ajiye IG abdulmajid.. Ta tsananin kamarsune zaka tabbatar itace tayi silar kawosa duniya, Ta saki kayataccen murmishi naganin jikokinnata da danta.....,Da sallamarsa ta ankarar da ita da zuwansu 
Da gudu sultan da iftahal suka nufeta.. 
Tayi saurin ware hqnnayenta suka fada ajikinta ta rungumesu ta sumbaci kawunansu.... 
Fuskar IG abdulmajid dauke da murmishi ya isa inda mahaifiyartasa take ya dan tsaya tare da sunkuyar da kansa yana murmishi.. Tasani matukar tana tsaye bazai taba zamaba har sai ita ta zauna... 
Ta dubeshi cike da kauna ta zauna tana murmishi akan daya daga cikin kayataccun kujerun dasuka gama kawata falon... 

Akan lallausan carpet din falon abdulmajid ya zauna adaf da kujerar da mahaifiyarsa take  ya sunkuyar da kansa kasa tare da cire dakakkiyar hular dake kansa... Cikin kulawa  AMMIE  ta dora  hannunta akansa tadafa cikin hausarta da bata fita take magana "Allah  ya albarkaci rayuwarka abdulmajid . Yakareka daga sharrin masu sherri yakuma kara daga darajarka,ya karama ka juriya da gaskiya ta fuskar aikinka .. Yasanya nutsuwa acikkn iyalanka ya kuma kara dankon soyayya da matarka... "

Tana idar da adduar abdulmajid ya kama hannun  mahaifiyartasa yakai bakinsa ya sumbata.. Cikin harshen larabci yake magana "nagode miki ammy nah"....
Murmishi takumayi na kasatattun mata,kuma wayayyun  mata tace "ina fatan jalilah tana cikkn koshin lafiya? "... 
Abdulmajid yakuma sunkuyar da kansa yace "ammy tana lafiya.. Tana kuma gaisheki sosai... ".. 
Cike da gamsuwa ammy ta jinjina kai tace "Allah  yayimuku albarka ".. 
Ya amsa da ameen...
Tuni masu aikin gidan sunfara sintirin kawowa shalelen ammie abubuwan sha da na ci ,sai da suka nisa da firar uwa da da kafin  Ammie tadan gyara zama tadubi iftahl da sultan.. Da suka mayar da hankalinsu akan wasa kafin ta dubi abdulmajid ta numfasa cike da nutsuwa  tace "ka kwana biyu baka je jumeta ka gaida iyayenkaba.. Ga uwa uba innah dak eta jajenka,...  nasan bakason zuwa jumeta saboda rigimar dangin mahaifinka daya kamata ace kasaba da hakan da kuma kawar dakai da wasu al amurran... dole ka iya daidaita kanka ,wannan zafin zuciyartaka sai ragesa ,Yanxu idan ka dauke kafarka da iyalanka daga jumeta dangin mahaifinka zasu zageni.. Zasuyi tunanin ganin mahaifinka baya raye shiyasa na hanaka zuwa gunsu "... 
IG abdulmajid ya dago da kansa ya zubawa ammiensa yalwataccun idanunsa kafin yasauke ajiyar zuciya Cike da damuwa yace "ammie.. Banaso inazuwa jumeta suna ciwa matata fuska... Aduk lokacin da mukaje jumeta da irin cin kashin da sukewa jalila.. Innah kuwa kawaicinta da hakurinta bazai barta ta hanasuba nikuma bazan zuba idanu suna ciwa mata ta fuskaba.. Saboda ta dalilina tazo garesu.... "
Yadan numfasa still yana duban ammiensa ya lumshe idanu cikin sanyayyar muryarsa yace ". Ammie nah miye laifin jalila dan ubangiji yayi ta yar talaka, shikenan talaka baida kima kenan,ko kuwa su ubangiji yafiso d. Ya azurtasu?. "
Cikin wata irin murya ya idar da zancen... 
Shafa kansa ammie tayi cikin harshen larabci tke basa hakuri "kayi hakuri abdulmajid komai mai wucewane.. Dole da dangin mahaifinka zakayi tutiya duk yadda suke... Nasan kanada zuciya Sai ka dinga kawar dakai a wasu al amurran...,na tabbata jalila bazat. Rike s. Azuciyarta b. ,macece mai hakuri ,kaidai kacigaba da godewa d. Ya bak. Nutsattsiyar mac. ADAMAWA STATE ,JUMETA ,UNGUWAR SHUNI....

Akasarin mazauna wannan unguwa fulanine yan boko da allah yayiwa arziki wasunsu suna noma da kiwone azamanance ,zaiyi wuya kaga wanda baya neman nakansa awannan yanki ,unguwar tasamo asalin sunane daga mutanen cikinta "shuni"duk da kalma ce da hausawa ke amfani da ita dake nufin "arziki,wadatuwa ko wani abu makamancin hakan,
Alh Ibrahim mai nagge ,bafulatanine kamar yadda bayani ya gabata , allah yayi masa arziki na dabbobi da kuma noma ,makiyayine da ya shahara awannan yanki .

Matansa uku ,saratu itace ta farko(hajiya babba) tanada yara biyar ,aminu da aliyu hafizu da kuma zuwairah sai kuma iklima..tabiyun hajiya jamila  tana da yara shidda saifuddeen shamsu sadiya, rukaiyya, rabiatu.  Sai kuma Ta karshen hajiya suwaiba (hajiya inna) itace allah yabaiwa  yaya biyu duk maza usman   shine mahaifin abdulmajid   sai hamza .
gidansa Babban gidane mai matsakaicin kyau da kuma abubuwan more rayuwa ,duk da kyawun gidan ba a fidda sashe sashe ba agidan ,dakuna ne manya manya ciki da falo guda uku sai dakin baki ,da kuma bangaren mai nagge ,sai dakin yara ,da bayi (toilet )guda uku, sai babban kitchen da aka zuba komai na bukata ,gai dakin rijiya da kuma famfo da yalwataccen tsakar gida da aka kawata sa da dashen fulawowi .gidan kuwa sai sambarka,domin gidane mai girma dakuma tsari.


Akallah 'ya'yan mai nagge  zasukai goma sha ukku koma fiye da haka ,acikin matansa hajiya jamila ce kawai yar jumetan ,amma hajiya babba Yar maiduguri ce   hajiya innah kuma daga agadez ya aurota ,hakan yasa kallo daya zaka gane ba jinsin hausa fulani bace,Buzuwa ce ta usul.
Zuriarsa Sun taso cikin so da kauna da kulawa da jin dajindadin rayuwa da kuma Koyar da  tsantsar tarbiyya Wa kyawawan zuriar   kamar yadda suka gado kyawun...zuria ce da duk wanda ya kalla sai yayi shaawarta ..Saidai duk taka tsantsan da tsare gida Na mai nagge ...Allah ya jarrabi wannan zuriya da yan ubanci dakuma son kai ...Hakan yaso yadan janyo rarrabuwan kawunan su ..Musamman da hakan yasamo asali daga kishin da iyayensu mata sukeyi wanda yasamo asali daga hajiya babba da duk wani tashi hankali dazai taso agidan itace ke asSasa shi ,masifaffiyar matace da kokadan bata kaunar zaman lafiya ,dan ma sosai mai nagge yake tsaye kan gidansa, hajiya innah kuwa bata biyemusu macece mai sanyin hali kawai ci da kuma hakuri ,tana kokari sosai akan ganin ta fidda yan ubanci atsakanin yayanta da na abokan zamanta hakan yasa ko atarbiyyya yaranta sun babanta ,bakidaya gidan ba'a sati baayi ceceku ce ba , duk da haka babu yadda mai nagge zaiyi da jarabawar ubangiji ta nuna yan ubanci da zuriar tasa keyi karara ,wannan abu na damunsa ainun,yana bakin kokarinsa akan zuriarsa.

Alhaji ibrahim Mai nagge ya tsayawaa yayansa maza ganin sunyi karatun zamani da na islama . Matan kuwa dasun kammala secondary yake aurar dasu ga 'yayan yan uwansa dan da kansa yake zabamusu mazajen aure  (tun awancan lokacin) hakan yakara habaka zuriar sosai ,sai suka mayar da abun tamkar al'ada zaiyi wuya subaiwa bare yayansu aure ,
Maza kuwa sai yazamana alokacin kowa da aikin daya dogara dashi da zai iya rike iyalansa ,wasu na aikin gomnati wasu kuma yan kasuwa ne,
  Alhaji usman ibrahim ya kammala karatunsa a fannin business administration A america.. Mahaifinsa tsaye yake akan yayansa da dauke duk wata bukatarsu..  . A shekarar da usman zai kammala karatunsa yadawo kasarsa tuni mahaifinsa yagama shiryamasa komai dan acewarsa yana dawowa aure zaiyi kafin ya fara aiki da sabon kamfanin da za'a budemasa daya daga cikin companies dinsa na takalma dake katsina..(kasancewarsa bafulatani makiyayi kuma ,shiyasa yake da sani yadda ake sarrafa fatocin dabbobi  )
Alokacin da usman ya kammala karatunsa ya dawo kasarsa anan ya tarar mahaifinsa ya zaba masa mata wato rahma yar kanwar sa rumaisa... Dayake rahama ta jima da soyayyar usman azuciyarta
Tayi farinciki sosai da allah zai cikamata burinta duk da babu wanda ta ambatamawa.. Dayake rahama irin matann masu shegen kankanba da iyayi da nuna itadin watace.. Zokaga iyayi da wani cika.. Da mahaifiyarta ke sanarmata yadda sukayi da mahaifin usman... Haka ta dinga izzah da yarfe yarfe a family wai ita ta kece saa takuma more miji daidai da ita dakuma zubin tsarin kyanta mahaifiyarta nakuma zugata da koda ta .. Sau tari idan aka hadu a family meeting haka za taita yarda magana wa sa'anninta wai kwarya tabi kwarya... Babu me biyeta ko da tayi  ,dan itama basu cikason zaman lafiya ba hakan yasa tasu tazo daya da hajiya babba.... 
sam basa bari hjiya inna tasha ruwa ,sun takuramata sosai ,saidai bata biye tasu ,bullowar maganar auren ne yasanya suka fara shigemata ajiki,da neman gindin zama ....


Bayan dawowar usman baiyi musu ba ya amsa da auren yar uwarsa rahama.. Nan da nan aka saka rana wata biyu masu zuwa... 

Acikin watanninne usman ya tafi saudiyya domin yin umra bayan ya kammala ibadunsa na tsayin kwanaki goma ya nufi gidan aminin mahaifinsa domin kaimasa ziyara daganan kuma sai yadawo kasarsa wato alh nura da aiki ya kaisa birnin riyadh shida iyalansa..
Shima mashahurin mai arzikine, dan unguwar dayake manyan attajiran larabawane ke rayuwa acikinta.... 
Kwanan usman biyu agidan alh nura.. Anan allah yahadasa da kyakkyawar balarabiya.  
Amna Balarabiyace gaba da baya,mahaifiyqrta balarabiyar sudan ce,mahaifinta kuma anan riyadh yake, iyayenta masu tarin arzikine anan birnin riyadh... 
Daga kallo daya dayayiwa bala rabiyar yarinyar ya kamu da matsanancin sonta sosai. Baisan kuma ya akayi hakan ta faru ba.. Duk yadda yaso fahimtar da zuciyarsa akan matar da mahaifinsa ya zaba masa ya yakyale amna amma zuciyarsa ta gaza.. Baibar kasarba saida ya tabbatar ya dasawa kyakkyawan balarabiyar, soyayyarsa azuciyarta ta yadda bazasu iya hakura ba har sai sunga junansu nacin so sukewa junansu sosai.. Saidai nata bangaren iyayen ta fafur suka ki amincewa dasuka fahimci da agullak take soyayya kumama aganinsu babban cibayane sukai yartasu aure a nigeria koda zasu amince saidai ya ajiyeta anan saudiyya ..tashin hankali makura ta shiga tagaza cire usman aranta. 
Ganin yadda duk ta fice da ga hayyacinta takuma daina walwala yasanya iyayennata amincewa bisa tilas.. Tunda taji ta gani.. Idan kuma tagane kuskurenta na auren agullak zasu kuma karbarta tunda sunsan soyayyace ta rufe mata idanu... 
Alokacin da alhaji nura ya sanarwa mahaifin usman abinda ke faruwa hankalin sa yayi mugun tashi.. Afujajan yace usman yadawo nigeria... Ranar da usman yadawo nigeria aranar yasha fada agun mahaifinsa wai zai mayar dashi karamin mutum.. Yamayi gaggawar cire yarinyar aransa... 
Amsar da usman ya baiwa mahaifinsa sosai yayi mamaki dan bai taba yimasa musu ba.. Amma wannan lokacin hakuri yabasa dan bazai iya hakura da amna ba yakuma roki mahaifinsa da ya janye maganar rahma domin matukar ya aureta bazai iya yimusu adalciba saboda ba ita yakesoba.. Shikuma bazaiso yakasance daga cikin azzalumai ba.. 
Duk yadda mahaifinsa yayi qbun ya cutura shiyasa ya hakura yakuma baiwa kanwarsa hakuri mai yuwa akwai wani babban rabo ne atsakqninsu wanda idan suka matsa akan hana auren a iya yi bayan ransu.. Kuma kana nakane allah nanasa,wannan kuma shine karon farko da wata bare  zatashigo ahalinsu.

Hajiya rumaisa tayi bakinciki sosai akan hakan dan anata ganin cin fuska alh ibrahim da dannasa usman suka  shirya mata dan su tozartata dakuma yarta ai usman din ba fin karfinshi yayi ba da bazai iya tilasta sa ba dole ya auri rahama ba, itakam ba'ayi d'a namijin da zai wulakanta mata 'ya ba. Duk da kuwa kokarin fahimtar da ita  da alh ibrahim yayi. Akan idan ya tilasta usman auren rahma tamkar anyi kuma ba'ayibane mai yuwuwa zumuncinsu yasamu matsala.. Tunda shi usman ya sauya raayi.. Gudun abinda kanje yadawo ahakura da auren bakidaya, rahama kuma Kamar ya take awurinsa koda itace ta sauya raayinta akan usman bazai mata doleba . rahama kuwa taci kuka kamar ranta zaifice... Hajiya rumaisa tadauki alkawari babu ita babu zuriar alh ibrahim kowa ya zauna mazauninsa.. Kuma kowa yaci tuwo da ita miya yasha...Amma duk da haka kawancensu na nan da hajiya babba  Daketa kara tunxurata  da zuga .... 
Auren alh usman da amna abduljalal shine MUSABBABIN rushe zumunci mai  karfi da rumaisa tayi dakuma kullatar usman azuciyarta da mahaifiyarsa. 
Shidai alh usman bai riketa azuciyarsa ba.. Hasalima daganasa bangaren yana sauke duk wani hakkin zumunci dake kansa...
 Itama rahamar Daga baya  tasamu miji saida aka fara auren usman kafi n nata
Ranar da aka daura auren usman da amna aka kawo amarya kasa nigeria a makeken rantsattsen gidanta da mijinta yakera agarin katsina kasancewar acan duk wasu harkokinsa na kasuwanci da companyn takalmansa yake.. Abinda ke kawosa jumeta Sai yazo gaida iyayensa ne.. 
 kuma alokacin da usman yayi aure bakowane maikudi bane zai iya narka naira agina gida mai tsqbar kyau da girma da tsari irin gidan alhaji usman.. Kowa santin gidan yakeyi... 

Aranar ne kuma rahma saida ta kwanta asibiti dan takaici da bakinciki .Musamman yadda yan gulma ke dada tunxurata , Hakan yakuma darsar da tsanar dan uwanta azuciyar hajiya rumaisa  ,Sosai takullacesu aranta ,Har lokacin da alhaji ibrahim yakoma ga allah.. Hajiya rumaisa tana rike dashi takuma dauki alwashi tabbas baza'a shata a banzaba.. Rasuwar alhji ibrahim dayayi yar gajerar jinya ta taɓa duk wani wanda ke familyn harma da abokan arziki kowa shaidar kirki yake masa... 

Sosai alh usman yaji mutuwar mahaifinnasa.. Saidai basuda yadda zasuyi sai hakuri da kuma yiwa mhaifinnasu addua.. Saida sukayi sati daya a jumeta shida matarsa amna  sannan suka dawo katsina cike da jimami

Alh usman yarike iyalansa cikin kyautatawa.. Amna kuwa ta fara sabawa da nigeria saidai har lokacin hausa bata zauna bakinta yadda yakamata ba... 
Shekararta biyu ta haifo kyakkyawan danta namiji wanda yaci sunan jaddinta (kakanta) wato *ABDULMAJID* saida aka kwashi shekaru takwas bata kuma ko ɓatan wataba bare sake daukar wani cikin , saiga wani cikin ta kumasamu ta haifo  mace kyakkyawa da ita aka sanyamata  suna maryam, Daganan itama haihuwar ta tsaya... 
Nan fa surutu yatashi wai daga mahaifiyar usman abun yake. Dan haka shima usman matsalar da ga gunsa take  dama ƙwayayan haihuwa biyune garesu gashi ta haifi biyu kuma ta tsaya alh usman ma ya tsaya da ga biyun ..wasu kuma suce ai daman larabawa basu cika son tara yara ba shiyasa amna ta tsaida haihuwar ,su zuriyar haihuwa ce dan haka babu wanda ya isa yazomusu da sabon salo,ire iren wadannan maganganun ne keta yawo a cikin ahalin ,saidai ko kusa hajiya inna batabk takansuba      
 ... Dan ita hajiya inna macece da bakomaine ke damuntaba ga tsabar hakuri da kau da kai.. Shiyasa ko da alh ibrahim na raye yafi jin dadinta dan tanada saukin kai 
 amna  kuwa da yanxu kyawawan yarannata ke kiranta da ammie Kacokan tamayar da hankalinta wurin tarbiyyantar da kyakkyawan yayannata... 
Abdulmajid yataso cikin gata da tsagwaron soyayya da iyayensa ke nunamasu.. Kyakkyawan gaskene  kallo daya zakayimasa sai ka rantse kace balarabe ne dan ba inda yabaro balarabiyar mahaifiyarsa .. ga tsatson asalin fulani da suka dauko ,tun tashinsa yake masifar spn kaki na police ,komai na police yana burgesa ,sosao mahaifinsa ke kara masa karfin guiwa da kara sanyamasa son abun azuciyarsa  dan haka bayan ya  kammala karatunsa a secondary school,a  abroad ya yi karatunsa awata babbar makarantar sanin makamin aiki na police,yaso ya fuskanci kalubale ta hanyar hari da mata ke kawomasa sau tari sunsha kawo kansu garesa da soyayya wasu kuwa sosuke suyi mu amula dashi... Sam baya bi takansu  hasalima haushi suke basa dan   yatsani macen dabata da kamun kai.. Kamar yadda baya shiga harkar kowa ko agida.. Shi mutum ne miskili magana ma bata cika damunsa ba haka nature dinsa yake yayinda wasu da dama ke ganin ya cika ji da kai da izza ko dan yana ganin shidin kyakkyawane.. Ko agida abinda yan uwannasa ke gani kenan... saidai ko kusa baya yadda araina sa ,yana matukar son agirmamasa...
saida yayi shekaru takwas  A abroad kafin yadawo gida nigeria ,da kyakkyawan sakamako,hakan yasa koda  yadawo aiki baiyimasa wahalar samu ,alokaci daya yafito da babban matsayi wato "inspector" musamman yadda kwalinsa yayo kyau,idan aka cire iyayensa babu abinda yake girmamawa sama da aikinsa ,mutum ne mai jajircewa ,gaskiya da juriya akan aikinsa,hakan yasa hukumar yan sanda take matukar alfahari dashi .alokacin yana da kananun shekaru dan bazai wuce 29yrs ba.   
Awannan lokacin tuni alh saifi da alh shamsu da sauran yan uwan abbansa suke yiwa abdulmajid kamun ya'yansu a wurin mahaifinsa ,bisa dole  yazo ya zabi daya daga cikin yan uwansa ya aura... har lokacin abdulmajid 'ya mace arayuwarsa ,sam babu tsarin aire atattare dashi,dan ko gun aiki ,akwai mata masu ji dakansu,dasuke kawomasa kansu da son aure ,da dama 'yayan manya manyan masu kudisunsha kawo kokon bararsu,saidai ko kulasu ba yayi bare yayi accepting nasu ,haushinsu yakeji sosai akan rashin kamunkai irinna yan matan zamani ,da rasa kunya ta malam bahaushe,zuwa wannan lokacin hankalin ammie yasoma tashi domin itakanta hotunan yayan abokanta da ta kawomasa ko budasu bayayi,Tayi tayi dashi yaje saudiyya yaga yar yayansa ,amma sai yakawomata uzirin yana busy a office .. dakin hutunsa ma tarin files ne ,sam baya wasa da aikinsa ,kamar yadda maganar aurema bata gabansa , daga karshe ma wasan buya ya dingayi da ammie ,sosai abbah ke kwantar mata da hankali,lokacine da lokacin auren yayi zaiyishi ko ba ya so ,kuma ko shekara 30 baiyiba zata dagawa kanta hankali kawai tayita yimasa addua,allah yazaba masa mafi alkhairi,da wadannan kalamanne abbah yakwantar da zuciyar ammie .. 
Abangaren dangin abbah a jumeta sun hura wuta akan dole sai abdulmjid yazo ya zabi daya daga cikin yaransu...
Ganin abun yakoma kamar neman rigima yasanya abbah yin shiru ,shima kuwa bayajin zai iya yiwa abdulmajid dole akan aure,dan gudun abinda ka iya taba zumuncinsu ,kamar yadda shima mahaifinsa baiyimasa dole ba...
Saidai yakan rarrashesu akan zaizo ya duba lokaci ne baiyi ba ...Duk da dai inspector  yakanje yagadasu duk karshen wata . 
Ammie kuwa  ta zubamasa idanu taga iya gudun ruwansa har lokacin kokonto take anya abdulmajid yana lafiya ?koma dai meye yabarta da tulin hotuna da take fatan yazaba daga cikinsu ,abun takaicin ko dubawa yagazayi.daman shikadai yarage takeda burin ganin aurensa dan maryam tuni anyimata aure ,d'an dan yayar abbanta ne dake jumeta ,barister ne ,bayan daura aure yadauki amaryarsa yakoma kano tare da ita

sau tari Wasu naganin ya cika abdulmajid dan kyakkyawane nagani na fada da keda cikakkiyar Kira dake haukatar da ya mace.. Dogo ne baza a cemasa ramamme ba yana da faffadan kirji  da manyan gabobi wanda kallo daya zakayimasa kasan allah yakera jarumin namiji awurin.. Kyakkyawar doguwar fuskarsa dauke take da Dara daran fararen idanunsa wanda idan yana kallonka dasu tamkar yanajin bacci ne dan zaka samesu a lumshe (sexy eyes) duk lafiyayyar macen da yayi tozali da shi saitaji wani abu.. Pink lips dinsa kuwa tamkar yana shafa jambakine.. Dogon hancinsa ya yiwa fuskarsa dass.. Sai kasumbar da ta kuma fito da tsarin kyakkyawar doguwar fuskarsa.. Daga kallo daya zaka iya rantsuwa dacewar abdulmajid bai taba furta wata kalma ta hausa.. Bare a dauka shidin bahaushene...wannan dalilinne yasanya yake wulakanta matan da suke binsa tamkar yadda wasu sukayimasa bahaguwar fahimta

Inspector Abdulmajeed ne zaune a office dinsa cikin nutsuwarsa yake aiwatar da aikinsa.. 
Hayaniyar da ya ke jiyowa ne a reception ne ta soma dagulamasa lissafi...lumshe manyan fararen idanunsa yayi tare da furzan da iskar bakinsa, bayason yawan hayaniya ko kadan, dan bayason abinda kan takurasa wurin aikinsa.. 
Waya ya daga ya kira daya daga cikin siragents din da ke a reception yasoma tambayarsa abinda ke faruwa a gun.. 
"Look ka shigomun dasu anan"... 
Iyakar abinda ya fada kenan ya ajiye wayar... 
Cikin mintunan da basu wuce biyu zuwa uku ba sirgent ya shigoTare da mutane uku... 
Sarawa Inspector AJ yayi  cikin girmamawa yace gasu nan sir... 
Aj kuwa da ya shiga rubuce²akan files din dake gabansa  bata re da ya dago kai ya dubesu ba ya baiwa sirgent din izinin tafiya... 
Ya dauki sekwanni takwas kafin ya dago da idanunsa cikin zallar nutsuwarsa yake kare musu kallo... 
Su uku ne dattijan maza biyune, yadda dayan ya kame da yanayin suturar dake jikinsa zai nuna shidin mai hannu da shuni ne,  sai yarinya da zai iya kiyasta shekarunta bazata gaza shekaru sha hudu zuwa biyar ba  lokaci bayan lokacin take goge hawayen da zubowa daga idanunta da gefen hijabinta.. Kasancewarta farar mata ce sosai fuskanta da idanunta yayi jajir, da zai tabbatar da yadda taci  kuka har ta gaji... 
Mayar da dubabsa ya kumayi da dattijon dake gefenta kallo daya zakayimasa ka tabbatar da shidinne mahaifinta... Musamman yadda fuskarsa ta gama bayyana da yanayin damuwa... 
Sauke ajiyar zuciya Inspector AJ yayi cikin gama nazartarsu bakidaya ya maido da dubansa ga mai kudin dan yasan ko tantama babu shi ne ya shigar da kara... 

"Meyafarune? ".. 
Atakaice ya tambayesa... 
Gyara tsayuwarsa yayi da katuwar malum malum dinsa ya dubi Inspector AJ da shi kansa kallonsa yake ya kuma duban bayin allahn dake gefensa... 
"Yallabai, shi wuyar talaka duk yadda aka kulkah dashi sai an warware akullum talaka baida buri da ya wuce yiwa mai kudi bakinciki da hassada... Wannan bawan allah da ka gani malam abdullahi, haya sukeyi agidana, idan shekarar karbar kudin hayar gidana yayi sai kowa ya jini dasu, da kyar nake samu suke biyana kudi na, yanxu haka ina binsu kudin shekara harda rabi basu biyani ba shi ne nace su tashi su barmin gidana, danni Bayar da hayar gida sanaa na dauketa ba shiririta da shashanci ba, ganin yaki biya na ne na gaggauta basu notice subarmin gida na, shi ne fa yarinyar nan tasa, saboda rashin tarbiya, dan kawai na bukaci hakkina shi ne takw neman batan suna da huce haushi wai  na nemeta da lalata da girmana da mutuncina.. Me zanyi da wannan kwailar nikam?, shiyasa nazo abiman hakkina domin da saka hannun iyayenta take neman yimin sherri.... 
Kuka matsakaiciyar budurwar yarinyar ta kuma sakawa mai sauti jin yadda alhaji garba ke neman yimata kazafi, da yadda ya gama tsaro labarinsa na kanxon kurege, kuma duk yayi hakan, dan ya cimusu fuska akan rashin samun biyan bukatarsa... 
Maido da dubansa Inspector AJ yayi akan ta yadan tsura mata idanu kadan kafin ya soma yimata magana "meye sunanki.. "

"JALILA "
ta basa amsa cikin shesshekar kuka.. 
Jinjina kai yayi yacigaba da magana  "abinda ya fadamin gaskiyane "...

Kudin shekara kadai yake bin babah na kuma babah na bashida ikon biyansa wadannan kudin, shi ne dan yaga mahaifina baya nan ya tagi kasuwa ya aika yaro akirani, yace na shigo motarsa ya nuna tamkar ba cutar dani zaiyi ba... 
Batare da na kawo komai araina ba na shiga nagaishesa.. Shi ne yasoma yimin maganar banza wai idan inaso yabar mana gidansa bakidaya sai na basa hadin kai yayi lalata dani kuma zai dinga bani kudade masu yawa... 
Da naƙi amincemasa ya nemi yayimin tilas sai na saka ihu dole ya kyaleni... Aguje na shigo gidan batare da na fadawa kowa ba dan nasan iyayena zasu shiga tashin hankali ganin hakan yasanya alhaji garba yin riga kafi dam ya wanke kansa gudun kar na fallasa sa, shi ne yayi rigakafin kawo mu police station ni da mahaifina, sai aka mayar da laifin akanmu "... 
Ta idar da dogon jawabinta tana mai fashewa da kuka... 
Mahaifinta yadora da cewa "yallabai ataimakamana gaskiya tayi aikinta, wlh bama nufin kowa da sherri..

"Kaji abinda tace ".. 
AJ ya cewa alhaji garba yana jifansa da wani irib kallo... 
Da hanzari alhaji garba ya kara matsowa gab da kujerar da Inspector AJ yake zaune cikin kasa da muryarsa yasoma magana "eh to yallabai bana ce aah ba, amma aduba lamarin ni din dai asamu ta wankeni ta karyata kanta, daga nan sauran maganar ta rage tsakanina dakai ".. 
Ya idar da zancen yana murmishi... 
Shima AJ murmishi yayi sosai ya na duban sa kafin yace "good alhaji ka saukakamin gameda bincike, asaukake ka tabbatar min da kai mai laifine, dan haka zaka bani cin hacci naci amanar aikina okey?! "
Yayi maganar ya na tsare Alh garba da idanunsa da suka soma tsoratar dashi... 
Tsawa ya dakawa alhaji garba da ya tilastamasa komawa inda yake ya tsaya yana mazurai.. 
Gyara zamansa AJ yayi sosai cikin hade fuska ya soma magana yana duban alhaji garba "bana daga cikin gurbatattun yan sandan da suke ragargaza martabar kasarsu... , waya yasa ka ya kira sirgent, cikin kankanin lokaci ya shigo ya saramasa, mayar da dubansa AJ yayi  ga alhaji garba da gumi yafara ketomasa, "na kamaka da manyan laifuka biyu, na farko yin amfani da raunin mutum (talauci) dan batanci, na biyu kokarin baiwa dan sanda cin hanci, wai mekuka dauki yan sanda ne, kwadayayyu ko kuwa, kajinku da kuke watsa wa tsaba... Kune batagarin kasarnan, .. then kudin hayar da ka biyo malam abdullahi sun koma kudin biyan kazafin da ka yimusu babu abinda zasu baka, so that sirgent arrest him "! 
Ya maido da dubansa ga sirgent cikin bada umurni... 
Saramasa sirgent din yayi ya cika umurnin ogansa ya tasa keyar alhaji garba zuwa sel... 
malam abdullahi kuwa hawayen farinciki ya shigayi yanayiwa AJ godiya, tabbas da duk jami'an kasar nan haka suke da kasar bata tagayyara haka ba... 

Jalila ma kukan farinciki tashigayi ta na goge idanunta.. 
Murmishi AJ yayi ya dubeta ya mayar da dubansa ga malam abdullahi.. "Babu damuwa wannan aikinmu ne ai, amma kamar yadda ya nema nan da wani lokaci yakamata ku bar masa gidan sa ko nemi wani "... 
Jinjina kai babah yayi ya sake yiwa AJ godiya da shimasa albarka, sannan ya basu izinin tafiya... 

Koda isarsu gida kuwa cikin farinciki jalilah ta shi ga sanarwa da mahaifiyarta komai.. Bakidayansu addua suka shigayiwa yallabai AJ da fatan ubangiji yakara dauka kasa domin tabbas irin su kasar nan ke bukata... 

       ****
Ranar jumaa da misalin karfe biyu na rana titin IBB WAY ya cika da ababen hawa kowa kokarin gudanar da sabgogin gabansa yakeyi.. Musamman da ba jima da saukowa daga masallaci ba... Bakidaya ababen hawa sunyi jingim akan titi sakamakon matsin go slow da aka samu aranar yayinda yan road safety ke kokarin ganin komai ya daidaita... 
Yallaɓai AJ dake cikin motarsa wanda harda shine go slow ya ritsa.. Lokaci zuwa lokaci yakan dubi agogon dake daure atsintsiyar hannunsa, cikin kosawa yake kallon gefe da gaban sa.. Hakan zai tabbatar maka da tattare yake da uziri mai karfi da yake kokarin aiwatarwa... 

"Asai gyada suyar ruwa ga arha ga dadi "... Yarinyar ta idar da maganar tana dan bubbuga bakin gilashin motarsa
Muryar da yaji ta doki kunnensa kenan, tabbas ya tabajin wannan muryar amma ya gaza gano inda ya tabajin murya... Abinda yasani kawai ya gaza mantawa da sautin muryar...

awannan lokacin komai a sanyaye yake yinsa ,

sai bayan sallar magrib ruwan ya tsagaita amma duk da haka baidaina zubaba,sosai garin yadauki sanyi da yanayi mai dadi ...AJ kuwa kawai ya tsinci kansa da tunanin kodai yakoma yaga halin dasuke ciki...

Waro idanu nafisa tayi tare da rike bakinta "wayyo allah dadi ,cikin zumudi take magana "kinsan me hajja?banga tazama ba ,bari naje nayi wanka na tsantsara kwalliya ,daganan kisa asabe ta girka masa friedrice da chicken sai kunun aya .,Na shige ciki,"....
Ta shige dakinta da gudu ...

Da idanu su biyun suka rakata kafin hajja ta lakuci yawu tacigaba da kirga kudinta ,zuwa can ta ja tsaki da alama wani abun na damunta "zuria sai kace mayu,ubankowa sonsu yakeyi,Yaro ga shegiyar isa da nuna izzah ,Ga tsinannen miskilanci kamar wani dan sarauta ,mtssww allah nagodemaka da hasashena yazama gaskiya da na bani da mitar nafisa ,yau dai miskilin mijinnata yazo"..

Cikin rashin fahimta hajiya ruqaiyya ta dubeta kafin tace "ya amsa ya na sonta ne har ansaka rana? ,kuma hajiya dole kowa fa ya nakikewa Abdulmajid ,nafarko ya tabo jinsin fulani ,daga bangaren mahaifiyarsa kuwa jinsin larabawa ya dauko,dole kuwa ya rikita yaranmu "...
Gyara zama hajja tayi "kinjimin zancen banza rukaiyya,to mudin a fada mana kyawu ne  ,kaf zuriarmu babu mummuna haka kaf zuriar mai nagge babu wanda allah ya jarabta da muni ,kuma wallahi auren nafisa wajibin alaihim,ko yaso ko kar yaso,haifarsa akayi bashi ne ya haifa ba ,abinda ubansa yayiwa yar goggonku  rumaisa wato zubaida ,ni da zuriata munfi karfin wannan cin kashin ,dolensa ne yayi,"ta idar da maganar tana huci ...
Yadda tafara daukar zafi ne yasanya hajiya ruqaiyya tsuke bakinta tayi shiru...

Nafisa kuwa ta bata lokaci tana kwalliya ..kaya kuwa tasa ta fidda sau ba adadi,daga karshe ta yanke
 Saka wasu english wears riga da skirt ,sun kama ta sosai ,duk da yalwar gashin dake gareta saida ta kara da attach ta feshe jikinta da turaruka masu kamshin gaske ,ta jefa chewing gum a bakinta ,tana tafiya tamkar zata tsinke saboda rashin kwari...
Kitchen ta nufa inda asabe take "kingama hada komai ?"

Cike da son gulma asabe tace "ai konayima bazai ciba kinsane "...
Rike karamin kugunta nafisa tayi tare d daure fuska "kamarya bazai ciba "...
Tabe baki asabe tayi "saboda lailah ta girka masa da kanta,kinsan bayacin abincinmu na masu aiki ,nakuma tayata kaimasa a sashin hajiya innah ,yanxu haka suna can suna ta soyayyarsu "...

Tuni zuciya ta kawowa nafisa wuya ,wani irin kishi yafara fizgarta ,kamar kububuwa haka ta nufi sashin inna ...
Labule ta dan daga kadan tare da tsayawa dan ganewa idanunta ...
Duk da alokacin bawai abincin AJ yake ciba kawai ya mayar da hankalinsa akan danne dannen wayar dayakeyi ,wanda bakidaya hankalinsa na gun su jalila ,baya tunanin zai iya kwana jumeta tamkar yadda yasaba , batare da ya san halin dasuke ciki ba ...
Lailah kuwa saboda tsoro da sanin halin yayannata tagaza cewa komai ,tamkar yadda shma ya banzantar da ita ...kawai saita maida hankalinta ga kallon tv n dake manne abango.

Karaf suka had'a idanu da nafisa dake musu labe ,murmishi lailah tasaki mai nuna allah yaba mai rabo sa'a "...Duk da itama din tasan bata samu karbuwa ba,tayi hakanne dan ta turawa nafisa haushi.
Hakan bakaramin konawa nafisa rai yayiba  ,jan kwafa tayi tare da barin wurin dan tasan halin gogan sam bayasan akuyanci da sait saka lailah takwashi kashinta ahannu tun anan.
 Adaddafe AJ  yakai 3:13pm ,bayan yaje duk ya gaggaida iyayensa ,suna afalon daddy hafiz ne da bai jima da dawowa daga gun aikiba ...Anan ya nuna bazai kwana ba a jumetaba Kt zai juya ....
Sosai nurain ,mujaheed ,anas da kuma,ibrahim  suka nuna sam bazai yuwuba ,dukansu matasan gidanne da zasu zo sa'a daya da AJ,
Saida ya nuna musu sosai akwai aikin ujila a office da ya taso agabansa wanda dole sai yana nan ,kafin suka amince ,yayi hakanne dan bayasob su cika takuramasa
 Da tambayar dalili,Dan bai cikadon tambayaba  ,
har parking area suka rakasa daddy hafiz kuwa dashi kadaine yafara dawowa gidan  daga office,sosai ya shimisa albarka da jindadin ganinsa yakuma jaddada masa da gaisuwarsa akan dan uwansa usman da matarsa ,saida motarsa ta fice daga estate din kafin baki dayansu su dawo cikin gidan....

Tun bayan barin AJ daga sashin innah ,jikin lailah yayi sanyi takuma karaya da samun karbuwa agun yayannata musamman yadda taga yana basarda ita saidai kuma har yanxu tana matukar jin sonsa aranta ...
Hatta innah ta fahimci yadda AJ din yayiwa yar uwarsa laila ,da nuna Shi sam bai fahimci inda tasa agaba ba ,itakam ta ga bani da wannan baudadden jikannata da ko kusa baya son zancen aure ,da duk kusan saaninsa sukayi wasu kuma na kan hanya ....

Jiki asabule lailah tayi sallah asashin innah ,ganin damuwa afuskarta yasanya innah rarrashinta tare da yimata alkawari shawomata kan AJ din...

Tattara flask din tayi ta nufi
Bakin famfo ,ganin nafisa tayi agabanta tare da hankade flasksdin dake hannunta atake su ka watse kasa ....
Cikin bacin rai lailah ta dubeta (dake kusan sa'anni ne,sai nafisa ta girme mata kuma basa shiri)cikin bacin rai ta yago mata magana "ai nasan wani akuyancin da danyen kai saike,kinyi ne kuma dan ki huce haushin  mallakar  ya AJ danayi a hannuna .....
Bata idar da zancenba nafisa ta wanketa da mari tare da nunata da yatsa,"inamai tabbatar ya AJ danni kadai a kayisa ,kuma nikadai ce tasa ,kisaka wannan aranki duk wanda ya shiga tsakanina da soyayyar da nake masa zandaukesa tamkar yadda ake dauke kara ahanya ina fatan kin fahimceni ...
Murmishi mai ciwo lailah cikin son tura mata haushi still hannunta na akuncinta ta dafe tace "koda ace ya AJ bai aureniba bazan damu ba ,amma bazanyimasa fatan auren ballagaza irinki ba,kamilalliyar mace kadai kuma tsabtatacciya ta dace da ya AJ ,ba irin kiba ,nima ina fatan kin fahimta"..

Kafin nafisa tace wani abu tuni mahaifiyarsu lailah hajiya halima ta karaso gun ,da alama tagama ganin duk abinda ya faru ,korar lailah tayi daga gun ...Saidai ko kusa bata isa tatankawa nafisa ba don yin hakan babban tashin hankaline agidan...
Kallon sama da kasa nafisa tayiwa hajiya halima kafin taja tsaki tabar gun ...Da idanu hajiya halima ta rakata kafin tayimata adduar fatan shiriya ....

Bakidaya nafisa rikicewa hajja tayi da kuka ,d fadawa hajjar karya da gaskiya akan abinda yafaru ....
haka hajja ta dinga balbala masifa da kumfar baki saidai babu wanda yabi takanta....

Sai wurin 9:34pm AJ ya shigo farin katsina ,sallah kawai yayi amasallaci ko gida baijeba ,sai waya dasukayi da abbah ,ya wuce gidan su jalila ...
Yadauki tsayin mintuna uku yana sallama akofar gidan ,babu alamu zaa amsa ,wrist watch din hannunsa ya duba da ta nuna 9:58pm,da alama sunyi bacci ...
Ya sauke ajiyar zuciya still bai bar kofar gidan ba ,Dandazon matasan da ke majalissa a gefen gaba da  kofar gidan ya dosa ,bayan sun gaisa yake tambayar masu gidan suna nan...

Cike da jimami wani matashi yagirgiza kai "inaaa,ai babu kowa gidannan ,bayan sallar magribannan naga keke na pep akofar gidan ,atakaice dai matar gidan ciwonta ya tashi ,ranga ranga aka kaita asibiti....."
Dafe kai AJ yayi tare da baton sunan allah ,"wace asibiti ce sukaje ?".

matashin ya basa amsa da cewar "eh to bazai wuce babbar asibitin gwamnati ba ,dan itace tafimusu kusa "....
Bai jira ya idar da kwatancenba ya shige mota dasauri yayi mata key ya nufi hanyar asibitin ...
Yasha wahala kafin yagano inda suke ...
Duk da dare ne ,amma hasken solar da ya mamaye reception din ya taimakamasa wurin gane fuskarta ,tsaye take ta jinginar da kanta ajikin karfen solar  ,taci kuka har ta gode allah ,akafarta kuwa babu takalma ,bakidaya kamar an kwatota daga bakin kura ,mahaifinta ne kadai mai karfin guiwar iya zama ,shima kuwa yar yarinyar da ake shayarwa akafadarsa har wannan lokacin bacci takeyi,bakidaya fuskokinsu cikin damuwa  suke da jiran tsammani ...
Ware jajayen idanunta yayi dai dai da tunkarosu da AJ keyi,bazata iya manta fuskarsa ba ko a ina ya shiga ,cikin mamakin ganinsa ta kura masa idanu ..Zuciyar AJ ta tsananta bugawa da karfin gaske ,yayinda kowane tako da yake na kara kusantarta zuciyarsa naka karfin bugawa da bai tabajin irin wannan bugawar zuciyar atare dashi ba .......

Itace kalmar da AJ yafada tare da cije lebensa na kasa ,ya dubi jalila da ta cigaba da rero kukanta ,kauda kai yayi tare da runtse manyan fararen idanunsa wani irin tausayin su na kuma shigarsa ,haka kawai yaji kukanta yafara addabar zuciyarsa da haifar masa da wata sabuwar dmuwa....

Har mota baba yarakasa yana ta yimasa godiya da sanya albarka ...

Murmishi tare da sauke ajiyar zuciya suka ziyarceshi alokacin dabaisan da zuwansu ba , zuge gilashin yayi yaja motar tare da barin asibitin ...


Yana isa gida ,koren ruwan tea kawai yasha wanda ammie ta hada masa ,ya wuce sashinsa ya watsa ruwa ya kwanta ,cikin wani irin sake sake da zuciyarsa kemasa har bacci yadaukesa ...


Kwanansu shidda a asibiti,cikin wadannan kwanaki kullum AJ sai yaje asibitin  duk da busy da yake zama a office ,acikin kwanakinne ya siyamusu sabon gida flat house mai dauke da mayalwantan dakuna da yalwataccen tsakar gida da bayi guda biyu  ..

A jumeta kuwa nafisa ta dagawa hajja babbah hankali sosai akan AJ ,dan ta gama fahimtar cewa kokarin kwantar mata da hankali hajja keyi amma sam AJ bata ita yakeba ...
Sosai ta dage da tadawa hajiya babba hankali dan ko abinci tadaina ci ,haka mahaifiyarta tayi rarrashin da ban baki amma abu ya cutura , tasha kulle kanta adaki ,wai ita bazata bude ba matukar hajiya bata sanya AJ ya auretaba ,ire iren wannan iya shegenne nafisa keyi ,musamman ganin yadda hajjah babba da mahaifannata ke kulafucin sonta ...

Ganin abun yaki karewa ne yasanya hajiya babbah hado kan 'ya'yan nata bakidaya akan shawo matsalar yar gwal dinta ,kowa haushin nafisa yakeji musamman yadda akanta hajiya ta ke kokarin bata musu lokaci  daga harkokinsu....

Gyaran murya hajiya tadanyi tafara magana "baki dayanku nan iko da ku ne yasanya nataraku anan ,bakomai bane illah da ya wuce maganar nafisa dake kulle adaki zata kashe kanta da wancan mutakabbirin yaron da yake fama da ji da kai ..." 
Gyara zama alhaji aminu yayi da dawowarsa kenan daga office ko hutawa baiyiba,hajja tayo kira ,"hajiya wane ne kike nufi hakan ,ban fahimci ma zancennanba "...

Dubansa hajja takumayi ,cike da takaici tace "waccan dan naku mana na wajen alhaji usman ,akan shi nefa nafisa ke son jefamu cikin masifa "...

Cikin bacin rai Alhaji aliyu da ya fahimci inda zancen hajja yasanya gaba yace "ta dai jefa kanta amasifa amma bamu ba ,shashashar yarinya kawai ,tunda baya sonta aiba dole ,gaskiya hajja duk wani abu da yarinyar nan keyi ,kina bada gudunmuwa"...

Ran hajiya zuwaira dake gefe yasoma  baci jin kalaman yayannata ,hajiya kuwa tsa
[5/27, 10:03 PM] ummu nasreen: bar takaici yasanya zuciyarta kara zafi "ba shakka aliyu fitsara zakamin kenan ,wato dan ba 'yarka bace ahalin da nafisa take ciki..,

Yadda suka bada duk hankalinsu akan hajja yasanya tacigaba da zancen cikin harshensu na fulatanci "ina so ku sanya usman da  ya tilastawa Abdulmajeed auren nafisa ,musamman ku biyunnan  Aminu da hafizu da kuke sama da shi ,ta hakane
Za 'a magance wannan damuwar ta nafisa ko na samu sauki ,baki daya yarinya ta susuce akansa bata da wata nutsuwa"...

Tsabar takaici kowa yayi shiru afalon  Kamar yadda kowa kejin haushin yadda hajja ke ririta nafisa acikin jikokinta tamkar ita kadaice jikarta,
Hajiya ikilima kuwa tab'e baki tayi tagaza furta komai ...
Alhaji aminu ne ya magantu cikin girmama mahaifiyartasu  "kenan mu tirsasa usman ya tilastawa Abdulmajeed ya auri nafisa ,?ya numfasa tare da girgza kai ya cigaba dacewa "hajja ba na ganin wannan abun mai yuwuwane"...
Tun kafin hajja ta magantu ,cikin damuwa hajiya zuwairah ta dubi yayannata "haba yaya ,ku taimaka na tabbatar ya usman zaiji maganar ku ,dan allah ku ceci nafisa"....
Kallon da alh aminu ya jefeta dashi yasanyata tsuke bakinta tayi shiru...

Cikin jin zafin abinda ya fada hajja ta dubesa "ba ita zaka harara ba ,nice zaka harara ni da na taraku",bazan jure inzuba idanu yarinya ta mutu ba abanza ,kamar yadda ubanta ya cire hannunsa kuma bijiremin zakuyi?"...

Sunkuyar da kai yayi tare da baiwa hajja hakuri yaja bakinsa ya tsuke ...

Alhaji hafiz yace "shikansa mahaifinnasa ai taurin kai ne dashi ,babu wanda ya isa ya sanya sa ko ya hana sa abu ya hanu ,ku dubi shekarun baya mana ,haka ya watsawa baba kasa a idanu yaki auren zubaida ,kalar zaren kalar yadin ne ,shima yaron ai akwai isa da girman kai ..."
Hajiya ikilima ta sake magantuwa "shi fa yaya usman ba bijirema babah yayiba ,zabi baba yabasa akan auren hajiya zubaida ,ba tilastasa yayi ba ,na tabbata da ya tilastasa wlh babu yadda zaayi ya bijerewa babah,kuma naga tunda Alh mansur (mahaifin nafisa)ya fitar da hannunsa gudun abinda kanje ya daw,ko abinda zai taba zumuncinmu mu hakura da zancennan kawai"...
cikin bacin rai hajiya zuwaira ta katseta "kiyimana shiru  ,wacce bata kishin yan uwanta mtsww,da bilkisu ce ahalin da nafisa ke ciki aida baki fadi hakan ba .. "
Ran hajiya babbah yakuma baci "banasan ce ce kuce kamar wasu kananun yara ,tunda kun gaza samo bakin zaren ni na tsayar da matsaya ,wajibi ne kusa usman ya sanya dansa ya auri nafisa ,ina kuma jiran jin sakamako mai kyau nan da kwanaki biyu ..."
Ta idar da maganar tare da sallamar kowa dan yadda takeji zuciyarta na suya,haka kowa yabar falon zuciya a yamutse.
 Acikin wadannan yan kwanaki kuwa tuni zance yagama zagaye mai nagge estate ,kowa mamaki yake wai AJ ne zai auri nafisa ,ganin abun sukeyi wani gamin gambiza ,zuwa wannan lokaci nafisa ta fara samun sukuni domin kuwa dukkan alamu sun nuna AJ zai zama nata ,saura taji da shawo kan sa bayan ta mallakesa ...
Yadda hajja ta kunno wuta akan lamarin ,yasanya kowa zuba ido ganin yadda zaayi wannan auren na sonkai Da shi kansa AJ baisan wainar da ake toyawa ba ,dan ko hajiya inna batasan meke faruwa ba ,hajiya jamila acikin gida ta tsinci zancen dariya tayi sosai cike da son ganin yadda wasan zaizo karshe ,cikin neman samun gindin zama agun hajja babbah baki daya yayan hajiya jamila sun shiga zancen bisa goyawa hajja baya akan kudirinta ....
Alokacin Alh usman yasamu kira sosai daga jumeta musamman da yan uwansa da suke kokarin sanyasa takurawa Abdulmajeed da har yanxu shi baisan wainar da ake toyawa ba ,ko kusa kuma abbah bayason yasani dan ya tabbatar din bazai amince ba duk da ranshi na matukar baci yadda AJ din sam baya zancen aure sai na aikinsa  , saboda baiyi aurenba shiyasa akeson alika masa auren nafisar ....
Takowane fanni  ,alh aminu ,alhj aliyu har ma da yayan hajiya jamila sun saka kansu ,sun kunnowa abbah wuta akan zancen auren .
Alh Aminu kuwa sosai yayi nazari akan tilasta faruwar hakan zai ida janyo rugujawar gidan baki daya ,baisan meyasa akullum mahaifiyarsu batason zaman lafiya agidan,duk wata fitina daga gunta take tasowa ,tana da sonkanta sosai ,ya yanke shawarar kiran AJ din yayi masa tayin nafisa ,matukar bai aminceba ,babu wanda zai kuma d'aga zancen ....
Hajiya innah kuwa ashanun tallah ta jiyo zancen ,wato ita tafi karfin asakota azancen saboda bata da yanci ,sosai taji zafin abun amma zata tsaya taga inda gudun ruwannasu zai tsaya ...

Matukar tashin hankali lailah ta shiga domin tagama saduda cewar hajja babbah ta kwatarwa nafisa AJ kenan ,daga baya kuma ta fawwalawa allah komai tare da neman zabinsa ,dan tagama lura ,sam hankalin ya Aj baya agunsu...Koda Ace AJ din bai auretaba ,tanayimasa fatan samun wacce ta dace da rayuwarsa amma ba nafisa ba ...
Hakan bakaramin kwantarwa hajiya inna hankali yayi ba yadda lailah ta ajiye nata makaman yakin ta rungumi qaddarar rashin AJ din batare da ta nemi hanyar bata zumunci ba tamkar yadda hajja babbah ta dauko ....,Innah kuwa tasha alwashin nunawa hajja babbah nata ikon akan AJ ,datake ganin ita din bakomai bace ...


*****
AJ kuwa gaza tsayuwa yayi har saida ya jingina ajikin motarsa hannayensa nade akirjinsa yake duban jalila ,duba mai dauke da tsantsar mamaki da ya sanya jalila fara tsarguwa da duban da yakemata...
Ta wani bangaren kuwa zuciyarsa ta fara dukan tara tara  alokaci daya ya fara kokowa da nemo nutsuwarsa ...
"kenan kin kusa aure ?to meyasa baki taba fadamin ba !"
Cikin daga murya da tuhuma yake mata zancen ...
Saida jalila tadan ja baya dan tsorata da irin yanayin da ta gansa ...
Dasauri ya dafe kansa ..Abinda ke cikin zuciyarsa shine yagaza boyuwa ...
Cikin yar nutsuwar da ya samawa kansa ya lumshe idanunsa tare da cije lebensa yasauke ajiyar zuciya ,shi kansa baisan meke faruwa dashi ba awannan lokacin ,ware idanunsa yayi tare da murmishi akan face dinsa ya dubeta "shknan ,allah ya tabbatar mana da alkhairi ,bari na shiga ...."
Ta gefenta ya wuce ya nufi cikin gidan ..
A dan tsorace jalila ta dubesa har ya shige ..Bakidaya kwanyarta ta toshe ta yadda bazata iya fahimtar komai ba ...

Cikin wani irin yanayi AJ yabaro gidan ,duk zuciyarsa babu dadi ,dan haka ko ahanya karatun alkur'ani  yasaka a motarsa domin sama masa nutsuwa....
.
Tun daga wannan ranar yasoma jin haushin kansa da zuciyarsa domin ya fahimci babu makawa bare dubara ,bakon yanayin so ne ya tsinci kansa aciki game da yarinyar ,haushin kansa yakeji sosai ta yadda zuciyarsa tafara son wacce zata auri waninsa ,batare da zuciyar tayi shawara da shiba ,daman wannan masifar shi ne so ?meyasa kaf mata itace ta ja hankalinsa ,_saboda tana da kyawawan dabi u daba kowace mace ke da suba_
Itace amsar da zuciyarsa ke basa aduk lokacin da ya tambayi kansa ...

A jumeta kuwa kiran sirri ne ALH Aminu yayiwa AJ ,dake sunday ce ,itace ranar da AJ ke samun ful time din kansa ...Yakuma sanar masa da kar yaje mai nagge estate domin zuwansa zai iya dagula komai ,yakuma lura AJ din baisan meke faruwa ba ,
Jirgin sha biyu na rana AJ yabi ,domin ya lura kiran ujila ne abbah Aminu kemasa ...
A guest house dinsa sukayi sallar azahar  tare da Abbah aminu ,bayan sun gama ne ,cikin nutsuwa abbah yasanarwa da AJ komai da abinda ke faruwa ,ya dora da cewar "banyi yunkurin sanarwa usman ba ,domin ko kusa banaso atauyeka inaso naji amsa kai tsaye daga bakinka ,idan ka amince alhmdlllh ,idan baka amince ba zance yakare zaa binnesa bazai kuma tashi ba ,abdul majeed meye ra ayinka akan nafisa ...

Cikin nutsuwarsa mai jan hankali AJ ya dubi Abbah aminu  cikin ladabi yasoma magana "ban tab'a duban nafisa da wani ido ba da ya wuce na kanwa ta ba ,abbah bawai nufin cin fuskaba amma nafisa bata cikin jerin matan da nakeso ,amatsayin kanwa ta jini nake dubanta kawai"...cikin ladabi ya idar da zancen
Numfasawa abbah aminu yayi cike da gamsuwa "na fahimceka Abdulmajeed ,nakuma ji dadin yadda ka fadamin gaskiya ,to amma kasani mu ma iyayenka muna da iko da kai bazamu zubamaka idanu kana yin rayuwa yadda kake so ba ,tunda nafisa bata zuciyarka ,yanxu kafadamin wacece azuciyarka ,ko kuma ni na zaba maka ayanxu wacce nasan ta dace dakai...

cikin faduwar gaba ya dubi abbah aminu dake tsimayin jin amsarsa ,bakidaya tunaninsa ya tsaya ,duk da yassn abbah aminu yana da adalci ,to fa shi kaifi daya ne akan abinda ya fada ....

Ganin yadda yanayin da AJ din yashiga yasanya alh aminu sassauta zancensa "na dibarmaka lokaci ne dan karami akan kafito da mata ,kowane lokaci zan iya tambayarka ,matukar babu amsa ,to kasani amatsayinmu na wadanda muka isa da kai zamu fitar maka da mata acikin zuriaru"...

Dafa kafadar AJ da ya sauke ajiyar zuciya Abbah aminu yayi cikin kulawa yasoma magana "Abdulmajeed matuka mahaifinka ya damu da rashin aurenka,yanayimin complain sosai ...Dan allah ka jaddada farinciki azuciyar mahaiifinka"....

Cikin girmamawa AJ ya dubiabbah Aminu da fararen idanunsa ya amsa da "insha Allah abbah zanyi kokarin yin hakan "...

Sosai abbah aminu yaji dadi ,sai uku da rabi  na rana AJ ya nufi airport wanda driven abbah ne yakaisa ,acikin garin katsina biyar na yamma tayimasa ..

Sosai Aj ke juya maganganun abbah aminu shikansa ya gaza samun mafita ,meyasa zuciyarsa ta banbanta da sauran mutane sam bakowace mace bace keda zarrar iya burgeshiba ,haka yake wani lokacin tamkar dutse ...Saidai a yan kwanakin sosai ya dage da addu oin neman zabin allah da istahara ,ya kuma nisanta kansa daga ziyarar iyayen jalila duk dan yasamu saukin zuciyarsa saidai ya gaza yaki da zuciyarsadatake ingizasa da son ganin jalila saidai lokaci lokaci yakanyimusu aike ,matuka jin haushin kansa yakeyi da zuciyarsa ta fada qaddarar son yarinyar da aka bada ta ........


Ganin yadda Aj tamkar yayi burus da zancen Abbah aminu ne (dayake yakira abbah yafadamasa yadda sukayi da Aj din )
Hakan yakuma batawa abbah rai fomin aganinsa matukar zaa biye ta abdulmajeed bazai taba yin aure ba .
Dan haka tun dawowar AJ daga office,ko hutawa baiyiba abbah yakirasa akan ya samesa apalonsa ...

Bai bata lokaci ba yaje,awannan kaon abbah bai saukakawa AJ ba ,yakuma tabbatar da tunda bayada niyyar fito da mata yabaiwa abbah aminu wuka da nama kada ya saurati AJ ya zabar masa matar da ta dace dashi tunda baya da niyyar yin komai ,...

Cikin tashin hankali AJ ya dubi ammie da dukkan alamu itama goyon bayan zancen abbah takeyi riko hannunta yayi "dan allah ammie kibaiwa abbah hakuri ,meyasa nima bazaa bani dama ba kamar kowa ?"...

Zame hannunta tayi tare da kawar da kai tacigaba da cewa koma miye abdulmajeed kai ka janyo kuma ayanxu lokaci yakuremaka ko wane lokaci zaka iya sanin wacece aka zaba mka nafisa ko kuma wata acikin gidan "..

Cikin tashin hankali AJ yacije lips dinsatare da runtse idanunsa da suka sauya kala ya waresu akan abbah ,
Cikin son samo mafita ya dubi abbah a yanxu ku bani dama ta ,saboda ina da wacce nake so ".....

Ga za cigaba da zancen yayi kamar yadda suka zubamasa idanu danjin abinda zai fada ...

Sassauta murya abbah yayi "wacece ita "....

Cikin sanyin jiki na rashin samun mafita AJ yace "sunanta jalila ...,yakuma fada musu komai dangane da ita ....

Duban abbah ammie tayidan kuwa tagane yarinyar abbah duban ammie yayi dan shidin batabajin zancen yarinyar ba...
a wannan lokacin duk sanyin AC dake falom AJ gumi yakeyi ,duk tunaninsa a hargitsa yake,maganar Abbah tadawo dashi hankalinsa "ka turomin da address na gidansu yarinyar domin a satinnan za aje aji magana ...tashi kaje ....


Duk da jarumtaka ta AJ saida zuciyarsa ta buga da karfin gaske ,ina ma mafarki ne yakeyi ayanxu ,tun kafin yabar falon abbah yakejin ya tabka babban kuskure akan abinda yafadawa abbah ,yakuma san hakan haramunne,wato neman aure acikin nema ,shikansa baiyi dan son zuciyarsa ba illah samarwa kansa ,baikuma taba tunanin abbah zaiyiwa lamarin gaggawa haka ba ,matuka yaji bai kyauta ba ,ta yadda yagaza samun nutsuwa awannan ranar ,bai kyautawa jalila da iyayentaba dan me zai masu haka ,bayan yasan yana da matukar daraja da kima a idanusu ......

: Gaza hakura AJ yayi saida ya tuntubi ammie akan maganar dan bazai iya tunkarar abbah  kaitsaye ba ...

: Gyaran murya mal Abdullahi yayi cikin nazari yasoma magana "sakonka ya iso ya kuma karb'u ,naji dadi matuka yadda ka girmamu duk da bamu da yanci ,kabi duk wasu sharuddan neman aure da addini ya tanadar ,hakan yakara nuna min lallai kai mutum ne nagari dakowa zaiyi alfaharin hada iri dakai ,wallahi Abdulmajeed ban baka jalila bisa dukiyarkaba ,illah dalilin  zallar nagartarka,da sanin darajar dan adam   da yakamata duk iyaye su dinga duba akai ba tarin dukiyaba ,ina fatan ubangiji ya tabbatar da duk alkhairan dake ciki ..Bari na shiga na turomaka ita ...

Bangaren su Abbah Aminu kuwa  tun bayan da suka koma jumeta daga nemawa AJ aure ..Babu wanda yasan zancen sai hajiya innah ,abbah aminu ne ya hana asanarwa mutanen gidan ,yafiso a nutse yafara fadawa hajjah babbah da itace babban tashin hankalinnasu ...
Ko da abbah aminu ya fadamata matukar nuclear ta tashin hankali saida ta tada ta.
musamman da Abbah ya fadamata AJ bazai auri nafisar ba yakuma nemi da tabar zancen ,allah yabaiwa nafisar wani ...
da tayi masifar tagaji kawai ta sanya kuka angwada mata iko akan AJ ,angwada mata ba ita ta haifi mahaifinsa ba ,dan da hajiya innah ce aida tuni an aiwatar da ke ita ce tahaifi alh usman ,takuma gitta ranta da babu makawa saita baiwa  nafisa abinda takeso ...
Baki daya yayanta sun taru kanta sai faman bata baki akeyi ....

bangaren hajiya innah kuwa sosai tayi sambarka da lamarin ta kumaji tasamu nutsuwa akan yarinyar duk da bata ganta ba ...

Nafisa kuwa kulle kanta takumayi a daki ,ita bisa dole saita rataye kanta dan bataga amfanin rayuwarta ba idan babu ya Aj ..
Hajja da hajiya zuwaira sunfi kowa shiga matsanancin tashin hankali ...
Taruwa sukayi abakin kofar dakin nafisar da ta datse suna bata baki ...Amma kamar zugata sukeyi ...
Su abbah aminu da yayi rarrashin hajja ya gaza shawo kanta ,sashin ya bari cikin takaici ,baikuma bi takansu ba ...
Baffah aliyu kuwa da ransa yayi kololuwar baci akan iskancin nafisa ....
Cikin zafin rai da zuciya  yafara bugun kofar da karfi ,yakuma rantse mata da allah ya balle kofar dakansa zai kasheta matukar bata budeba............
