nafisa kuwa tayi matukar tsorata dajin furucin baffa aliyu ,kwarai tasani baida wasa kamar yadda ya fada din ko bai kashe ta ba sai ya illata ta ...

A tsorace ta murda key din kofar ..Basu jira ta bude kofar ba baki dayansu suka bankado kofar dakin ....

Tartsatsin wuta tare da gilmawar taurarin wahala na fisa ta gani akwayar idanun ta sakamakon jin tagwayen zafafan mari a kumatunta...Wanda baffah aliyyu ya sakar matasu ....
Dasauri hajja babbah ta rungume nafisa yadda taga baffa aliyu na huci kamar kububuwa ,da yadda yake shirin yo kanta da duka ...

"shashashar yarinya kawai ,ta silarki duk wanda ke gidannan saida  hajja ta daga masa hankali ,da ke bakida tausayi dan ance baza 'a aura miki shiba shi ne zaki kashe kanki ,to saidai ki mutu din dan kuwa yace baya sonki din ...Ki kashe kan ki dan allah kiga idan zaifasa auren wata bakeba .,dan har yanxu makabartu basu cikaba kullum rana ta allah kai mutane akeyi ,koda kinkashe kanki din babu wanda ya kaiki asara ,domin wuta zaki ,babu ubanda zai hana kasantuwar hakan ,iyaka ayi zaman uku zaman cin shinkafa amanta da ke sakarya....."

Cikin bacin rai hajja ta katsesa "ka fice kabar dakinnan kabarmu da dakon takaicin da dan uwanka (alh aminu ) ya dora min ,..tunda sam baku kaunar farincikin nafisa Idan kuma zaka tsaya bakin cikinka ya kasheni  to sai inji....

Ta idar da maganar tare da fashewa da kuka ...cikin matukar bacin rai baffah aliyu yabar dakin ,zuciyarsa nayi masa suya ,domin duk abinda nafisa keyi daurin gindin hajja dana iyayenta ne tasamu...
Nafisa kuwa har lokacin gunjin kuka takeyi ,har zuwa lokacin  bata daina ambatar ita saita mutu ba ....

Duk wanda ke dakin ganin kwam kawai sukeyi ,hajiya zuwairah kam itama kuka tasanya dan bilhakki tadauka naffisar kashe kanta zatayi ....
hajiya ikilima takaici ya hana ta tsayawa ,baffah aliyu na fita tabi bayan sa takoma sashinta ....yarinya duk ta gigitasu saboda kulafucinsu akanta.

Hajiya jamila kuwa saida akaci aka tsire agabanta sannan ta baro sashin tana dariya bayan tagama yiwa hajjah famfo Itakuma tana zirarewa ....

Hajiya innah kuwa ko leqe bata leka ba ,domin tasan tsabar shagwaba da iya shegene kawai ke damun nafisa ,koda labari yazomata ,allah yakyauta kawai tace ...
Su lailah da kanwarta suhaila dake falon innah ,dariya sukayi sosai,tamkar comedy  suke kallom abun  ,sukam dama anbar banzar ta kashe kanta idan tana iyawa din ...

Ko ina kashiga a mai nagge estate ,jujjuya zancen akeyi,wasu na dariya wasu na allah yakara  ...
Abbah aminu kuwa yafi karfin sati idan ya gaida hajja bata amsa masa gaisuwar .
 Hajja na nan akan qudirinta ,akan kota halin kaka sai AJ ya aure nafisa ,dan bazaa zira masa idanu ya dinga kawo cikas a zuria ba domin hakan tamkar bada kofa yakeyi wa matasan gidan da su dinga bijirewa al adunsu na auren zumunci ...
Zuwa wannan lokacin hajjah dakyar ta shawo kan nafisa dake barazanar ita zata kashe kanta ,saidai awannan lokacin nafisa ta koma yar zaman d'aka dan batada aiki sai afa kwaya da kurban syrup taita bacci ,dake hajja bata san abinda kan takuramata yasanya kowa yakawo idanu ya sanya ma nafisar...
Hajiya rumaisa dake aminiyar hajja da taji labarin abinda ke faruwa ,har gida ta iske hajjah ,ta cigaba da zugata da mata famfo ..Hajjah kuwa tabi tazauna daram dan kuwa ta rantse ta maya babu boka babu malam sai AJ ya auri nafisa idan kuma ba haka ba bata amsa sunanta na suwaiba ba,itadai bata damu da auren yake kokarin yiba ,kawaidai ya amince da auren nafisa.....

----------
Gaza furta komai AJ yayi sai zubawa jalilar idanu yayi wadda yagama fahimtar kallon tuhuma take masa,dakyar yasamu abun cewa amatsayinsa na wanda yake ganin yamata laifi "look..Jalila ..Nasan ni mai laifi ne da nayi kutse acikin soyayyar da kikewa wani ,ba haka nake ba ,bana nufin hakan kuma abun ne duk yazo da rashin fahimta wanda ni ne na janyo wannan kuskuren ,but ..Ni zan samu babah awarware zancennan ,banason auren dole banason ta silata ayimiki ..Kingane ?"...
Ya idar da zancen cikin wani irin yanayi tare da sauke ajiyar zuciya ...
Sai alokacin jalila ta dubesa sosai cikin fahimtar zancensa  ...

"yallabai abinda na fada maka na cewar aure zaamin ina nufin duk sanda allah yakawomin miji tsayayye to zasu aurar da ni ne ...To amma ...Meyasa duk kyawawan mata masu aji da kudi basuyi maka ba ,sai ni ce kadai zan iya jan hankalinka ,ina tababa akan hakan ,bawai kuma dan baka isa aso ka ba ,a"ah ,matsayina da naka tamkar hadin gambiza ne ...".


Sauke ajiyar zuciya AJ yayi yacigaba da kallonta tcikin sanyin murya yace "babu dan adam din da zai iya yiwa so bugun gaba ,haka babu wanda zai iya hanasa shiga zuciyar kowa ye ya tunkara ,tamkar mutuwa ce da idan tazo saita dauka ,to haka so yake da nufi zuciya sai ya shiga batare da shawara ba ..Ki zagaye kaf duniya da tambayar mutane ,meyasa so yake shiga inda baa gayyacesaba ?amsar itace babu !!!idan kinsami amsar za'a cemiki "saboda ubangiji ne ya sanyasa atsakanin bayinsa bai kuma banbance sarki ko mai kudi da talaka ba ...Zai iya shiga inda yakeso babu wanda zai masa shamaki "....

Wani irin shock ne jalila taji tun daga kwalwarta har ya tsanta ,awannan lokacin dad'a jin muryarsa take kamar sarewa ,....
Ganin batada abun cewa yasanya AJ bude motarsa ya dauko wata leda ya mika mata ,"ga wannan ,yakamata ki shiga gida dare yayi "...
Yayi maganar yana kallon agogonsa da ta nuna 9:01pm ....

Yacigaba da cewa "kiyi shwara bisa adalci akan abinda zuciyarki ta baki amma karki tilastawa zuciyarki "...

Cikin sanyin jiki jalila ta karba ,kawai ta tsinci kanta da gaza hada tsimammun idanunsa da nata ...A sanyaye tayimasa godiya ta karba ...

Saida yaga shigarta gida kafin yabar kofar gidannasu .....

A daren jalila gaza bacci tayi ...cikin nutsuwa take shafa sumar madina dake rungume ajikinta tana murmishi tana tuno  kalamansa masu dadinji ...
Ta dubi innah dake kan katifa tana ta baccinta ,tasaki murmishi da sauke ajiyar ...
Bata bukatar shawara da zuciyarta domin zuwa yanxu ta harbu da kwayar son yallabai ,taya zaace bazata so shi ba ,bayan damar samun irinsa dakowace mace ke tsimayin samu arayuwarta ....Ga nagartasa da iyayenta suka shaida hakan ,itace yau yallabai keso ?anya?ji takeyi kodai mafarki takeyi ne ,idan kuwa gaskiya ne wajibine akanta ta tashi ta godewa allah ,hakika bahaka kawai ubangiji yakawo su kasar nigeria ba ,saidan hakan yazama *MUSABBABIN* warwarewar dukkan matsalarsu ...
A hankali ta kwantar da madina ,da bata iya bacci sai ajikinta ,ta fita ta dauro alwaala tayi salatus shukur ..Bayan ta kammala ne tasamu kwarin guiwar bude ledar da ya bata ...
 Kayan makulashe ne kawai aciki  .. Sai  tsadadden turare dake cikin kwali mai sanyayyen kamshi ...

Lumshe idanu jalila tayi lokacin da ta shaki sanyayyan kamshin turaren ...
A daren dakyar ta iya rintsawa wani irin farinciki ne fal a zuciyarta ....

Saida akayi kwanaki hudu sannan AJ ya dawo ...
Kafin kwanakinnan ,matuka yayi yaki da zuciyarsa domin jalila damar tunani akansa ,to haka bangare jalila kuwa matukar kaguwa itama tayi ganin kwanakin sun cika ,hatta innah saida ta fahimci sabon sauyin jalila ....

Yasamu karbuwa matuka r gaske agun jalila duk da awannan lokacin matukar kunya ta hanata furta masa amma ta zahirin soyayyar da take masa ya fuskanci hakan ...

Soyayya mai karfi tayi nisa tsakaninsu ,zuwa wannan lokacin AJ ya siya mata babbar waya ,..
Batare da andauki lokaci ba su abbah aminu su kayiwa iyayen jalila tunin sa rana kamar yadda al adar hausawa take,Tare da sadaqinsu naira dubu dari cib,limamin masallacin jumaar unguwar shi babah yasaka tun lokacin da aka fara zancen auren ,kuma shine yakarbi sadaqin yabaiwa baban . ...babah kuwa har kauyen ringizau yaje ya gwadowa yadikko atine sadakin tare da karin bayani akan auren ...Sosai tasanya albarka akan lamarin

Wasa wasa dai biki ya kusa, 2months remaining ,bangaren AJ duk wani shiri dayakamata yayi ,ya yi ,yabarwa su majaheed da ibrahim da suketa rawar kafa akan bikin ...Sosai dai hidima ta fara kankama ....

Ranar juma'a dake kurarren lokaci ne da yamma likis AJ ya baro office ...Yana cikin driving ne jalila ta kirasa ..Batare da bata lokaci ba yayi picking ,saidai tunkafin ya yi magana "sautin kukanta ya daki kunnensa ...Kuka take yi sosai ta yadda bata iya furta komai...

Cikin matukar tashin hankali AJ yasauka daga akan titi tare da  tambayarta abinda ke faruwa ...Rashin samu amsarta ne da kuka yahana ...

Hakan yasa ya tada motar tare  cilla  motarsa akan titi yayi u turn ya nufi unguwarsu dan sanin tashin hankalin da  yake faruwa ..
 Abinda ya tarar ya matukar daga masa hankali ,mutanene akofar gidan sunyi jugum jugum ,hakan ya tabbatar masa da rashi akayi kenan ,to waye ya mutu?...Cikin wata irin faduwar gaba ya balle murfin motar ya fito da sauri ,inda babah yake ya tunkara ,tun kafin AJ yayi magana baba ya share kwallar da ta gangaromasa cikin jarumta da tawakkali yake sanarwa AJ da rasuwar mai dakinsa wato mahaifiyarsu jalila ,ciwon ta ya tashi na dare daya....

ambaton sunan allah kawai AJ yake yi kafin nutsuwarsa ta dawo ...Sosai yakeson ganin jalila dan sanin halin da take ciki ayanxu ....

Sai gab da sallar magriba aka kai innah gidanta na gaskiya ....

acikin gidan kuwa makwabta ne ke ta rarrashin jalila da zuwa lokacin hawaye kawai ke zuba sautin kukan baya fita ..yadikko atine tayi rarrashin har ta zubawa sarautar allah idanu ...Baki daya dai gidan babu dadi ...
Bayan andawo ne daga kaita babah yayiwa AJ jagora cikin gidan domin yin gaisuwa ....
.Hankalin AJ yayi muguntashi ganin yadda lokaci daya jalila ta fice daga hayyacinta fuskanta tayi jajir ....Bayan sun gama gaisuwar, kudi masu yawan gaske ya ajiyewa su yadikko atine , yana  fita soro yakira layin jalila  amma akashe ,yanaso matuka ya rarrashi abun kaunar tasa da yanayin da yaganta kadai ya kara haddasa masa damuwa ...zuciyarsa adagule ya koma wurin taaziyar ...Sai 10:30pm ya bar gidansu jalila ....

Washe gari kuwa driver yasanya yakawo kayan abinci kala kala da duk abinda ake bukata ..
.Ammie da abbah da su abbah aminu  ,har dasu mujaheed ,ibrahim ,salimduk sunzo gaisuwa daga jumeta  ,hajiya innah ma ta aiko da sakon gaisuwarta da kudin sadaka masu yawa ...
Ranar adduar uku kuwa 100k ammie ta bada domin ayi sadaqa ,abbah kuwa wasu nau in kayan masarufin ne ya bada ...Dake akwai wadataccen abinci sai gidan ya tumbatsa da jamaa ....
Har lokacin AJ baisamu lokacin ganin jalila ba ,saidai time by time yakanyi amfani da wayarsa wurin tura mata kalamai masu sanya tsantsar tauhid a zuciya ,hakan kuwa yayi tasiri wurin rage mata damuwa....

Bayan anshare zaman uku ne kowa yakama gabansa ..Gidan ya rage sai yadikko atine kadai ,tunda basuda kowa da ya wuce ita ...Alokacin mutuwar ta dawowa jalila sabuwa dal ...Taci kuka tamkar bazata daina ba ...Matsanancin tausayin yar marainiyar kanwarta ta takeji da ba'a cireta daga mama ba ,saidai madara da ake bata alokacin ....sosai takejin kaunar yaribyar aranta da batada sama da ita aduniyar nan ,haka kaddarar tasu tazo ,tana fatan ubangiji ya basu ikon cinye wannan jarabawar ...
Babah kuwa duk jarumtarsa dole ka gane yana cikin matukar damuwa da kewa na rashin mai dakinsa ...
Bakidaya dai gidan yaki musu ba dad'i...

Bangaren AJ kuwa ya tsananta kulawarsa akan jalila domin babu abinda take bukata sama da hakan ,matukar tausayinta da kaunarta ke kara linkaya azuciyarsa ...

Fannin saka rana kuwa babu abinda yasauya ,domin cewar babah ,abinda allah ya kaddaro kenan kuma ya riga ya faru ,d'age bikin bashida wani amfani .."

Hakan bakaramin kima ya dada jawowa babah ba domin matuka su abbah aminu su gasgata dattako irin na dattijon..
Ya dikko atine kuwa da zata tafi ta nemi babah da yaba ta madina tazauna ahannunta tasha magani tacigava da shayar da ita ...

Rikicewa jalila tayi domin gani takeyi za 'a ida ravata da farincikinta ,domin yarinyar kadai take duba tasamu sa'ida arayuwarta ...Kuka sosai ta saka akan bazaa rabata da ita ba,domin ana bata madara tana sha ,ga shakuwa sosai tsakaninsu ...Da babah yafara kokarin rabasu din ,dakatar dashi yadikko tayi ..Domin tasan tilas aure zai rabasu ....
Haka dai yadikko atine takoma kauyensu ..Zuciyoyinsu duk babu dadi .....

A haka kuma biki na ta karasowa bangaren jalila babu abinda takeda niyhar yi ,koda AJ yabata kudi kin karba tayi domin sam bata tunanin akwai abinda zatayi dasu ...
Bai matsa mataba domin yasan har yanxu radadib zafin mutuwar mahaifiyarta ne ke damun ta ...
yadikko tun saura kwanaki goma ta kuma dawowa ,tsab ta fara gyara amarya sosai da nagartattun kayan gyaran jiki na mata ,haka da na sha ...
Har kuma lokacib jalila bata yarda anrabata da madina ba saboda hidima ba ,idan baka sani ba zaka iya cewa itace ta haifeta dan tsananin shakuwar da ke tsakaninsu ...
Acikin wadannan kwanakinne aka kawo lefe na gani na fada ,ba acewa komai domin matuka AJ ya narka dukiya acikin akwatunan guda shidda da yayo ..Saidai ayi sam barka

A jumeta kuwa ,tamkar anan ne zaayi bikin dan gidan yafara nuna hakan ...Ran hajja idan yayi dubu yagama baci dan gani takeyi tamkar da biyu ake wasu abubuwan ...Har zuwa wannan lokacin bata tankawa abbah aminu koda yagaisheta ,domin aganinta shi ne jaa gaba azancen auren ...Bama shi kadai duk wanda yayi ruwa yayi tsaki acikin yayanta akan zancen auren fushi takeyi dashi .
Ganin abunnata bana karewa bane  yasanya abbah aminu dakansa yakai karar hajja agun yayanta alh gidado...
Har gida yazo ya sameta yayi mata fada sosai ,da kara nusar da ita karfin tawakkali yakuma gargadeta da duk wata bakar manufar dake ranta akan bikin ta janyeta,duk abinda ubangiji ya kaddara faruwarsa babu wanda ya isa ya hana  ...

Sanin yadda takejin maganarsa ne yasaka tagaza cewa komai sai kuka da ta sanya bayan tafiyarsa ,wai kamarta zaa akai kararta  da tsufanta,saboda an maida batasan metakeyiba kenan? ,wannan wani cin mutunci ne kawai alh aminu yayi kudirin yi mata ...

Tilas ta zubar da makaman yakinta badan ta goge abun azuciyarta ba ....
Nafisa kuwa ganin bakinciki na shirin kasheta yasanya mahaifinta alh mansoor gaggawar sama mata admission a malaysia domin fara karatu acan,kasancewar akwai abokin business dinsa dake zaune da iyalansa acan ....yayi hakanne dan kawai ya faranta mata rai ..
Hajiya zuwairah kuwa sosai tayi rarrashin nafisar akan ta dangana daga shegen nacin son da takewa AJ ....

Tun saura kwanaki biyar nafisa tabar nigeriar cikin bakin ciki da takaicin wata ceya Aj zai aura ba ita ba ,hajja babbah ta cikuka sosai ......

To kowa dai gidan na farinciki Banda hajja da magoya bayanta ...
tun saura kwanaki biyu bikin mutanen jumeta suka fara tafowa kt ...
Lailah tun saura kwanaki uku sukayo gaba ...
: Tako ina gidan Alh usman yadauki haramin yan biki,tuni anfara shagulgulan biki ...
haka bangaren ammie daga saudiyya yan uwanta duk sunxo ,anayi dasu
Cikin  shagulgulan  bikin,har da bikin  al adarsu na fulanin usul da suka gabatar daren biki wanda yayi matukar kayatar da mai kallo ...
bangaren amarya jalila ,babu wani ahagali da akayi sai waazi da aka gabatar adaren biki ...Shikansa AJ daya fahimci bason shagulgulan bikin takeyi ba ,sai ya kyaleta ,amma lokaci zuwa lokacin yakanyi mata aiken kudi ko,zata yi wani abun ....
Babah dakansa ya samu jalila adaki yayimata nasiha mai shiga jiki akan aure yakuma gargadeta daduk rintsi kada ta manta itadin wacece ,ta iya hakuri da kauda kai ,duk da yanada matukar yakini akan jalila domin tasani shiga cikin wannan ahali mai yawa dole saitayi hakuri ,amatsayinta na wacce batada kwa sai wanda ya aureta din ....

Matukar gaske tayi kuka sosai dan kalaman babah sun shige ,musamman akan riko da sunnar manzo (SAW)
Washe garin ranar asabar ne aka daura auren jalila Abdullahi da inspector abdulmajeed usman ,daurin auren daya samu halartar manya manyan yan kasuwar kasar nan , daga bangaren abbah kenan ,bangaren amarya kuwa limamin masallacin jumaa ne deke unguwarsu shi ne ya bada auren jalila ....

Hamdala AJ yayi ga ubangiji adaidai lokacin da dubban jamaa suka shaida daurin aurennasa ,tahmidi yakeyiwa ubagiji da ya cika masa burinsa na ganin wannan rana da kowa ke fatan ganinta ,abokan aikinsa da yan uwansa sai tsokanarsa sukeyi ,sam baya biye su ,yau dai kam allah ya qaddaro jalila tazama matarsa ta har abada...hakika aure rahama ne shikansa awannan ranar rana ta musamman da yakeji atattare da shi ...haka aka sha pics tun awurin daurin auren ..Grapher ya sha kudi ,makida da maroka duk sun samu rabonsu ,dan babu kunya AJ ya dinga barin kudi tamkar tsintosu yayi ..
da yamma likis wasu tsadaddun motoci ne suka dira akofar gidansu jalila domin daukar amarya

Amarya jalila kuwa kuka takeyi mai dauke da tashin hankali dan alokacin da motocin dazasu kaita dakinta suka zo ,nasiha babah yakara yimata musamman akan hakuri da sabuwar rayuwar da zata tsinci kanta aciki ...Da kauda kai ...duk da yasan jalila bada gayaba domin ita macece mai sanyin hali Shikansa babah saida yayi hawaye ,mutuwar matarsa ta zame nasa sabuwa ....

har yadikko ta  shigar da jalila mota acikin jerin motocin da AJ yakawo sama da guda shida ,babah bai daina sanya mata albarka ba ....Haka itama jalila bata daina kuka ba ,musamman na rabata da kanwarta da akayi ,da takeji tamkar an rabata da wani sashi na rayuwarta ...
Babu laifi kam yan unguwa sunyiwa jalila kara,dan kuwa batada wasu kawaye wadanda takeda ,suma yan unguwa  galibinsu ganin kom ne yakaisu.....

Kerarran gida ne mai shegen kyau da tsari AJ yayi dake anan lay out ,matuka naira tayi numfashi agidan daya gaji da haduwa ...Babu ce kawai ke babu a makeken gidan komai na gidan daga waje akayi order ..Komai saidai ace masha allah,kowa dai santi yakeyi matuka...haka yadikko tadinga al ajabin kyawun gidan 
Mutanen jumeta ma da suka cikin gidan saida suka yaba da tsantsar haduwar gidan ...
Su hajiya rabi atu kuwa har sunfara baza kananun maganganu akan funitures na gidan wai komai AJ  ya yi ,babu abinda bangaren amaryar sukayi,tamkar babu kan gidansu amaryat tak@rene  ..Sunso dai sudanyi karamar magana to babu wanda ya kulasu ,hakan yasa suka bige da tabe baki ...Su laila da fatima husna duk suna gun amarya jalila ,duk da tana cikin yanayin kuka amma sun bada gudunmuwa gun rarrashin ta...

Sai da DR junaidiyya tazo dake duk da ita akafara bikin sannan jalila tadan fara samun nusuwa ,duk da yadikko dake gefenta amma bata saka musu baki a firar tasu ,tadan saki jikinta domin acikin su dr kadai tasani ,duk da ta yaba da wayewarsu lailah ...


Sai tara da rabi na dare sannan yan rakiyar amarya aka maidasu gidajensu ...Ya rage sai mutanen jumeta da yan uwan ammie kadai ,suma sai wurin 10pmdrivers suka mayar dasu gida ..

amarya ita da yadikko,sai madina da tagama wasanta tayi baccinta ajikin jalilar ,sukadaine suka kwana agidan  tamkar yadda al adarsu take ...

Aje kuwa koda ya shigo gidan a sashinsa ya wuce ,daman gidan sashe uku ne ...
Yaso kiran jalilar amma sanin yanayin gajiyar biki yasanya ya fasa ..Wanka kawai yayi ya kwanta cike da tunanin abar begentasa

Washe gari kuwa manya manyan flask neshake da nau ikan abincin karin kumallo driver ya kawo,wato sakon ammie ..Kadan jalila taci ,taji ya isheta ,yadikko kuwa rufeta tayi da fada akan dole sai tqci dan gudun yunwa ..Ganin yadda jalilar batason cin abincin yasanya yadikko kyaleta ita tacigaba da ci ...

Da yamma likis yadikko tayi wa jalila jagora domin damka amanarta ahannun surukarta wato ammie ,driver ne yakaisu...


Matukar karamci da dattako yadikko tagani agun ammie dan sosai ta yaba da kirkinta musamman yadda takarbesu hannu bibiyu dakuma yadda ta nuna rike jalila din tamkar ita tahaifeta duk da baagida daya suke ba ...

Bayan sun dawo ne yadikko tasanya jalila tayi wanka da ruwan turaren da ta hadamata ta shirya sosai cikin wani leshi mai kyau Da tsada .

Nasiha yadikko tayimata mai kara shiga jiki da tasanya jalila sake barkewa da wani kukan...
Cikin hikima yadikko ta rarrasheta takuma tabbatar mata da cewar da madina zata tafi domin kuwa zaman aure tazo ba zaman rainon kanwartaba dan ba a rasa wanda zai rike madina ba ...Kuka jalila takuma fashewa da shi tare da kankame madina da batasan meke faruwa ba "dan allah yadikko kada ki raba ni da deenah ta ,itakadaice yar uwa gareni da nake duba take debemin kewar innah ...Ki dubi shakuwarmu da madina yadikko"..

Ta kuma fashewa da kuka..Asanyaye yadikko ta zame madina daga jikin jalila ,yarinyar na kuka ta yadikko ta goya ta ,itama duk jikinta yayi sanyi ,amma babu yadda zaayi su kyale madina ahannun jalila ...Cikin rarrashi tasoma rarrashinta tare da hikima "kiyi hakuri jalila zan dan tafi da ita ne nawani lokaci kafin nan da wata biyu saina maidomiki ita ..."

Ta haka ne tasamu ta rarrashi jalilar saidai duk da haka batadaina kukanba...
Wuraren magriba yadikko tayiwa jalila sallama tare da fatan allah yabasu zaman lfy ....

Kuka jalila tayi mai tsuma zuciya dataga antafi madina datayi matukar sabo da ita dan itama kukan takeyi ...

Saidatayi mai isarta kafin ta wanke fuskarta tadauro alwalar magrib tayi tare da issha ...

Tana nan zaune kan carpet har tara da rabi tayi ,zuciyarta cike da tunani barkatai ,sai karewa hadadden dakin kallo takeyi ,yanxu wannan duk mallakinta ne ..Tana yiwa deenarta fatan ta dace da miji irin nata ..Sauke ajiyar zuciya tayi Sosai kuma kewar mijinta kedamunta domin tunda aka fara hidimar nan basuga juna yadda yakamataba  ...


Tun kafin ya shigo bedroom dinsanyayyen kamshin turarensa ya ziyarci hancinta ..Lumshe idanu jalila tayi tare da jin sautin faduwar gabanta yadda yake tsananta .......

_tsagwaron soyayya da kuma sadaukarwa_

                 *~LITTAFIN~*

        *MARYAM HAMISU YUSUF (MAMAN BOY)*



*_14_*

Koda ya shigo dakin ya fahimci yadda take matukar jin kunyarsa awannan ranar ,tundaga yadda ta amsa masa sallamar kadai ya gane hakan... 
Cikin wani irin farinciki mai saukar da kasala da wanxuwar bakon yanayi AJ yakarasa inda take zaune akan sallayar ...
Ajiye manyan ledojin da ya shigo dasu yayi a bed side ..Shima akan carpet din ya zauna agefenta tare da tsareta da tsimammun idanunsa ...Tilas jalila takuma sunkuyar da kanta sosai tana wasa da hannunta,dayasha lalle , yayi  mata kyau matuka ,cikin tsadadden boyel yake milk color,hular da ke kafe akansa ta dace da kalan cover shoe din dake kafarsa ,sai kamshi da yaketa zubawa,kai dagani kasan ranar tasace ...
Aj kuwa,ya gaza furtamata komai  idan banda sauke ajiyar zuciya da hamdala babu abinda yakeyi ,sai kayataccen murmishin dake fuskarsa dake nuna zallar nutsuwa....

Ganin yadda duk ta makure ne yasanya mikewa tsaye still har lokacin fuskarsa da murmishi ,ya ajiye hularsa da agogonsa da yacire ..toilet ya wuce kawai ya dauro alwala ..
In 5mins ya fito yana  fitowa cikin kulawa da soyayya ya umurceta taje ta yi alwalar ...
 Kanta akasa tabasa amsa da cewar tanada alwala ,anan ya jasu sallah sukayi nafila raka'a biyu bayan sun idar sosai yayimusu addua taneman tsari da ga dukkan hallita dakuma wanxuwar zaman lafiya atsakaninsu ..Yana idarwa ya dafa kan jalila yayi adduo i sosai tare da shi kansa ,bayan sun kammala ne ,anan ya bude tsarabar da shigo musu da ita bisa sunnah,kaza ce da farfeson yan ciki ,sai fresh milk da sauran nau ikan makulashe ...
jalila dai kam kadan taci shima saida  ya nuna damuwarsa ...
Koda su kagama ,Sashinsa yakoma yasauya kaya zuwa na bacci ....Yaso yabar jalila ita kadai ko ta saki jikinnata ,to saidai yana ganin rashin kyautuwar hakan ...
Koda yakoma bangarenta har ta sauya kaya zuwa na bacci ..dan harta fara bacci ,har ya shigo batasan shigowarsa ba ...
Sosai tamakure akan gadon ...
Murmishi kawai yayi ,sam bazaiyi abinda zai takura ta ba ,kamar yadda ta dauka ba...
Gadon ya hau ,ya tsuramata idanu cikin nutsuwa take baccin ...Ahankali ya shafi fuskarta tare da rungumeta ajikinsa da zummar shima ya samu bacci ...
Anan fa zancen yasauya salo ...Matuka ya gaza control din kansa acikin bakon yanayin da yake ciki har saida ya tabbatar y samu nutauwa atare da ita ,ya kuma sanyata ajerin mata 
Washe gari kuwa cikin matsanancin nishadi ya tashi ..Jalila kuwa dakyar ta ke iya motsa jikinta da yayi mata tsami ,saida AJ ya taimakamata kafin tagyara jikinta ...Cikin jin matsananciyar kunyarsa...

Tafiya tayi tafiya ,soyayya kuwa tayi nisa ,kulafucin so sukeyiwa junansu da tsantsar kulawa ,satin jalila daya ,su kaje kaiwa ammie ziyara ,sosai tayi murna da ganin su musamman yadda taga kwarjini ,kwanciyar hankali yakaru atare da dan nata ,ita din saita samu nutsuwa sosai...hatta abbah yayi sam barka,yakumaji dadin ganinsu
 Saida yamma likis bayan AJ yadawo daga office ya biyo ya dauketa ,shatara ta arziki ammi  tahada ma jalila,hakama abbah baibarta hakanan ba saida yayi mata alheri  tmsunji dadi matukar gaske,ammie kuma  takuma yaba da nutsuwar jalila ...
A cikin satindai sunyi zumunci sosai ,bayan zuwan ta gida gaida babah har gidan DR junaidiyya saida yakaita ,sosai kuwa jalila taji dadi acanma saida ta wuni,
dr junaidiyya tazage gun baiwa jalila shawarwari akan inganta rayuwar aure ,da kayan matan da zadu inganta zaman aurensu ...jalila kuwa tayi matukar jin dadi ,sai dare sukayi sallama ...Kawance sosai ya kullu tsakaninta da junaidiyya ..Maryam kuwa akai akai takan kira jalila su gaisa kasancewar tana umra itada mijinta akayi bikin ...Duk da basu hadu fuska da fuska ba amma matuka tasu tazo daya ,dan jalila macece mai saukin kai da saurin sabo
Satinsu uku da aure sukaje jumeta tare da jalila musamman yadda hajiya innah ke ta ciwon baki anki kawomata kishiyarta ..

Aikuwa jalila tasamu karramawa daga gun hajiya innah takuma yaba da mutuncin tsohuwar sosai tasaki jikinta da hajiya innah ,Anan AJ yabarsu bayan sun gamacin abincin rana ,shikuma suka shiga sallah a babban masallacin dake gidan ....
Saida jalila tayi sallah kmta huta sannan ,sannan ta sanya husna yar hajiya rukaiyya kai ta sashe sashin cikin gidan ..
Haka kuwa akayi ,domin ko ina aka shiga da jalila saita fito da alheri .. tayi farinciki matukar gaske ..Koda sukaje sashin hajiya zuwaira kuwa ,maganganu na cin mutunci da aibantawa tadinga yiwa jalila ,acikin harshen fulatanci,dan ba iya hausa tayi yadda yakamata ba  ,saukinma jalila bata fahimtar abinda take fada hakan yassnya husna tayi saurin fito da ita daga sashin sam bataji dadi ba ...
Itama jalilar duk da bata fahimtar yaren amma tafahimci ba magana mai dadi hajiya zuwaira ke fadamata musamman yadda take jifanta da wani irin kallo ....
Bangaren hajja babbah kuwa duk kanwar ja ce saida ita tayi musu wankin babban bargo ne da harshen da jalilar zata gane ta kuma hada da cin fuskar iyayenta ...Matuka jalila taji ciwo amma kawai ta danne abun azuciyarta ...Saida suka fito ne husna ke dada bata hakuri da fada mata duk abinda ke faruwa ...Jinjina kai kawai jalila tayi ,domin itama taso ta fahimci wani abun ...

Hajiya jamila kuwa babu yabo ba fallasa haka takarbesu ...
: Har dai gidan hajiya rumaisa saida aka kai jalila .....Itama kam babu yabo ba fallasa ,duk da akwai ta ciki atare da ita ....
Aranar dai sunsha yawo sosai ,sai bayan magrib suka dawo wanda driver ne yakaisu akuma sashen baffa hamza suka yada zango ...
Da yake da daddare ne zaka samu ganin iyaye mazan gidan ..To saida daddare ne jalila ta samu gaisawa da su baffa aminu ,sosai sukaji dadi ...

Washe gari kuwa da sha tara ta arziki suka baro jumeta ta ga soyayya taga kiyayya ...Haka dai suka zo innah nata mita ..Akan sukara kwana sam AJ yaki ya wai zaa wahalar masa da mata ....

Koda suka dawo bai barta ta rintsa ba adaren ,saida ya tabbatar nutsuwar ta cudu da gangar jikinsa atare da ita  ...Jalila kuwa bata taba kosawa ba dan ta fahimci mijinnata yanada yawan bukata ..Ahankali har yasabar mata da hakan ........
zuwa lokacin kuwa jalila ta zuba ido ganin an maido mata deena ahannunta amma shiru  .Hakan yasa ta nemi izini gun AJ akan ya barta taje gida saidai bata fada masa komai zatai ba ..Sabon drivern su yasanya yakaita,yakuma ce tagaishe da Baban  ,
Koda tajewa babah da zancen rarrashinta yasoma yi akan ta hakura ,domin deenah bazata dawo gareta ba ,Dubara dai kawai yadikko tayi mata ...Cikin tashin hankali da zubar hawaye jalila ta dubi babah "babah kenan deenah akauye zata tashi kuma a aurar da ita akauyen?"..
Yadda tayi maganar yasanya sa yin shiru tamkar mai nazari ...
Hakan yasa jalila fashewa da kuka "babah kana da dama dan allah ka karbo deenah ,kauyen su yadikko atine ko wutar nepa babu ba ruwan famfo bare makaranta ,acikin irin wannan rayuwar deena zata taso ...?
Numfasawa babah yayi yasoma rarrashin jalila hakika tana da gaskiya to saidai yadikko ce da kafiya ya tabbatar tunda ta tafi da deenah bazata maidotaba musamman yadda bata d'a bare kuma jika ...
"rarrashin jalila yayi akan ta hakura da zancen kawai dan bayason rigimar yadikko ,wannan itace qaddarar madina ,qaddara kuma baa iya guje ma ta ....
Sosai jalila tasaka kuka cikin damuwa kafin ta share hawayenta zuciyarta awani irin cunkushe take ....
Dazata tafi kuwa sosai babah yayimata fada akan kada tasake ,damuwrta tahaifarwa da AJ da damuwa ,dan ya lura yadda yake son jalilar ...
.Saida tayimasa alkhairi ,yakuma sanyamata albarka kafin ta bar gidan ..

Sosai tayi yaki da zuciyarta akan boye abinda ke damunta ..Saidai a yan kwanakin matuka takejin sauyi ajikinta,wani lokacin da zazzabi take kwana hakan yasa ta kira dr junaidiyya awaya tafada mata abinda takeji ...Koda junaidiyya tazo ta duba ta atake tagane tana da shigar ciki na sati goma sha biyu
 Cikin farinciki take sanar da jalila ,atake ta kira AJ tayimasa albishir,dan farinciki ya rasa inda zaisa kansa suna gama wayar yayi tura mata 50k ta account dinta wato goron albishir ...

Jalila kuwa cikin farinciki take godewa allah kafin su tsagaita tasanarwa da junaidiyya damuwarta akan deenah ,kamar yadda suka saba ,basa boyewa juna komai ,shawara junaidiyya ta bata akan ko kusa kada ta dauko deenah yanxu ,tunda dai agida take duk da kauyene tabari sai gaba kadan ,dan basu san yadda AJ zai karbi zancenba ...

sosai jalila ke samun kulawa agun AJ zuwa dai wannan lokacin har ammie tasan da cikin ,yana matkar son yara musamman abinda ya fito daga jalila ...
Awannan lokacin ne maryam su zo ganin amarya jalila da uwar tsarabar da akayimata ...Alokaci daya allah ya hada tasu ,sosai sunyi kama da AJ ,saidai yafi tilon yar uwartasa kyau da hasken fata ... 

Alokacin jalila ta nemi alfarmar da AJ ya barta taje kauyen ringizau tagano madeenah ,yaso ya hanata ganin halin da take ciki ,to yanayin yadda ta damune yasanyasa amincewa ,kudi masu yawa ya turowa jalila wadanda zatayi tsaraba ,kasancewar baya garin daga bangaren jalila kuwa tulin kayan yara ne masu shegen kyau da tsada ta siyawa madina ...
Koda taje kauyen babu laifi yadikko na kula da madeena sosai saidai duk yanayin yarinyar ya sauya zuwa na daban  ,hakan ya matukar damun jalila ...
Ya dikko kuwa tayi farinciki ganin jikartata ,musamman yadda taganta da juna biyu ,,takuma sanya albarka akan kayan arzikin da AJ ya aikomusu
: Jalila da ta fara rokon ya dikko da tabata madina ,fafur taki har abun ya haifarwa ya dikko da bacin ganin hakan yasanya jalila yin shiru ,dan tun fil azal bamai son hayaniya bace ...kwananta uku driver yazo ya maidata ..Cike da kewar madina da tausayinta jalila ta bar kauye harda matsar kwallah a mota ...

Tafiya na tafiya cikin jalila ya shiga wata na bakwai ,cikin na girma cikin koshin lafiya da kulawa ,awannan lokacinne kuma takardar jarabawar karin girma ta hard workers fitowa su AJ wanda harda sunansa a wadanda zasu je course din a kasar canada ....
Wannan babban cigaba ne atare da shi ...sosai suke cikin farinciki ,koda lokacin tafiyar yayi
Ya nuna tare da jalila zaya tafi,fafur ammie taki wai bazai tafi da mai ciki haihuwar fari ba akasar da basu san kowaba
: Sam hakan bai yiwa AJ dadi ba ,yafison koya ya motsa ya jisa tare da  abun kaunarsa ..Jalila kam batada zabi illah bin abinda ammie tace ranar da zasuyi tafiyar har airpot suka rakashi tare da yimasa fatan nasara arayuwarsa ...

Bayan tafiyarsa ne akayi bikin su lailah da husna da fatima da sauran yayan baffah aliyu
: Saidai jalila bata samu damar zuwa ba ,hakama ammie dan bazata iya barin jalila ahalin yanxu haihuwa ba ,
ilas dai suka isar da sakon fatan alkhairinau ga maauratan ....
Ranar alhamis itace ranar da mai nagge family bazasu taba mantawa da ita ba ,domin aranar ne mummunan sako ya sauka kunnensu na accident din jirginsama da abbah yayi a hanyar sa ta dawowa daga dubai akan wani business dinsa wanda yayi sanadiyyar rasa ransa ...
Matukar tashin hankali sun tsinci kansu aciki ,ta yadda babba da yaro cikin jimami da alhini yake ...Ammie kuwa tayi kuka matuka na rashin maigidanta a kwatsam,jalila ma matukar tashin hankali ta tsinci kanta aciki ga tsohon ciki ga tunanin halin da aj zai shiga idan aka sanar masa wannan mummunan labarin ...
A ranar da baffah aminu ya sanarwa AJ rasuwar ,matuka ya tsinci kansa atashin hankali ,duk jarumtarsa saida ya yi kuka tamkar karamin yaro ,dakyar dai baffah aminu ya rarrasheshi akan ya cigaba da masa addua ,yakuma bada himma akan abinda yaje nema ...shikansa baffah aminu karfin hali kawai yakeyi
 Hajiya innah ma tayi kukan rashin dan nata sosai ,dan kuwa a halin yanxu baffa hamza kadai ya rage mata ..
baki dayansu suna akt amsar gaisuwa ....
Baban jalila ma dashi aka share zaman uku agun amsar gaisuwa ..Sabuwar matar da yayi kuwa ita ma saida akayi uku kafin ta koma gida

Bayan anshare ukune su hajiya innah suka koma jumeta domin cigaba da karbar gaisuwar a can ...

Maryam kuwa saida akayi bakwai kafin ta tafi bayan rarrashin ammie dasukeyi ita da jalila da haryanxu ammien taki dawowa a yadda take ...

Bayan tafiyar maryam ne jalila sosai take debewa ammie kewa ,hakan kuwa yayi tasiri sosai ...
Ankwashi kwanaki arbain da rasuwar abbah ,allah yasauki jalila lafiya ,tahaifo santalelen danta mai kama da mahaifinsa sak ,sai lokacin farincikin ammie yadaidaita ...Komai ita keyiwa mai jego yadda yakamata ,tamkar ita din daice ta haifeta
 Koda yadikko tazo zaman bakwai kawai tayi dan ammie cewa tayi karta damu zaa samo wacce zata dinga dorawa jalilar ruwa ,musamman data fahimci yadikko bata cikason zama wuri daya ba ...Anan ne takewa ammie gaisuwar mijinta dan bata garin lokacin daya rasu ....

Ranar suna kuwa kyakkyawan jaririn ya amshe sunan abbah wato usman ana kiransa da sultan ,taro ya yi taro na mai babban suna tamkar yadda hajiya innah ke cemasa tun ranar da sukazo ganin jariri tare da shatara ta arziki wa jariri da mai jego ...

Aj kuwa kullum sai an turasa pic din baby boy dinsa dayake matukar kauna ,kamar yaddda kullum cikin waya suke da jalila sosai yake bayyana mata yadda yayi matukar kewarta ...
Tafiya ta nisa kwanaki sunja ..Aj ya kwashi shekara daya rabi a canada inda kuma waadin dawowarsu ya cika ..
: Ranar da ya diro nigeria ya zo gida mutuwar abbah tadawo masa sabuwa ,ammie ce takoma rarrashinsa ..Haka jalila ma ,..
AJ kuwa ya dangana matukar gaske ..
Koda yaushe yana manne da sultan dinsa yana matukar son yaron ...Kwanaki biyu kawai suka kara suka koma gidansu dake layout wanda aka kuma sabunta fentin gidan da kuma kerarrun funtures ..Anan sukabar cike da kewa ..

A ranar dasuka koma saida AJ yasauke tarin gajiyarsa agun jalilla matuka ya nunamata yayi kewarta ,hakuri kawai yayi agaban ammie dan karta gane dan nata ya cika jara ba .  

ko sati basuyiba jerin manyan ranks din da aka lika a board da ke headquarter na police ya fito ,sunan AJ shine a sama tare da babban mukamin AIG ,matukar farinciki bazai misultu ga AJ da ahalinsa ba domin mukami ne da sai masu shekaru da yawa suke samun irinsa ,amma allah ya cidasa da mukamin ..Bakomai ne yayi tasiri ba illah adduar iyaye da kuma jajircewarsa akan aikinsa ....

haka dai gwamnatin tarayya tayi bikin karramasu wanda manya manyan gidajen tv da radio suka shaida hakan ...
Agida ma kuwa haka ammie ta yi kayatacciyar walima da gayyatar abokan arziki  da yan uwa ...haka dai akayita rabon memo da calender daga bangaren amkie da jalila da itama tayi ta ta walimar ...

Akuma wannan lokacinne allah ya sake baiwa jalila wani cikin ..Tahaifi yarta kyakkyawar gaske saidai tayi kama da madina sosai ,hakan yasa jalila take kulafucin son yarinyar ..Ranar suna ya ta amshe sunan mahaifiyarta wato aisha
 Ana kiranta da iftahal...

Cikin so da kauna suke tarbiyantar dakyawawan  yaran nasu ,masu matikar burge mutane..

Aj kuwa hutu da naira takuma zama ajikinsa ,matukar gaske zaija hankalin duk wanda ya dubesa domin alokacin ya na tattare da kuruciyarsa da kyau ga uwa uba babban mukami .
Jalila ma tazama babbar hajjaju,dan jari mai tsokar gaske AJ  yabata nashigo dakaya da fitar dasu ,komai dai na tafiya daidai .. Ta gina makarantun islamiyya da masallatai fisabillah...

Tako ina haske suke gani arayuwarsu ,ga zamansu maidauke da zallar farinciki da nutsuwa....
Alokacin ubangiji yasake baiwa AJ mukamin kasancewa IG daga AIG ....hakika gaskiya ce matakin nasara dan ita zamemasa mataki ga cimma gaci da kuma jajircewarsa ...

Duk da lokacin ya zama busy amma kokadan baya wasa da lokacin iyalansa da na ammie ,haka ma jumeta yakanje duk karshen wata tare da jalila da yaransa ...

Nafisa kuwa tun lokacin da taje malaysia ,bata sake waiwayar nigeria ba saida takwashe shekaru 8 ...Tu n hajiya babbah na kewa har ta zubawa sarautar allah idanu ,mahaifiyarta kuwa tayi tayi ta dawo ,fafur ta kiya saida ta muli tasha iska sannan ta dawo
Ba hajiya babbah ba hatta mahaifiyarta hajiya zuwairah sun tsorata da salon wayewar da nafisa tadawo da ita ..Dan kallo daya zakayimata kasan taci duniya sosai ,idanu nata tsaitsaye suke ,haka kowa yakawo idanu yasanyamata ....

Ranar dasu AJ suka je kai jumeta kuwa ,matuka nafisa ta kadu da ganin jalila da kyawawan yaran da ta haifowa AJ din ...Atake wani bakin ciki mai dauke da zallar kishi ya tokareta tare da fama mata tsohon mikin son AJ ,musamman yadda taga ta ko ina jalila ta dara ta ...AJ kuwa cikin basar da ita tare da miskilancinsa dake damunsa lokaci zuwa lokaci  ya amsa gaisuwar da ya samu daga nafisa da sam ya manta da ita aduniyar ..Abun ya dad'a yimata ciwo a zuciyarta ...Hakan yasanya ta dinga takular jalila da  masifa ,da maganganu na cinfuska saidai rashin saar da taci jalilar bamai son fitina bace shiyasa sam bata bi takanta ba ...

A wani corridor sukayi kicibis da nafisa jalila kuwa tagama alwala kenan afamfo ..Ta tafo taji nafisa ta bangajeta ..
Wannan lokacin jalila ta tanka mata "meyasa ke bakison zaman lafiya ne "...
Aikuwa abinda nafisa ke nema kenan ta wanketa da mari ,agaban yan aikin gidan ...Tsaki taja tare da jifarta da kalma
Yar matsiyata kibiyoni ki rama "tayi maganar tare da wucewa..
Girgiza kai kawai jalila tayi tayi tafiyarta abunta ...

Tuni labari yaje kunnen hajiya innah ,wanda agaban AJ yaji komai ...
Wannan lokacin bazai yadda ba da cin mutuncin matarsa ba dole yadauki mataki...

Cikin isa da izza yasanya aka kira nafisar ...Bai jira komaiba yawanketa da mari ,dan yasan jalilar bazata iya ramawa ba ...Atake ya umurci nafisar da ke dafe da kunci da tabaiwa jalila hakuri ....
.Cikin zallar bakin ciki da cunkushewar zuciya ta bata hakuri "dan tasan halinsa ,kuma har yanxu tana shakkarsa ...

Sam hajiya innah bataji dadin abinda AJ yayiba hakama jalilar dan batason ta silarta ta na kawomusu cikas agida ...

Hajiya babbah kuwa ta cika ta tumbatsa musamman yadda taga sawun hannun AJ kwance a fuskar nafisa ...

baffa aliyu hajja tasanarwa ,saidai atake ya shaidawa hajiya babu ruwansa da lamarin nagisar dan tafi karfinsa ....

Tun kafin su AJ su iso kt ,hajja babba ta kira ammie tasauke cin mutuncinta akanta da ta jawa danta kunne akan nafisar ...

AJ kuwa babu ko dar a zuciyarsa suka baro jumeta ...
Haka dai zamansu ya cigaba da wakana cikin kwanciyar hankali...

lumshe idanu jalila tayi tare da hamdala ga ubangiji ,da samun cikakkiyar damar maido da DEENAH ahannunta ..ko babu komai rayuwarta zata haskaka daga duhu ...
Cikin nishadin da tajima bata tsinci kanta acikiba take walwalarta ,matuka deenah cikamakon farincikinta ce ....
Gaza hakuri tayi saida takira DR junaidiyya ta sanar da ita amincewar yallabai akan dawowar deenah hannunta ...
Itakanta dr taji dadi domin koba komai ganin deenah atare da ita zai yaye mata wani kaso daga damuwar halin da take ciki ...
Jalila kuwa batayi yunkurin fidda lokaciba saida ta tabbatar IG yasamu lokacin kansa domin dashi zaayi tafiyar kamar dai yadda ya bukata ,yanaso matuka kulawarsa akan jalila ta shafi duk wanda ya jingina da ita ....

Adaren da zasu tafi ne sukaje akayiwa ammie sallama ,tun adaren IG ya umurci atsara komai na tafiyar musamman abinda yadanganci tsaraba ,haka kuwa akayi domin saida aka cika boot da kayan masarufi da aka debo su daga store ....
Tafiyar ta kasance ta sanyin safiya ce dan haka tun 10 na safe ,jerin rantsattsun motocin rakiyar IG da iyalinsa suka shige gaba gun ficewa daga gidan yayinda motarsu ce a tsaka ,qbayansu kuwa motoci biyu ne dauke da jamian tsaro ...
Raras suka jera cikin nutsuwa suka fara ficewa daga gidan ..Kafin gate keeper ya rufe gate din
 Tafiya motocin keyi ahankali har aka soma barin cikin garin kt ....
 Babu abinda kakeji sai sautin sanyin AC din dake ratsa motar da mutanen cikinta ,driver kuwa sosai yamaida hankalinsa akan tukinsa,jalila kuwa a zahiri duban yaranta take cikin murmishi yayinda tunaninta kab yana akan irin rayuwar da deena zata dawo ....

Murmishi sosai IG yayi cikin zallar nutsuwarsa yasanya lallausan hannunsa acikin na jalila ,hakan ya tilastawa jalila dawowa hankalinta tare da kallonsa da murmishi da yagaza boyuwa afuskarta ...

Tsura mata idanu IG yayi cikin wani irin yanayi da kaunarta ke fizgarsa ...
"a zahiri da badini ,farinciki yafi kyalkyali da walwali a kan fuskar nan da annuri baya barinta "....

Cikin jin dadin kalamansa jalila ta langabar da kanta akafadarsa tare da sauke ajiyar zuciya tagaza furta komai  .
Hakan yasanya IG dago da fuskarta ya dad'a dubanta ,irin duban dake darsar da kasala agangar jikin duk wanda akayiwa ...Akasan makoshi yayi magana tattarre da kulawa "ba dan ni ba ,amma kidaina gayyato damuwa da zata zauna afuskarki domin sam ba bagiranta bane anan ,pls hajjaju ta ,"...
Cikin son shagwabar da ita yayimaganar
 Murmishi takumayi tare da kara matse hannunsa dake cikin hannunta ..Ahankali ta furta "nayi alkawari yallabai bazan kuma ba "...

Cikin gamsuwa da zancenta ya jinjina kai tare da kwantar da kanta akafadarsa ....
Babu wanda yasake cewa komai ,saidai kowa cikin nutsuwa da yanayi mai dadi yake ,har suka isa kauyen ringizau ...
Dan karamin kauyene mai dauke da tsirarun gidaje ,da yalwatattun bishiyoyi ..

Jerin motocin da yan gari suka gani shine ya janyo hankalinsu yayinda maza  da ke tafiya ahanya kanyi tsaye tare da bin rantsattsun motocin da idanu ,wasu matan kuwa ta gajerun katangunsu suke lekowa dan ganema idanunsa ...Yayinda yara suka cigaba da bin motocin tamkar nama da k'uda ..

Saida motocin suka tsaya akofar gidan yadikko atine kafin jamian tsaron su soma fitowa da sauri ....yayinda matasan da ke gun 
suka fara shiga da kayan masarufin dake booth 
Budewa jalila kofa sukayi  tafito tare da kara karewa garin kallo ,sosai ya sauya mata dan saida tayi da gaske ta iya gane gidansu yadikko dan ahekarun da ta kwashe bata zo kauyenba ...

Cikin nutsuwa ta bi bayan matasan , yayinda iftahal da sultan suke amota ,wai bazasu fito ba ,sai tare da abbansu dake tsimayen jiran iso agidan yadikko ...

Dake gidan yawa ne ,saida jalila ta ratsa gidaje kafinsu isa sashin yadikko ....

A sanyaye tayi sallama ,yayinda matadan suka fara sauke buhun shikafa da catton na taliya ..Hakan  yayi daidai da fitowar yadikko daga bandaki ...

Cikin faraa da farinciki yadikko ta amsa tare da rike baki ganin irin arzikkn da yasauka gidan ,sannu tayita yiwa matasan dake yan gidanne ,jalila kuwa da dubu dubu tabisu suka fita suna godiya ,yiwa jalila iso yadikko tayi  har cikkn dakinnata
 Tabarmar dake bayan ganbun (kyaure)dakin  ta dauko tare da warwareta atsakar dakin ,sai faman yima jalila tsiya takeyi ,duk sun kwashi lokaci basu hadu ba ..Tun kafin jalila tazauna tasanar mata tare suke da IG ,agaggauce yadikko takara kimtsawa kafin tayimasa iso ...



AJ kuwa yana amota ta cikin duhun gilashin motarsa yake karewa garin kallo da yanayin mutanen garin da suka nisanta da wayewa ...
Lokaci zuwa lokaci yakan dubesu cikin nazarin yadda suke gudanar da rayuwarsu.isowar jalila tasanyasa sauke gilashin kadan ...Iso tayi masa har dakin yadikko shida yaransa ,sanye yake cikin yadin cupter mai ruwan madara ,matuka manyan kaya ke zama ajikinsa da fito da tsantsar kyawun zatinsa na hausa fulani ...

baki dayansu Zama sukayi akan tabarmar  ,cikin ladabi AJ yagaida dattijuwar,,baki a washe yadikko ta amsa tare da Rantaba masa godiya tamkar bazata daina ba ,lokaci daya kofar dakin yadikko ya cika da matan gida ,na ganin bakin birni ...,ruwan tulu ne maisanyi yadikko ta debo musu ,sosai AJ yasha ruwan duk da kuwa na roba dake akwai amota ...hakan bakaramin dadi yayiwa yadikko ba ,anan jalila tafara tambayar madine ...Hassu yar kishin saurinta yadikko tasanya ta takira madinen da ta ce ta tafi gulbi da shan mangwaro..,aikuwa aguje hassu tabar gidan ta nufi gulbi
AJ kuwa sun dan taba fira ta raha ,shida yadikko ,tamkar sunsan juna sosai kafin yakoma mota dan basu wuri su tattauna bisa ganin kyautuwar hakan dayayi...anan yabar su iftahal awurin yadikko ,dasukejin kansu awani bakon wuri.
koda yafito haka yabi matan gida da dari biyar viyar...Suna ta godiya har yakoma amota ...

Cikin burgewa yadikko ta dubi jalila "yar nan kin more miji kekam ,ga hankali dasanin yakamata da kuma ganin girman mutane  ,allah ya cida madine ta itama"..

tabe baki yadikko tayi "babanki mana  ,kinsan nayi waya kwanannan ,shi ne sahalu mai caji  ya saka min numbersa naje kan dutse mukayi waya ,shi ne yake sanarmin komai ..."

gyara zama jalila tayi ,"dan allah yadikko ki taimaka kibani deenah tadawo ahannuna ,idan yallabai ne baida matsala ya amincemin hakan....

"ki taimaka ydikko"...

"ke kau yaya atine kibarmusu ita su tafi mana ,ke kanki kya maro ,hankalinki ya kwanta ,yaranmuma su samu sauki daga zaluncin madine ,Da bata barinsu su sha ruwa indai tana agidan   ..."


Face majina yadikko ta kumayi "anki din ,kinga ladiyo ,arayuwa gulmace zatayi ajalinki ...

Zama ladiyo tayi tafara magana "fiyeda hakan hajjaju ,yarinya ta sangarce duk abinda tayi daidai ne gun yaya atine ,dazarar zaa mata fada kk duka akan abinda tayi badaidaiba yaya atine saita hana cewar marainiyar allah ce ...Bakidya yaranmu wahalarta suke sha ga mugunta da keta ga uwa uba tsiwa..."

jalila ta katseta ta dora dacewa ,"mijina shine bango agareni dake kanki ,dan haka kada kisake kisaba masa ko ki bata masa ,ki daukesa tamkar yayanki ,kamar yadda ki ka daukeni ,ki girmamasa sosai fiyeda yadda zaki girmamani ,kinji ni ko"...

Tabe baki deenah tayi tare da jinjina kai ....
 
fita sukayi daga dakin ,jalila nata nuna mata gidan ,itakuwa sai kauyanci takeyimata ....

kwanan deenah uku ,tun tanajin kewar yadikko har ta ware ,ta saki jikinta sosai da antinta ..hakan ya yiwa jalila dadi musamman yadda ta lura deenah batada bakunta...Saidai har lokacin Deenah  haushin mijin yayartata takeji duk da bata cika ganinsa ba agidan ,bai cika sakin fuska ba ,haka koda tagaishesa a dakile ya ke amsawa takuwa dauki aniyar bazata kuma gaishe shiba ...

Da yamma IG yadawo daga office ruwa ya watsa yasaka doguwar jallabiya ya nufi garden...Tamkar dai yadda suka saba idan Yadawo office da wuri..
Dake ranar alhamis ne sultan da iftahal sun nesa kadan da abbannasu suna ball...

yana zaune kan kujera yana kallonsu cike burgewa da kaunarsu ,musamman yadda suke gudanar da wasan ...

Da murmishi ta dubiyadda Ig ya shagala da kallon yarannasa ..

Iskar bakinta ta dan hura mai ,hakan yatilastamasa lumshe idanunsa tare da  ajiyar zuciya ya ware su  akanta ...
Ya amsa sallamar ...

Fira suka shigayi irin ta masoyan da suka amsa sunansu ,bayan ta tsiyayo masa lemu ne ta mikamasa ta sunkuyar da kanta a sanyaye tacigaba da magana "yallabai inaso na baka hakuri game da deenah ,na lura sam tamkar bata girmama ka duk da iya bakin kokarina da nake akanta ,kasan inda muka dauko deenah ,sai ahankali dai dabiunta dan allah kayi hakuri da wasu abubuwan...

Lumshe idanu yayi bayan yasauke dubansa akan jalila ,yayi tsadadden murmishinsa da ba kowa yakewa irinsa ba ahankali yasoma magana "kada ki bari wannan abun ya dameki ,duk da dai k'anwar tamu tana da tsiwa,so rashin hakurina bazai yi tasiri akanta ba ,wannan kuruciya ce da kauyanci ,ki bata karamin lokaci zata ware "....

Yayi maganar cikin son kwantar mata da hankali ,dan shi anasa ganin da wuya idan deenah na da cikakken hankali ,tamkar tababbiya yake ganinta musamman abubhwan da take ,wanda duk tsabar sangartane ..

Sauke ajiyar zuciya jalila tayi tare murmishi "nagode abban kids ..."

Madine ce tashigo garden din da gudu,inda su sultan suke ball ta nufa .
Da idanu jalila da AJ suka bita ..

Ta furta asanyaye ...
"me akayi"..

"me kike so aunty"..

Turo baki ta danyi batare da tasamu gamsuwa da zancen ba ta jinjina kai ...

washegari da yake daya daga cikin ranakun aikine tun 8:am driver yakai yara makaranta ,itakuwa deenah agida aka dauko malamin dake mata lesson kafin a sanyata makarantar ,hakama islamiyya ,malaminsu sultan dake koyar dasu agida ,shi yake kkyar da ita ..Babu laifi kam tana ganewa ,amma ba can ba ..

 sai 7:36am ta tashi Akasalance tayi wanka tare da daura alwala tayi sallah duk jikinta babu kwari. Afalo tayi kicibus da deenah sanye cikin school uniform dinta hijabinta da socks tare da sandals  dinta ahannunta da dukkan alamu dakin jalilan ta nufa... 
Ganin jalila da tayi yasanya ta sakin dariya tare da karasawa inda take ta rungumeta.. 
Cike da kulawa jalila ke rike da hannun deenah har suka zauna adaya daga cikin kujerun  dake falon... 
Kamo hannunta jalila tayi tare da zaunar da ita akusa da ita sosai.. Tun kafin tayimagana  deenah tafara mata ƙorafi "abbansu sultan yace wai bazan ganki ba kinyi bacci bakyajin daɗi, kuma nasani dan kawai bayaso naganki ne ".. 
A shagwabe tayi maganar... 

Shafa kanta jalila tayi cike da murmishin da yakasance dabiarta tace "eyyah kanwata, yallabai gaskiya ya fadamiki, banajin dadi ne sai kuma nayi bacci, "gyara zama jalila tati tare da Kara gyarawa deenah fuskarta da uwar powder da ta Saka afuskanta, tallabo afuskanta tayi "ƴar ƙanwata, banaso kina yiwa yallaɓai musu ko gunguni shi babba ne... "

Turo mitsitsin bakinta deenah tayi  kamar yadda tasaba "to ai baya sona aunty shiyasa, kuma baya sakarmin fuska... "

"Yallabai yana sonki kamar yadda yakeson ƴaƴansa, kawai yanaso ki canja ne kizama wayayya kidaina yiwa mutane tsiwa "..

Kalaman deenah yakuma kashewa jalila jiki, ashe deenah zata iya fahimtar shakuwa, soyayya, uwa  uba kulawa? ya zataji idan ta wayi gari babu mai yimata hakan arayuwarta... "

Zazzafar kwallace tafara kokarin fitowa daga kurmin idanunta.. 
Runtse idanunta tayi tare da rungume deenah ta share hawayen idanunta batare da ta bari ta fuskanci wani abuba.. Saida ta daidaita nutsuwarta kafin ta dago da deenah ajikinta ta saka mata hijabin ta tare da socks d'inta da sandals.. 
Har ta karasa deenah na kallonta tana murmishi 
"Aunty kinfi yadikko sona da kuma ji da ni "..

shima batare da ya dubi inda takeba ya amsa ya mayat da dubansa ga jalila

Me zakaci yallaɓai? "...

Cikin jin dadin kalamanta ya tsareta da idanu batare da furta komai ba.. Yana masifar son jalila, komai tayi burgesa takeyi baya son abinda ke nisantasa daga gareta.. Sai tilas

Nafisa kuwa cikin dagulewar zuciya take driving ji takeyi tamkar zuciyarta zatayi bindiga dan bakin ciki, tafara jin haushin kanta akan nacin son da takewa AJ, ko amafarki bata dauka wulakancinnasa yakai haka ba ashe har ya zarce haka.. Bata jin zata hakura batare da haƙar ta ta cimma ruwa ba... 

Dan karamin tsaki taja tare da sauke motar daga titi, saida tayi packing agefen tati kafin ta ciro wayarta ta danna wata number ta kara akunnenta zuwa lokacin idanunta sun fara kawo ruwa. 
Ajiyar zuciya tasauke bayan an dauki kiran ta re da fadar "wannan hanyar babu nasara nabila, ki kara fadamin wata mafitar, "cikin sanyin jiki da sarewa tayi maganar still miryanta na rawa

Ɓangaren da aka kira da nabila ta fara magana "hakan kar ya sa ki karaya, tunda har nadauki damarar taimakamiki wlh duk taurin kansa bazai tsallake kaidinmuba koda ya ketare na shedan, ina so ki nutsu ki daɗa saka karfin guiwar mallakarsa ina mai tabbatar miki tun a shirinmu zamu wining kafin mu haɗa da makircin shaidan duk da aƙasanmu yake. "

Shiru nafisa tayi tana cgaba da sauke ajiyar zuciya da gamsuwa da zancen nabilar  "to yanxu ya zanyi, infact na yanke shawarar zuwa gidansa sai naga kuma yadace na tuntubeki naji... "
"Da kin tafka kuskure kuwa, da kuma kin ɓata komai ".. 
Wacce aka kira nabilarce ta katse nafisa.. Yanayin yadda suke firar tasu zai nunamaka abota da aminantakar dake tsakaninsu, nabila tacigaba da cewa "karki manta kinfadamin cewa yana masifar son matarsa, so nasan halinki zuwanki gidan zai iya rushe komai, dan zafin kishi da rashin hakuri dake addabarki, mafita daya ce, kibar hotel ki koma gidansu da zama anan zaki yi kamun kafa , domin can din tamkar gidanku ne  tunda gidan baffanki ne  Daga nan kuma zan fara karantomiki yadda abun zai tafi ".. 

Ajiyar zuciya nafisa tasauke "yanxu na samu nutsuwa, amma kaina yadauki zafi wlh, zan wuce nadauko trolley bag dina a hotel. komai kenan zamuyi waya "...

 ta dan sha wahala kafin ta gano gidan baffah usman . Sai da ta isa parking area kafin ta fito tana kuma karewa gidan kallo, dan tsayin lokacin data dauka bata zo gidanba, duk da bawani zuwa takeyi ba... 

Ya mutsa fuska taketa famanyi, batare da ta dubi mai gadin daketa faman yimata sannu ba.. "Ka shigo min da jaka ta, tana aboot"... 

Abinda ta fada kenan ta kutsa kanta acikin gidan...

Dago da kai nafisa ta yi ta dubi ammie tare da girgiza kai "lafiyaklau  kawai naga yadace ne nazo nagaisheku naɗan kwana biyu anan, na tabbatar zam ɗebe miki kewa ta wani bangaren.  

Murmishi ammie tayi tare da jinjina kai "masha allah, hakan yakamata, abinda yakamata kunayi kenan wato kula da zumunci naji daɗin hakan kwarai, yakamata ki ƙara hutuwa yanzu zuwa anjima saiki fito muyi fira ko "...

 kai, sosai takeson tambayar ta AJ amma ta danne so take tabi komai asannu dan bataso lokaci daya ammie ta gano manufarta

Sai yamma nafisa takuma tsala uban wanka cikin wasu damammun riga da skirt na leshi sai faman fitar kamshi takeyi.  tafito bata tarar da ammie ba hakan ya tabbatar mata tana sashinta.. sai faman kai da kawowa nafisa  takeyi daga babban falo zuwa harabar gidan, tana ji ajikinta duk inda AJ yake tana da yaƙinin dole ne yazo gun ammie, falo ta dawo ta zauna ta cigaba da kallo wanda baki daya hankalinta ba agun kallon yakeba.. Uwa uba yadda jalila tagama mamaye duk wani lungu da saƙo na zuciyar ya AJ, saidai wannan lokacin yaki ne tsakaninsu dan ko ta halin ƙaka sai ta samu abinda takeso... 

Har aka kira sallar magrib tana falon sai alokacin ammie ta fito.. Fira suka ɗan taɓa wacce duk tambayar mutanen jumeta ammie keyiwa nafisa akan yadda ta barosu, duk da ba wannan firar nafisa takesoba bisa dole ta dage da baiwa ammie amsa akan firar..

...

A can jumeta kuwa tunda tasa ƙafa tabar gidan hajja babba ta shiga tashin hankali dan batassn ina nafisar zata dosa ba, ga uwa uba rashin yimata bayani da batayi ba.. Abun duniya duk ya damu hajja musamman yadda nafisa ta daina ɗaga kiran ko wa na gidan,haka hajja da ta sanarwa su baffah aminu halin da ake ciki, allah yakyauta kawai sukayi baffah aliyu ya dora da cewa "abarta taje idan duniya ta shiga zata fadamata gaskiyane dan ita duniya makaranta ce, yarinya batajin maganar kowa sai abinda tayi niyya, kuma duk hajja ce ta janyo hakan, hakan kowa keta dora mata laifi itada mahaifiyar nafisar hajiya zuwairah... Yadda kowa yayi burus da lamarin hakan bakaramin konawa hajiya zuwairah rai yayi ba, wai kawai andai azawa nafisa karan tsanane kuma duk inda take lafiya zata dawo... 

Wasa wasa har aka kwashi kwanaki biyar da tafiyarta, babu labarinta a jumeta... Dul da boyon maganar batan nafisan da hajja keyi saida ta fallasa agidan. 
Saida mujaheed sukayi waya da AJ yake fadamasa halin da ake ciki na ɓatar nafisa... 
Anan AJ ke sanar dashi tana kt gun ammie ba ɓata tayiba . 
Shikansa mujaheed yasha mamaki yakuma dago da manufarta tayin hakan  sauke ajiyar zuciya yayi "nayi mamakin jin hakan, amma bro uh have to take care, nafisa tanada hatsari , idanunta buɗe suke ina tsoron abinda zaija ya taɓa zumunci ko wani abu da ya danganci iyalinka, babu alkhairi atattare da dawowarta katsina "

Murmishin gefen baki AJ yayi yana cigaba da juyawa akan kujerar da yake zaune akai  "babu abinda zai faru, hasalima wannan lokacin dakaina zan koyamata hankali, zan rabata da dakikancin dake kwanyarta, tunda akullum neman tada hatsaniya take a family, yadda tazo haka zata koma, babu abinda zai faru"... 


Haka Suka dan taɓa fira kafin suyi sallama.. 

Ɓangaren deenah kuwa acikin yan kwanaki ta mayar da hankalinta sosai akan karatunta musamman yadda suke fuskantar living out examination ita da sultan, dake jumping akayimata dan wani irin girma dayafara bijiromata dayake kuma tana ganewa babu laifi shiyasa akayimata jumping, kowane lokaci tana tare da sultan yana nunamata wasu abubuwan dake yafita ganewa, sam bassda hutu sai daddare shima na ɗan lokaci ne, sai weekend.. 
Yadda suka mayar da hankalinsu akan karatu bakaramin daɗi hakan yayiwa jalila ba musamman akan deenah da duk wani gidadanci da duhun kai yasoma barin ta..ahnkali take fahimtar yadda nutsuwa ke shigar ta  
Ɓangaren jalilar kuwa wani irin sauyi na daban takeji atattare da ita, duk da tana cikin koshin lafiya dan ta tsare shan maganinta, saidai sauyin da takeji nadabanne awannan lokacin, bata ɓata lokaci ba ta kira junaidiyya take sanar mata domin tafara zargin kamar cikine gareta, dan hakan zai iya kawo barazana a lafiyarta musamman maganin dr junaidiyya ta dorata akansa, duk da matakin da suka ɗauka akan dakile hana daukar ciki, har lokacin da zata gama shan maganinta ......

Hankalin junaidiyya saida ya tashi da bayanin da jalila tayimata awaya, batare da ta bari ta fahimci hakan ba, ta tabbatar mata da zata biyo idan ta tashi gun aiki ta duba ta...[10/3, 2:10 PM] Maman boy: *_MUSABBABI_*

Duk wani test dazai tabbatar da shigar ciki saida junaidiya tayiwa jalila shi, tare da tabbatar da shigar cikin jalila na tsayin sati sha uku .... 

Cikin matsanancin sanyin jiki junaidiyya ta yi shiru mai tattare da damuwar da ta gaza ɓoyewa... 

Dubanta itama jalila takeyi sosai cikin yanayin damuwa kafin ta girgiza kai da kawar da kanta gefe "main yuwuwa  lokaci na ne kawai ya yi nakusan barin duniyar nan, wannan cikin da wuya na haifesa, shiyass ubangiji ya jarabceni da samunsa mai yuwuwa shine ajalina"... 
Cikin rawar murya mai tattarr da karewar zuciya ta idar da zancen.. 

Da sauri junaidiyya ta riƙo hannayenta duka tasaka anata dan sama mata nutsuwar da ke kokarin barin gangar jikinta "dan allah karkice haka jalila, irin wadannan maganganun matukar tadamin hankali suke kuma sam baidace kina faɗar haka ba, babu ta yadda  ubangiji baya jarabtar bayinsa, ki ƙara hakuri wannan duk nau in jarrabawane, zan samo mafita akan hakan
Shiru falon yadauka, jalila kuwa ambaton sunan allah  kawai ta shigayi a zuciyarta, bakidaya hawayen idanunta sun kafe ta karaya ta kuma mikawa ubangiji lamarin... Dr junaidiya kuwa kai da kawowa ta somayi kafin ta dakata tare da duban jalilar "inaga zan tuntunbi dr rufa 'i abokin aikinane, likitane sosai tabangaren ciwon daji, bi iznillah zai samo mana mafita... "

Zubamata idanu jalila tayi har lokacin bata iya furta komai ba, hakan yasa dr cigaba da magana asanyaye "amma jalila anxo gaɓar da dolene yallaɓai da duk wani na kusa dake yasan halin da kike ciki, dan kuskurene da tauye hakki na rashin sanarmusu musamman yallaɓai, rufe maganar a tsakaninmu b abu bane da ya kamata ba "... 

A razane jalila ke kallon junaidiyya tare da girgiza kai "ba yanxu ba Dr  sai kin fara sanarwa dr rufai muji irinnasa taimakon "... 

"To ai dole sai ya bukaci sanin mijinki ko wani naki, na tabbatar koda yallabai AJ zai tada hankalinsa to zai kasance da tauhidinsa domin yasan meyakeyi kuma yasan babu abinda zai samu bawa batare da ubangiji yavkaddaro masaba ...
Kwantarwa jalila da hankali dr ta shigayi, tare da ƙaramata karfin guiwa akan lafiyaklau zata haife cikin da yardar allah , takuma dora da bata shawarwari sosai... 

Hakan yasa jalila tadan ji nutsuwa tafara shigarta... 

Share kwallar idanunta tayi "nagode sosai dr da k'ok'arinki akaina, "


Dafa kafadar ta  junaidiyya tayi "babu damuwa, duk yadda mukayi da dr rufai zan faɗa miki komai kenan, amma ki kwantar da hankali, ki sameni a asibiti jibi in allah yakaimu Dan Ina komawa zantuntunbesa  "

Cikin karfafa mata zuciya sukayi sallama, 
Zama jalila tayi akan kujera tana sauke ajiyar zuciya tare da ambaton sunan allah da yake sama mata sassauci azuciya...
 Koda su deenah suka dawo daga makaranta bata bari sun fahimci komai ba, duk da akwai kuruciya atattare da su bataso wataran su fahimci irin yanayin datake ciki
 Har zuwa lokacin da ubangiji zai kawo Mata iyaka,  bangaren IG kuwa tana zullumin abinda zai ɗaga hankalinsa amma batada zaɓi da ya wuce bin shawarar junaidiyya, domin koma miye watarana dolene yaji ko da abayan rayuwartane alokacin zai yi bkinciki da  bazata samu damar rarrashinsa ba... 

Da wannan tunanin jalila ta wuni, saidai tana tare da yaranta da suke debe mata kewa.. Tunda suka dawo daga islamiyya suke afalo bakidayansu suna fira da kallo.. 
Saida aka fara kiran sallah kafin ta umurcesu da gabatar da sallah baki dayansu... koda suka kammala afalon suka dawo bakidaya har sukayi dinner, sai wurin nine tayimusu saida safe bayan ta tabbatar sun koma dakinsu, kafin ta nufi sashinta, cike da kewar yallabai AJ, ta turamasa da sakon saida safe dayake baya kasar saida ta tabbatar sakon ta ya isa kafin takashe wayar takwanta  cikin ikon allah bacci yayi awon gaba da ita. 


Nafisa kuwa takai akallah kwanaki goma a gidan ammie, amma babu wani cigaba da ta samu, sosai ta dage da kutsa kai da duk wani abu datasan zaijanyo hankalin ammie akanta, ko ta yaba, hatta gyaran falon ammie ta hana mai aiki yi ita ce ke gyarawa ta goge, saidai ammie dakanta ke gyara bedroom din nata.. Itakanta ta nasane da yadda nafisar ke kokarin shiga jikinta, tadai saka mata idanu ne ganin gudun ruwannata..
So take taji wani abu da ya danganci jalila ko yaranta daga bakin ammie, amma tagaza samun hakan,dan sam ammien batada wasa, duk da son mutane da takedashi da sakin fuska, zaiyi wuya ka iya sakin jiki kayi fira da ita, dan mace ce mai kwarjini da cika idanu 
  nabila kuwa ta gargadeta da zuwa gidan Ya AJ dan gudun rushewar tsarinnasu, uwa uba abinda take kulafucinso ba kasafai take saka shi a idanunta taba da tun zuwan da yayi gaishe da ammie bata kuma saka shi a idonta ba, cikin hikima ta ke tambayar ammie AJ nan take shaidamata baya garin, amma taci burin  samun number dinsa takowani hali da zata iya jin motsin sa, ita kanta tasan tana matukar azabtuwa da soyayyar sa ta yadda matukar yana adoron duniya saita mallakesa takuma juyo da hankalinsa akanta, ta sanya ya mance da kowace ya mace face itakaɗai... 

  
Fitowarta kenan daga dakinta taci karo da ammie da dukkan alamu dakin nata ta dosa... 

Duban nafisa ammie tayi cikin nazari kafin tace "kisameni falo zamuyi magana "/.. 

Iya abinda ammie tafada kenan ta juya.. 

Yanayin dataga ammien ya sanyaya mata jiki Tare da ɗarsar da faɗuwar gaba ba tare da bata lokaci ba ta bi bayanta asanyaye.. 

Zama nafisa tayi akujerar dake gefen ammie tana fuskantarta da tsammanin abinda zai fito daga bakin ammie... 

Daidaita dubanta ammie tayi akan nafisa kafin ta numfasa tace "meyasa kikabaro gida batare da kinsanar da mutanen gida inda kika dosa ba, kuma sam kinki daga kiran kowa, inaso kimin bayanin abinda ke faruwa dan tun zuwanki nagaza fahimtar komai, na lura akwai wani abu akasa "... 

Sunkuyar da kai nafisa tayi kamar wata mumina, cikin shagwabar da ta saba ta dubi ammie araunane " kawai ni nagaji da gidanne babu mai ƙaunata, babu mai son farincikina, shiyasa nazo nan  tunda duk sun juyan baya "... 

Daidaita gilashin dake fuskarta ammie tayi idanunta akan nafisar ta numfasa tare da fadar "wannan ba hujja bace, babu yadda zaa ace babu mai kaunarki hatta iyayenki
Saidai wani hali na daban da zasu ƙi atattare da ke, amma badai ke dinba ".. 

Duban ammie tayi kawai dan batasan meyakawo zancenba..
Kamar ammie  tasan abinda nafisar ke tunani tace "banji dadin abinda kikayi ba, kin tada hankalin mutanen gida, kinsan halin da kika saka iyayenki kuwa, komenene  sukayi miki basu cancanci hakanba, shine zaki kama hanya ki tafo batare da sunsan inda kika dosa ba amatsayinki na ya mace "... 

Turo baki nafisa ta shigayi, yadda ammie ke mata faɗa abin yafara dagulamata kwalwa dan basabo tayi da fada ba... 

Amaimakon shirun da tayi kawai ta sanyawa ammie kuka
Zubamata idanu ammie tayi har ta yi mai isarta, kafin ammie ta ɗora da nata faɗan mai tattare da nasiha anan take shaidamata baffah aliyu yasanarmata da komai akan tafowarta a katsina, duk da shikansa baisan dalilin ta nayin hakanba, ammien ke sanar masa nafisar ta na nan, shima anan yake fadamata mujaheed ne yasanar dashi yadda sukayi da AJ shiyasa yakira yaji abinda yakawota katsina".. 

Sosai ammie ta nuna ɓacin ranta akan abinda nafisar tayi dan sam bai kamata ɗa na kwarai yayi abinda tayiba.. Anan ta nuna dole saita koma jumeta kuma tabasu hakuri.. 

Kuka nafisa tasanya wa ammie akan takyaleta anan Zata Kira tabasu hakuri.. 

Zubamata idanu Ammie tayi Kenan akwai wata manufa atattare da zuwan nafisar data ɓoyemat  ayanxu, itafa Sam tagaza gamsuwa da zuwanta katsina da bbu dalili KO hujjja.. 
Ganin yadda  duk ta  ɗaga hankalinta yasany ammie kyaleta tare da tabbatar mata da takira tabasu hakuri akan abinda tayi.. 

Da haka tasamu ta shawo kan ammie saidai ta fahimci matuka yadda ran ammien ya ɓaci akan abinda tayi.. 

Saida ta tabbatar ammie tagamsu da abinda ta fada mata kafin tasamu nutsuwa.. 
Koda takoma ɗakinta tufka da warwara ta farayi, bisa tilas takira su hajjah dan ta tabbatar dole ammie tamayat da hankalinta akanta. 



Tun 8:am jalila ta gama shirinta na tafiya asibiti.. Drivern da ke kai su sultan makaranta ya wuce da ita asibitin bayan ya ajiyesu deenah amakarantar.. Kai tsaye asibitin dr junaidiyya ta umurcesa da y dosa.. Cikin saa tasamu ganin dr rufain da tunkafin zuwanta dr tayimasa bayanin komai.. 
Da taimakon yan uwansa  kwararrun likitoci ya sake duba jalilar.. 
Saidai yadda binciken lafiyarta ya tabbatar dole saidai adakile rayuwar cikin da manne ajikinta domin ceto ta ta rayuwar, domin gudanar cikin ajikinta tamkar tufka da warwara ne na lafiyarta... Saka hannun mijinta ko wani nata kawai ake bukata, acire mata cikin aranar, ta ɗora da shan magungunanta

Cikin nutsuwa da kwantar da hankali sukeyi mata jawabin... 

Hankalin jalila yayƙara mugun tashi, duk a tsorace . 
Sanin yadda zata tashi hankalinta yasanya junaidiyya taso tasanarwa da koda ammie ne, dan jalilar ta sanar da ita har lokacin babu wanda yasan da zuwanta asibitn sai driver, bata ɗauka abun zaizo ahakaba , yallabai kuwa baya akasar bazata iya faɗamasa da zancen awayaba... 

Batare da ɓata lokaci ba anan office din dr rufai  junaidiyya ta fara kokarin kiran ammie da wayarta  dan tasanarmata jalila na asibiti...
 Batare da jalila tayi yunkurin hanata ba, har takira ammien tasanar mata Halin da ake ciki... 

Cikin tashin hankali ammie tafara ambaton sunan allah da ya janyo hankalin nafisa dake gefenta tana faman danna waya... 

Da idanu nafisa ta tsareta har tagama fahimtar wayar da ammie takeyi.. 

Cikin son jin abinda yafaru ta dubi ammie  da ta sauke wayar daga kunnenta tace "meyafaru ammue? "... 

"Jalila ce ba lafiya tana asibiti, bari na ɗauko mayafina "... 
 cikin daburcewa ammie ta nufi sashinta bayare  da ta kuma bin takan nafisa ba ta ... 

Tashi nafisa tayi zaune daga kishingidar da take cikin matsanancin farincikin abinda taji, bayan ammie tabi da harara tare da tabe baki yadda taga duk ta wani damu tamkar ajikinta akace ciwon yake sai abun yabata haushi... 

Lokacin  da ammie tafito, tashi nafisa tayi cikin damuwar data gayyato afuskanta tare da warware karamin gyalen data yane kanta dashi tabi bayan ammie, dan acewarta bazaayi ba da ita ba, koba komai damace ta samu...

Koda driver yakai su ammie asibiti kai tsaye office din junaidiyya ammie ta nufa, anan reception ta cewa nafisa ta jirata, badan taso hakanba ta zauna adaya daga cikin kujerun dake wurin tana faman jan tsaki 
Koda ammie ta isa office din   "a tsanake Dr tayiwa ammie jawabin komai, da yayi mugun dagawa ammien hankali,duk da kokarinta na kwantar mata da hankali  tayi mamakin yadda abun har yakai matakin hakan batare da sanin wani ba.. Musamman abdulmajeed... 

Sauke ajiyar zuciya ammie takumayi "yanzu ina jalilar wane hali take ciki? "... 

"Ki kwantar da hankalinki hajiya, tana cikin koshin lafiya, yanxu haka tana ɗakin hutu, sa hannu kawai muke jira a cire cikin". 

Gaza cewa komai ammie tayi sai innalillahi wa inna ilahair rajiun da take karantowa... 
Cikin kwantar da murya dr ta fara rarrashin ammie "komai zaizo da sauki mugodewa allah domin akwai mafita hajiya, zaki saka hannu acire cikin amaimakon yallaɓai da baya nan, pls"... 
Cikin rarrashi da hikima junaidiyya ke rarrashin ammie har ta saka hannu... 

Gaza zama office din junaidiyya ammie anan reception ta fito tana kai da kawowa, ta rasa waye zata fara sanarwa da wannan tashin hankalin amma kuma tilas iyayen jalila su sani... 

Ta gefen idanu nafisa ke satar kallonta lokaci zuwa lokaci take tabe baki, tana bukatar sanin ainahin abinda ke damun jalilar da ya ɗaga hankalin ammie haka, to saidai a wannan lokacin ta fahimci ammien bata cikin nutsuwar yimata bayanin.. 

Har wurin azahar suna asibitin, zuwa lokacin jalila na dakin da za'a cire cikin... 

Karfe uku narana Kamar daga sama kiran ya Aj yashigo wayar ammie hakan ya tabbatar masa ya shigo nigeria musamman da number da ya kirata... 

Gaisheta yayi, anan yake fadamata jirginsu baijima da sauka ba yadawo gida gashinan tare da yara duma basu  jima dawowa saga makarantaba   bai Tarar da jalila agidan baby  ya kuma  Kira dukkan wayoyinta bait samu ba... 
Cikin nutsuwa ammie ya numfasa kafin tace "muna asibiti tare da ita yanxu haka, dakuma junaidiyya ".. 

Fito da dukkan girman eyeball dinsa AJ yayi cikin faduwar baba take tambayar ammie abinda ke damun jalilar... 

Ba ta iya yimasa bayani ba sao rarrashinsa da ta soma.. 

Arude ya kashe wayar, yana kokarin barin falon, sultan da deenah dake afalon sun gama fahimtar wayar da Aj din yayi .. 

"Ina aunty ta tafi ne, meyafaru da auntyna? '..

Tambayoyin da ta shiga yiwa ya AJ  kenan, da baida niyyar amsamatasu dan ba ta its yakeba... 

KO kaya bai sauya  ba ya fito gun ajiye motoci da sauri driver ya bude masa mazaunin baya ya shige tare da fadamass asibitin da zasuje... 

Deenah kuwa dagudu ta karaso inda AJ yake tana hawaye "kafadamin ina auntyna taje, ko bata lfiya ne ".. 

Karasowa sultan yayi a gaban motar yadda deenah tasaka kuka, shima kawai yassnya kukan "abie ina ummie ta tafi ne "... 

Cikin yar nutsuwar datai saura ajikinsa AJ ya shafa kansa  "ku bari indawo yanxu, tana asibiti yanxu zanje na tafo da ita "... 

Kuka deenah ta kuma sakawa ta nace bisa dole sai ya tafi da ita ta ga auntynta "... 

Cikkn bacin rai da dagulewar zuciya AJ yakwatso mata tsawar da kaɗa hantar cikinta akan ta wuce ta basa gun, dan bakidaya bacin ransa neman sauka kanta yakeyi... 


Rushewa da kuka deenah tayi tare da ɗora hannu akai, duk da atsorace take sosai amma takafe sai antafi da ita... 

Tsaki AJ ya ja tare da furzan da zazzafar iska, batare da yakuma bi takantaba ya baiwa driber umurni suka fice daga gidan... 

Wurin karfe uku da rabi na yamma dr ta shigo dakin da jalila take, jalila  na fitowa daga toilet din dake dakin ta iske dr.. 

Asanyaye suke kallon juna har jalila tazauna abakin gado tare da lumshe idanunta tana sauke ajiuar zuciya... 

Yanayin da dr takuma ganin jalilar duk ya sanyayamata jiki dafa kafadarta junaidiyya tayi tasoma magana "duk wanda yayi tawakkali da allah ya isar masa akomai, inaso ki saka aranki cikin sauki za a yi aikinnan akuma samu nasara "... 

Girgiza kai jalila tayi "nima zanso hakan dr, amma yadda ubangiji ke jarabtata irin haka, sai nake ga kamar rayuwatace ke gab da barin gangar jikina, dan akowane lokaci zan iya mutuwa, musamman a irin wannan lokacin, saidai inafatan ubangiji ya karɓi rayuwata ta sauki, mijina kuma..... 


Yadda junaidiyya tarike hannayenta tsam cikin naga ya hanata idar da zancen said hawaye da ya vara zarya akumatunta...

Yadda Dr junaidiyya tarik'e hannayenta tsam cikin nata ya hana jalila  idar da zancen sai hawaye da ya fara zarya akumatunta... Murza yatsun jalila tayi ahankali cikin rarrashi da kwantar da  murya tacigaba da rarrashinta 
"Irin wannan yanayin dakike ciki bai kamata kina Kara tauye lafiyarki da damuwa ba, mu kanmu kina cutar damu da damuwar da mike gani atare dake, ga ammie can a recepotion area har yanxu tagaza nitsuwa bare tasamu taci abinci tun safe.. Ga shi ina ganin ta sanarwa da yallaɓai halin da ake ciki, dan yakamata ya sani ... haba jalila yakamata kisan komai allah keyi"... 

Jinjina kai jalila tashigayi cikin juriya da karfin guiwar da ta soma shigarta kafin ta share hawayenta.. 
Gefe daya kuwa zuciyarta na hararomata tsantsar tashin hankalin IG zai shiga  da junaidiyya tasanar mata yaji komai... 
Cikin son kara mata karfin guiwa junaidaiyya ta sake magana "bisa ga yaƙini, da yardar allah yanxu zaa yi komai, kici gaba da rayuwarki kamar kowa,  wankin cikine kawai zaayi dan abinda ke cikin baiyi kwari ba  "... 
Saida ta tabbatar jalila tasamu yar nutsuwa kafin tayi yunkurin barin dakin. 
Dakatar da dr junaidiyya jalila tayi dake kokarin barin dakin dan kiran likitan da zaiyi aikin.. 

Gaza furta komai tayi sai sauke ajiyar zuciya da tayi ta lumshe idanunta tare dakomawa takwanta ., ahankali tacigaba da karanto hasbunallah a labbanta. 

Itama junaidiyya sauke ajiyar zuciyar tayi tadan tsuramata idanu dan batasan da wadanne kalamai zata kuma tausar jalila ba da har yanxu taki kwantar da hankalinta.. 
Girgiza kai kawai tayi ta fice da ga dakin cikin tsananin tausayin ta... 




Yallabai AJ kuwa koda suka karaso asibitin cikin tashin hankali ya balle murfin motar batare da yabari driver ya bude masaba, kaitsaye office dr junaidiyya ya nufa yayinda driver ke binsa abaya cikin hanxari dan yanayin daya ga maigidannasa... 

A reception ammie da Nafisa suka hangosa  yanayin tashin hankalin da ammie ta gani shimfide afuskarsa yasanya ta saurin tarbarsa.. 

"Whats happining with her, lfyklau nabarta agida, and where is she pls ammie "... 

Yanayin kalamansa kadai zai nuna ba anutse yakeba.. 
Kamo hannayensa ammie tayi tazaunar dashi a daya daga cikin kujerun dake a reception din, hakan yadan sama masa nutsuwa, zubawa ammie manyan idanunsa yayi da suka soma sauya launi  yanajiran abinda zata fada, da har lokacin ta gaza furta masa komai.. Zuwa can ta numfasa "ka nutsu abdulmajid cikin dake jikinta ne yadan samu matsala, kuma bai fadi ba, a halin da take ciki dolene cikin sai ya fita, shine samun mafita, yanxu haka anshiga da ita ciki zaa fara aikin... 

Tsurawa ammie idanunsa yayi, cikin yanayin mamaki dan  baisan da shigar cikin jalila ba, runtse idanunsa yayi tare da cakuɗa sumar kansa ya cije lip dinsa na kasa  da karfi kafin ya ware idanunsa akan ammie dake ta faman bubbuga kafadarsa.... 

Cikkn wata irin disasshiyar murya yasoma magana "ammie, pls wani hali aka shiga da ita ciki ".. 

cikin kulawa mai tattare da tausayi ammie tasoma magana yadda taga lokaci daya jarumin ɗan nata yakoma kamar wani nusari, wannan dalilin yasanya ta boyemasa bayanin da junaidiyya tayi mata har lokacin  dazasu koma gida.. 
"Lafiya kalau ka kwantar da hankalinka komai zai wakana cikin nasara wannan ba matsala bane  ".. 

Sauke ajiyar zuciya yayi tare da lumshe idanunsa  da cije lebensa ya soma ambaton sunan allah.. 
Nafisa kuwa wani irin zallar bakin ciki mai tattare da zafin kishin dake kona kirjinta ya tasomata, yadda ta iya karanto tashin hankalin dake fuskar AJ dan kawai matarsa bata lafiya tamkar anmasa bushara da mutuwarta, saidai dole ta iya takunta a irin wannan halin...
Cikin karfin hali da daidaita nutsuwarta ta zo inda ammie suke cikin yanayin damuwar da ta gayyato afuskarta,  a zuciyarta kuwa babban abinda zai sata farinciki bai wuce ayimata bushara da mutuwar jalila ba ayanxu, duk da har lokacin batasan ainahin abinda ke damuntaba, gashi ta lura ammie sai jan jiki take da al amurranta tamkar dai da abinda ake boyemata.. 


Dubanta ammie tayi cikin yanayin kulawa  "nafisa , yakamata ki koma gida ki huta, kiyi wanka tunda tun safe kike nan gashi kingaza cin komai "... 

Cikin jindaɗin yadda ammie ta yabeta agaban AJ dako kallon inda take baiyiba,   marairaice  fuska tayi  cikin damuwa "babu komai zan zauna anan atare daku koda naje gidan bazan samu nutsuwa ba  dan hankalina yana nan, amma ammie miye kedamunta  haka? "... 
Ta idar da maganar cikin satar kallon yanayinsa AJ din, da har lokacin bai iya dubantaba, dan bakidaya hankalinsa baya gunsu... 

 yadda ammie  ta fadawa   AJ haka ta fadawa nafisa..

Zama tayi gefen ammie bakidayansu sunyi jugum jugum, yallabai AJ kuwa kai da kawowa yashigayi, lokaci zuwa lokaci nafisa ke satar kallonsa, dr junaidiyya ma tana tare da su ammie, suna jiran abinda zai faru, kowa cikin damuwa yake, 

 Yadda nafisa ta dage akan baza ta iya tafiyaba  ne yasanya ammie ta kyaleta,driver ta umurta da ya je daukosu deenah daga gidan tunda babu kowa, ya wuce dasu acan ...

Bakidaya hamdala suka shiga yiwa allah, banda nafisa dake ta faman yake., dan taso labari yasha bambam.. 

Sai alokacin yallabai yasoma samun nutsuwa, massallacin dake nan asibitin ya wuce sakamakon kiran sallar magrib da aka fara, haka su ammie masallacin mata suka shiga dan gabatar da sallah

Duk da itakanta ammie hankalin nata a tashe yake, rayuwa da mutuwa duka ahannun allah take, nasan da wannan tunanin tasamu nutsuwa ta hakan nakeson kema kisamawa kanki nutsuwa, duk wanda ya mutu kwanansa ne yakare, wuya bata kisa sai kwana yakare "...
