Yadda  exam dinsu ta gabato shi dakansa ke guiding dinsu ganin sun dage da karatun exam

Bangaren deenah ta tsani yadda sir Aj ke takuramusu akan karatu, ga zafin da suke dauka tabangaren lissafi.. Dan sam bagane lissafi takeyi ba illah ida harmutsa kwalwarta da yake. 

 Matuƙar yana gida musamman weekend batada sukuni kullum cikin karatu sukeyi.. Sam yasanya dokar hana kallo bayan gama cin abincin dare sai karatu.. Zasu kai kusan goma nadare suna karatu kafin ya basu umurnin tafiya su kwanta. . har murna 
takeyi idan baya kasar ko kuma yabar garin, dan tasan sosai zata saki jikinta, tafi fassara abinda yakeyi musu da yanayin ƙeta da mugunta irin tasa, musamman ita da sam bai fiye sakarmata fuska, kawai ta gama fahimtar kaunar ta ne ke bayayi anata tunanin, shiyasa sam itama bata kaunarsa.. Kawai tana girmamasa ne dan faranta wa aunty jalila.. Ya cika takura, ga isa da izzah da shegen miskilanci da zaiyi wuya kaga dariyarsa ko faraarsa matukar ba tare yake da aunty jalila ba . Sam wadannan halaye da deenah ta jingina masa ne ya sanya bakidaya take jin haushin mijin yayar tata... Musamman lokacin da ya taɓa sanya teacher din su maimusu extra lesson ya bata kashin tsiya, dan bata iya karatuba, ga tsiwa da rashin kunya gashi ba kasafai take gane komai ba... 
Shima malamin sosai ya huce haushinsa ajikin deenah kamar yadda sir IG ya umurcesa... 
Saida yayi mata bulala mai yawa har lokacin IG bai bashi umurnin dakatawa ba.. Tun tana matsar kwallah harta kaiga fasa kuka.. 
Sai dan kansa IG yabasa umurnin ya dakata, yakuma tabbatar masa da kowaye ya sake sanya wasa akaratu irin wannan dukan zaiyi masa.... 

Sai alokacin deenah ta sake jin tsanar sir IG takuma tabbatar da ba kaunarta yakeba... Tayimasa allah ya isa sau ba adadi aranta.. 
Tunda lokacin ta tsani ganinsa batada yadda zatayi ne, har zuwa wannan lokacin... 


Saukowar sir IG kenan daga upstairs zuwa falon... 

Duban sultan yayi atsanake "kai kaɗai ke karatun yau "... 

Dago da kansa yayi ya dubi abbannasa "eh abbah, iftaahal tafara sai tayi bacci, ummi (jalila) ta tafi takaita dakinta... "

"Deenah fa? "... 

Cikin tsoron baiwa abbanasa amsa yasoma in ina "abbah...tace ko za..a kasheta.. Bazatayiba.. Saidai idan kasa ankasheta akaiwa yadikkonta kai da kafafunta".......

*_28_*
cikin tsoro sultan ke  baiwa abbie ɗinsa amsa  yasoma in-ina "abbie cewa tayi ko za'a kasheta  bazatayi karatunba, saidai idan kasa an kasheta akaiwa yadikkonta kai da kafafunta"... 
Tunda sultan yakai aya ya tsuke bakinsa yayi shiru 
Jalila da shigowarta kenan falon taji abinda sultan ke faɗa.. Cikin mamaki taja tayi tsaya duk atunaninta deenah tafara  mallakar hankalinta ashe har yanxu ɗanyen kai ne da ita... 

Sir IG kuwa murmishi yayi da zaiyi wuya lokaci daya ka iya fassara sa "good... "
Shine abinda yafaɗa yana jinjina kai... 

Duban sultan yayi da yayi tsuru tsuru 
"Je ka kiramin ita"..

Dagudu ya tashi ya nufi dakin deenah... 

Jalila kuwa tsabar takaici da kunya gaza karasowa falon tayi.. lallai dole ta ɗauki mataki mai tsauri akan deenah.. Tunda har yanxu sakarci da gidadanci na addabar kwalwarta.. Magana kuma batasan wacce zata fadawa babba ba.. Babu ruwanta duk wacce tazo mata faɗa takeyi... 

Akan gado sultan ya isketa tana ta game... 
"Kizo abbie nakira "... 
Shine abinda yafadamata yayi tsaye yana jiranta... 

Saida kirjinta yayi mummunan bugawa, cikin karfin hali ta taɓe mitsitsin bakin ta kafin ta dubi sultan "bazanje ba, wani dukan zaisa ayimin, abbienka sam baya ƙaunata, dan haka ba inda zanje "... 
Ta yi maganar tare da mayar da hankalin ta akan game din datake bugawa

Cikin ƙosawa da halinta sultan yasoma magana "abbie bazai  dakekiba "... 
Tsuramasa manyan idanunta tayi cikin son yarda dashi... 
Hakan yasa sultan ya jinjina mata kai dan ta sake samun gamsuwa, wurgi tayi da game din ta diro daga kan gadon.. Suka fice tare...tana fadar matukar ya sanya aka sake dukansa akan ɗansa (sultan) zata rama..  

Tunda suka shigo falon itama ta daure fuskarta.. 
"Gani "... 
 Cikin tsiwa tayi maganar  tare da tsayawa nesa da sir IG... 

Sai alokacin yadago ya dubeta, kanta ko kallabi babu, bakin gashinnan nata sai sheƙi yake jelar kitson daya da akayimata sai lilo take agefen kafadarta... 

Wani irin kallo yayimata da ta sha jinin jikinta tare da sunkuyar da kanta... 
"Me kike jira da baki fara naki karatunba? ".. 
Yanayin yadda yayi maganar yasanyata gaza yimasa musu ko basa amsa.. 

Gyara zama sir Aj yayi yana shafa sajensa "dama naji sakonki, to zansaka a yanka ki ɗin sai akaiwa yadikkon kai da kafafunnaki, daganan zan ga me zai biyo baya".. 

Yayi maganar yana ƙara dukkan girman idanunsa akanta... 

Wani irin bugawa kirjinta yayi itama ta fiddo idanu, tare da duban sultan da ta tabbatar shine ya sanar masa, itakam ayadda take ganin yallabai zai iya ma gunduwa²da ita, tunda dan sanda ne kuma bai ƙaunarta lallai  zai kasheta kuwa.. 

A idanunta suka ciko da kwallah lips dinsa yasoma rawa saidai tagaza furta komai sai wiƙi wiƙi da takeyi... 

"Zakiyi ko bazakiyi ba "... 
Sir IG yayi maganar da tsareta da idanunsa da suka soma tsoratar da ita... 

"nifa bani ganewa... "

Yadda tayi maganar cikin rawar murya yaso yabasa dariya... Sake daure fuska yayi "ok in yanka ki kenan kamar yadda kika fada? "... 

Saurin girgiza kai tayi, tare da nufar hanyar fita "... 
Dakatar da ita yallaɓai yayi "wannan ne karo nakarshe da zan sake ganinki babu ɗankwali akanki.. "
Tsaye tayi ta jinjina kai alamar to... 

"Get out from hare"... 
Tsawar da ya saba kwatso mata aduk lokacin data nemi ɓata masa rai, itace yayi mata ma yanzu.. 
Ahanzarce tabar falon. 

Murmishin gefen kumatu 
Aj yayi, yagama fahimtar yadda hantar deenah ke kaduwa, ya tabbatar zatayi karatun dan ta tsoratar, ga tsiwa ga tsoro...
Duban sultan yayi "idan batayi karatunba kasanarmin my boy"... 
Yayi maganar tare da shafa kan sultan da ya gyaɗa masa kai.. 
 
Tashi yayi ya fice daga falon.. Kai tsaye garden ya wuce dan can yake kassncewa aduk lokacin da yake gida..


Deenah Tana shiga ɗakinta trolley bag tajanyo ta cika tada kaya.. Hijab ɗin islamiyyar ta tasaka shima ajirkice  tana matsar kwallah ta fito .. Karo sukayi da jalila da itama ɗin dakin deenah zata... 
Fashewa deenah tayi da kuka.. "Aunty gidan babanmu zan tafi mijinki kasheni zaiyi ".. 

Cikin jin zafin yadda deenah ke aibata mijinnata ta dubeta tace "to waye ya hanaki, kitafi mana, ai yafi kitafi da ki tsaya kina gaggawa mijina maganganu san ranki ".. 

Daina kukan deenah tayi cak ta tsaida dubanta ga auntyntata da ko kusa bata taɓa tunanin hakan zai fito  daga bakinta.. Ba kuma ta taɓa ganin zallar bacin ranta ba akanta irin na yau... 
Ba ta karaya ba tacigaba da cewa "Aunty wlh cewa yayi zai kasheni, kwanaki haka yasanya malamin mu yayimin ɗan banzan duka..... "

Rufemin baki,! Ainaji komai, har cewa kike yi yakaiwa yadikko kai da kafafunki, ke marar kunya ko, bazan laminci yarinya karama kamarki da bata wuce yar cikinsa ba tana cimasa mutunci ba... Ki tafi gidan zanzo nima na faɗawa baba irin halayenki.... "

Ta idar da maganar tare da juyawa ta wuce cikin bacin rai da son nunawa deenah kuskurenta..  

Da idanu deenah tabi bayan jalila a sanya ye.. Aza hannu tayi akai "nashiga uku ita ma aunty ta daina so na.. Kuma ko na tafi bazata maido niba kenan.? ".. 
Maida jakar tayi ta yanke shawarar fasa tafiya dan bata samu nasara ba.. tasamu goyon baya gun jalila ba...
Dole ta dauko littafin ta fito falo simi² tazauna, satar kallonta sultan keyi yana dariya har ta zauna.. 


****

Hajiya rumaisa ce ita da hajja babba afalo zaune suna tattauna matsalar data danganci nafisa... 

Gyara zama hajiya rumaisa tayi tana duban hajja babba da ta narke aduniyar alhini.. 
"Wannan wane irin naci ne haka? Abu kusan shekaru goma shabiyar amma yaƙi ƙarewa, duk sa o in nafisa sunyi aure sun hayayyafa, dan me zata tsaya jiran gawon shanu.. Yo jiran gawon shanu mana wannan miskilallen yaron da yayi gadon taurin kai gun ubansa..... 

Cikin takaici hajja ta katse hajiya rumaisa "ai bashi ne abinda zaifi baki haushiba, sai ɗaukar ƙafa da yarinyar nan tayi ta koma gidansu.. Kiji min zubar da ƙima da kaskantar da kai agun maƙiyi tasani kwarai ba kaunar hajiya karama (innah) nakeyi ba.. Amma shine ta zabi ta kunyatarda ni takoma gidan ɗan ta... "

Murmishi hajiya rumaisa tayi irin nasu na gogaggu kafin tace "karkiga laifinta, huce haushi tajeyi, ai kunƙi bata gudunmuwa, shiyasa ta zaɓi kunyatar da ku.... "

"Nikam bansan yazanyi da nafisa ba bakidaya iyayenta sun zame hannu akanta ".. 
Cikin damuwa hajja ta idar da zancen... 

"Ai ba zira mata idanu zakuyi ba, kada kusare cigaba da zakuyi bakin rai bakin fama, Idan banda wauta ta yaro awannan zamanin makirci ko wani kissa na 'ya mace bazai ƙwatar miki ɗa namiji ba, idan kinason wufff da namiji mallake dan banza sha yanxu magani yanxu.. "
Faɗar hajiya rumaisa kenan... 

Sororo hajja babba tayi tana dubanta cikkn rashin gane zancen ta, hakan yasa hajiya rumaisa zuge jakarta ta ɗauko wani tandin kwalli aciki ta mikawa hajja babba da take dubanta cikin yanayin rashin fahimta.. 

"Wannan fa? ".. 
Cewar hajja babba... 
"Ki karba sai nayi miki bayani, kinsan bazan cutar dake ba "... 

Karɓa hajja babba tayi tana juya kwallin ahannunta..
Gyara zama hajiya rumaisa tayi, tasoma korawa hajja bayani "daga gun karɓo maganinnan nake, zainabu jikata ta wajen fiddausi itace na karbomawa ita kuma wahalar mijin take sha ya juya mata baya, takoma bora kishiyartace moya, shi ne na karɓo mata shi, atakaice dai wannan kwalli na baƙar mallaka ne, zizarasa kawai zata yi a idanu da sun hada ido zance ya kare zaayi komai a wuce wurin... Naga nafisa ta fi zainabu bukatarsa dan ita zainabu duk rintsi gidan aure take ita kuwa nafisa bata kaiga shigaba.. Dubu talatinne bayawa".. 

Tayi maganar tare da taɓe baki.... 
Washe baki hajja ta shiga yi tare da godiya ta dora dacewa "amma kuwa hajiya rumaisa na rasa bakin godiya gareki aiko dubu hamsin kikace zan biya, dan nasan matukar kwalliya tabiya kudin sabulu zamu kwashi romon damokwaraɗiya ".. 

Dariya sukayi tare da tafawa.. 

"Ai karki damu hajja kin wuce haka gareni, bayan kwanannan ina fama da rashin kudi da ƙwandalarki bazan karɓa bazan karɓaba, nima nafisa ai jika ce agareni, kuma abinda nayi taimakon kai ne.. Dan banmata wulakanci da tozarci da mahaifinsa yayimin ba na ƙin aurrn rahama aƙallah shekuru kusan arba'in kenan, inda nasamu sassauci ya garzaya barzahu,, bana ganinsa adoron kasa"... 

Ta idar da zancen da dariya..... 
Cikin raha suke firarsu, saida hajja ta cikawa hajiya rumaisa jakarta da kudi kafin suyi sallama, hajja saifaman godiya yakeyimata... 

Bayan tafiyar hajiya rumaisa hajja takira momy take sanarmata.. 
Hakan yayi matukar faranta ran momy domin aganinta acan ɗin raguwar dubara  akayi da anbi hanyar hajiya rumaisa da tuni anwuce wurin.. Saura yanxu susan yadda zasuyi nafisatazo jumeta.. Idan har akayimata wannan albishir din dole tabaro katsina babu shiri tunda ba cikin uwarta bane, "cewar hajja dan har yanxu tana jin zafin nafisar akan abinda tayi...


Ɓangaren jalila  kuwa ta tattara deenah ta ajiye gefe sam ta daina shiga harkokinta, ko gaisheta tayi adaƙile take amsamata, so take ta fahimci kuskuren data aikata, kaza na taka ƴaƴanta badan bata ƙaunarsuba saidan tsagwaron kaunar da take nuna musu, so take ta sauyawa deenah ɗabiunta da halayenta harma da yadda zata iya zama da mutane.. 
Itama deenah anata  bangaren ta shiga tsananin damuwa yadda lokaci daya aunty jalila ta fita shirginta babu inda zata raɓa taji sanyi.. Takuma fahimci dalilin yin fushin nata da aunty jalila keyi akanta.. Duk da batajin nadamar abinda tayi aranta amma taji babu  daɗi yadda jalila ke shareta... Gashi  jalila taƙi bata fuskar da zata nemi afuwarta... 

______

Dayake suna cikin exam dinsu ta first term tun 12:45pm driver ya maidosu da ga makaranta.. 
Kai tsaye deenah dakin aunty jalila ta nufa bata sametaba hakan ya tabbatar mata da ta na kitchen kenan.. 
Batare da ɓata lokaci ba ta nufi kitchen din. 

Kai tsaye inda jalila take  ta nufa, ta baya ta rungumeta... 


Hakan yasanya jalila juyowa tare da dakatawa daga buɗe fridge din da takeyi.. 
Fuskanta babu yabo ba fallasa ta dubi deenah tare da dagata daga jikinta.  "Meye haka kuma"..? 
Marairaicewa deenah tayi.. "Kiyi hakuri ".. 
Ta fada kamar zatayi kuka.. 
Kallonta jalila tayi ta watsar ta cigaba da hidimarta.. 

Kiran sunanta deenah tayi.. Bata amsamata ba hakan yasa deenah tacigaba da magana cikin rawar murya "kince bani da kowa aduniya saike ni kadaice yar uwarki nikuma ke kadaice yayata, haka kika fada aunty.. Kince bazaki taɓa bari nayi kuka ba.... ".. 
Bata idar da maganar ba tacigaba da zubar da hawaye da dukkan alamu abun na taɓa zuciyarta.. 

Cikin sanyin jiki jalila ta dafa kafadun deenah da dukkan hannayenta.. 

"Dubi rayuwarki madina,da ahaka kike?, yanxu idan nace zan mayar dake ƙauye zaki amince? "... 
Tayi maganar tare da zubawa deenah idanu tana dubanta.. 

Saurin girgiza kai deenah tayi"allah yasawake wallahi bazanjeba "
 hakan yaba jalila kwarin guiwar cigaba da magana  "good, wanda kika tsana yake kokarin ganin kinzama cikakkiyar mutum, da amincewarsa kike rayuwa amaraya, kici mai kyau kisha mai kyau ki kwanta amakwanci mai kyau, yabaki nagartaccen ilimi bai banbanta ki da yaransa ba bai taɓa kyashin kashemiki ko ƙwandala ba cikin arzikin da ubangiji yayimasa, madeenah kina ganin wannan bawan allahn ya dace ki yimasa ko kallon banza bare fadar bakar magana, meyasa tsiwarki da rashin kunyarki bata kyale har shi ba? ".. 
Yadda jalila ta tsareta da idanu yasanya deenah share kwallar data b'ata mata fuska.. 
"Kuma aunty dan ya yayimin wannan shine zaimin wulakanci, kuma ke kika min bashi ba kece yar uwata ko? "'.....

*_29_*

"Dani dake duka muna ƙarƙashin  inuwar arzikinsa ne, deenah matuƙar bazaki girmama mijina ba, duk wani girma da zaki bani bana buƙata ki rik'e.. Saidai inaso kisani idan kika fidda iyayenki da suka haifeki babu wanda yayiwa rayuwarki gata sama da yallaɓai "... 

Cikin jin haushin yadda aunty jalila ke danganta rayuwarta da  sir IG deenah ke duban aunty jalilar tare da karawa manyan idanunta girma "aunty har da ke da yadikko kenan? '.. 
 Furzar da iskar bakinta jalila tayi cikin ƙosawa da wautar deenah.. 
Bata tanka taba hakan yasa deenah cigaba da magana a raunane "ni dai  aunty kiyi hakuri, bazan sake yimasa haka ba... "

Duban tsanake jalila tayimata  tana nazarinta "kin tabbata? ".. 

"Eh aunty"! 
Tabata amsa cikin ƙaguwa... 

"Bani kikayiwa laifi  ba, inaso kije kisamesa kibasa hakuri kuma yimasa alkawarin bazaki sake yimasa rashin kunya ba ko gardama idan yace kiyi wani abu"... 
Shiru deenah tayi tare da sunkuyar da kanta aranta kuwa batajin hakan mai yuwuwa ne.. Yaji  daɗin kara wulakantata kenan... Koda zatayi hakan saidai tayi dan farincikin yayarta da ta gama mutuwa akan mijinta... "Shkanan idan nayi hakan zaki daina fushi dani ko aunty? ".. 
Araunane tayi maganar.. 
Shafa fuskarta jalila tayi tare da murmishin da bai bayyana haƙoranta ba "sosai makuwa, ".. 

Atare suka baro kitchen din.. Jalila na ta yiwa deenah nasiha.. Musamman akan yadda zata dinga yiwa mutane magana mai daɗi...
Deenah kuwa tamkar abun zama ya ke akwalwarta sai bin jalila take da to... 


As usual kamar yadda jalila ta saba ne, idan har   sir Aj yana gari, irin wannan lokacin (bayan issha) tana turakar mijinta... 
Saidai wannan lokacin a kayataccen falonsa yake sir Aj yana zaune yana kallon labarun karfe tara (pm)  sosai ya mayar da hankalinsa akan labarun.. 
Jalila ce ta shigo falon tare da sallama hannunta dauke da tire na koren shayin  (green tea) din da ta hadomasa... 
Amsa mata sallamar yayi  cikin kulawa fuskarsa dauke da murmishi.. Kafin ya mayar da dubansa ga tv din har lokacin da jalila ta karaso ta zauna agefensa.. A maidaidaicin center table din dake kusa da shi ta ajiye tire din tare da zama..

Remote control sir IG. Ya danna yakashe tvn  dan dama angama labaran, kanun labaran ake maimatawa... 

Cikin kulawa ya dubi jalila dan tunda yamma ya fahimci akwai abinda take kokarin sanar masa... 
"Mene ne kike so ki fadamin, na lura da abinda kike son sanarmin "... 
Cikin nutsuwa ta tsiyayo tea din aflask ta mika masa acup yakarba yana dubanta dan jiran abinda zai fito daga bakinta  jinjina kai tayi  lokaci  daya  tayi kasa da kanta "yallabai game da zancen deenah ne "... 
Shiru tayi dan batasan ta ina zata soma basa hakuri ba.. Saida ya kurbi tea din  ya dubeta Ƙara tattaro dukkan nutsuwar sa yayi akanta tare da dan ya mutsa face dinsa "me kuma deenah tayi "... 

Dago Da idanunta tayi ta dubesa "dan allah kayi hakuri akan abinda deenah keyi maka.. Sam banajin daɗin hakan nadauki kuma kwakkwaran mataki akanta  bazata sake yin hakanba na sani komai yafaru ni ce na janyo dan nice na dage da zaman deenah atare dani... Ina mai... 

"Ya isa! "
Yayi maganar cikin sanyin muryarsa tare da daga mata hannu still fuskarsa dauke da murmishi, saida ya lumshe idanunsa kafin ya waresu akanta "idan kina fadar haka sai naga kamar kina nunamin deenah ke kaɗai ke da ita, nafiso ki sanyamana idanu, ina nufin kibarni da ita da sannu zan saita zamanta agidannan, tun ranar da deenah tazo gidannan nakeji kuma nadauka tamkar ni na haifeta, ban taɓa jin wani abu da daya tak azuciyata da zaisa naji bani ne na haife taba,haƙƙin tarbiyantar da ita na ratayasa awuyana,  zan taya ki tsayuwa tsayin daka ganin deenah ta daɓiantu da irin ɗabiar da kowane yaro akeso yakasance da ita... 

Cikin jindaɗin kalamansa take dubansa tana  lumshe idanunta "nagode yallabai, totally bansan kalmomin da zanyi amfani dasu ba gurin gode maka ba.... 
Amma yallabai deenah tamkar kanwa ce agareka ba 'ya ba, ".. 

Juya  cup din dake hannunsa yake yana duba nta  "Akwal laifi dan na riketa amatsayin 'ya duk da kanwace agareki?.. Mun  isa ajiye 'ya kamar deenah, ko da zata girmewa yaronmu ba da wani abu bane "... 
Ya idar da maganar yana tuhumar yanayinta.. 

Murmishi ta ɗanyi mai kama da yake tana duban yallabai.. "Hakane amma ka dinga dubanta ayadda na ɗauketa.. Ka barta amatsayin yar karamar ƙanwarka"...

Cike da walwala yakamo hannayenta ya sumbata "karki damu madam, ni ne fa? Nakumayi miki alkawari amma sai idan kindawo nigeria allah yabaki lafiya okey? ".... 
Bata fahimci yanayin da ya karɓi zancen ba, ta amsa da ameen tana murmishi... 
Kamar dai yadda yafaɗa jirgin karfe sha ɗaya yabi zuwa kasar nigeria .. 

Tun bayan barin sir Abdulmajid akasar india da kwanaki uku kullum sai ya kira ammie yaji jikin Jalila bakidaya  hankalinsa ya koma a asibitin... Alhmdlllh ubangiji yana kawo sauki acikin lamarin.. 
Bayan gama sallar laasar da jalila tayi, yadikko ta taimaka mata ta shiga bayi  kamar yadda jalila ta bukata.. Fitowar su kenan jalila ta rike kanta da karfin gaske tana karanto addu o i dan yadda kan ta ke sara mata ne sam bata iya ganin komai... 
Hankalin yadikko yayi kololuwar tashi.. Tun kafin tayi yunkurin kiran ammie.. Daya daga cikin nurses din dake kula da jalila tashigo.. Da sauri ta karasa tare da taimakawa yadikko suka kwantar da jalila dake kokarin sulalewa akasa.... 
Nan da nan nurse din tafara duba jalilar, yadda zafin jikinta ke tsanantuwa yasanya ta fita dasauri dan kiran likita.. 
Yadikko kuwa duk addu oin da suka zo bakinta tofawa jalila take tana hawaye cikin matsanancin tashin hankali... 

Likitoci biyu ne suka shigo dakin a hanzarce cikin kankanin lokaci da sanin makamar aiki suka shiga duba lafiyar jalila, yayinda suka da yadikko a waje.... 
Ganin yamdda yadikko ta fito tana kuka yasanya su ammie da baba dake waje karasowa inda take suna tambayar ta lafiya... 
Amsar da ta basu ta ɗagamusu hankali bakidayansu kai da kawowa suka shigayi suna ambaton sunan ubangiji... 
Acikin dakin da jalila take lokaci daya suka haɗamata iskar oxcegen dan lokaci daya numfashinta ke kokarin janyewa.. Duk yadda sukaso kokarin ceto rayuwar jalila abun ya faskara, domin kuwa kana naka ne allah na nasa ta allah itace karko... Idan kwana ya kare kuma babu tsumi babu dubara amsa kiran ubangiji wajibi ne ga duk mahaluki.... Bakidaya likitocin cikin wani irin yanayi suka tsaya suna duban jalila, domin duk bincikensu ayanxu ya tabbatar musu da babu ruhi agangar jikinta.. 
Yadda suka fito bakidayansu da sanyin jiki yasanya babah da ammie  tunkarar inda suke. Yadikko kuwa zama tayi kawai tana kuka.... 
Da harshen turanci suka yiwa ammie bayanin da ida hargitsa kwalwarta.. Babah sam bai fahimci abinda suke fada ba, cikin tashin hankali yake duban ammie da duk jikinta yasaki tana ambaton sunan allah.. 
"Innalillahi wa inna ilaihi raji'un wanda ya fimu son jalila ya karɓi abarsa, ashe saukin da kika samu na tafiya ne.... ".. 
Shine abinda ammie ke fada wa babah ta kutsa kanta acikin dakin.. Babah da yadikko har karo sukeyi suka bi bayan ammie... 
Cikin karfin hali baba ya yaye mayafin da aka rufe jikin jalila da fuskarta da shi... 
Tamkar bacci takeyi mai tattare da nutsuwa ba mutuwa ba tamkar kayi mata magana ta tashi.. 
Hasbunallahu waniimal wakil baba ke karantowa "allah sarki jalila, kafin rashin mahaifiyarki bantaba rashi da ya girgizani kamar na ki ba, nayi rashin 'ya, ina alfahari dake domin kin mutune abisa darajar ki ta matar aure, kin tafi batare da mijinki yana fushi dake ba, kin tafi babu fushi na iyayenki akanki babban abun alfahari na kin mutu ne da darajar igiyoyi uku dake kanki, insha allah zaki kasance acikin yantattun mata a aljannah.. Bana kukan takaicin mutuwarki jalila.. Ina kuka ne narashin nagartacciyar  ya da nayi aduniya "... 
Gaza cigaba da kalaman sa babah yayi sakamakon muryarsa da fara rawa saboda hawayen da ya fara xubarwa... 
Daga ammie har ya dikko kuka sukeyi awannan lokacin sosai... 
Wannan rana basu iya rintsawa ba.. Yayinda suka tasa gawar jalila agaba suka cigaba da yimata addu oin da cewa da rahamar ubangiji... 
Washe gari kuwa asibitin tayi handle case din gawar jalila tare da mika musu gawar bisa samun visar gaggawa zuwa kasar nigeria...
Har suka shigo kasar nigeria ammie batayi yunkurin kiran sir Abdulmajid ba, batasan ta yaya zata fara kiransa ba ta sanar dashi wannan mummunan labarin ba ta yaya zaiji sakon mutuwar matarsa... 
Driver kawai ta kira tasanarmasa yazo airpot ya daukesu.. 
Kai tsaye  gidan sir Abdulmajid aka wuce da gawar jalila domin acanne yakamata ayi janaizarta... 
Cikin kankanin lokaci ammie tasanarwa da mutanen jumeta.. Kafin wani lokaci su maryam harda nafisa duk sunzo agidan dr junaidiyya ma haka tasamu wani hargittsatsen labarin.. 
Lokaci daya gidan ya ida rikecewa da koke koke hatta ma aiktan gidan basa cikin nutsuwarsu.. Ita kanta maryam tashin hankalinta yadda ya Abdulmajid zaiji mutuwar jalila.. Nafisa ita kanta kuka takeyi sosai saidai nata kukan yasha banban da irin wanda mutanen dake gun keyi domin kuwa kuka takeyi na zallar farincikin wannan rana, naganin gawar makiyiyarta akwance, hakan ya tabbatar mata da komai zaixo mata da sauki...

Babah kuwa yana ababban falo shi kaɗai, dan kai tsaye dakinta aka wuce da gawarta (jalila), kiran asabe yayi yasanar da ita sun dawo nigeria, jalila kuwa ciwo ya warke allah yakarbi abunsa... 
Kuka goggo asabe tasaka tare da salati tayi wurgi da wayar, tattara kan su deenah tayi da basu jima da dawowa daga makaranta ba, arikece ta tarar musu da mai adaidaita suka wuce gidan yallaɓai, a hanya deenah ke tambayar goggo abinda yasanyata kuka, saidai har lokacin goggo bata iya baiwa madina amsa ba, hakan ya haifarwa da deenah da sanyin jiki taja bakinta tayi shiru tana kallon goggon.. 

Sir Abdulmajid kuwa yana office ganin kiran ammie da numberta ta nigeria sai zuciyarsa ta buga da karfin gaske... Cikin faduwar gaba ya ɗaga kiran batare da yace komai ba.. Daidaita nutsuwarta ammie tayi  tare da kiran sunansa "Abdulmajid kazo gida mundawo "... 
Shine abinda ta fada ta kashe wayar dan batasan me zata kuma faɗa ba... 
Cikin tashin hankali yabi wayar da kallo yayinda zuciyarsa ta cigaba da tsananta bugawa , nan da nan jikknsa yafara rawa... 
Yanayin yadda yafito daga office dinsa cikin tsananin tashin hankali zai nuna maka ba a nutse yake ba... 
Wannan lokacin dakansa yaja motarsa dan bayada jimurin jiran driver ya yi driving dinsa ahalin da yake ciki yanxu baya fatan abinda zuciyarsa ke ayyana masa yazama gaskiya.... 
Ubangiji ne kawai yakawosa gida lafiya tun afarfajiyar gidannasa yadda tsirarun mutane suka fara haduwa ya haifarmasa da wani jiri da sanyin jiki.. Kallon mutanen yake yana girgiza kai tare da duban yanayin yadda ma aikatan gidan suka koma... 

Cikkn sauri da rudewa yakutsa kansa acikin ababban falon inda ananne ya tarar da babah... 
Tashi babah yayi tsaye ganin sir Abdulmajid kamar an tillosa.. 
Wani irin kallo mai tattare da tsoron abinda zai fito daga bakin baba sir keyiwa baban.. 

Cikin dauriya da sigar tausayi babah yafara rarrashin sir da yayi surander yana kallonsa... 
"Kayi hakuri Abdulmajid kullu nafsin zaikatul maout, ubangiji yafimu kaunar jalila,".. 

Bai jira babah ya karashe kalamansa ba ya kutsa kai a sashen jalila, batare da yana duban gabansa ba... 

Surunder yayi atsaye alokacin da ya turo kofar da bedroom  din jalila da  karfin gaske .. 
Agicce yaga gawar jalila  akan carpet din daya mamaye bedroom din  yayinda yadikko da ammie da dr junaidiyya ke gefenta kowa yana sharar hawaye.. 
Su maryam da suke falon jalilar  yadda taga sir Abdulmajid ya wucesu cikin wani irin yanayi yasanyata biyo bayansa... 
Zuwa wannan lokaci bai gama yarda da ba mafarki yakeyi ba musamman yadda brain dinsa take juya masa... 
Cikin mutuwar jiki yakaraso inda gawar jalila take hannu yasanya ya janye mayafin daya rufe fuskarsa.. Lokaci daya ya saki wani sautin kuka da ita kanta ammie bata tabajin yayi irinsa ba, dan a iya saninta saidai taga hawayensa badai taji sautin kukansa ba.... "
Hannunsa yasanya yafara girgiza fuskanta   "kinyimin alkawarin kasancewa tare dani kowani irin yanayi jalila, ki tashi daga baccinnan, pls kidaina wasa da rayuwarki, wakeup jalila "... 
Wani kukanne da ke daga hankalin duk wanda yajisa yakuma katsesa.... 
Rike hannayensa ammie tayi daga fuskan jalila da yake girgizawa "bazata iya tashi ba Abdulmajid, bamuda yadda zamuyi da qaddarar ubangiji.. Kuka bazai taɓa maido maka da jalila ba... 
Fita kowa yayi daga dakin, dan babu wanda zai iya jurar ganin kukan sir Abdulmajid da irin tashin hankalin da ya shiga ciki... 
Ammie ta shiga basa baki da tausarsa, akan kada yabari tauhidinsa yabar gangar jikinsa, ta sani ta kuma fahimci abinda yakeji ayanxu, addua kawai zaicigaba da yiwa jalila ayanxu... 
Saka gawar jalila yayi agaba yana kallo bai iya cewa ammie komai ba, shikuwa fata yakeyi yafarka daga wannan mafarkin da yake ciki dan jin komai yakeyi tamkar amafarki... 
Lokacin  da su goggo asabe suka karaso kofar gidan sir Abdulmajid, tuni kofar gidan har ma da harabar gidan ya fara cika da jamaa da wasu daga cikin manyan masu kudi agarin.. Haka wasu daga cikin yan jaridu duk sun hallara, kafin wani lokaci garin katsina da kewaye ya dauka da rasuwar matar IG Abdulmajid.. 
Har lokacin da suka shiga  harabar gidan madina bata gama fahimtar komai ba, amma dai ta tsinci kanta da jin mumunan faduwar gaba, suna shiga amain falo, ganin jamaa afalon yasanya madina fara tambayar auntynta da bata gani ba haka sultan neman ummiensa yafarayi, iftahal ta na rike da hannun goggo asabe... 
Madina Batare da ta jira amsar da mutanen dake falon zasu bata ba ta kutsa kanta a sashin jalila dan zuwa yanxu zuciyarta tafara bijiromata da wani irin mummuna abu game da auntynta.. 
Cikin tashin hankali tare da fasa kuka madina ta karasa inda gawar auntynta take kwance... Ko ba a fadamata yanxu ta tabbatar da tunaninta.. 
Kankame gawar aunty jalila tayi tana kuka sosai da duk wanda ke wurin saida ya tausayamata.. "Kince bazaki barni ba aunty, daman tafiyar da zakiyi bazaki sake dawowa garemu ba, kuka fa nakeyi kince bazaki taɓa bari nayi kukaba, idan hankane ki tashi ki sharemin hawayena "... 
Ta idar maganar cikin wani irin kuka da bata tabayiba arayuwarta tare da ɗora hannun jalila akan fuskanta akan dole saita sharemata hawayenta... 
Dakyar goggo ta raba deenah daga jikin Jalila "kayyah madina, hakuri zamuyi, da kuka zai maidomana da jalila da ta tashi tun kafin zuwanki... 
Awannan rana tashin hankalin da suke ciki bazai iya misultuwa ba, sai karfe uku na yamma akayiwa jalila sutura.. Dake janaizar ta la'asar ce kafin lokacin mutanen jumeta kusan bakidayansu sun iso mazansu da matansu , karfe hudu na yamma aka kaita gidanta nagaskiya.. (Rayuwa kenan rai ba abakin komai yakeba, allah yabamu ikon cikawa da imani, yasa ranar mutuwarmu takasance ranar farincikinmu) 
Amakabarta kuwa Sir Abdulmajid   ya jima akabarin jalila yana addu oi masu yawan gaske da nema mata gafara agun ubangiji... Wannan lokacin babu hawayen da zai iya zubarwa yaji sauki azuciyarsa,illah adduoin da yakeyi yanxu da suke sama masa sassauci azuciyarsa.  wani irin zafi idanunsa keyimasa.. 
Saida baffah ibrahim ya dafa kafadarsa "ka cigaba da hakuri Abdulmajid, ubangijin da ya baka jalila shi zaiyi maka sauyi mafi alkhairi, duk abinda ubangiji yayi game da bayinsa mai kyau ne...  
"
Tashi muje kaji"
Yayi maganar tare da dagosa... Baffah ibrahim na rike da hannunsa har suka isa bakin mota yayinda ss dinsa suka bude masa mota ya shiga.. Zuwa yanzu sir Abdulmajid baya iya gane komai bare kowa... suna isa gida, jiri ya fara kokarin kayar da shi afarfajiyar gidan... 
Mujaheed da aliyu da kuma taimakon baffah ibrahim ne suka shigar da shi har sashinsa akan gadonsa suka kwantar dashi tare da sanarwa ammie halin da yake ciki.. 
Batare da ɓata lokaci ba ammie tasaka aka kira family doctor dinsa ya duba sa.. Bayan likita yagama duk bincikensa ya tabbatarwa da su ammie cewa jininsa yahau tare da tsanantar bugawar zuciya amma yayimasa allurar bacci, kafin lokacin bayason abinda yadanganci hayaniya... 

Sosai yanayin da tilon dannata yake ciki yakuma saka ammie damuwa, halin da yake cikine yasanya ko manya manyan baki daga kasarnan da suke zomasa taaziyya basu samu ganin sa ba... Dan ko maigirma gwamna da yazo bai samu ganinsa ba, saidai su baffah yayiwa ta aziyya.... 
Bangaren madina kuwa tun bayan da aka fita da gawar jalila daga gidan ta rufe kanta adakinta tana kuka, dakyar kuma aka rabata daga jikin gawar... Yadikko da Dr junaidiyya  sunyi bugun kofar amma taƙi buɗewa.. Saida yadikko tayi dakyar kafin ta buɗe kofar.. Tana buɗe kofar ta fada jikin yadikko tare da kankameta takuma saka wani kukan "bazan sake ganin aunty ba yadikko, bazan sake fira da ita, bazata kuma rarrashina idan ina kuka ba.... Yadikko wlh duniyar babu daɗi nima kubarni na mutu.. Tunda aunty jalila ta tafi ta barni ".. 
Kalaman madina yakuma tsinkawa yadikko zuciya ahankali dr junaidiyya ta zvzaunarda madina  akan gefen  gadonta cikin damuwar tasoma magana "rashin jalila bake kaɗai ya shafaba madina, kada rashin jalila yasanya kifice da ga imaninki , kafin jalila kin rayu batare da mahaifiyaba , kuma baki fasa rayuwa ba, haka yanxu rashin jalila bazai dakatar da rayuwarkiba  fa ce idan kwananki ne ya kare.. Allah baya barin wani dan wani yaji daɗi, da kuwa yana yin hakan da bai dauki rayuwar mahaifiyarkuba yabarku kunyi maraici ba... Kowa na rayuwane bisa lokacin da ubangiji ya dibarmasa da zarar wannan lokacin yayi babu tsimi kuma babu dubara.. Adduar neman dacewa da rahamar ubangiji zamu  cigaba da yimata ba kuka ba ... "
Cewar dr 
Da ire iren wadannan kalamanne  yadikko  tacigaba da rarrashin madina, har zuciyarta tafara sanyi... 
Da akayi adduar ukune gidan yafara raguwa ciki kuwa har da su mommy, itace ke takawa nagisa burki akan yadda takeso ta dinga shiga jikin sir Abdulmajid a irin yanayin da yake ciki yanxu, ta daina ganin asirinsu ya fara tasiri, so ya fi komai tasiri arayuwa, ta bari har lokacin da zai dawo nutsuwarsa daga nan ssi ta cigaba da kokarin shigemasa, kafin lokacin da zata sanya yamanta da jalila bakidaya arayuwarsa... 
Bisa haka yasanya nafisa bin umurnin mommy dan samun masalaha akan shawararta... Tunda dai masu iya magana sunce aski idan yazo gaban goshi zafi ne da shi.. Zata idar da hakurinta tunda komai yazo karshe. 
Hajiya innah kuwa sai washe gari suka koma.. Har lokacin tana tausar zuciyar Abdulmajid da yake kokarin zama majnoon a yanxu

Da ƙarfin guiwa madeenah ke bin bayan jalila har suka cimma koramar... 
Wuri ne mai kyau da daɗin yanayi mai daukar hankali sosai. 
Cikin sauri madina ta saki hannun jalila tare da ida karasawa inda ruwan yake ta durkusa ta sanya dukkan  hannayenta tana kamfato ruwan tana sha,  yadda take shan ruwan zai tabbatar maka da cikin yanayin jin kishi take, saidai duk da daɗin ruwan da takeji bai kashemata kishin dake addabarta ba .. 
Hakan yasanyata saurin dago da kanta da zummar yiwa jalila   korafi akan ruwan... 
Cikin tashin hankali ta mike tsaye dan ko kusa bataga jalila akusa da ita ba ta nemeta ta rasa, tamkar wacce ta b'ace.  awurin da ta gaza gane daji ne ko sansani ko wani wuri  makamancin haka ...
Wani irin tsoro da firgici ne ya lullubeta aruɗe take kwalawa jalila kira mai amsa kuwwa awurin  lokaci ɗaya ta sanya kuka mai tattare  da  tsoro da iya karfinta"... 

Goggo asabe da ke dakinta da sautin ihun madeenah yatasheta daga bacci Dagudu  ta shigo dakin su deenah tare da kunna tocilan din dake hannunta akan fuskar deenah da ta kankame pillow tana k'ara... 
Cikin rudewa da rashin sanin abunyi goggo ta gumtsi ruwa abakinta ta fesawa deenah afuska... 
Ba shiri deenah ta wartsake daga baccin da takeyi.. 
Agigice ta rungume goggo tare da saka kuka ta ko ina jikinta rawa yakeyi ga gumi dake keto mata... 
Dago ta goggo tayi daga jikinta  dan ta lura ba a hayyacinta take ba "ke nutsu mana, ko ma miye mafarki ne kike yi ba gaske ba kidawo hankalinki  , fadamin meyafaru, dan tun adaki cikin bacci nakejiyo ihunki wai karta tafi ta barki, wacece wacce zata tafi tabarki din? "... 
Sai alokacin deenah tayi shiru tare da zubawa goggo manyan idanunta ashe kuwa mafarki ne takeyi ba gaske ba.. Yanzu ne take aduniyar zahiri...  
Ganin yadda deenah ta tsareta da idanu ya sanya goggo asabe tunanin kodai madina tanada shafin iska ne kafin tacigaba cigaba da magana "kinga irinta ko? Na tabbatar da zaki kwanta baki yi addua ba, zaiyi wahala ma idan kina addua kafin ki kwanta, badan allah yasanya malam na da nauyin bacci ba da har shi saikin razana da ihunki.. 
Shiru deenah tayi cikin yanayin sanyin jiki yayinda zuciyarta har lokacin ke bugawa da karfi... Kamo hannun goggo tayi ta dora akan kirjinta tasoma magana "goggo kinji yadda kirjina ke bugawa kuwa?  wani irin mafarki nayi akan aunty jalila.... "
Itakanta goggo saida kirjinta ya buga tayi saurin katse madina da zata sake magana "kinga koma ki kwanta tsakiyar dare ne yanxu , mummunan mafarki ba a bayyanasa ki barwa ranki kikuma dinga addua koma miye zaizo da sauki,  kada ki tada yarannan ".. 
Tayi maganar bayan ta tabbatar madina ta koma ta kwanta.. 
Asanyaye goggo asabe tatashi Zata fice tana nazarin kalaman madeenah.. 
"Goggo dan allah ina aunty jalila taje ".. 
Madina ta jefowa goggo tambayar  dake kokarin barin dakin . 
Tsayawa tayi tana duban madina cikin nazari kafin tace "na fadamiki taje gaishe da yadikko kauye ko baki yarda bane? ".. 
Saurin girgiza kai madina tayi "nagaza samun nutsuwa akan hakan, kawai naji inason kasancewa da aunty awannan lokacin... ".. 
"To kikoma ki kwanta yanzu darene, kiyimata addua allah yamaidota lafiya "... 
Ta idar da maganar tare da janyomusu kofa ta fice daga dakin tana tunani akan zancen deenah, mai yuwuwa akwai abinda ubangiji ya nunawa deenah acikin baccinta, koma miye tana fatan allah ya mayar da shi alkhairi.. 
Deenah kuwa dakyar tasamu wani baccin ya kuma daukarta, dan yanayin da jikinta yayi sanyi sambatajin dadin jikinnata..
Washe gari ma haka tatashi jikinta sam babu kuzari, hatta baba yagama nazarin madina... 
Ko abun kari da goggo ta hada musu kadan ta iya ci, ita kanta goggo saita tsinci kanta cikin sanyin jikin yanayin da ta ga madina... 
Koda baba ya tambayi madina abinda ke damunta dan yaga sam ayau babu kwaramniya da rashin nutsuwar da ta saba atattare da ita.. Bata boyemasa komai ba ta fada masa, saidai bata fadamasa yanayin mafarkin da tayi ba da goggo tace mata babu kyau bayyana mummunan mafarki... "
Cikin nutsuwa baba ke rarrashin madina akan tasaki jikinta, gobe ko jibi yayarta zata dawo... 
A soro kuwa da goggo ta raka baba da ke shirin fita, cikin damuwa take masa zancen madina "tun jiya da yarinyar nan tayi mafarkinnan jikina yayi sanyi, ina fatan ubangiji ya tashi kafadun jalila, gashi ka ce har yanxu bata frko ba".. 
Ta idar da maganar tana goge kwallar da zubomata...
 Shima baba jiki asanyaye yake duban  asabe saida yasauke ajiyar zuciya kafin ya magantu "babu abinda jalila ke bukata yanxu da yawuce mu tayimata addua allah yabata lafiya, babu wanda ya isa acikinmu ya canja kaddarar ubangiji, abun tausayi bakidaya Abdulmajid  yana cikin wani irin yanayi da dole atausayamasa dan ma yana da karfin zuciya da jarumta, addua kawai zamu cigaba da yimata... "
Cikin alhini asabe ta amsa da "ubangiji allah yatashi kafaɗunta ".. Ta dora da yimasa allah ya tsare dan daganan zai wuce asibitinne daga nan sai ya wuce kasuwa... 
*****
Ahankali tasoma buɗe kwayar idanunta dasukayima ta nauyi sosai... Biji² take duban nurse din dake tsaye akanta tana kokarin cire mata ledar drip din daya karashe shiga jikinta... 
Yanayin wurin jalila tasoma bi da kallo duk da bakasafai take iya gane komai ba sakamakon Biji² da take ganin komai amma ta fahimci a inda take... 
Ganin farfaɗowar jalila yasanya nurse din fita ahanzarce taje tasanarwa likita... 
Cikin lokaci ƙankani likita ya shigo tare da nurse din... 
Motsa lips dinta tasoma yi tana lumshe idanunta da dukkan alamu magana take so tayi... 
Cikin disasshiyar murya tasoma magana "ina mijina yake? "... 
"Kina bukatar isasshen hutu ayanzu madam,.. "
Saurin girgiza kai tayi tare da katse likitan dake kokarin yimata allurar bacci.. 
"Ina bukatar ganin wani nawa anan, nasan su na tare dani, ba na bukatar wani hutu ayanxu"... 
Ta idar da maganar agajiye,... 
Yadda ta dage ne ya sanya likitan fita.. 
Awani corridor da su ammie suke  dake kusada ward din da jalila take ciki likitan ya nufa  da yake driver bai jima da kawo ammien ba itama anan ta tarar da baba... 
Sir IG  kuwa yana masallacin da ke cikin  asibitin tunda yayi sallar la'asar bai dawo ba saida yayi adhkar din yamma kamar yadda yasaba tare da nemawa jalila sauki agurin ubangiji.. Bakidaya a yan kwanakin  ya fice dagakammaninsa, baya samun isasshen bacci atare dashi, haka ko a office baya iya aikata komai, rashin lafiyar jalila ta dagula masa lissafi sosai... 
Ganin likita ya doso su yassnya su tashi bakidayansu cikin yanayin fargabar abinda likitan zai fad'a, tun kafin ammie da babah su magantu likitan yasanar dasu jalila ta farfaɗo saidai ta dage da son ganin mijinta ko wani nata... 
Hamdala suka shigayi bakidayansu, Cikin matukar farinciki ammie da baba suka kutsa kai a ward din...
Ganin ammie da babah da jalila tayi lokaci daya ta saki murmishi tare da hawaye.. 
Saurin riko hannunta ammie tayi tanayimata sannu tare da hamdala da farfadowar da tayi... 
Baba ma kuwa cikin karfin zuciya yake yiwa yar tasa sannu.. Matsanancin tausayin jalila yakeyi sosai... 
Duban baba tayi ta mayar da dubanta ga ammie dake shafa hannunta dake cikin hannun ammien.. 
Lumshe idanu takumayi lokaci daya wasu hawayen suka sake zubomata.. 
Hannu ammie tasanya ta goge mata fuskanta cikin sanyin murya tasoma magana "haba jalila ba kuka zakiyi ba, ubangiji zaki godemawa da ya farkar dake daga doguwar sumar da kikayi"... 
Ahankali jalila ta soma magana tamkar mai koyon magana "bawai ina kuka bane saboda cutar da ubangiji ya jarrabeni da ita bane, ina kuka ne saboda ina tsoron lokacin rabuwa da  makusantana,  da bazan sake ganinsu arayuwataba "... 
"Waye ya fadamiki zaki mutu, cuta ba mutuwa bace jalila"... 
Cikin karfin zuciya da tauhidi baba yayi maganar... 
Sauke idanunta da sukayi mata nauyi tayi akan baba tare da riko hannunsa."wannan magana ce kawai da hausawa ke amfani da ita, amma awannan matakin da nake, bani kadai ba ku kanku nasan kunsallama da lamarina,  inaji kamar  bankwana nakeyi  da duniyar nan... Babana tun bayan rasuwar mahaifiyar mu kai kaɗai ne garemu nida madeenah, gashi yanzu zan tafi na barku, madina kadai zata rage atare da kai, yayinda babu innah nima kuma zaa wayi gari babu ni, ina neman alfarma da kayafemin baba"... 
Ta idar da maganar hawaye nakuma zubomata.. 
Shikansa baba awannan lokacin hawaye ne yakeyi.. 
Hatta ammie hawayen takeyi... 
Shigowar sir Aj ya sanya bakidayansu daidaita nutsuwarsu tare da goge hawayen idanunsu... 
Rungume jalila Aj yayi tsam akirjinsa
Cikin matsanancin farinciki ya sumbaci goshinta yana hamdala tamkar an masa bushara da cewar tawarke ne bakidaya ... 
Murmishi takeyi sosai tare da saka hannunta ta shafi fuskarsa cikin wata irin ƙauna dasukeyiwa juna suke kallon juna.. 
"Inaji ajikina madam zaki samu lafiya, komai zai dawo kamar bai faruba... Kingani ko ammie? "
Cikin farinciki sir yamayar ga tambayar ga ammie dake dubansu, da nazarin matsananciyar kaunar da sukewa junansu... 
Murmishi jalila takumayi still tana dubansa "zanso haka yallabai, amma karka sare gurin yimin addua, a yadda nakeji yanzu kamar bankwana nakeyi daku, kamar idan na rufe idanuna bazan sake buɗesu akankuba "... 
Rufemata baki sir yayi da hannunsa cikin wani irin yanayi yake girgiza kai "kidaina faɗar hakan, how do you think can I live without uh, look yanxu haka 4days ya rage mana zamu fita kasar india a sake duba lafiyarki, pls dont say that again pls.. "
Cikin yanayin raunin da bata taba ganinsa acikiba yayi maganar... 
Nurses ne guda biyu suka shigo dakin, daya daga cikin su ce ta dubi sir Aj dan ba boyayye bane, cikin girmamasa tasoma magana "yallabai lokacin shan maganin madam yayi, yanxu haka zaayi mata allurar bacci, tana bukatar ta huta, im sorry "... 
Ta idar da maganar tana duban su Ammie da babah... 
Jinjina kai sir yayi batare da ya dubeta, ya shafa hannun jalila dake hannunsa tare da sake sumbatar gefen fuskanta yayi mata saida safe ya fice cikin yanayin rashin jin daɗin zuciyarsa...
Suma su ammie bayansa suka bi yayinda jalila ta bisu da idanu tana sauke ajiyar zuciya... 
.. 
Nafisa kuwa tun bata damu da rashin ganin ammie agidaba har ta damu duk da yanzu ta canja takunta, dan bazata kuma zuwa da salon kutsa kai ga ammie ba.. Tunda maganin matsalarta ahannunta yake..amma duk da haka tayi trying number din ammie bata samu ba.. 
Tana zaune ababban  falon  ita  kadai tana kallon wani indian series  bakid'aya gidan yayi mata girma, dan su huhaira ba zama suke a falon ba matukar nafisa na nan... Dan basa sakewa ko alama musamman idan ta fahimci ammie batanan, bata barinsu su sake .. 
Sam bata ganin da cewa tana afalo mai aiki na falo itama tana kallo, wannan ai kasakanci ne da raini wanda sam bazata dauka ba... Saɓanin ammie da har fira takeyi  zama tayi dasu.
Salame ce daya daga cikin masu aikin ammie ta 
a shigo falon  , hannunta dauke da tire na fruits salad da nafisa ta ce ahado mata, ahankali ta ajiye atable din dake gefen nafisan.. 
Batare da nafisa ta dubetaba tace "kuna nufin har yanxu bakusan inda matar gidannan ta tafiba? "'
Tayi maganar hankalinta na kan tvn da take kallo... 
Yanayin sunan da ta ba ammie yasanya salame dubanta tare da yin kasa da kanta tafara magana"ta dawo da yammannan tayi wanka ta fita alokacin kina bacci, saitace kar atasheki ".. 

Kallonta nafisa tayi ta watsar tacigaba da cewa "to ina take tafiyane...? "
"Madam din yallaɓai ce bata lafiya  yanxu haka tana asibiti ankwantar "... 
Gyara zama nafisa tayi tare da mayar da hankalinta ga salame, "meke damunta "...
Cikin son jin amsar da salame zata bata tayi maganar tare da tsareta da idanu... 
Cikin  alhini salame tafara jinjina kai kamar wata kadangaruwa kafin ta fadawa nafisa komai akan abinda tasani.. 
Wani irin farincikine marar misultuwa ya ziyarci zuciyar nafisa da ya gaza boyuwa afuskanta mai tattare da mamaki... 
Bata kuma bin takan salame ba tatashi da sauri ta nufi sashinta infew minutes ta gama shirinta ta fito.. Har lokacin salame na tsaye sororo tana nazarin nafisa... 
Nafisa kuwa kai tsaye asibitin tawuce.. dan ta tabbatar asibitin fam dinsu da suke da files acan aka kuma kai jalilar... 
Amota takira mommy da hajja duk tayimusu kyakkyawan albishir, wannan lokacin sun sake shiga farincikj tare da tabbatar mata da ya Aj mallakinta ne ita kaɗai gashi kuma zata gani, ita kanta ayadda take gani ubangiji na kaunarta da zai dauke mata matsalarta (jalila) alokacin da take tsaka da bukatar abun kaunarta, bakidaya amatsayin gawa take duban jalilar dan yadda aka tabbatar mata da ciwon cronic cancer ke damunta.. Bata dauka yaƙin zai kare cikin sauki haka ba.... 
Da wannan tunanin ta isa asibiti cikin farinciki.. 
Aparking risk ta hango ss din sir yayinda suke kokarin bude masa mazaunin baya a rantsattsiyar motarsa da dukkan alamu barin asibitin zaiyi... 
Da sauri ta karasa da motarta agun tayi parking akusa da motarsu.. Tun kafin ta fito ta bude handbag dinta tafito da kwallin ta shafa a idanunta Dan hajja ta tabbatar mata kada tayi kuskuren hada ido dakowa idan ba Aj ba matukar tashafa kwallin... 
Da hanzari ta balle murfin motar ta fito, alokacin sir ya shige motarsa amazaunin baya... 
Knocking din glass din tayi, ahankali sir Aj yassuke glass din da hannunsa,  nafisa ce yagani atsaye   haka kawai yaji zuciyar sa tabuga da karfin gaske...
Itama dubansa takeyi cikin yanayin alhinin data lullube fuskarta da shi tasoma  gaishesa da tambayarsa mai jiki cikin salon sauya murya.. 
Fuskarsa ba yabo ba fallasa yake dubanta tare da amsa mata, saidai sosai ta lura da yadda yanayinsa ya sauya wanda ta tabbatar rashin lafiyar mayyar matarsa ne ya haifarmasa da yanayin.... 
Yana kokarin zuge glass din ta dakatar da shi "yayana awane part ne madam take a asibitin... ". 
Tayi maganar cikin nazarin irin amsar da zai bata... 

Dubanta yasakeyi tare da ɗan yamutsa fuska yasoma magana "babu nisa gaba kaɗan zakiyi idan baki gane ba kikira ammie..." 
Ya idar da maganar tare  da zuge glass din motar sakamakon yadda zuciyarsa ke tsananta bugawa aduk lokacin daya dubi nafisar... 
Umurni yabaiwa driver yaja motar suka fice... 
Itakuwa ɗaga kafadunta tayi tabi motar da kallo tana murmishi , duk da fama da shegen miskilancin da yakeyi zuwa yanxu ta iya ganin tasirin maganinta atare dashi.. Bazatayi garaje ba zata cigaba da binsa har lokacin da zaizame mata raqumi da akala... 
Bata sha wahala tagane kwatancan,  dan tana matsawa gaba ta ga su ammie... Hawaye tafara matsowa bisa dole tashiga tashin hankali akan halin da jalila ke ciki... 
Sosai nafisa tayi nasarar rainawa su ammie hankali, dan duk wanda ke gun saida ta nemi ta sake daga masa hankali da kukanta.. Bata samu ganin jalila ba domin alluran baccin da akayi mata.... 
Tun saura kwanaki biyu tafiyar su india takardun su na tranfer da Visa ya kammalu... 
Zuwa wannan lokacin tuni yadikko ta zo dan baba ya sanar mata komai.. Taci kuka sosai, haka baba ya dinga rarrashinta dai... 
Haka mutanen jumeta su baffah ibrahim da baffah aliyu da sauran kannen abbah duk sun sami labari kuma sunzo sun duba jikin jalila.. Hatta hajiya innah , mommy  zuwairah da su mujahid da matarsa  duk sunxo.. Mommy ma tamkar daga sama sai gata,haka maryam jirgin safe tabiyo itada maigidanta, dan yadda sir abdulmajid yayimata bayanin rashin lafiyar jalila abun yabata tsoro, musamman da taji ana kokarin fitar da ita waje   kowa ya nuna alhininsa da fatan samun lafiya da samun  nasarar aikin da zaayiwa jalila... 
Su baffah ibrahim kuwa koda suka zo nunawa sukayi sam tamkar basu san da nafisa ba aduniya dan bakiɗaya sunfice daga harkarta barema baffah aliyu da yafi kowa zuciya... 
To ita kanta nafisar bata damu ba dan tasan nan da lokaci kadan zasu saki, kuma dakansu zasu nemawa yallaɓai Aj aurenta.. Hakan yasa itama ta tattara baffannin nata ta watsar dan ko gaishesu bata iya yiba, hakan kuwa da tayi yayiwa mommy dadi sosai ita kanta tazo ne dan kar ayi babu ita .. 
Su baffah ibrahim kuwa basu kwana ba aranar suka koma.. Su hajiya innah kuwa saida suka kwana washe gari suka dauki hanya kowa jikinsa asanyaye... 
Maryam kuwa tashiga zallar tashin hankali ganin lokaci daya yadda ya abdulmajid ya yi rama sosai, yadanyi duhu amma ba can ba, ga dukkan alamu baya samun bacci barekuma cin abinci ga yanayin aikinsa... 
haka ta dage da basa baki da shawarwari akan ya rage saka damuwa aransa, duk abinda ubngiji yayi alamarinsa mai kyau ne babu abinda bawa zaiyi da ya wuce biyayya... 
Jalila kuwa ana gobe tafiyarsu india ta matsawa babah da tanaso taga yaranta da kuma deenah, tanaji ajikinta baizama lallai ta dawo nigeria da rayuwa ba, mai yuwuwa ganin karshe ne zatayiwa yaranta...
Zuwa yanxu kowa yagama fahimtar zafin ciwo ne kesaka jalila wasu maganganun.  
Bayan sallar magrib bakidaya baba yazo dasu harda goggo asabe, yadikko kuwa daman tana asibitin.. kuka sosai deenah tasanya ganin auntynta a gadon asibiti daman wannan shine kauyen da goggo asabe tace mata ta tafi?, tun ranar da yadikko ta shigo garin batareda jalila ta gaza samun nutsuwa".. 
Kamo hannunta jalila dake zaune alokacin tayi da d'ayan hannunta ta sanya tana share ma deenah hawayenta.. Lokaci daya ta rungume yar uwar tata tare da saka kuka marar sauti.... Ganin haka yasanya sultan da iftahal dake gefen goggo asabe suma suka rungume ummin tasu suma kukan suka sanya da ya raunana zuciyar duk wanda ke gurin... 
Kankamesu jalila tayi bakidaya akirjinta kafin ta dago da deenah dake jikinta tana goge mata hawayenta... 
"Kimin addua kanwata, ina cikin halin ciwo na tsakanin rayuwa da mutuwa,koda na kasance bana arayuwarki  ki zamo mai juriya, hakuri da karfin hali arayuwarki, saiki kasance mai cimma  nasara arayuwarki "... 
Zuciyar madina takuma tsinkewa dan tabbas irin wadannan kalaman ne aunty jalila tafaɗa mata amafarkinta, amma meyasa take jinsu azahiri yanzu? "... 
Kuka takuma sanyawa kamar yadda taga jalila ta gaza tsayar da nata hawayen.. 
Sake rungume aunty jalila tayi tana girgiza kai "babu wanda zai rabani da ke aunty duk inda zakije nima zan biki "... 
Dr junaidiyya ce tayi karfin halin janye madina daga jikin jalila.. Ita kanta wannan lokacin hawaye take zubarwa.. 
Cikn rarrashi da raunin murya take magana "haba jalila dubi yadda kika tsinka zuciyar duk wanda ke gurinnan ke kanki ƙarawa kanki damuwa kikeyi.. Fatan nasara akan aikinnan da samun yaƙini shi ne yakamata ki dinga yi ba irin haka ba... "
Da bayan hannunta jalila ta goge hawayenta.. Zatayi magana ne sir Aj ya shigo shida maryam ... 
Karasowa yayi sosai da inda jalila take yagama fahimtar kuka tayi alokacin.. 
Tun kafin yayi magana jalila dake dubansa tun shigowarsu ta fara magana, "tun dazu nakeson magana dakai yallaɓai, amma bansan ta inda zan fara maka ba, gashi lokaci yana kuremana "... 
Tayi maganar  rinannun idanunta 
Acikinnasa.. 
Zama yayi agefen gadon da take gab da ita tare da mayar da hankalinsa akanta cikin sanyin jiki yake dubanta... 
Ganin haka yasanya su babah da goggo asabe  harma dasu maryam fita domin basu wuri domin kuwa tsakanin mata da miji akwai sirri atsakaninsu.. 
Dr junaidiyya ma dake kokarin fita jalila ta rike hannun ta tana dubanta... "Tare da ke nakeso muyi maganar".. 
Ta idar da maganar idanunta akan junaidiyya da ta cika da mamakin zancen da har ya shafeta, bayan tsakaninta ne da mijinta... 
Duban juna sukayi ita da sir, kafin su mayar da dubansu ga jalila cikin tsimayin abinda zai fito daga bakinta... 
Kamo hannun yallaɓai Abdulmajid jalila tayi 
Ahankali tasoma magana"kana so na ko yallaɓai "...  
Dubanta yayi sosai yakuma duban dr domin yadda tayi masa tambayar yafara tunanin kamar ba a hayyacinta takeba... 
Jinjina  kai yayi cikin wani irin yanayi tare da cije lip dinsa na ƙasa yacigaba da dubanta cikin kulawa "baki bukatar wannan amsar yanzu domin kindade da sanin amsar nan, saidai nasake tabbatarmiki idan hakan zai sakaki farinciki, ina sonki fiyeda yadda nake son kaina.. Uh all know that... "
Ya idar da maganar cikin raunin murya... 
Sauke ajiyar zuciya jalila tayi tare da zubamasa idanu tana murmishi mai tattare da hawaye  "nasani bazaka bani kunya ba yallaɓai, ina neman alfarma agareka da ka sadaukar da soyayyar da kake min ga madina, ina nufin idan ubangiji ya kaddaro wannan jinyar da nake akai itace ajalina, ina so kamin alkawarin zaka auri madeenah amadadina, zansamu nutsuwa akan hakan domin na tabbatar madina zata kasance acikin farinciki har iya rayuwarta, zaka iya canja madina kanada wannan jarumtar, karka damu da karancin shekarun madina ko wautarta ko bayauba zata sauya , kaso deenah kodan farincikina, da farincikin dana tanadarwa rayuwarta, dan allah  kadamu da farincikina da kake tattali aduniya, yallaɓai na kaine irin mijin da nake yiwa madina fatan samu arayuwarta... Dan allah kamin alkawarin zaka auri madeenah bayan mutuwa ta .... "

Tayi maganar tare da saka dukka hannayenta acikinnasa ta rike gam.. 
Cikin wani irin faɗuwar gaba da hargitsewar tunani yallabai AJ  ke duban jalila tare da ƙarawa manyan idanunsa girma akanta lokaci daya ya sauke dubansa ga junaidiyya da itama din ta daskare acikin wani irin yanayi... 
Zuwa wannan lokaci yana tantamar da wuya idan ciwon nan bai shiga kwalwar jalila ba.. Domin kalaman da takeyi masa tamkar tatsuniyace marar makama ko wani abu mai kama da kwancewar kwalwa.......

Maryam ce tashigo falon sir Abdulmajid tare da  sallama , babu abinda kake ji a yalwataccen falon sai sautin na'urar  sanyaya daki, dake sanyaya falon.ganin babu kowa afalon yasanya   Kai tsaye  ta wuce bedroom dinsa   inda ta tabbatar yana aciki, saida takuma yin sallama kafin ta shiga aciki. 
Akan praymat ta iske  sa   yagama lazimi idanunsa alumshe suke tamkar mai bacci ya jinginar da kansa a jikin gadonsa... 
Zubamasa idanu tayi Wani irin tausayinsa yakuma darsuwa acikin zuciyarta...  Bata jin daɗi ko kadan idan tana ganinsa a irin wannan yanayin.. 
Karasowa tayi ta zauna   akujerar dake fuskantarsa tare da gyaran.. 
Wanda hakan yayi daidai da bude jajayen idanunsa akanta.. 
Ajiyar zuciya yasauke ya cigaba da kallonta batare da ya ce mata komai ba... Ko bai fadaba ta fahimci irin zafin raɗaɗin rashin matarsa da yake gallabawa rayuwarsa ahalin yanxu... 
Locker dinsa ta bude da kanta ta dauko magungunansa ta ajiye agabansa ta bude fridge ta dauko robar ruwa da cup ta tsiyaya tare da mikamasa alokacinne itama ta magantu "lokacin shan maganinka yayi, samun tabbacin da baka sha bane yasanya ammie tace nazo domin induba, dan allah ya kadaina wasa da lafiyarka "... 
Cikin tsinkewar zuciya ta idar da zancen.. 
Batare da ya dubetaba ya karbi cup tare da ajiyesa agefensa ya dago da idanunsa yana duban maryam da itama nata idanun suka ciko da kwallah... 
"Dukkan wadannan magungunan bazasu warkar dani daga abinda ke damunaba  maganin damuwata bata doron duniyar, hanyar neman warakar ciwo na shine  ku dingamin addua allah ya sassautamin radadin rashin mata ta azuciyata..., 
Goge hawayen da suka zubomata maryam tayi tana dubansa " yaya likita ya tabbatar mana da har yanxu blood pressure dinka bai daidaita ba, "
Shiru tayi tare da dora hannunta akan nasa cikin sigar rarrashi tasoma magana "na fahimci radadin da kake ji atare da kai, kada soyayya ta rufemaka idanu ka manta da girman hukuncin ubangiji da abinda yake hukuntawa akan bawansa, kafin jalila tashigo rayuwarka, kana rayuwa kamar yadda ka saba, yanzu ma kasanya wa zuciyarka salama sai ubangiji yayi maka sauyi na alkhairi "... 
Cikin nutsuwa tare da damuwa take magana ... Kafeta yakumayi da idanunsa yana cije lips dinsa da karfin gaske, lokaci daya ya dauke idanunsa daga kanta tare da furzan da iska abakinsa... 
Sam baya cikin yanayin son magana ahalin yanxu ya kuma fi bukatar kaɗaituwa a irin wannan yanayin,
"Is ok kije zansha maganin yanxu "... 
Yayi maganar yana nuna mata tabbacin hakan yana gyaɗa mata kai... 
Tashi maryam tayi jiki a sanyaye zata fice ya katseta da cewar "ina sultan da iftahal suke? "... 
Jiyowa tayi tana fiskantarsa ta basa amsa "suna gun ammie basu jima da gama cin abinci ba "
"Madina fa taci abincin? ".. 
Ya jefo mata tambayar..yana dubanta  
Ajiyar zuciya maryam tayi cikin nazari "zuwa yanxu bansani ba, amma bana tunanin ta iya cin komai, suna adakinsu inaga yarinyar she's abnormal ... "
Jinjina kai yayi ya shigayi kafin yace "shkanan"

Saida yadawo daga sallar issha kafin yashigo babban falon, babu mutane sosai afalon.. Saisu ammie da goggo asabe ...maryam kuma na falon baƙi tana sallah.. sai nafisa dake gefen ammie da tun shigowar sir ta ke satar kallonsa 
Cikin kankanin lokaci ya rame, yayi duhu kadan, duk da haka babu abinda yasauya daga   tasirin kyau da sigar da allah yayimasa yayinda kowani dakika take kuma azabtuwa da matsananciyar kaunarsa da shaawa dake fizgarta acikin jinin  jiki da ɓargo... 
Shikuwa sir Abdulmajid yana gama gaishe da su ammie dakinsu  deenah ya wuce dan ya duba jikin ta ... 
 Acan ya tarar da su sultan da iftahal.. Shigowarsa dakin tayi daidai da fitowar yadikko daga bayi da  ke rike da deenah cikin yanayin galabaita... 

Karasowa ya ida yi cikin dakin cikin mamakin ganin yadda deenah takoma , tayi rama sosai da wani irin haske wanda da ka gani kasan ba na lafiya bane... Lokaci n farko da yaji matsanancin tausayin yarinyar ya kamasa,  Gaishe da yadikko yayi dake kokarin kwantar da deenah akan gado ta rufamata jikinta da bargo sakamakon rawar darin da takeyi... 
Shikansa hankalinsa na gun deenah... 
amsa masa  gaisuwar yadikko tayi  takuma tamnayarsa nasa jikin, .. 
Saida ya amsa kafin yace 
"Deenah batajin daɗi ne "... 
Yayi tambayar yana kuma nazartar yanayin madina kafin ya maida dubansa ga yadikko.. 
"Bansan ya zanyi da madina ba, batajin rarrashi, kullum abu ɗaya kuka, ba ci babu sha yarinya ta zabge kamar wata bulala, ai tunda bazata hakura ba sai ta kashe kanta a wofi, ita dai jalila ta tafi ba dawowa zata yi ba "... 
Tunda yadikko ke magana hankalinsa na gun deenah dake kwance , ta cikin bargon datake rufe dashi ne yake hango yadda take rawar dari... 
Cikin nutsuwa da kulawa ya isa gun gadon nata, hannu yasanya yadan zame bargon  atsakanin wuyanta yasanya bayan hannunsa , wani irin zafi yaji da ya tabbatarmasa har lokacin akwai zazzaɓi ajikinta... 
Duban yadikko yayi asanyaye kafin ya cigaba da magana ahankali "jikinta da zafi har yanxu tasha magani kuwa, ko kuwa akira likita ne ya dubata ".. 
Yayi maganar yana mai sake duban yanayin deenah da jin zaa kira likita ya tilastamata bude jajayen idanunta da suka yimata nauyi tare da kumburi... "Junaidiyya tayimata allurai ta kuma bata magunguna tasha, shi ne ta harar yanxu abayi ".... 

Shiru yayi na wani lokaci kafin ya dubi madina dake ta faman matsar kwallah.. Wannan lokacin cikin nutsuwa yakira sunanta "madeenah mekike so ki ci yanxu bakyaso ki samu lafiya ".. 
Cikin rarrashi ya idar da zancen, itakanta madina saida tadan dago dakanta ta dubesa, yanayin tausayin da bai taɓa nuna wa ba kenan agunta... Kullum saidai harara da kallon banza da yasabar mata... Karo nafarko da ya tausasa kalamansa akanta. 
Dauke idanunta tayi kamar bazata tankasa ba  cikin disasshiyar muryarta ta fara magana "banajin yunwa, ni bana bukatar komai, kuma kar asakemin allura zanji sauki.... ".. 
Ta idar da maganar tana zubar da hawaye.. 
"Kya mutu kuwa da ciwo uwar taurin kai*... 
Cewar yadikko cikin kufula.. 
Sassauta murya yakumayi "is ok, im sorry, jalila bazata dawo ba, na tabbatar a islamiyya malam ya fadamuku duk wanda ya mutu baya dawowa, kiyi hakuri mu cigaba da nunawa jalila soyayya ta hanyar yimata addua "... 
Zubamasa idanu deenah tayi lokaci daya idanunta suka kuma ciko da kwallah, shikansa cikin tsananin karfin hali ya ke rarrashinta.. Lips dinta tasoma motsawa da alama kuka ke shirin ƙara kwacemata.. 
Iftahal dake gefen yadikko ta kamo hannun sir Abdulmajid dake cikin wani irin yanayi "abbie umminmu bazata sake dawowa ba? Yaushe zamu sake ganinta?..., 
"Abbie ammie tace mu dinga yiwa ummie addua,inda ta tafi muma zamu isketa amma aunty deenah kullum sai tayi kuka? "... 
Cewar sultan shima da ya karaso inda sir yake... 
Cikin matsanancin tausayin yarannasa yake dubansu tare da shafa kawunansu ya rungumesu ajikknsa.. Zuciyarsa na bugamasa da karfin gaske... 
Saida ya fizgi nutsuwarsa kafin ya dubi yadikko da ita kanta zuciyarta ta tsinke.. Cikin wata irin murya yasom magana "yadikko kicigaba da baiwa madina baki tana da kulafuci sosai akan jalila, kuma tana da naci, tana bukatar rarrashi sosai... "
Tab'e baki yadikko tayi tasoma magana "itadai wannan bataji ko kaɗan, ayi mutum duniya babu tawakkali da hakuri, ai duk abinda hakuri baiyiba rashinsa bazaiyi sa ba... ". 
Haka yadikko ta dinga mitar halin madina..
 Saida madina tasamu bacci kafin sir Abdulmajid yayiwa su yadikko saida safe... 
Shikansa wannan lokacin   shine karo nafarko da yaji tausayin deenah aransa da damuwa da irin yanayin da yaganta aciki dan tunda akayi rassuwar bai ga deenah ba sai awannan lokacin... 
Cikin wani irin yanayi ya isa sashinsa.. Kayan baccinsa ya sauya ya dauro alwala kamar yadda yasaba tare da addu o i ya kwanta tare da lumshe idanunsa tamkar mai bacci, bakomai yake tunawa ba face daddaɗan rayuwar zaman aurensu da marigayiya mai cike da zallar soyayya da kulawa.. Da ta na akusa dashi ayanxu da tashiga kirkirar hanyoyin da zata faranta masa rai da gayyato nutsuwa atare dashi.... 
Saurin ware idanunsa yayi agefen da yake kwance tamkar zaiyi tozali da ita agefensa cikin sakakkiyar fuskarta mai tattare da murmishi da baya gushewa.. 
Tashi yayi zaune tare da. Furzan da iskar bakinsa ya runtse idanunsa yana ambaton sunan allah, tunanin jalila nema yake yafara sanya masa tana yimasa gizo... 
Cikin sanyin jiki ya sauko da kafafunsa daga kan gadon... Tare da duba agogon dake manne abango da ta nuna 12:48am dare ya raba kenan. 
Ahankali ya zage labulan window tare da bude gilashin windown. . 
Naɗe hannayensa yayi a faffadan kirjinsa yana karewa kayatacciyar farfajiyar gidan kallo, ko ina yayi tsit baya jin sautin komai sai haushin tsirarun karnukan dake kai komo afarfajiyar gidan... 
Runtse idanunsa yakumayi da suke yimasa zafi sosai cikin yanayin matsananciyar kewar dake addabar zuciyarsa ya dawo ya zauna cikin wani irin tunani da ya bijiro abrain dinsa... 

_ina fatan ubangiji ya azurta madina da samun miji kamarka na tabbatar da ta mori nagartaccen namiji,_... 
_deenah kamar ƙanwa ce agareka, ba 'ya ba"..._
_laifi ne dan na rike deenah amatsayin 'ya?, karki manta kusan agemate take da ɗanmu_... 
_inaso kasadaukar da soyayyar da kakemim ga deenah..... _ _"Kayimin alkawari madeenah zata maye gurbina, amatsayina na matarka, ko bazan samu wannnan alfarmar daga gareka ba?!  "_....... 
Saurin ware manyan idaninsa yayi domin ji yake yi kamar alokacin jalila ke masa magiya, dan har gizon muryarta saida yaji akunnensa... 
Meke shirin faruwa dashi ne? Meyasa yake kokarin zama majnoon a wannan lokacin son jalila nema yake ya firgita lissafinsa
 Yanxu ne ya fahimci dalilin da yasa jalila ta ke yawan dangantasa da zancem madina, saboda ubangiji ya jiyar da ita ajikinta bazata tashi daga wannan jinyar ba... 
Bashida buri da ya wuce ayanzu cika burin jalila ta haka zai tabbatar ma da kansa  soyayyar da yake mata hakikanin gaskiyace,.. 
Amma ta ya zai iya sadaukar da soyayyar jalila akan madina, ta ina zai fara bullo da wannan zancen, bai taɓa duban deenah da wani ido da ya wuce yar cikinsa ba mai tarin  kuruciya da wauta.. Taya wannan duban da yakemata zai rikiɗe zuwa na soyayya da kulawa musamman irin ta maaurata.. Bayan shida yarinyar da bata wuce yar cikinsaba tamkar wuta da ruwa suke... To amma koma miye cikar burin jalila da ta mutu dashi aranta wajibine ya sadaukar da wannan soyayya... Da alkawarin da ya daukar mata tun kafin ruhinta yabar jikinta da yahaifar mata da farinciki... 

"Azahiri kin tafi awata duniyar, amma har yanxu soyayyarki araye take, zancigaba da kokarin ganin ta rayu bisa aminci, I will do! amma saboda farincikinki, hakan yamiki ko? Tell me idan akwai wata alfarmar da tafi wannan zanyi, zan raini farincikin madina i promised to uh".. 
Ya faɗa afili tare da zama agefen gadonsa yasanya dukkan hannayensa akansa yana cakuda sumar kansa.. 
Kafin ya tashi yakuma dauro alwala yagabatar da nafilfili bayan ya idar ne ya cigaba da karatun alkur'ani har wuraren asuba... 
Randa akayi adduar bakwai ranar su yadikko suka fara shirin tafiya harda goggo asabe.. Wannan lokacin tare da madeenah aka shirya tafiyar dan zata koma hannun goggo asabe ne da zama har zuwa lokacin da aure zai rabasu, tunda mai rabawa ta rabata da yar uwarta.. 
Awannan rana duk wanda ke gidan saida mutuwar ta dawomasa sabuwa.. Haka goggo ta cigaba da tattarawa deenah duk wasu  kayanta.. 
Ammie ma wani irin yanayi marar dadi ta tsinci kanta aciki  na tafiyar su yadikko, sosai ammie taso abar deenah ahannunta ta hada dasu sultan ta rike, musamman yadda suka shaku da junansu, koba komai zata dinga debemata kewar marigayiya jalila dik da suna da banbancin halaye... To ganin yadda yadikko tafi nutsuwa da tafiya da madina ne yasanya ammie barin zancen azuciyarta gudun karta takura su, saidai daga bangaren karatunta babu abinda zai sauya, driver zai dinga daukota yana mayar da ita tamkar dai ada .. 
Itakanta madina gidan sam batajin dadinsa, tun ranar da aunty jalila ta fita daga cikinsa take jinsa kamar wani kurkuku sam babu daɗin zama, dan bata ganin fitilar gidan.. Ita kanta zama dasu yadikko sai tafijin dadinsa ayanxu..
 Har parking area ammie tarakaso itada su sultan da maryam harda nafisa .. 
Rungume deenah ammie tayi tare da dago fuskanta ta na shafawa cikin kulawa "idan kintafi zaki dinga zuwa mana weekend ko, ko bazakiyi kewarmu ba? ".. 
 Ta idar da maganar tana dago haɓar deenah cikin kulawa.. 
Idanun madina suka kuma kawo ruwa ta fada jikin ammie, sosai takejin kewar ta, koba komai ammie nada saukin kai da sakin fuska da ta sha banban da fuskar ɗanta... Dazaiyi wuya sarautarsa tasa  ka iya ganin dariyarsa, 
Deenah  bata iya furta komai ba sai hawaye da ta farayi.. Dago fuskanta ammie takumayi tana sharemata hawaye tare da rarrashinta.. Har deenah ta daina kukan, bisa alkawarin da ta yiwa ammie akan bazata sakeyiwa jalila kuka ba sai addua... 
Haka suka shiga mota kowa zuciya adagule, cikin wani irin yanayi marar dadi sukayiwa juna sallama har lokacin da driver yaja motar ya fice daga gidan,
Deenah bata daina dagawa su ammie da su sultan hannu ba..
Nafisa kuwa sai taji wani irin sakat da suka tafi, allah yasauki tsiya lafiya, wannan lokacin sunyi fam fam fam da kwallon mangwaro sun huta da ƙuda.. 
 Haka ammie da maryam suka koma acikin gidan cikin wani irin yanayi.. Sai gidan yakumayimusu shiru.. 
Shikansa sultan tunda suka koma ciki yake kuka sai yabi deenah, iftahal kuma da ta fara kukan maryam ta lallabata da chocolate har tayi bacci...
Sir Abdulmajid kuwa da ya dawo daga office da yamma, ammie ke masa zancen tafiyarsu deenah, da hakan ya tattaro masa wata irin faduwar gaba yana duban ammie dan sam baisan da tafiyarsu ba... 
Anan take sanarmasa yadikko ce ta matsa, da yake tsohuwar bata cikason zama gu daya ba.. Yanayin da yake cikine ya sanya ammie batayi yunkurin kiransa ta sanarmasa... 

Shiru yayi cikin yanayin rashin jin daɗin abinda yafaru na tafiyar su yadikko da deenah da yakejinsa wani banbarankwai, haka kawai yaji gidan yadaina yimasa daɗi... 

Yanayin da ammie ta kira sunansa ya tilastamasa dawowa nutsuwarsa tare da dubanta cikin yanayin damuwar data gaza barin fuskarsa... 
"Abdulmajid, muma gobe in allah yakaimu zamu koma baki daya, saboda anyi adduar bakwai.. Zankoma da yarannan ahannuna zanci gaba da kulawa dasu , nasani zaka kuma jin babu daɗi to amma dole ne ka cigaba da yaƙi da zuciyarka da dangana akan abinda ubangiji ya kaddaro maka, duk abinda ubangiji yayi akwai abinda yakeson fitarwa aciki.. Ubangiji shi ne mai dai dai.. Iyaka namu mubi... Komai yana da dalili da MUSABBABI arayuwa.. Idan kayi hakuri ka cinye wannan jarrabawar, da sannu ubangiji zaiyi maka zaɓi maifi inganci arayuwarka, ka dauka wannan jarrabawa ce.... "
Tunda ammie ta fara magana kansa na akasa har ta idar tare da shafa kansa... 
Sai alokacin ya dago da rinannun idanunsa tare da kamo hannun ammie yasumbata... Lokaci daya zazzafan dumin hawayensa yasauka akan hannun ammie... 
Zuwa wannan lokacin ita kanta ammie hawayen take zubarwa cikin tsananin tausayinsa... 
Haka ta dinga rarrashinsa da basa  baki... 
Ahankali tacigaba da magana "kaje kayi wanka ka huta kaci abinci... "

Batare da ya ce komai ba ya tashi ya fice daga falon.. Yayinda kansa ke cigaba da sara masa...
