  yanayin kallon tuhuma da sir Aj keyimata da kokaɗan babu annuri afuskarsa, hakan bai dakatar da ita da ga shirya masa  kayansa da take a wadrobe ba.. 
Cike da bariki nafisa  ta dubesa tare da sakar masa shu umin murmishi cikin lankwasasshiyar muryarta take yimasa sannu da zuwa still bata daina abinda takeyi ba... 
Sir Abdulmajid kuwa naɗe hannayensa yayi afaffadan kirjinsa yana cigaba da kallon ta "mekikeyi adakina? "... 
Itace tambayar da yayiwa nafisa har lokacin fuskarsa babu annuri... 
Rufe wadrobe din tayi cikin wata irin kaunarsa dake fizgarsa da takeji kamar ta yiwa jikinta masauki a faffadan kirjinsa, ta karaso inda yake tana yauƙi... Saidai fa ya iya daure fuskarsa kawai amma ayanzu tafi karfin guntun wulakancinsa shiyasa ko ɗar batajiba da ya shigo wannan lokacin bazai gujewa tarkon kaunarta ba da iya kalamanta taci alwasshin lallasa zuciyarsa... 
Akasalance tasoma magana kamar mai shirin yin bacci
"A irin halin kaɗaici da kake son tunkuɗa rayuwarka, bai kamata kana janye jiki ga mai naci irina dake muradin  jingina rayuwata da taka ba, amma kayi hakuri idan hakan da nayi ya ɓata maka rai "... 
A marairaice ta idar da maganar tana kuma kusanto inda yake tsaye ta yadda har zata iya jiyo daddan kamshin turarensa, da ya fara kokarin fizgar mata zuciya... 

Dubanta yakeyi cikin nazarin sabuwar barikin da take kokarin gayyatowa batare da yadauke idanunsa akanta ba ya cigaba da cewa "har yanxu baki bani amsata ba, waye ya baki iznin shigo min daki, answer me! "
Ya idar da maganar cikin tsawa yana karawa dukkan red eyeball dinsa girma akanta, tamkar zai kaimata duka .. Sai alokacin nafisa tadan tsorata bata dauka har yanxu akwai wani abu dazai iya aiwatar wa akanta ba... "me wanki da guga ne ya kawo kaya shi ne ammie tace na kawo maka adakinka, ban  ɗauka akwai abinda zaimin shamaki ko iyaka da wani abu naka ba amatsayina na yar uwaka mai kuma dakon soyayyarka tsawon shekaru goma biyar, wace irin zuciya ce wannan da ko kaɗan bata tausayin abinda ke ƙaunarta, dai dai da second daya baka taɓa yimin wani kallo mai tattare da soyayya ba ko so na yan uwantaka, me zanyi duniya na burgeka, shin ko tsayin wadannan shekarun da na dauka bana kula kowa saboda kai ban cancanci kaso ni ba,? "... 

Furzan da iskar bakinsa yayi lokaci daya yadauke idanunsa akanta tare da cije lebensa, babu yadda zaayi ammie ta umurcesa da shigo masa daki kai tsaye domin kuwa tasan haramcin hakan kawai ta tsara zancene dan kuma samun kusanci dashi.. Ayanxu kam zai iya cewa halayenta yafi ki bawai itaba... 
Maida dubansa yayi ga nafisa dake faman matsar kwallah..ya fahimci barikinta tatttare take da rainin hankali 
"Fita! ".. 
Ya nuna mata hanya da hannu.. 
Dubansa tayi da idanunta da suka sauya kala dan ta tabbatar da abinda ya faɗa... 
Jinjina mata kai yakumayi tare da sake nuna mata hanya ya cigaba da cewa "karo na karshe da zaki sake shigomin daki, okey".. 

Bakin ciki yakuma cika zuciyar nafisa, duk hikkmarta da tsara zancenta wurin ganin ta narkar da zuciyarsa amma bata samu nasara ba ta lura zuciyarsa tamkar dutse ce kuma duk abinda ya sanya agaba shi yake yi, amma ko ahakan zata iya ganin yadda ta tasirantu a zuciyarsa, badan aikin maganintaba babu yadda zaayi ya iya jumurin sauraronta haka... 
Kuka tasaka tare da raɓasa ta fice
Da gudu tana kukanta.. 
Da idanu yabi bayanta awani bangare na zuciyarsa yakejin kukan na ratsa zuciyarsa, karamin tsaki yaɗanja tare da jin haushin kansa ya rufe kofar yakoma bakin gado ya  zauna ,  baki daya zuciyarsa dagule take yarasa meke masa daɗi, ga tafiyarsu deenah da take damunsa ji yake tamkar yayi nesa da alkawarin da ya daukarwa jalila, tamkar tafiyar deenah nada nasaba da gaxa cikar burin jalila. Sam gidan yadaina masa daɗi... 
Nafisa kuwa cike da takaici ta isa daya daga cikin guest room din dake gidan da suka sauka.. baki daya sir ya ɓata mata shirinta wane irin mutum ne shi, nan da nan daukar zafi, duk da tasan abinda tayi ya sabawa sharia amma bai kamata ya hantare ta haka ba... Tsaki takuma ja akaro na ba adadi "wlh saika so ni kuma saikayiwa soyayyata biyayya fiyeda da ta mataciyyar matarka, idan takamarka taurin kai, nikuma ina ji da naci, da sannu zan malleka ahannuna yallabai ".. 
Tayi maganar afili cikin son karawa kanta karfin guiwa... sauke ajiyar zuciya tayi tare da zama  adaya daga cikin hannun kujerar dake falon, wani irin feeling dinsa na fizgarsa aduk lokacin da ta saka kwayar idanunta acikin nasa bakaramin yaƙi take da zuciyarta da gangar jikintaba wurin ganin tadaidaita nutsuwarta Akansa... Soon ruwan kashe kishinta na nan zuwa gareta... Abinda tafaɗa azuciyarta kenan... 
 tafiyarsu ammie daga gidan bakaramar damuwa ta sake haifarwa da sir Aj ba sai ya zamana dagashi sai ss dinsa kawai ke rayuwa agidan ,yayi kokarin boye damuwarsa  dan bayason ya kuma haifarwa da ammie wata damuwar, musamman a irin wannan yanayin da take nuna damuwarta sosai akan yanayin da yake ciki,  yanxu ne yake kuma jin mutuwar matarsa na cakar masa zuciya, alokacin da yaransa suka nesanta da shi, ashe akwai ranar da zata zo masa da zaiga gidansa tamkar filin yaƙi, gidanda da jalila ta cikasa da dumbin farinciki da nutsuwa da zaman lafiya.. Ashe akwai ranar da wannan farinciki zai tarwatse, akowane lokaci idan ya doshi gidansa koda abacin rai yake, zai kasance acikin nutsuwa da farinciki da aka tanada domin sa, yau anwayi gari babu mai basa wannan farincikin babu yaransa atare dashi, rayuwar deenah ma agidan ta zama tarihi, ubangiji kenan mai juya al'amurra alokacin da yaso,
Tun kuma bayan rasuwar jalila 'yan mata suka sakosa gaba, babu ranar banza da zaiyi checking inbox dinsa bai cikaro da sakonni barkatai wanda duk akan soyayya ne, manyan masu kuɗi  kuwa agari  ko d'aga kafa basayi wurin yimasa tayi ya'yansu, wanda duk suna kwadayin hada aure dashi ne dan kuɗinsa da kuma matsayin da yataka akasar nan, sam basa gabansa baya kula ta tasu, cikin hikima yake kokarin ganin yarabu lafiya da kowa,  har yanzu zuciyarsa na gun jalila, a gun jalila ne ya san tsagwaron soyayya sonso domin allah badan abinda ya mallaka ba  

Ranar jumaa tun 5pm yadawo daga office, wanka yayi ya sauya kaya, ya shirya kansa Cikin wani lallausan farin yadi da yayi matukar zama ajikinsa, yayi kyau sosai, duk da yar ramar da yayi hakan bai boye kyawun zatin da ubangiji yayi masaba... 

A parking area ya wuce hakan yasa sajent saeed karasowa da hanzari dan ya tabbatar fita zasuyi tare da mai gida... 
Yana kokarin bude motar cikin girmamawa.. Dakatar da shi sir yayi "no need yau da kaina nakeso nayi driving "... 
Yayi maganar tare da mikawa sajent saeed hannu daya basa key din motar.. 
Cikin girmamawa ya mikamasa key din tare da bude masa motar yashiga ya zauna, saida ya ɗan ƙame ya sara masa, har saida sir yaja motar yabar gun kafin shima yabar wurin... 
Kai tsaye gidansu deenah ya nufa domin duba jikinta bisa ganin kyautuwar hakan da yayi... 
Tsayawar motarsa akofar gidan yayi daidai da fitowar babah daga cikin gidan... 
Balle murfin motar  yallabai yayi ya fito kai tsaye inda babah yake ya tunkara.... Cikin tsananin girmama dattijon ya gaishesa... 
Amsawa babah yayi cike da farincikin ganin yallabai da ya gaza boyuwa afuskarsa.. Iso yayi masa har cikin gida, akan katuwar tabarmar da goggo asabe ta shimfidamusu suka zauna bakidayansu.. Gaishe da goggo asabe  yallabai yayi.. Ta amsa masa fuskarta asake tare da tambayarsa su ammie dakuma jikinnasa... 
Ya amsa da lafiyaklau.. Yadora da cewa "ya madina takumaji da jiki ina fatan ta warware ".. 
"Ta samu sauki sosai ko in ce ta warke, yanxu haka ta tafi islamiyya bayan angama artabu da ita yau bazata je ba wai hutawa zatayi, kajimin wauta ta madina waye ya faɗamata ana hutu da karatu.. "
Cewar goggo asabe.. 
Tunda goggo asabe ta fara magana yake murmishi "mai yuwuwa sai gaba kadan deenah zatayi hankali, da sanin ciwon kanta, girma takeyi amma halinta kullum sabo yake dawowa "... 
Abinda yake faɗa kenan aransa, afili kuwa murmishi yayi da bai bayyana hakoransa ba tare da furta "allah  yakara lafiya "/. 
Goggo ta amsa da ameen 
Babah kuwa Ahankali  yake nazarinsa, akallah sati uku cikin na huɗu da rasuwar jalila amma har yanxu babu abinda yasauya daga yanayin Abdulmajid... 
Gyara zama babah yayi, cikin hikima yake kuma yiwa yallabai nasiha "tafiya nata tafiya Abdulmajid gashi jalila ta kwashi kwanaki ashirin da shidda cif a kasa, izuwa yanzu yakamata kasaki rayuwarka, kafara tunanin hango wacce zata maye gurbin uwar yaranka, kodan saboda yaran ka, Abdulmajid shekaru sundan jamaka kai ba yaro bane, kada soyayyar jalila tasaka kayiwa rayuwar yaranka illah, kabar ganin ka rasa jalila, kadauka ubangiji bazai baka wacce ta fita ba, kada ka karaya domin kuwa darajar ɗanamiji bazata taɓa cika ba matukar babu mace arayuwarsa, ina fatan ka fahimceni? ".. 
Tunda baba ya fara magana kan yallabai na ƙasa sai alokacin ya dago da idanunsa tare da bugawar zuciya ya dubi babah... 
Sauke ajiyar zuciya yayi ahankali ya furta "insha allah babah zanyi abinda kace... "
Deenah kuwa akofar gida tacikaro da rantsattsiyar mota, ko tantanma batayi ba azuciyarta ta tabbatar ta sir Abdulmajid ce duk da motar sabuwa ce dal bata taɓa ganinta ba.. Hararar motar tayi tamkar mai motar da takejima haushi take kallo, da dogon tsaki tashiga cikin gidan... 
Saida ta shigo tsakiyar gidan kafin tayi sallama... 

Daure fuskarta takumayi ganin tabbatacin sir Abdulmajid agidannasu, bisa dole itama saita rama miskilanci da shan kamshin da yakeyi na ji da kansa shidin wani ne.. Dauke kanta tayi tare da cire hijabinta ta shige daki.. 
Baki daya da idanu suka bita cike da takaici shikuwa babah dan kunya gaza cewa komai yayi duk da dai Abdulmajid ba bako bane, amma yanayin da tayimasa abun yabaiwa babah kunya da takaici... 

Sir Abdulmajid kuwa tunda ya dubeta ya dauke kansa sakamakon yadda zuciyarsa ke bugamasa da karfin gaske.. Musamman yadda yake tariyo kalaman jalila akan deenah... 
Fitowa deenah tayi ko ajikinta ita bisa dole saita basar dashi, ta dauki buta tashige bayi bayan ta fto ta wanke hannayenta ta kasan ido take duban yanayin yallabai, _wace tsiyar takawosa gidanmu_ shine tambayar da ta yiwa kanta.. Rashin samun amsar tambayar ne yasanya ta tabe karamin bakinta tare da ajiye butar tana kokarin komawa daki.. 

"Ke madina zo nan! "
Babah ya dakatar da ita cikin tsawa... 
Karasowa tayi ta zauna agefen goggo asabe, idanunta akasa kai kace wata mumina ce.. 

"Hala idanunki basu gwadamiki kowaye wmazaune ba "... 
Cewar babah.. 
Turo baki deenah tayi tadan saci kallon yallabai da shidin ita ya ke kallo, zuwa wannan lokacin halin deenah yadaina basa haushi, abun dariyama  yake so yabasa, ta wata sigar kuma gani yakeyi tsabar kauyancine da har yanxu bai saki yarinyar ba shi ke damunta... 
"Nagansa".. 
Can kasan murya ta baiwa babah amsa tare da saurin janye idanunta akan yallabai... 
"Yafi karfin ki gaishesa kenan? ".. 
Cewar babah akufule... 
Sake turo baki tayi tare da hade fuskanta takuma kallonsa, yadda babah ya harzuka tasani matukar batayi abinda yace ba zai iya kaimata duka... 
"Ina wuni! "
Yadda tayi gaisuwarkadai zai nuna maka tilastata akayi.. 
Murmishi gefen baki yallabai yayi, ya amsa da "lafiya, ya karfin jikinki".. 
Yayi maganar yana kuma dubanta... 
"Ni lafiyaklau nake babu abinda ke damuna "... 
Adan tsiwace ta basa amsa tare da saurin tashi fuuuu ta shige daki..
Babah kuwa Cike da takaicin yadda deenah ta kunyata sa yabi bayanta da idanu "sha sha sha kawai, da kin tsaya dana daka ki".. 
Itama goggo tsaki taja cikin jin kunya tayi magana "madina idan tana wasu abubuwan tamkar mai shafin aljannu".. 
"Babu wasu aljannu sai zallar iya shege,da gidadanci da sannu kuma zan gyara zamanta agidannan "... 
Cewar babah ya jiyo da maganar yana fuskantar yallabai tare da basa hakuri akan abinda deenah tayi masa.... 
"Babu komai babah ai zata daina nawani lokaci ne "... 
Cewar yallaɓai... 
Sun dan taɓa fira wacce bakidayanta nasiha ce babah keyimasa.. 
Sosai yasamu nutsuwa da kalaman babah, dazai tafi kuwa kudi masu yawan gaske ya ajiye gefen  baba tare da yimusu sallama ya fito...haka babah yadinga shimasa albarka har ya fice..  
Madina kuwa tana daki tana jiyo firar da sukeyi har ficewar yallaɓai, goge hawayen ta tayi da ya zubomata musamman dataji firar data danganci aunty jalila, ta kasa sabawa da rashin aunty jalila tana jin kewar ta sosai aranta.. 
Goggo asabe ce ta shigo dakin rai aɓace tafarayiwa deenah fada akan abinda tayiwa sir Abdulmajid , "sam baki kyauta ba wallahi kin tabbatarmin har yanxu ke bakauya ce, koma miye abban sultan yayi miki idan baki girmamasa ba amatsayin wanda ya isa haihuwarki ai kya girmamasa amatsayinsa na mijin yayarki,   
"Idan na gaishesa fa sai yaga dama yake amsamin wani lokacin harda harara ta yakeyi "
"Mekikayimasa" 
Goggo ta jefowa deena tambayar.. 
"Hakanan fa kawai ya tsaneni".. 
Cewar deenah tana gunguni... 
Nazarinta goggo ta gamayi tsaf ta fahimci deenah kawai tana liƙawa yallabai abinda bai yiba dan tanajin haushinsa duk da kuwa ta fahimci bai cikason doguwar magana ba ,"uhmmm kyaji da kayan sherri, natabbatar idan ma yanayi maki haka bai wuce saboda shegen halinkiba, amma kisani bawan allahnnan yafi karfin wulakanci domin shi sutura ne ga rayuwarmu "... 
Cewar goggo asabe tana dallawa madina harara cikin takaicin yadda ta kunyatar da su agabansa... 
Cikin dacin rai deenah tabi bayanta da kallo, tamkar ta fasa kuka dan takaici yadda Goggo ke daɗa alakanta rayuwarsu da ta sir Abdulmajid tamkar suna rayuwane da alfarmarsa kenan.. _dani dake duka muna rayuwane akarkashin inuwar arzikinsa_
Wannan shine kalmar da aunty jalila ta taɓa  fada mata alokacin da take raye.. Cikin dan karamin nazarinta tasake juya zancen goggo da yadyi shige dana aunty jalila, tsaki tadanja  Koda alfarmarsa ko babu, babu abinda zai canja kasancewarta rayayya! Itafa bazata juri wulakanci ba... 
Zancen da takeyi kenan azuciyarta dake kuma darsar mata da zafin zuciya... 
Sir Abdulmajid kuwa a hankali yake  driving yana murmishi shi kadai, kawai tunanin deenah yakeyi da dabiunta da abubuwan da suka faru ayau , ba iya kauyanci kadai bane ke damunta, yarinyar ji takeyi da kanta... 
Murmishi yayi da ya bayyana fararen  hakoransa ya shafi sajensa ya cigaba da murza sitiyarin motar cikin nutsuwa yake driving dinsa har ya isa gida... 
Kwanaki sun dan ja wasa wasa har jalila ta kwashi kwanaki arba'in da biyu a makwancinta,bayan anyi adduar arba'in ne da kwanaki uku sir Abdulmajid ya zaɓi fito da zahirin abinda ke ransa, ji yakeyi har yanzu akwai nauyi na alkawari da ya dauka akansa.. Wanda cikasa shine samun nutsuwa atattare da shi, sam baya hango kalubelen dake tattare da aiwatar da hakan, cikar muradin jalila ya fiyemasa komai... 
bayan yayi sallar magrib da issha amasallaci kai tsaye gidan ammie driver ya kaisa.. 
Afalo ya tarar da ammie itada kids dinsa da shigowarsa yasanya sukayo kansa da gudu... 
Rungumesu yayi bakidaya tare da sumbatarsu, ya riko hannayensu baki daya suka ida karasowa  tsakiyar falon... 
Murmishi  ammie keyi cike da jin dadin ganin dannata har ya samu gu ya zauna agefenta akan lallausan carpet din daya mamaye kayataccen falon... Bayan ya gaisheta ne ta amsa cikin kulawa, takuma dubansa anutse, ta fahimci yadda nutsuwarsa ke dad'a dawowa agangar jikinsa, yanxu babbar damuwarta shine ganin ya sanya maganar aure agabansa, domin sam bata ganin dacewar zamansa ahaka, abun babu wani tsari... 
murmishi ammie takumayi tare da tambayarsa abinda zaici,.. 
"Bana bukatar komai ammie akoshe nake "... 
Jinjina kai tayi, zuwa yanxu ta fahimci akwai abinda ke bakin Abdulmajid.. To amma meyasa bazai fadamata abinda yakeso yasanar mata ba kai tsaye... 
Shikansa wannan lokacin ya tsinci kansa da jin faɗuwar gaba, yassn tilas abinda zai sanarwa ammie  tajisa a banbarankwai.. Mai yuwa ma tagaza fahimtarsa ta da kilar da zancen, da bayajin zai iya musu da abinda ta gindaya agaresa.... 
Ahankali takira sunansa "Abdulmajid me kake so ka faɗamin, yanayin zamannan da kayi, nafahimci kamar akwai magana abakinka... ". 
Sosa keyarsa yadanyi tare da gyara zama ya dubi su sultan da suka mayar da hankalinsu gun kallo.. 
"Sultan ku shiga ciki kuyi kallon acan ".. 
Cewar yallabai cikin umurtar yarannasa... 
Ba musu suka tashi suka  nufi dakinsu...

. 
"Ahnkali yasoma magana "nasan zuwa wannan lokaci nutsuwarku bai wuce ganin nayi aure ba tun bayan rasuwar jalila "... 
Jinjina kai ammie keyi cikin nutsuwa da jin dadin yadda yadauko mata maganar aure yau dakansa, abinda take kwana kuma take tashi dashi kenan... 
Ahankali yacigaba da magantuwa ".. Wannan dalilinne yasa  nake  so na nemi auran madina da domin ta maye gurbin jalila "... 
Cike da faɗuwar gaba ya idar da zancen dan  baisan ya ammie zata karbi zancen ba... 
Ammie kuwa saurin sauke farin  gilashin dake manne afuskarta tayi, tadan fiddo idanunta cikin mamakin abinda ke fitowa daga bakin dannata... 
Bakidaya tarasa me zata cemasa sai juya zancen take azuciyarta... 
Yadda yaga yanayin ammie yasauya musamman ganin mamakin zancennasa kwance afuskarta daya hanata magantuwa.. Cikin karfin hali yasoma magana "ammie nasan kin fahimci yadda ubangiji ya jarrabeni da soyayyar jalila, kuma nassn zakiyi mamaki akan maganar madina da nazomiki da ita... Ammie banason alakar dake tsakanin mu ta yanke dan mutuwar jalila, shiyasa na yanke wannan shawarar, amma duk abinda kika fada ashirye nake da bin umurninki.. ". 
Yayi maganar cikin son gamsar da ammie abinda ya faɗa mata, yakuma ɓoye mata jalila ce ta buƙaci hakan, bawai shiɗin ba shikuma sunayiwa soyayyar junansu biyayya ne.... 
Sai alokacin ammie tasauke ajiyar zuciya tasoma magana "wannan halin da kake ciki, kana bukatar wacce zata raini farincikinka da na yayanka, bawai wacce zaka auro kuma ka yi rainonta ba,ba auren madina kadai zai jaddada alakarmu da suba tunda ga yara sun shiga tsakaninka da marigayiya,  madina duka nawa take, zan iya  cewa zaka kusan haihuwar yarinyar nan, har yanxu batasan kanta ba amatsayinta na ya mace ba saboda yarinya ce karama sosai,bare tasan yadda zata kula da kai  bawai ina kyamatar abun bane saboda ubangiji bai haramta hakanba ni ban kai na haramta ba , sai dan dalilan dana zayyano maka, amma nasani kasan me kakeyi, bazaka taɓa aiwatar da abuba saida dalili na kuma amince maka akan hakan, amma nima amtsayina na mahaifiyarka inada haƙƙi akanka musamman wanda yadanganci cigaban rayuwarka... saidai a.  inada nawa sharadin da zan gabatar maka ayanxu....

Ƙwararrun likitoci ne suka duƙufa  ganin sun ceto rayuwar jalila da har lokacin bata san waye akantaba... 
Sir Aj kuwa kai da kawowa kawai  yake areception hannayensa goye abayansa, zuciyarsa na tsananta bugawa   , sam baya cikin nutsuwarsa. 
hatta drivernsa   sajent sa'id yagaza samun sukuni, ganin mai gidansa a irin wannan hargitsattsen yanayin bakidaya ya daga masa hankali, ..
 Dr rufa'i ne yafito daga dakin da aka shiga da jalila ahanzarce,   hakan ya sanya sir  yowa kansa cikin zafin nama da ruɗewa ya rike dukkan hannayen dr "wane hali matata take ciki? "

duban sajent sa'id yayi  da rinannun  idanunsa yayi kafin lokaci daya ya saki hannayen dr rufa'i  da al amarin sir  yasoma basa tsoro, furzan da wata  irin zazzafar iska yayi tare da zama adaya daga cikin kujerun dake wurin yana rike kansa dake barazanar tarwatse masa  tare da runtse idanunsa...

Babu ɓata lokaci drivern ammie yakawota itama hankali tashe...
A parking risk sajent  sa'id yaje ya tarbo ammie, cikin tsananin girmamata yake fada mata dukkan abinda yafaru akan idanunsa game da jalila, har suka karaso inda sir yake... 

Ganin ammie yasanya Aj tashi da sauri yakaraso inda ammien take, da ita ma kokarin zuwa inda yake take... 

Gaza furta komai yayi sai soft lips dinsa dake motsawa.. 
Ganin haka yasanya ammie zaunar dashi, dan ta fahimci babu sauran nutsuwa atare dashi ..

Kwance take tana ta fama da baccinta na   asara, mai tattare da ɓacin rai  wayartace tafara vibrating akarkarshin pillown da ta tada kai .

Haka wayar ta karaci ruri batare da tadaga... 
Dakiran yasake shigowa, cikin kosawa ta kuma jan wani tsakin tare da hanging up calling din batare da ta duba sunaba... 

dukunkunewa takumayi ablanket  tare da yin cilli da wayar . Saidai yadda kiran yasakeshigowa yasanyata tashi a harzuke ta  duba sunan wanda ya addaba mata da kiran.. 
Sunan mommy ne tagani shimfiɗe a screen din wayarta...

A dan hassale momy tayi magana 
"Bacci nakeyi ".. 
Nafisa ta bata amsa atakaice..

Dasauri nafisa ta tashi zaune cikin yanayin da takejin kamar mafarkin da ta saba yi ne.. lokaci daya tace "amma mommy idan nazo nagane cewar gadar zare kukamin abinda duk baku tsammani zan aikata wa kaina ".. 

Murmishi mommy tayi maisauti yadda nafisa zata iya jiyota.. "Na amince da hakan, dan hakan kiyi sammako agobe, dan tun jiya hajja ta damka min sakon ahnnuna... "

Cikin zallar farinciki da gamsuwa da zancen momy nafisa ta magantu "no mommy bawai gobeba yanxu zan shirya inzo "..

Ko makeup bata tsaya yiba tadauki key din motar ta da dan  gyalenta, bata dauki kayan sakawaba sai wallet dinta dan komai zaifaru bazata kwana jumeta ba yau zata dawo katsina... 
Takalmanta masu tsini tasaka ta feshe jikinta da turare.. Saida ta fito falo ta yafa gyalenta kai tsaye sashin ammie ta nufa, da kalaman da tagama tsarawa ta fadawa ammie duk da tasan baizama lallai ta amince da ita ba...

Cikin girmamawa suka gaishe da nafisa.. 
Batare da ta amsa su ba tasoma tambayarsu inda ammie take... 

"Ta jima da fita, saidai yanayin da tabar gidannan hankalina bai kwanta da shi ba, amma bansan ina ta tafi ba".. 
Cewar huraira.. 
Taɓe baki nafisa tayi bayan tagama nazarin kalaman huraira, koma miye babu abinda zai hanata  zuwa jumeta yau.. Kawai zata kira ta tayimata bayani.... 

Agaggauce ta isa parking area ta kunna motarta da  tunda tazo gidan ba ta hau taba... 

Tun shigarta mota mai gadi ya bude mata gate, har lokacin da ta fice yana jero mata addu oin allah ya tsare. 

Sai uku da rabi na  yamma ta isa jumeta cikin farinciki daya Gaza boyuwa afuskarta ta tura hancin motarta a estate d'in tasu, babu abinda ya sauya Mata komao yana nan yadda ta sansa..... 

Kai tsaye babban falon hajja nafisa ta wuce bayan ta ajiye motarta.. Dan acan mommy tace su haɗu... 
Abunka da zaman yawa tuni gida ya dauka cewa nafisa data gudu ta dawo... 

Hajja da momy sunyi matukar farinciki da ganin nafisa musamman hajja da kewar yar lelen jikar tata ya cika ta.. Abinci da na sha kala² ta sanya aka kawowa nafisa.. 

"Ni fa hajja duk ba wannan ba kawai abani saƙona "... 

Cewar nafisa da ta kosa sosai... 

"Ja ira ai kyaci abincin tukunna wato mu sam bakya kewarmu, mukadai kika bari da kewa"... 

Cewar hajja dake kallon nafisa cikin kulawa... 
"To miye na gaggawa tunda kinxo, saiki bari Ki ci abinci ki huta ".. 
Cewar momy, yadda suka takura matane yasanya ta cin abincin, saidai sam badan tana jin dadinsaba sai dan yunwa da ta gama kulle cikinta dan ko breakfast bata tsaya yiba... 
Sosai kuwa taci abincin hajja nata yimata tsiya... 
Ita kanta nafisa tayi kewar hajja sosai.  
Mommy ce ta miƙa mata kwallin tare da faɗar "ai nasan bazaki bari na huta ba matukar ban baki abinda ya kawoki ba"... 

Mikawa nafisa kwallin mommy tayi ta dora da yimata bayani kamar dai yadda hajja tayi mata, ta cigaba da cewa "Ina mai tabbatar  miki, wasan yazo karshe yayinda kece da nasara.. Muna azaune abdulmajeed zaizo Neman aurenki, tare da ƙaskantar da kansa wa iyayenki, zai kuma yi nadamar wulakantaki da yayi, hatta hajiya inna, da take tutiyar ita tahaifi ubanta da baffanninki da basa son ayi auren, saida suji anayi... "

Kalaman mommy sunyi matukar faranta ran nafisa, yanxu kam tana da yaƙinin mallakar ya Aj ahannunta.. Cikin farinciki ta rungume mommy "am speechless my mom, damn haka kuka damu da farincikina? '.. 

Hajja ta cabe zancen da cewa "yanxu kin yarda fa zancen da nake faɗamiki cewar "ta yaro kyau takeyi bata ƙarko, shekaru sha biyar kenan kullum cikin laluben hanyar dazan farantamiki nakeyi , amma kikayi facali da mu, wannan lokacin kada ki sake kiyi wasa da damarki... "
Cikin farinciki take duban hajja tana jinjina kai, alamar gamsuwa da zancenta.... 


Karfe 5:20pm nafisa ta tashi zata ta koma kt... 

Cikin mamaki mommy ke kallonta "ina kuma zakije ne? "
Ta tillomata tambayar.. 

"Zan koma katsina ne dan banzo da shirin kwanaba '.. 

Zaro idanu hajja tayi, cikin mamakin abinda ke fitowa daga bakin nafisar "wace irin komawa katsina kuma ayanxu, komawa katsina yanxu basauki bane kamar ficewa daga estate dinnan ba "... 

Dagewa nafisa tayi akan ita sam bazata kwana ba zuwa karfe tara ta tabbata tana katsina... 

Cikin damuwa mai tattare da rarrashi momy tasoma magana dan tasan duk abinda nafisa tace zatayi babu wanda ya isa ya hanata saita aiwatar "ko ƙannenki fa basu gankiba, baffanki yana abuja gobe zai dawo, kodan saboda shi ai kya bari sai jibi ki koma "... 

Fafur nafisa taƙi... Sun san kafiyarta dole suka kyaleta tare da yimata fatan alkhairi.. Har bakin motarta suka rakata... 

Amota nafisa takira nabila ta fada mata komai... Sosai nabila ta taya kawartata murna.. Saidai yadda ta shigo jumeta ko nemanta batayi bane shine ta nunawa nagisa bacin ranta... 
Cikin raha take rarrashin kawartata har sukayi sallama... 

Sai 9:37pm ta iso katsina... 
Horn daya tayi mai gadi ya bude mata gate din.. 
Sauke gilashin motarta tayi tasoma tambayar sa ammie  ta dawo?.. 

Yabata amsa da cewar "tun safe har yanxu bata dawo ba ita da driver... "

Ajiyar zuciya ta sauke dan tasan bazata samu tambayoyin tuhuma da ga ammie ba... Tunda batassn fitar taba bare kuma dawowarta.. Koma yunkurin kiranta bazatayiba kamar yadda ta tsara abaya... 

****
Ammie ce mai ƙwarin guiwar tambayar likitocin halin da jalila take ciki... Sir kuwa dubansu kawai yakeyi cikin yanayin faɗuwar gaba... 

Gaza cewa komai junaidiyya tayi sai,sai wani kwararren balaraben  likita ne ya magantu cikin harshen turanci "muje office acan zanmuku bayani.... "

Yayi maganar tare da wucewa, yayinda sauran likitocin suka koma kan aikinsu... 

Harda junaidiyya tabi bayan su ammien... 


koda suka je office din saida likitan yayi rubuce²nsa.. Bayan ya kammala yamikawa yallabai file din, tare da cire glashin dake manne afuskarsa... 

Sir Aj kuwa cikin wani irin yanayi ya karɓi file din, ahankali ya buda yasoma karantawa... 
Tsantsar tashin hankali ammie ke karantowa afuskar sir Aj lokacin da yake duba file din, karawa eyeballs  dinsa girma yayi da suka ƙara sauya Kala zuwa launin ja , kafin lokaci daya  ya mayar da Duban  sa ga likitan , cikin wani irin hargitsattsen yanayi. 

Jinjina kai likitan ya shigayi  cikin hausarsa da bata fita yasoma magana"im sorry to say, abinda kagani arubuce shi ne zan sake yimuku bayani , akwai bukatar yiwa madam tiyata ta gagggawa,kafin nan muna kokarin haɗa takardun transfer din ta daga asibitinnan zuwa asibitin dake kasar india  domin kuwa cutar taci karfinta har ta fara kokarin shiga jininta da taɓa zuciyarta.... ".. 
Shiru likitan yayi cikin alhinin daya kama duk wanda ke office din, shikansa saida ya sauke ajiyar zuciya kafin yacigaba da magana cikin harshen larabci "rayuwa da mutuwa duk ahannun allah suke, amma a hasashenmu zaiyi wuya madam ta ƙara koda rabin shekara araye, acikin watanni shidda  nan na rayuwarta da ta rage  komai zai iya faruwa.... ".. 
Ya karashe jawabinsa yana duban ammie... 
Zuwa wannan lokacin ita kanta ammie zuciyarta ta tsinke, hawayene suka fara zubomata... Hakama dr junaidiyyya sharar kwallah ta soma yi... 

Sir Aj kuwa, idanunsa ne sukeyimasa wani irin zafi, zuciyarsa da brain dinsa duk adagule suke, jin komai yakeyi kamar a mummunan mafarki,  cigaba yayi da ambaton sunan ubangiji, cikin nutsuwar da ubangiji ya saukar masa alokacin...
Bakidayansu a sanyaye suka baro office din, sir Aj kuwa akofar award ɗin da jalila  take aciki ya tsaya, tare da harde hannayensa a faffadan kirjinsa ta window  yake iya hango jalila, duk da akwai yalwatattun labulaye awindow din.. Hakan bai hanasa hango yanayin da take ciki ba... Kwance take akan gado tamkar gawa, yayinda iskar oxcegyn dasauran na u 'ro-ri  ke  taimakawa wurin shige da ficen numfashinta, da nuna akwai sauran rayuwa agangar jikinta .. Har lokacin bata farko daga doguwar sumar da ta tafiba... 
Wata irin ajiyar zuciya ta kwacewa sir  ,wani irin jiri ya shiga ɗibarsa lokaci daya yarike kansa da ke barazanar tarwatse masa .... 

dasauri ammie ta rikesa tare da  taimakamasa ya zauna, Ada ya daga cikin kujerun dake gefensa.. 

Cikin karfin hali take basa baki, tasani awannan lokacin kwarai taga tasirin jarumtarsa, haka ubangiji yakeyi wa bayinsa sai ya sauko musu da nutsuwa alokacin da ya jarrabe su,, zuwa wannan lokaci addua kawai jalila ke bukata daga bakunansu... 

Cikin hikima ammie ke kara kwantarwa da sir hankali.

Zuwa wannan lokacin tuni ammie tasanya driver yaje yasanarwa babah (mahaifin jalila). Halin da ake ciki dan abun ya wuce asanar tawaya... 

Drivern ammie kuwa bai samesa agida ba goggo asabe ce ta haɗa sa da yaro yakaisa har kasuwar da baba yakeyi... Itakanta ta shiga damuwa da tashin hankali.. Duk da ba ita tahaifi jalila ba, amma tanacin moriyarta da ko mahaifiyarta bata ci ba, jalila Tana matukar girmamata amatsayinta na matar mahaifinta.. Haka gogo asabe ta kasance cikin sanyin jiki... 

Babah kuwa hankali tashe suka isa asibitin, ammie ce tasanar dashi dukkan abinda yafaru da jawabin da likita yayi musu... 
Yanxu haka zaa fara shirye²n fitar da jalilar zuwa ƙasar india... 
Duk da karfin zuciya irinta dattijon saida ya zubda hawaye.. .. 
fatansa shi ne baiwuce allah ya tashi kafaɗunta ba anan nigeria dan shi sam hankalinsa bai kwanta da fitar da ita waje ba... 

Goggo asabe ma wuraren azahar tazo asibitin dan ta gaza samun nutsuwar zama agida, batare da tasan halin da jalila take cikiba... 
Har lokacin da ta zo, babu alamun likitoci zasu bari aga jalila dan har alokacin bata farfaɗo ba... 
Sai yamma babah yace ta koma gida... 
Driver ammie tasa ya maida ta gida tare da umurtar driver ya dauko su deenah daganan ya wuce dasu agidanta.. 
Goggo asabe ce tace " abatasu ta tafi dasu acan ".. 
Haka kuwa akayi, saida driver ya biya ya dauko su deenah acan da basusan abinda ke faruwa ba kafin su wuce can gidan tare dasu.... 

Iftahal kuwa kuka tasaka alokacin tana tambayar umminta... Dake sultan bawani kulafuci ne dashi ba sam bai wani damu ba... 
Har ita kanta deenah haka tashawa goggo asabe kai da tambayar "  Ina  aunty ta kuma tafiya?...'.. 

"Taje ta gaishe da yadikko ".. 
Cewar goggo asabe da deenah ta matsa mata da tambaya.. 

Taɓe baki deenah tayi itakam baxata iya zuwa kauyen su yadikko ayanzu ba, dan sauro da sanyi zai iya illata ta... "

Dubanta goggo asabe kawai tayi tare da murmishi ta girgiza kai "madine akwai iyayi.. Tamkar ba a kauyen ta fito ba... Lallai wannan ita ce dan wake kafi ubanka... "

Azahiri kuwa goggo asabe cigaba tayi da ayyukan gidanta.. 
Bayan sallar magrib ta kammala girkinta.. 
Babbar tabarma ta shimfida a tsakar gidan tamkar yadda ta saba... 
Atare da yaran suka yi sallar magrib bayan sun kammala ne ta zubo musu.. Tuwon shinkafa miyar busasshiyar kubewar da tayi duk da babu man shanu amma miyar ta wadatu da kayan dandano.. 
Fafur deenah taki ci... Wai ita bazata ci tuwo ba.. Kuma miya ko nama babu bare kifi... 

Sororo goggo asabe tayi tana dubanta cikin mamaki, duk yadda ta dauki madina ta wuce nan.. Wato tuwon ma tafi karfin ta cisa.. 
Da takaici ya ishi goggo asabe kyaleta tayi tace idan taji uwar bari taci... 

Goggo asabe da kanta ta dinga baiwa sultan da iftahal abaki har suka koshi.. Deenah kuwa sam taƙicin tuwon.. 

Har akayi sallat issha babah bai dawo ba hakan yakara dagawa goggo asabe hankali.  Tana fatan dai allah yassnya shirun na lafiya ne,gashi takira layin babah baya tafiya.. 

Gyarawa su deenah dakin dazasu kwanta tayi takunna musu maganin sauro ta sakarmusu net a akatifar da ke dakin.  
Ta dawo nata dakin tana zaman jiran tsammani... 
Zuciyarta taf da tausayin yaran jalila akan cutar da ubangiji ya qaddarowa mahaifiyarsu... 

Madina kuwa d'ame cikin ta tayi tayi kwanciyarta dan tunda tace bazata ciba to bazata cin ba., kafiya ce da ita shiyasa goggo takawo idanu ta saka mata....
Haka tayita juyinta tana jan tsaki har bacci yayi awon gaba da ita.

Tafiya sukeyi sosai mai kuma tsayin gaske.. Ita kanta madina ta rasa gane inda suke dosa, bata kuma san ina zasu tafiba... Kawai tana saka kafa ne aduk inda taga aunty jalila tasaka... 
. sunyi tafiya mai nisan gaske.. Wacce tagajiyar da deenah sosai gashi har sunfara kokarin barin cikin gari, babu alamun gajiya atattare da jalila bare ta dakata, cikin nutsuwa da annashuwa take tafiya... 

Durkushewa deenah tayi agun datake cikin matsananciyar gajiya tamkar zatayi kuka take duban auntyntata "aunty ki fadamin ina zamu je ne? Nagaji kishirwa nake ji.. Kafafuna zasu ɓalle? '... 


Murmishi jalila tayi daya kara tattaro sigar kyawunta tasanya hannu ta dago deenah da keta uban haki.. 
Tallabe fuskarta tayi cikin kulawa tasoma magana "kada ki sare madina, ki zamo mai juriya, hakuri da karfin hali arayuwarki, saiki kasance mai  nasara arayuwarki ".. 

Shi ne abinda jalila ta fada ta riƙe hannun madina suka cigaba da tafiya.. 
"Bazan iya cigaba da tafiya ba, kafafuna fita zasuyi, nidai ki goyani.. Kuma ki bani abinci da  ruwa insha ".. 
Dakatawa jalila ta karayi tana duban madeenah "kada ki nemi taimakon kowa sai na ubangiji, domin shi ne alamurran ki ke hannunsa, ".... 
Cikin kosawa da kalaman jalila da rashin fahimtarta madina tafara kokarin saka kuka.... 
"Kishi zai kashe nifa, mukoma gida ko kuma kisamomin ruwa "... 
Ta idar da maganar tana zubar da hawaye tare da magiya wa jalila... 

Cikin rarrashi jalila ta sake rikota,"kiyi hakuri kanwata, saura kaɗan yarage na sada ki da haskenki, shi ne kuma gishirin rayuwarki "..  Ta idar da maganar  tare da nuna mata wani guu nesa da su kadan... 
Wuri ne mai kyan gaske da haske mai daukar idanu... Daga inda suke zasu iya hango korama dake gefen guun.....

Ya dikko ma tun saura kwanaki goma bikin ta iso, taji daɗi matuka yadda taga goggo tabada gudunmuwarta wurin gyare yar lelen jikalen tata tsaf,  dan ko itace ta haifi deenah iyakar kokarin d zatayimata kenan ta fito sak amarya, sai kyalli take da daukar ido ga sheki da fatar jikinta da takeyi,.. 
Dr junaidiyya ma tayi rawar gani acikin hidimar, dan kuwa tarin turarukan wuta dana jiki da sanyayyar humra, kaya kanshi masu yawa ta kawowa goggo abaiwa amarya... 
Haka goggo suka karɓa itada yadikko sai yiwa dr godiya sukeyi, dazata tafi ma saida tayimusu alkhairi sosai,
Madina kuwa anata bangaren babu abinda ya dameta  kusan kullum sai ta baiwa sultan sako amakaranta ya faɗawa abbiensa ya kawo mata chocolates din dayayimata alkawari zai dinga kawo mata kullum,hakan yasa kusan kullum IGP cikin cika alkawarinsa yake dan duk bayan kwanaki biyu sai ya sanya driver yayi mata shopping yakaimata, to ganin abun nata na neman wuce makaɗi da rawa yasanya ya daina sanyawa ana kawomata , dan ya lura sam yarinyar batasan ciwon kanta ba,itadai kawai taci, batare da tayi la'akarin yawan shan zaqin zai iya illah ga rayuwarta ba , ya daɗe baiga yarinya irin madina da batasan metakeyi  arayuwartaba. Da har yanxu quruciya bata fara bankwana da rayuwarta ba.... 

Acan jumeta kuwa su mommy da hajjah dasauran masu neman gindin zama agunsu sosai suka shiga cikin hidimar biki, musamman yadda bikin keta karasowa, hidima sukeyi na bikin sosai harda na neman suna, musamman yadda IGP ya haukatasu da tarin kayan lefen da yayiwa nafisa  daya kashe naira tamkar bayaso, mommy kuwa tsabar rashin ta ido haka ta dinga rangaɗa guda agida da yade yaden habaici,datakeyiwa mutanen gida, ciki kuwa harda innah da tunda tayi fatan alkhairi taja bakinta ta tsuke takoma gefe tana kallon ikon allah, sai da  mommy rumaisa tayimata magana tare da nuna bacin ranta akan abinda takeyi sannan tadaina dan ko babu komai innah uwa ce agaresu, dan tagama yiwa mommy halacci dan itace ta haifo uban Abdulmajid din dasuke taƙama dashi yanxu.. Hakan yasa mommy tsagaita yin wasu abubuwan.. 
Itama nafisa ta girgiza ganin irin dukiyar da IG ya narko alefenta, duk yadda take tunaninsa ya wuce nan, tana fatan dai ace ba komai iri daya akayimata da kucakar yarinyar nan ba, domin hakan tamkar ya zubar da ajinta, haka ta dinga daukar lefen hoto tana dorawa asocial media, cike da alfahari take turawa abokanta pictures din lefen nata.. 

Tun saura sati daya biki suka fara shagali, kusan events bakwai suka tsara, kuma komai na karya da alfahari sukayi ita da abokanta dan sosai IG yasakar musu kuɗi, kai kace kudin sata ne, 
Banda tsadaddun ankunan da suka fiddo na kece raini, nafisa kuwa jinta takeyi awani matsayi na daban wanda bakowace mace bace takai ta tsinci kanta acikiba... 

Duk events din da aka tsara babu wanda IGP ya samu ya halarta, hakan bakaramin batawa nafisa rai yayi ba, dan aganinta ya naso ya watsa mata kasa ne a ido... 
Haka ta kirasa tana kukan shagwaɓa tanayimasa korafi... 
Cikin rarrashi yasamu ya rarrasheta tare da yimata alkawarin final events dashi zaayi, dan shima daga nasa bangaren manya manyan bakinsa sunfara zuwa musamman na nesa da tsofaffin abokan business din mahaifinsa da har yanxu suke zumunci da wasu... 
Da haka yasamu ya rarrasheta har sukayi sallama.. 
Da daddare kuwa kawai ya tsinci kansa da tura mata sako da yassn zuciyarta zata kuma sanyaya, haka kawai yaji ya damu da irin yanayin bacin ran nata, yarasa meke faruwa dashi ashe akwai ranar da zai baiwa wata ya mace da ba jalila ba kulawa ba, aduk lokacin da ya tuna jalila sai yayimata dinbin adduar nema mata rahama ga ubangiji... 

, Bangaren ammie ma tun saura kwanaki uku gidan yafara karbar baki, maryam kuwa sai washe gari ta shigo nigeria ita da mijinta da yaranta sosai suka fara shiga cikin hidimar bikin. 

Sir Abdulmajeed  kuwa Kamar yadda yayiwa nafisa alkawari , jirgi suka biyo shida abokansa zuwa adamawa,dayake daman sai anfara daura auren nafisa kafin na madina, shiyasa tun daren daurin auren suka isa adamawa, sai da sukayi bucking na hotel da zasu sauka.. Suka huta sukayi lunch sai yamma  suka isa katafaren hall din da zaa gabatar da fulani day,... 
Sosai abun ya kayatar musamman yadda aka shirya al adun fulani kan tsari, dan ko magana awurin da yaren fulatanci akeyi,... 
Matuka abun ya kayatar da yan uwa da abokan arziki, da sauran family dama yan kallo.. Ansha hotuna sosai angabatar da duk wata al ada ta bafulatani , nafisa kuwa sai faman mannewa IGP takeyi, ita bisa dole ga mijinta atare da ita, anci ansha ankumayi facali da naira, kowa sai sambarka Yan jaridu kuwa tamkar jira sukeyi, suka fara harbawa asocial media, bikin IGP, sukutum tare da sabuwar amaryarsa a fulani day "wannan shi ne kanun labaran... 
Sai 10:24pm taron ya tashi.. 
Nafisa kuwa taso keɓencewa da angonnata dan cike take da kewarsa,  yanayin yadda taga yashiga busy da abokansa yasanya tilas ta hakura, haka dribobi suka debesu suka mayar da su gida,... 
IGP Kuwa ko gidan bai wuce ba suka koma masaukinsu, dan amatukar gajiye suke... Koda suka koma saida ya turawa nafisa text na bangajiya tare da bayyana mata irin kyan da tayi ayau.. 
Sosai taji daɗin sakon,  ta karantasa yafi kuɗin kirga, dan lokaci ne na  farko da IG yafara yaba kyanta... 

Washe gari karfe goma daurin auren ya kasance ababban masallaci unguwar shanu anan  jumeta, dan haka tun 9:30am wurin ya cika  da dan musulmi harda yan jaridu , IG kuwa sai 9:54am yasamu yakaraso shida abokansa da ss dinsa, yayi matukar kyau, dazaiyi wuya ya gibta baka juya ka sake kallonsa.. Cikin dakakkiyar shadda ruwan makuba, dinkin babban riga yayi shirinsa, agogonsa da hula da tsadadden cover shoe dinsa duka sundace da kalar shaddarsa, sai baza kamshi yakeyi, tamkar wani matashi haka yafito kallo daya zaka yimasa ka fahimci ranar tasace... 
Karfe goma, jamaa suka shaida daurin auren nafisa haroon da kuma IGP Abdulmajeed usman.. 
Tuni maroka suka fara aikinsu yayinda abokan IG suka shiga yimusu barin kudi, haka aka sha hotuna bayan angama akaci aka sha, kowa ya tafi da sam barka.. 
Daga nan kuma suka dunguma zuwa BIRNIN TA DIKKO..  Inda ananne zaa daura aurensa da madina, da yake daurin auren na karfe biyu da rabi ne, tun 1:49pm suka shigo birnin katsina, a masallacin unguwar, aka kuma daurawa IGP Abdulmajeed auren madina abdullahi,dubban jamaa suka shaida hakan,  kamar dai a jumeta haka yan jarida da maroka suka bayyana, dan kuwa IG sananne ne ba iya gari ba harda kasa, bayan andaura aurenne iyaye da 'yaƴa, da sauran abokan arziki akayita hotuna.. 
Sosai abun yakayatar da jamaar unguwar kowa sai sam barka yake... 
IGP Kuwa wani irin farinciki ne  daya jima bai tsinci kansa aciki ba shi ne ya rufesa, yana fatan ubangiji ya basa ikon yin adalci atsakanin matansa, yasanyamasa hakuri da juriya na zama da madina... 

Nafisa aka fara kawowa agidan, dan ita aka fara daura aurensu, da yake su anesa suke, Bayan kammala daure dauren aurenne,da yamma liƙis  aka kawo amarya nafisa daga jumeta zuwa gidan  mijinta, ba iya abokanta kadaiba, hatta yan uwan hajja saida suka jinjina kyau da tsari na gidan, anzuba kayan  more rayuwa tamkar baza a bar duniyar ba, ita kanta nafisa ta tabbatar sanin IG ya wuce gaban tunaninta, hakan yasa kullum kara nutsu akaunarsa take, musamman yadda taji abokanta da yan uwanta na koɗa kokarinsa... Sai murmishi takeyi kanta nakuma yimata girma, ashe  burinta zai cika watarana, gashi ta mallaki Abdulmajeed ahannunta gata a gidansa amatsayin matar aure, ba iya yar uwa kaɗai ba... Kowa yakalli nafisa awannan ranar yasan tana cikin tsananin farinciki, domin kuwa farincikinta yagaza boyuwa afuskarta.... 

Bangaren madina kuwa, tana shirin zuwa tahfeez ne aranar daurin auren nata da batasan da shi ba, babah ya hanata, cikin mamaki ta tsaya tana kallon babah, ta ga lokutta da dama wani lokacin da kansa yake korata zuwa makarantar, amma  yau shine da dakatar da ita kada ta fita, taɓe baki tayi batare da ta nemi jin baasin haka ba ta cire hijabinta ta wurgi da ita ta ajiye jikkar makarantar, dama bawani kaunar zuwa makarantar takeyi ba, dan batada yadda zatayine take zuwa, allah yasanya baban ya fiddata daga makarantar baki daya ma ta huta.. Wannan shine fatan madina, saidai abinda bata fahimta ba, abubuwan dake faruwa agidan, musamman yadda makota ke shigowa su nata faman allah sa alkhairi,ga yadda goggo ta dauko mai gyaran gashi na gargajiya ta gyarawa madina gashinta, yayi kyau sosai bakinsa da tsayinsa yakuma fitowa, haka mai kunshi ta zo tayiwa madina kunshi, sosai kunshin ya fito radau afarar fatarta, musamman yadda aka zana jajayen fulawoyi, abun masha Allah dole yadauki hankalin mai kallo, itakanta madina tana sane da irin kyan da tayi na musamman, saidai tafi alakanta hakan da mai yuwuwa biki zasuje da Goggo akauyensu, shiyasa tayimata wannsn uban gayun, har lokacin ta lura da yadda mutane ke faman shigowa gidan su,  tadai zuba ido tana kallo, domin atsakanin yadikko da Goggo duk wanda ta tambaya abinda ke faruwa agidan  basar da ita sukeyi, sam basu amsa mata tambayoyinta... 
Shiyasa itama tafita sabgoginsu, ta cigaba da abinda yasha mata kai.. 

Babah kuwa sai bayan sallar laasar ya shigo gidan da goro mai yawa da alawa da biscuits anan ya baiwa su goggo da yadikko, suma su shaida daurin auren madina.. Cike da farinciki suka karba suna fatan allah yasanya  alkhairi, yadikko kuwa guɗa ta rangaɗa lokaci daya ta saka kukan daɗi na ganin wannan rana da tayi... Tana fatan allah ya gwadamata ya'yan madina tagani, 
Har yamma tayi sosai ango baizo yadauki amaryarsa ba kuma bai aiko da mota ba... 
Da magrib ne goggo ta kuma hadawa madina ruwan wanka, mai gauraye da lalle da ganyen magarya da  ruwan turare, bayan ta fito daga wanka ne ta shirya madina cikin sabuwar atamfa dinkin riga da zani... Ta haɗamata tea mai kauri da ɗan sinadari ta bata, tas deenah ta shanye shayin.. 

IGP kuwa yana afalonsa  cikin abokansa, suna tsokanarsa angon amare biyu, yayinda wasu kemasa korafin har yanxu basuga dayar amaryarba, da dukkan alamu tsada gareta tunda har yanxu basu ganta ba... 

Duk firar dasukeyi hankalinsa na gun madina, so yake ya samu ya sulale shikadai yaje ya daukota, yafi nutsuwa da hakan, duba da ita ɗin batasan abinda ke faruwaba, musamman yadda yaga karfe tara har takawo jiki... 
Cikin hikima dai yasamu ya bar falon, yadauki mota ya nufi unguwar su madina, cikin wani irin yanayi da yarasa na farincikine koko...? 
Akofar gidan yayi parking ya fito, hakan yayi daidai da fitowar babah daga cikin gidan... 
Cikin girmamawa IG ya isa inda babah yake ya gaishesa.. 
Amsawa babah yayi a mutunce tare da yimasa bangajiya, da daɗa yimasa fatan alkhairi a aurennasa... 

"Babah ayi hakuri bisa rashin zuwana da wuri, mutanene sukamin yawa.. "
IG ya idar da maganar yana sunkuyar dakai... 
Cikin fahimtar zancensa babah yace "ai babu komai Abdulmajeed, bari nashiga gidan "...
Babah yana shiga yasanarwa su yadikko zuwan Abdulmajeed  dan yazo ne ya yafi da matarsa... 
Hijabi yadikko ta dauko tana kokarin sanyawa , dan raka amarya... 
Da katar da ita babah yayida cewa "kiyi hakuri yadikko, idan allah yakaimu da safe sai kuje, amma yanxu ki barsa ya tafi da matarsa ".. 
Cewar babah kenan.. 

"Yanxu madina kadai zata tafi dakinta babu mai rakata? "
Cewar goggo... 
To akwai haramcin hakanne?meyasa kuke ɗabbaqa al ada akan addini, koba komai   Ai hakanma dattako ne, tunda dai yarinyar nan tazama muharramarsa ai babu wani abu aciki, kuma yazo shi kaɗai kunga kenan shikadai yakeso ya tafi da matarsa, zuwanku tare da madina zai haifar da wani abun, kunsan yarinyar nan dai".. 
Cewar babah da yakeyi musu bayani dallah dallah... 
"Yanxu kije ki kiramin madinar"
Babah ya umurci goggo.. 

Dakinta goggo ta nufa inda acan madina take, kwance ta isketa akan katifa tana ta baccinta, yadda take baccin zai tabbatatmaka da baccin yayi nisa kuma taji daɗinsa... 
Saida goggo tajima tana tashinta kafin ta farka dan shegen nauyin bacci ne da ita... 
"Tashi muje babanki nakira ".. 
Hannu tasaka ta murtsike idanunta tana karawa kwayar idanunta girma,... Hakan ya nunawa goggo har yanxu cikin baccin madina take... 
Saida goggo tayi dagaske kafin deenah ta wartsake, atsakar gida kuwa ruwa ta bata abuta ta wanke  fuskarta tare da mika mata hijabi, ta saka suka ida karasowa inda babah da yadikko suke... 
Haka babah ya sanya madina agaba har zuwa kofar gida.. 
Suna fita yadikko tafara matsar kwallah, wannan wane irin kai amarya ne haka, abu ko gata babu... 
Rarrashinta goggo tayi da cewar kidai yimusu adduar zaman lafiya mai dorewa... Haka sukabi madina da addu o i da fatan shiga gidan mijinta a sa a,.. 

IGP Kuwa da ya koma ajikin motarsa ya jingina, fitowar babah da madina saida zuciyarsa ta buga da karfin gaske... Tun kafin su karaso IG ya buɗe mata mazaunin gaba... Ahaka Har suka karaso.. 
"Shiga mota madina".. 
Cewar babah cikkn umurtar madinar.. 
Duban baban tayi ta dubi IGP, duk da dare ne sarai taganesa tun kafin su karaso.. 
Tsayawa tayi tana duban IG da shi kansa duban yanayinta yakeyi taso ta yi musu, sai tunaninta ya bata kila gun ammie zai kaita, dan haka babu musu ta shige motar, har lokacin baccin bai ida barin idanunta... "
Haka babah ya umurci sir Abdulmajeed da ya ja motar su tafi, ya bisu da addu o i da sanya musu albarka arayuwar aurensu.. 
Haka IG ya shiga motar yayi mta key, cikin matukar kaunar dattijon aransa, matuka mahaifin madina keda dattako, hakika ba tarin dukiya kadai bane arziki, ubangiji yayi maka baiwar dattako hakan ma arziki ne... 


Tunda ya fara driving babu wanda yayi magana tsakaninsa da ita, agogo ya duba data nuna goma saura na dare, yadan saci kallonsa ga madina da fara yin gyangyaɗi amotar, murmishi yayi na gefen kumatu tare da dauke idonsa akanta, yacigaba da tukinsa cikin nutsuwa, hakan yayi daidai da farkawar madina daga gyangyaɗin, sai faman mutsu mutsu da raba idanu takeyi, da dukkan alamu so take ta kwanta.. 
Duban sir Abdulmajeed tayi da yake ankare da duk wani motsinta,  azahari kuma ya mayar da hankalinsa akant tukinsa . ". 

"Wai ina zamuje yanxu"? 
ita ce tambayar da deenah tayimasa tana kokarin gyara zamanta... 
Yajita sarai, amma yayi kamar bai jitaba.. 
"Wai bakaji, nace ina zaka kaini? ".. 
Ta sake maimaita masa tambayar.. 
Ta gefen ido yasaci kallonta ya lura wannan lokacin adan tsorace tayi tambayar . 
"Siyar dake zanyi".. 
Ya bata amsa atakaice batare da ya dubi inda takeba... 

Madina kuwa zabura tayi ta re da wartsake wa daga baccin dake idonta ta fiddo manyan idanunta, duk da atsananin tsoro take hakan bai hanata fara kwararo addua ba, dan malaminsu yace "addua makamin mumini ce"... 

"Bismillahirrahmanirrahim a uzu billahi minasshaidanirrajin, innalillahi, salamun kaulan mirrabirrahim"... 
Itace adduar datake karatowa tana tofe sir Abdulmajeed da yawu ba kakkautawa.....

 Da tunani Kala² IGP ya isa gidan su deenah, kofar gidan yayi parking kamar yadda yasaba, fitowa yayi ya jingina ajikin motar yana duban gidan tamkar mai nazari, deenah zai aika akiramasa ko kuwa shiga zaiyi, idan har yasa aka  kira masa deenah hakan ya nuna zance yazo gun yar  yarinyar da bata wuce yar cikinsaba,hakanma janyowa kansa raini ne zaiyi, dan karamin tsaki yaja  tare da girgiza kai ya cije softlips dinsa, he can't do that.. Cikin samun mafita yakoma mota yazauna tare da dauko memo da biro  ya yagi takarda yadanyi rubutu da bai wuce layi uku zuwa hudu ba ya ninke takardar yasaka acikin ledar da yayi mata shopping, dan guntun tsaki yaja dan baya tunanin deenah ta iya koda karatun hausa ne, anyway wannan kawaice mafitar da ya zabarwa kansa... Balle murfin motar yayi ya fito hakan yayi daidai wucewar wani yaro dake kokarin shiga gidansu madina..

Da idanu IG yabi yaron har ya shige cikin gidan, furzan da iska yayi ya shige motarsa yayimata key  yabar layin.
Koda yaron ya isa cikin gidan dakin goggo asabe ya wuce yayi sallama ya miƙa mata ledar. 
"Wannan fa daga ina? "
Cewar goggo ta karbi ledar tana juyawa... 
 Anan yaron yayimata bayanin da   yallaɓai yayimasa... 
Jinjina kai goggo ta shigayi dan tagane sakon daga sir Abdulmajeed ne.. 
Koda madina ta dawo daga islamiyyar yamma saida daddare goggo ta dauko mata sakon ta bayan sungama cin abincin dare, karba deenah tayi tare da buda ledar ta haɗiyi yawu ganin tarin chocolates da biscuits da tayi, bata tambayi goggo ba asi ba kawai tafara bare ledar chocolate takai baki... 

"Ai kin tambayeni daga ina aka kawomiki bawai daga baki kikama ciba, "..

Dan guntun tsaki goggo taja kafin ta dan sanyaya muryarta "abbiensu sultan ne yace akawo miki, kuma naga kamar hada sako aciki ko dubawa bakiyi ba "... 

Dan hadiye fuska deenah tayi tana duban goggo tace "to ni ina ruwana dashi da zai kawomin wadannan kayan,? "..

Tayi maganar cikin dariya mai kama da ta mugunta... 
Murmishi goggo tayi aranta tace "har yanxu bakisan kan gabas ba yarinya, idan yayi wuff dake kya ƙyaƙyata da hujjah... "
Afili kuwa tashi tayi ta nufi dakinta dan kunna maganin sauro, kasancewar yanayin garin yanayin damina ne... 
Gyara zama madeenah tayi ta zazzage kayan dake cikin ledar takardar tadauko ta warware, ƙarawa eyeball din ta girma tayi dan ko kusa bazata iya karanta wannan rubutun mai kama da na likitoci ba, saida tayi dakyar tare da hada kalmomi kafin tafara karantawa _nasan zakiji daɗin chocolates dinnan sosai, idan sun kare ki sanarwa da sultan a school  zankuma kawomiki wasu, asha daɗi lafiya..._

Koda tasamu dakyar  da haɗa kalmomi ta gama karanta sakon, dauke idanunta tayi gefe daya tana nazari "wato ya maidata aljanar zaƙi kenan, shi ne harda wani raina mata wayau da cikomata leda da kayan zaki, koma miye taci banza, kuma zatacigaba da cin banzar... 
Yayyaga takardar tayi ta watsar atsakar gidan ta tattare ragowar ta zuba aleda ta nufi daki.. 

Aɓangaren su baffah kuwa kamar yadda suka faɗa acikin satin suka zo kt suka nemawa IG auren deenah, babu bata lokaci babah yabasu domin tun ranar da Abdulmajeed yayimasa zancen yabasa madeenah duniya da lahira, haka suka gabatar da dukiyar nara dubu dari, sai sadaki dubu dari biyu, dan babah yaƙi sanar musu da nawane sadakin, shiyasa suka yanka... 
Sadakin yarinya kadai babah yakarba naira dubu dari biyu dan shi ne ginshikin aure, dukiyar aure kuwa cewa yayi bazai karbaba... Sosai suka tattauna akan batun auren dan so sukeyi asaka rana awannan zuwannasu... Sun nemi tajin bakin babah, anan babah ya nuna musu akwai bukatar nasu shirin daga bangarensu, baffah hafizu ne yace "idan wannan ne, aika kafi karfin haka malam, babu abinda Abdulmajeed bai tanada ba agidansa, baya bukatar komai sai yarinya..., 
Ai an riga anzama daya malam Abdullahi, 
Cewar baffah aminu... 
Cikin jindadin halaccinsu babah ya jinjina  kai, "hakane, to nabaku wuƙa da nama ku sanya ayadda shi Abdulmajeed din ya bukata "... 

"Eh to kamar yadda muka sanya na dayar yar uwar tasa inaga abarsa daidai danata sai ayi alokaci  daya, wato watanni biyu masu zuwa'.. 
Cewar baffah shamsu... 
Cikin gamsu wa suka jinjina kai da na am da zancensa.. 
Basu ne sukayi sallama da babah ba saida suka tsaida ranar auren IGP Abdulmajeed da madeenah, akan wata biyu masu zuwa... 
Haka sukayi sallama da babah cike da jin daɗin tarbar da suka samu...
Babah kuwa washe gari, ƙafa da ƙafa yaje kauyen ringizau yakaiwa yadikko, alewa da dabino da biscuits na saka rana.. 
Kukan farinciki yadikko ta sanya, ashe da rabon taga aurrn gudaliyar jikartata da ita kadai ta ragemata aduniya... 
Haka tabi mutanen gida da alewar da dabino akan kowa yasanya albarka a auren yar lelenta, haka mutanen gida suka dinga sam barka da fatan alkhairi,.. 
Haka sukacigaba da tattauna lamarin auren, da fatan alkhairi akan lamarin.... 
IGP Abdulmajeed kuwa, haka kawai yajisa cikin nutsuwa da wani irin farinciki na daban da baffah aminu yasanar dashi yadda sukayi da mahaifin deenah da kuma saka ranar da akayi, Duk sa ba wani son madina ta fuskar aureba amma ya gaza fahimtar irin farincikin da yakeyi na jin zancen, dan ko a office haka ya tsinci kansa cikin walwalar da yajima bai tsinci kansa aciki ba, amma yafi alakanta hakan da farincikin sa ya ta allaƙa da cikawa jalila burinta da zaiyi.. Bawai dan yanajin yarinyar aransa ba... 
Daga jumeta kuwa haka yake ta amsa waya akan saka ranar da akayimasa da mata biyu alokaci daya, yayinda su mujaheed keta tsokanarsa wai angon mata biyu, yacika zari da yawa, haka da sukayi waya da maryam ya fahimci bataji dadi ba yadda lokaci daya zai aure mata biyu, dan tasan yayannata sam bayada burin zama da mata biyu, abu ne da ya haye masa yazama qaddara musamman haɗinsa da nafisa da ammie tayimata bayani, dan kowa yasan wacece nafisa a fam da irin rashin nutsuwa, sam batayi tsari da shiga nutsattsiyar rayuwa irin ta dan uwanta, abangaren madina kuwa ta rasa me yayannata ya gani ajikin yarinyar har yake shaawar aurennata, yanayin yadda tayimasa fatan alkhairi ya fahimci abun bai kwanta mata araiba, to shikansa ɗin ya ya iya?, hakuri da qaddarar data hayemasa lokaci daya shi ne mafita... 

Bangaren nafisa ta ɗan tada hankalinta jin cewar, yarinyar nan kanwar tsohuwar matarsa, da ita yake kokarin haɗa ta kishi, to miye ajikon yarinyar, dako makerin yan matancinta bai baiyyana ba, dama tajima tana jin haushin yarinyar, ta wannan hanyar ne zata koyamata hankali ta kuma sanyata taraina kanta, ita da kwadayayyun iyayennata da suka ƙaƙabawa IG aurrnta dan shgen kwadayin su da son abun duniya, ta tabbatar iyayen yarinyar basu bar IG hakanan ba dole sun haɗa da asiri, idan ba haka ba kuwa babu abinda zaiyi da wannan kwailar yarinyar, dako matsayin mai gyaramata gafo bata kai ba, ko ba miyr ta dauki aniyar sai yarinyar ta ɗandana kudarta matukar tace zata yi gogayya daita akishi, kuma  da kafarya zata sanya ta tabar gidan.. Cikin wannan tunanin ta samarwa da kanta nutsuwa, har lokacin bata daina mamakin da ficiciyar yarinyar da ake kokarin hadata kishi da ita ba... 
Kwanci tashi biki na ta karatowa dan bai wuce sati biyar ba yanxu.. Zuwa wannan lokacin babu wanda nafisa bata sanarwa da bikinnata ba acikin abokanta maza da mata.. Alokacin ne mommy ta umurceta da tayi gaggawar dawowa jumeta domin su samu su kintsa ta yadda yakamata.. 
Haka nafisa ta jewa  ammie da zancen komawarta jumeta, a wannan satin, fatan allah yakaimu ammie tayimata.. 
Kafin lokacin tafiyar kuwa sha tara ta arziki ammie ta haɗa mata.. 
Ranar da zata tafi kuwa sai bayan sallar azahar ta gama shirinta saida ta turawa yallabai sakon bankwana yake baya kasar, kuma yanzu babu laifi yakan danyi mata reply da takaitattun kalmomi.. 
Ammie kuwa har bakin mota ta raka nafisa , sannan sukayi  sallama... 
Anasu bangaren su hajjah kuwa basuyi kasa aguiwa ba, wurin daɗa karkato da zuciyar Abdulmajeed akan nafisa ta karkashin kasa, itakanta nafisar tafara ganin tasirin hakan, domin ko da yaga sakonta ba iya text ya rubotomataba, kiran ta yayi yaji ya ta isa gida lafiya, tare da yimata ban gajiya, duk ta lura uaba busy ne dan bawata firar kirki sukayi ba yakashe wayar, hakan yakara bata tabbaci kan cewa yallabai yazo hannunta.. 
Tun ranar da ta dawo jumeta mommy keta faman banka mata magungunan mata tundaga na sha da na matsi har zukunno, hakama hajjah ta dage ta kowabe nafisa karɓar wuta takeyi, banda wanda itakanta take hadawa  kanta tana sha... 
Tun saura sati uku bikin suka gama tsara jadawalin events din da zasuyi ita nabeela data kasance ta hannun damar amarya.. 
IGP kuwa haka yasakar musu kuɗi, tamkar ba wahala yasha yanemo suba, hakan bakaramin daɗi yayimusu ba, shiyasa suka fara kiraranta bikin da bikin yar gata, hakan ya haddasa kananun maganganu acikin estate din musamman yadda hajja da mommy keta faman jefo kananun maganganu... 

Bangaren lefe kuwa tuni ammie ta saka angama komai, dan ita tadauki nauyin hakan komai order tasaka akayi from abroad... 

Kayatattun akwatuna ne manya guda goma sha shidda kowannensu shaqe yake dakayan alatu, tundaga manya kuma tsaadddun suturu suwa turaruka  dss, duk wanda yagani dole ya ji jina wa amaren da suka dace da akwatunan... Haka kowacce ta tashi da akwatuna takwas ² kowane kuma shaqe yake da kaya... 
Nafisa aka fara kaiwa nata, na deenah kuwa aka kaisu agidan IG  tamkar yadda IGP din yace abarsu anan gida... 
Anasa bangaren babu wani shiri dayayi sai sabunta fentin hadadden gidannasa da yayi, ya canza duk abinda ke cikin gidan, tundaga kayan kitchen zuwa funutures, labulaye da kuma carpet, haka ya raba kowace ra sashinta, sai dakin yara, abun saidai ace masha allah dan gida yafito sak aljannar duniya.... 
Deenah kuwa tun saura sati uku goggo asabe tafara gyarata tundaga dulka zuwa alewa, da turarem kamshin jiki tuni fatar jikinta ta mulke takara laushi da sheƙi da kuma kara fito  da hasken fatarta, sam bata bata wasu magungunan mata ba, saidai fruits da take blending dinsu tana bata tana sha da madara peak, da kunun aya mai kwakwa, wanda tasan koda agaba zaiyiwa madina amfani hakan yasa jikin  Madina yayi lumi lumi  takara cikowa  banda turare na wanka datake zubawa madina acikin ruwa takaimata bayi ,Itakuwa madina dabatasan dawan garinba sai daɗi takeji yadda goggo ke gatantata da kayan daɗi, da kaimata ruwa a bayi,  har idan bata bataba dakanta take tambayarta, dake fannin kwadayine itakam goggo   aranta cewaa tayi, badan sanin jarabar da namiji datayi ba da saitace, ba sai deenah tasha kayan tsattsafo da niima ba domin babu abinda zai dauki hankalin namiji ajikinta bare ya kusanceta ayanxu...

Cike da takaicin shirmen deenah goggo ke kallonta har ta karaci shirmennata , cigaba goggo tayi da aikin gabanta saida tayi kamar bazata kula ta kafin tadago kai ta dubi deenah, daketa faman rera wakokinta " 'ya mace dai kike kuma girki shi ne adon duk wata mace, musamman agun mijinta, idan har kikace lalaci da son jiki zaki saka arayuwarki gaki can kinzama hoto agidan miji, ki dawo hankalinki yar nan, ".. 
Cike da hannunka mai sanda goggo keyiwa madina faɗa... 
Deenah kuwa jinjina kai tayi tare da taɓe baki alamun zancen yabi iska, kallon goggo takumayi dake nazarin yadda tadauki zancennata "nidai goggo kidainamin wani zancen aure, kibari sai nayi auren tukunnan "... 
Ta idar da zancen tana daukar kwandon wanke wanken ta nufi kitchen, da idanu goggo ta rakata cike da takaici kafin ta girgiza kai, lallai akwai kallo a wurinnan... 
Babah kuwa dake dakinsa yana jiyo firar tasu ya kuma fahimci inda zancen asabe yasa agaba... Girgiza kai kawai yayi tare da linkaya aduniyar nazari... 

Da daddare kuwa shida Goggo ne atsakar gida tana gefensa yana cin abincin dare, deenah kuwa ta tafi islamiyyar dare... 

Tsame hannunsa yayi daga kwanon abincin yadubi goggo asabe "naji firar dakukeyi keda yar taki adazu, amma mekika fahimta game da aurennan da zamuyiwa madina? "..

Tunda tafara magana babah ya shiga nazarin kalamanta zuwa can ya numfasa yace "hakane asabe, saidai tunda shi Abdulmajeed yanemi auren madina, to bazan iya hanasa ba, ba madina kaɗai idan akwai abinda na mallaka ya nuna yakeso wallahi zan iya sadaukar masa, har abada bazamu iya saka masa da abinda yayimana fa ce addua, bare kuma auren madina da yin hakan ko kusa bai saɓawa sharia ba illah ɗabbbaƙa sunnar ma aiki (SAW) da zamuyi..ita kanta yadikko dana sanar mata tayi farincikin hakan ta kuma sake jinjina hallaci irinna yaron nan ".. 
Shiru sukayi bakidayansu yayinda goggo tashiga jinjina kai cikin gamsuwa da kalaman babah.. "Inaga akwai mafita dan gudun yarinyar nan kada ta watsamana kasa a ido ta kunyatar damu, za a daura aurennasu batare da tasan ita akayiwa aurenba, game da karatunta kuwa nasan Abdulmajeed bazai dakatar da hakanba, dan yasan yarinyace kuma tana bukatar samun ilimi, har zuwa lokacin da zaa kaita gidansa daganan zaisan dubarun da zaiyi a matsayinsa na namiji ya daidaita zaman, tunda yaji ya gani ahakan, har zuwa lokacin da zatayi hankali ta gane matsayinsa agunta, insha allah zanyiwa shi Abdulmajeed din wannan bayanin na tabbatar zai fahimceni tunda yasan wacece madina".... 
Tunda babah ya fara magana Goggo ke gyada kai, wannan ne kaɗai mafita kuwa domin idan ba haka ba silar wannan auren sai deenah ta ɗaiɗaita zumuncin da ke tsakaninsu... 

"Tohm allah ya shige mana gaba".. 
Cewar goggo tana cigaba da jinjina kai... 
 
Amen babah ya amsa dashi anan yake sanar mata cewar acikin satin nan iyayen Abdulmajeed din zasu zo dan nemamasa auren madina.., kuma yayiwa babah bayani sosai  akan yadda lokaci daya auren nafisa ya hau kansa, sosai babah ya gamsu da bayaninsa tare da yimusu fatan alkhairi dan aganinsa wannan ba matssla bane... 

Haka yayiwa goggo bayani.. Sosai takumayin mamakin hakan, mata biyu kenan alokaci daya, "allah ya kwaɓe fitina yabasu zaman lafiya da kwanciyar hankali ya kuma haɗa kawunansu, ya nuna mana lokacin da rai da lafiya"/. 

Cikin jin dadin du a inta babah ya amsa da amin..

Haka su baffah ibrahim sukayiwa hajja sallama kowa ya nufi gun aikinsa zuciyarsu cike da farinciki, ta wata sigar kuwa abun na baiwa kowa mamaki yadda lokaci daya IGP Abdulmajeed ya nuna sha'awar aurensa akan nafisa, bayan ƙin amincewa da yayi ashekarun baya... 
Bayan fitarsu kuwa hajja ta fara laluben number hajiya rumaisa tayi mata albishir
 Dariya hajiya rumaisa tayi kafin ta magantu "waya faɗa masa barno gabas take, ai tasirin wannan kwallin ya wuce duk tunaninku, lanqwasa mai taurin kai, shine sunan maganin... "

"Ahhh karki damu tsakanina dake ba wannan,amincin dake tsakaninmu nayimawa  allah dai yasa ayi damu ".. 
Cewar hajiya rumaisa..

Cewar hajja..
"Ba kaɗan ba kuwa "
Cewar mommy, daga bisani suka cigaba da tattauna zancen... 
Hajja kuwa taso kiran nafisa amma tafasa acewarta tunda har bata sanar dasu komai ba suma zasu nuna mata tamkar babu abinda suka sani... 


IGP Abdulmajeed kuwa tamkar yadda ya faɗawa baffah aminu rabar da zai shigo jumeta  aranar ya diro garin adamawa, 
Ɓangaren su hajja  kuwa tun safe tasaka  ma aikinta shirya girki na musamman kala kala, da tarkacen kayan makulashe na tarbar yallabai, kai kace wani baƙo ne ba ɗan gida ba.   
Baffah aminu kuwa tun kafin ranar ya sanarwa da yan uwansa zancen zuwan Abdulmajeed dakuma *MUSABBABIN* abinda ke tafe dashi, baffah hamza kuwa awaya aka sanar dashi kasancewar yana umrah shida iyalinsa, sam maganar auren batayimasa daɗi ba, yakuma rasa dalilin amincewar Abdulmajeed da auren nafisa ayanzu, bashida zaɓi da ya wuce yimusu fatan alkhairi, da yasanya albarka alamarin.. 
Ƙarfe 3:09pm rantsattsiyar motarsa ta danna hancinta a cikin estate din,  cikin nutsuwarsa yake karewa estate din kallo tamkar wani bako ahankali yake sauke ajiyar zuciya mai tattare da nutsuwa... Yana matukar jin daɗi idan ya zo jumeta yanason kasancewa da ahalinsa matuka... 
Dasauri drivernsa ya Buɗe masa mota adaidai lokacin daya gama parking din motar aparking area... 
Fitowa yayi daga motar cikin shigar boyel mai ruwan madara da yazauna ajikinsa matuka musamman da ya kafe hula (zanna bukar) data dace da kalar yadin, matuka cikar zatin da ubangiji yayi masa ya gama bayyana atattare dashi.. 
Kai tsaye kutsa kansa yayi acikin gidan, sashen hajiya innah yayi tsinke.. Tare da sallama ya shigo falon, cike da farincikin tsimayin ganin jikannata ta amsa masa sallamar tare da yimasa barka da zuwa cikin zolaya har ya karaso yazauna aɗaya daga cikin kujerun dake falon yana amsawa cikin raha da annashuwa ya gaishe da innah.. 
bayan sun gama gaisawane ta sanya ladiyo mai aikinta kawo masa tarkacen ababen sha da na ci, banda girkin da tasa aka  shiryamasa, wainar shinkafa da miyar agusi sai kamshin man shanu ke tashi asama.. Ruwa da sanyayyar madarar shanu kawai ya iya sha bai ci abincinba dan sam ji yayi bayajin yunwa... 
Dubansa innah tayi cikin mamaki ganin bai ci abincinba "ka yo wannan bulaguro amma kaƙi cin abinci, kai dai ba mata ba bare ince an cika maka ciki agida har kayo guziri ".... 
Innah ta karashe zancen tare da zolaya... 
Dariya marar sauti IGP yayi idanunsa akan innah yace "kinga innah ajiye korafinki sam banajin yunwa ne "

"Uhmmm, na lura wani farinciki ke dibarka dan bada jima wa ba zakazama sabon ango ka ajiye tsohuwa kadauki sabuwa ko? ".. 
(Dayake innah tassn zancen zuwannasa... 
Tunda innah ta fara magana ya kafeta da manyan idanunsa yana dan nazari shikam sam baya farinciki ko bakinciki ga wannan auren da zaiyi akaro na biyu, yana farinciki ne kawai domin zaiyiwa soyayyar jalila biyayya da kumacika alkawarin da ya daukarmata, ga gefe daya ammie na bukatar dole ya nemo wata ya haɗa, baki daya kansa yadauki caji, bakidaya rayuwar juyasa take kamat tamaula, har yanxu kuma bai samu mafita game da zancen ammie ba, ajiyar zuciya kawai yasauke tare da murmishi yana duban innar.. 
Batare da ta fahimci komai ba tacigaba da cewa "allah ya tabbatar mana alkhairi yasanya wannan din da za a aure tana da kyawawan dabiu irinna marigayiya... Cikin gamsuwa da farinciki babah ya amsa da allah yakaimu... 
Sallama ammie tayima goggo t
Deenah kuwa jin muryar ammie nayiwa goggo sallama ne yasanya ta fitowa da gudu gun ammie...

"Saina sake dawowa ko? "

Haka babah ya yini juya zancen da ammie ta xo masa dashi, duk abun nabasa mamaki matuka amma yayi farinciki da hakan.. 
Koda yasanarwa da goggo bukatar da takawo ammie.. Sauke numfashi tayi cikin ta ajibin abinda kunnuwanta suka jiyemata tace "tirkashi, yanxu Abdulmajeed din ne kabaiwa auren madina,? "Kinga dakata asabe wannan kalubale dakike rantaɓowa babu abinda zai canja maganata, yarinyace na riga na basa duniya da kiyama, mutuwa kuma ta rabasu.. Kinsani babu abinda Abdulmajeed zai nema aduniyar nan inada ikonsa ingaza yimasa, zancen kuma kuruciya kikeyi, iyayenmu da akayimusu aurrn da kuruciyar akansu, mutuwa sukayi koko gaza zaman auren sukayi, ita kanta marigayiya (mahaifiyarsu) tana da shekaru sha biyu aka wankemin ita aka kawota dakina, bare madeenah ita nasan tayi shekaru sha uku.. Cewar babah yadda yaji goggo na ta zayyano kalubale...

Hajjah kuwa tamkar an tsundumata cikin ruwan aljannah take jin kanta... Sai famab washe baki takeyi tana amsa addu oinsu da ameen...

Kutsa kanta tayi kai tsaye ta shige ciki tare da cewa mai gadin ya  biya mai adaidaita kudinsa...

Tadauki tsayin mintuna uku ahaka kafin ammie ta sauko daga upstairs, tun kafin ta karaso take duban madeenah duk da tadan razana da ganin yanayinta, musamman yadda fuskanta ta kumbure da idanunta da sukayi ja.. Allah dai yasa ba wani shirmen tayiwa AJ ba ya doketa dan tasan halin yan mazannata da rashin hakuri... Cike da fargaba ta ida saukowa da matattakalan benen tayi gyaran murya tare da tambayar deenah lafiya...
