". 

Tunda ta fara magana ammie ke dubanta kafin taɗan bubbuga kafadar ta tace  "ya isa kiyi hakuri, abdulmajeed din, baisan kin tafo ba kenan? ".. 
"Uhmm".. 
Deenah ta bata amsa tana gyadwa ammie kai tare da sharbar majina... 
Jinjina kai ammie tayi cikin gamsuwa.. Kafin ta cigaba da rarrashin madina akan ta share hawayenta zata daukar masa mataki.. 
Dahaka ta lallabata ta wanke fuskanta, atare sukayi dinner da ammie.. Bayan sun gama ne tacemata taje ta kwanta ta huta... 
Ammie kuwa nazari ta shigayi akan  lamarin, taso kiran Sir Abdulmajeed alokacin dan ta sanar da shi amma ta fasa, ta tabbatar da har ynxu baisan deenah bata gidansa ba, may be bai dawo daga aiki ba yanxu.. 
Zata bari kawai dasafe ta tuntubesa taji... 

IGP kuwa saida yayi sallar magrib da issha sannan yashigo gida,bai tarar da nafisa a falo ba hakan ya tabbatar masa tana sashinta, baibi takan kowaba ya shige sashinsa dan agajiye yake,  wanka yayi, yayi shirinsa na bacci  bai damu da cin abincin dare ba, dan ba kasafai yafiye sakin jiki yaci abincin masu aiki ba, already yasan nafisa bazata moru afannin girkiba, bare kuma madeenah, shikam babu yadda zaiyi da yadda rayuwa ke bugasa kamar tamaula... 
Coffee ya dan sha kafin yazauna bakin gado yajanyo system dinsa ya shiga sarrafa ta.. Sosai yayi nisa acikin aikinsa.. Kafin ya dauko wayarsa, haka kawai yasoma kiran layin deenah, yasanta da taurinkai mai yuwuwa batayi abinda yasata ba... 
Yadda yayi zato haka yakasance dan layin arufe yake aka sanar dashi.. 

Furzar da zazzafar iska yayi lokaci daya ransa yasoma ɓaci, dole yakuma tsaurara matakinsa akan deenah tunda batajin maganar uban kowa... 
Rufe system din yayi dasauri ya fice daga dakinnasa, cikin bacin rai ya isa bangarenta.. Ganin kofar abude ne yasanyasa kutsa kansa kai tsaye, bai ganta afalo ba ya wuce bedroom dinta nan ma babu ita, har toilet ya duba bai ganta ba, sai wayar da kayan da ya kawomata a kan gado... 
Ransa ya kuma mugun ɓaci, fitowa yayi daga sashinnata cikin sauri mai tattare da zafin nama, sashin nafisa ya wuce, kwance ya isketa akan doguwar kujera tana waya, shigowar sa yasanyata saurin ajiye wayar tare da tashi zaune... 
"Ina deenah"... 
Ya shiga tambayarta... 
Yamutsa fuska ta danyi"kamarya? Bata a sashinta ne? "... 

Bai bi takan tambayoyinta na rainin hankali ba ya fice.. 
 Da idanu ta bisa tana dariya kasa² takoma ta kwanta tana danna waya... 
Wurin SS din dake bakin gate ya nufa yana tambayarsu, wanda ya baiwa deenah izinin fita, saidai bakidayansu nuna masa sukayi babu wanda yaga fitarta, faɗa ya shigayi musu baji ba gani, tamkar sunyi laifi, shi aganinsa babu yadda zaayi ace deenah tayi batan laya acikin gidan.. Sun matukar tsorata da yadda ya rufesu da fada, suj tabbatar cikin tsananin  bacin rai yake... 
Haka ya dawo falo zuciyarsa sai faman turiri takeyi, babu abinda zai hanasa ya ganar da deenah  kuskurenta, rainin da tayimasa yakai har ta saka kafa tafita baisaniba, bayan yayi warning dinta akan duk inda zataje saida saninsa.. 

Wuri yasamu ya zauna akan kujera, yana tunanin may be gida taje ne.. Waya ya ciro ya kira ammie dan yasanar da ita.. 
Ammie da har ta kwanta kiran AJ yashigo wayarta, murmishi tayi abayyane ganin sunansa ascreen din wayarta ta tabbatar matukar ya ankare bata nan sai ya kirata.. Daga kiran tayi ta kara akunne tare da sallama... 
Amsawa yayi ya gaishe ta batare da bata lokaciba yafara sanar da ita komai, saida ya kai aya, kafin ta sauke numfashi tace "ka nutsu deenah na nan wajena yanxu haka tayi bacci, kaima yakamata ka kwanta da safe sai kazo muyi magana yanxu dare  ne ".. 
Ta karashi maganar tana duban agogon dake kan locker data nuna 10:24pm... 
Sauke ajiyar zuciya ya yi, yayiwa ammie saida safe ya kashe wayar yana tunanin ta yadda akai deenah tabar gidan batare da sanin SS dinsa ba... Haka ya koma sashinsa, bayan ya kakkashe gloves din falon... 
Washe gari kuwa karfe tara nasafe kafin ya wuce office yana falon ammie zaune agabanta, tunda yagaisheta yaja bakinsa ya tsuke, har lokacin baiga wulgawar deenah, ita kanta ammie ta fahimci har yanxu cikin bacin rai yake... 
Murmishi ammie ta danyi irinnasu na manya kafin ta ce "kasan dai wacece madinah, so dole wannan saurin fusatar naka ya daina tasiri akanta, bazan gaji da fadamaka cewa komai zai zama tarihi, kaima kuma yaksmata ka rage mata wasu abubuwan, dan ita komai tayi gani take daidai ne domin batasan da wata igiyar aurenka akanta ba, to kabi komai ahankali "... 

Tunda ammie tafara magana yake sauke ajiyar zuciya yana biting lip dinsa yana kokarin samawa kansa nutsuwa.. Yagaza furta komai... 
Gyara zama ammie tayi taci gaba da cewa "ka bar ta ahannuna zuwa wani lokaci, zanyi kokari ganin na fahimtar da ita wasu abubuwan, har zuwa lokacin da zan sanar da ita kaidin mijinta ne cikin hikima zanyi mata bayani.... "
Dago da idanunsa yayi ya dubi ammie yadan jinjina kai ta re da kakaro murmishi, "shknan ammie duk yadda kikace ".. 
Cikin jindaɗin kalamansa tace"bari nasaka akirama ita dan tun da na tasheta tayi sallah takoma bacci ko kari batayi ba ".. 

"No kyaleta kawai ammie, ".. 
Yayi maganar yana mikewa tsaye dan bakaramin haushinta yakeji ayanxu ba... 
Sallama yayiwa ammie.. Yana kokarin ficewa daga falon deenah ta fito... 
Kallonta yayi ya watsar, ya fice, duk da tatsorata da ganinsa hakan bai hanata bin bayansa da harara ba... 

Karasowa tayi afalon ta gaishe da ammie, cikin kulawa da kuma janyota ajiki ammie ta amsa kafin ta ce "kije ki karya ga abun karinki can adining".. 
Cikin jindaɗin yadda ammie ke tarairayarta ta amsa da "to"
Tare da isa dining table din.. 

**
Nafisa duk wani shiri dayakamata tayiwa jikinta tayi sakon hajjah ma tuni ya iso mata, dan haka ayau taci burin nunawa yallabai tsagwaron soyayya dabai taɓa jin irinsa ba... 
Tun yamma ta caɓa uban adonta cikin wani leshi mai kalar jinin kare dinkin riga da skirt da ya matseta sosai, dan ita din dabiar tace saka matsattsun kaya... Har sallar magrib IG bai shigo gidanba gashi takira wayar sa is busy..., bayan sallar magrib ne ta kuma fesa wani wankan na atamfa mai kalar kore da white tayi shima dinkin riga da skirt ne, har lokacin ba taga shigowarsa ba... 
Bayan sungama dinner ne tana zaune afalo itakadai tana kallo, su sultan kuwa dakinsu suka koma dan har yanxu sun gaza sabawa da nafisa dan ko kusa bata sakarmusu fuska wannan dalilin  yasa suke  ɗari² da ita,.. 
Takai almost 35mins zaune tana jiransa, cikin damuwa mai tattare  da kewarsa ta cigaba da kiran wayarsa saidai har lokacin bata samuba, sako ta tura masa  _honey idan ka bude wayar ka kirani, tun dazu nake jiranka_
Saida ta tabbatar sakon ya isa sannan ta tashi ta shige sashinta tasauya kayanta zuwa na bacci ta haye gado tayi lamoo, babu abinda take tunowa sai zazzafar soyayyar da sir AJ ya dandana mata, ko ahaka tasan cikakken namiji take aure.. 
Lumshe idanu tayi ta janyo pillowcta rungume, wani irin feelings ke tasomata ahaka har bacci yadauketa... 
Sai 2:19am ta farka, cikin magagin bacci ta lalubo wayarta bayan ta dubi agogo, har yanxu bai kirata ba.. 
Ziro kafafunta tayi daga kan gado ta saka silapas ta nufi sashinsa.. Murda handle din kofar tayi taga ta buɗe hakan yatabbatar mata da yadawo kutsa kanta tayi har bedroom dinsa akan sallaya ta iskesa yagama sallah yana karatun alkur ani  ahankali sauti kasa².. 
Zama tayi agefen gadonsa tare da nade hannayenta akirji har ya kammala karatun ya dora da addu o'insa da ya saba.. 
Saida ya shafa adduar ne kafin ya dubeta "me ya fito da ke cikin darennan,? "

"Laifi nayi dan nazo turakar mijina,? Inada dama dazanzo inda kake akowane lokaci naso, anya honey kana tausayina kuwa".. 
Ta idar da maganar cikin shagwaba da kalar tausayi.. 
Sauke ajiyar zuciya yayi, ya fahimceta sosai, shikansa bayason ubangiji yakamasa da laifin danne hakkin matansa,.. 

Zama yayi agefenta tayadda har numfashinsu  yake gauraya, kamo hannunta yayi cikin sigar rarrashi ya soma magana "Im sorry bana nufin cutar dake, ban aureki dan  hakanba.. '

Cikin jin dadin kalamansa ta yiwa  kanta masauki afaffadan kirjinsa lokaci guda ta shaki ssnyayen turaren dake jikinsa.. "Shknan ya wuce honey, but ina kaje wayarka bata shiga kuma bakadawo da wuriba har sako na tura maka... "
Tayi maganar tana  dago dakanta still tana ajikinsa... 
AJ kuwa tunda suka samu body contact da nafisa, wani irin abu yasoma fizgarsa, tamkar mai shirin ficewa daga nutsuwar ga wani irin salo da ta farayimasa da ya gagara taka mata burki.. 
Cikin sarkewar murya ya bata amsa da cewa "ta kofar baya.. Na. Shigo... Wayata. Akashe take... "
Iyakar abinda ya iya faɗa kenan yaji harshen nafisa abakinsa... 
Cikin wani irin salo take tsotsar harshensa da softlips dinsa... 
Tamkar jira yake yasoma mayar mata da martani, zazzafar soyayya ta cigaba da wakana atsskaninsu, awannan dare sai Sir AJ ya tabbatarwa da nafisa shidin cikakken gwarzon namiji ne dan baiyimata da wasa ba, saida tasha wahalarsa sosai.. Bai kyaletaba saida yasamawa kansa nutsuwa, kafin baki dayansu du samu bacci... 
Da asuba ya tashi as usual, raba nafisa yayi dake manne akirjinsa ya tashi zaune tare da dagulewar zuciya.. yasamu gamsuwa babu laifi atare da ita saidai ayadda ya jita, sam bata tare da budurcinta, dan ba 'a budurwa ya sametaba, ba kamar jalila ba da saida yaci wahala kafin ya santa ya mace... 
Sauke nannauyar ajiyar zuciya yayi yana dubanta, sai baccinta take cikin nutsuwa.. Dauke idanunsa yayi akanta yana kokarin kore duk wani tunani dake bijiromasa abrain dinsa, amma tabbas yasan ba budurwa yasametaba.. 
Cikin wani irin yanayi marar daɗi ya wuce toilet ya tsarkake jikinsa ya dauro alwala ya fice zuwa  masallaci... 
saida gari yayi haske sosai yadawo, har dawo kuma bata tashiba.. Dubanta yayi ya dubi agogo data nuna 7:47am.. 
Watsar da ita yayi ya shirya ya wuce office.... 
Har zuwa wannan lokacin bai nunawa nafisa komai ba, baikuma gaza wurin sauke haqqinta ba, datake kulafuci akansa, wani lokacin har mamakinta yake yadda take nuna zalamarta akansa.. 
A irin son da ya tsinci kansa yanayimata babu abinda ya ragu sai ma karuwa da yayi dan zuwa yanxu bayason yaga ta bata fuska ta dalilin bata mata rai kullum cikin kokarin sakata farinciki yake da tarairaya da zalkar soyayya.. 
Cikin kankanin lokaci nafisa ta kuma murjewa, babu abinda ke damunta, batun deenah kuwa ta riga da ta gogeta akwanyarta tun ranar da tabar gidan, taba da tabbacin mai yuwuwa ya saketa, dan shikansa bai taɓa yimata maganar deenah ba, itama bata taba tuntubarsa ba... Allah sauki tsiya lafiya "
Abinda take fada kenan aduk lokacin da ta tuno da deenah.. IG kuwa yasakar mata kuɗi sosai akan tayi duk business din da takeso, hakan yasa  jin kanta da dagawa ya kuma bunƙasa, jin kanta take awata tsiyar.. 

Bayan Watanni biyu nafisa taje jumeta, itada direbanta da shine yakaita a cikin dalleliyar motar da IG ya siya mata.. Saida tayiwa su hajjah da momy uwar siyayya ta tsaraba ta ware tasu daban, sai ta sauran jamaar gida...
Sosai su hajjah sukaji dadin ganin nafisa da yadda ta sauya lokaci daya kamar ba itaba, basu gama rudewa ba saida suka ga uwar siyayyar da tayimusu da kuɗi masu yawa da IG yace aka womusu tare da isar da gaisuwarsa garesu kafin ya zo... 

Haka nafisa ta dinga rabar da kudi agidan, babu babbah ba yaro, hatta samarin gidan da yan mata sun shaida da zuwanta  cikin son neman suna da ayaba mata, takeyin komai, so take ta gwadawa yan gidan irin matsayin da take kai yanxu, musamman wadanda ko kusa basu so aurenta da IG ba... Baffanninta ma bata barsu abayaba wurin basu nasu hasafin, saida tayi kwana biyu cur ajumeta kafin su juya zuwa kt, bayan makudan kudin da ta baiwa hajja akan sabon aikin da zaa kumayimata akan IG, kuma har ta gama zamanta ajumeta bataje ta gaisheda hajia innah ba, baffah hamza ma saidai labarin zuwanta yaji har ta koma dan ko sashinsa bataje ta gaishe da iyalansa ba, mujaheed ma abun yayimasa ciwo matuka, bakidayansu suna falon hajiya innah suna tattauna zancen cikin jin zafin yadda nafisa bata daukesu da kima ba.. 
Mujaheed yayi yunkurin kiran AJ yasanar da shi, baffah hamza ya hanasa.. 
Hajiya innah tayi murmishi irinnasu na manya ta dubi mujaheed yadda yaketa huci ", malam mujaheedu menene na damuwa haka? Aikowa yasiyi rariya yasan zata zubar da ruwa, dan allah kubar zancennan haka, karku dago da wani hayaniya, nifa ba gaisuwarta nake jira ba, idan bata gaisheni ba ai iyayenta sun gaisheni, abar maganar haka karku hadata da mijinta "... 
Shiru kowa yayi cikin zafin rai, tunda hajiya innah ta nuna bataso suka kauda komai aransu, amma sun tabbatar da gayya nafisa taki zuwa gaishe da innah "... 

Satin deenah uku  cikin na huɗu awajen  ammie  ta saki jikinta sosai da wata irin shakuwa da tashiga tsakaninsu da ammie, gashi lokaci daya ammie ta saka mata son karatu sosai, dan yanzu deenah har yawan kallace² ta rage, ita kanta batada wani isasshen lokaci nayin hakan, daga boko sai tahfiz da ake koyamata anan gida, zuwa yanxu ta farayin harda, duk bayan sallar asuba sai ammie ta sakata tayi mura ji'a kafin ta koma bacci... Akankanin lokaci tagama fahimtar sauyi atattare da deenah da nutsuwa data soma shigarta, tarage rawar kai da shiririta, sai yanxu ammie ta fahimci ba komai ne ya haskaka kwanyarta da saka mata nutsuwa a zuciyartaba, face falalar karatun alkur ani mai girma musamman yadda ta himmantu wurin yin hardarsa, lallai alkur'ani haskene dake haskaka rayuwa da ga tsananin duhu zuwa haske, kuma waraka ne, shiriya ga mutane, falalarsa ta wuce duk yadda kake tunani, saidai muyi fatan ubangiji ya shiryar da mu da zuriarmu atafarki na gaskiya da tsarkin alkur'ani mai girma... 
Zuwa yanxu deenah ta soma mantawa da wata rayuwar gidan AJ, shikansa mantasa takeyi sai idan taji labarin yazo gaishe da ammie idan zai wuce office, kassncewar alokacin bacci take komawa, shiyasa basu fiye haduwa ba.. Shikuwa AJ kullum sai ammie ta fada masa irin yadda deenah ta sauya, yadan samu nutsuwa da hakan.. Haka kawai yaji shi kansa yanaso ya ganta, tamkar dai yana kewarta, itama ammie ta lura da hakan, dan haka ta dauki alkawarin duk lokacin da ya kuma dawowa saitakira masa matarsa ya ganta... 

***
Deenah ce kwance akan gado, tunda suka gama karatun tahfiz ta dawo daki ta kwanta, tun jiya takejin jikinta babu daɗi gashi batajin dadin komai taci ga wani irin ciwo da kasan cikinta (mararta)  keyimata , musamman yau abun yafi tsananta mata, dunkulewa tayi cikin bargo hannayenta duka dafe amararta sai faman juyi take tana cije lebe tare da matso kwallah tana shirin kuka dan ko kadan bata da hakurin ciwo... 
Tana cikin wannan halin ammie tashigo dakin tare da sallama, dan duk atunaninta deenah bacci takeyi, tashigo ne ta tasheta tayi sallar azahar sannan taci abinci... 
Ganin halin da deenah ke ciki yasaka ammie saurin karasawa tare da yaye bargon da ta rufe jikinta dashi... 

Adunkule ta ga deenah hannayenta dafe da mararta sai mukurkusu take yi tana kuka. Dago ta ammie tayi dasauri ta rungumeta tana tambayarta lafiya... 
Saidai tunkafin ta rufe bakinta taga amsar tambayarta, jinine tagani a inda ta daga deenah, ga wani dan kaɗan daya fara biyo kafarta... Hakan ya tabbatarwa da ammie al adace deenah ta fara...

Ganin jini da deenah tayi ajikknta ya sakata saka wani sabon kuka mai tattare da firgici  tare da aza hannu aka cikin radadin ciwon marar dake addabarta take ihu "wayyo allah na shiga uku, menene wannan?  ciwo naji ne ammie? '"
Arude take tambayar ammien, duk tabi ta rirriketa... 
Ammie kuwa rarrashinta tashigayi "ba ciwo kikaji ba, tashi muje bayi".... 
Tayi maganar tana dago deenah, ta fahimci madeenah batassn ko jinin meye ya zomataba, batasan cewa matakin girmane ta fara takawa ba...
Sosai ammie ta taimakamata ta wanke jikinta da ya ɓaci ta riko ta ta maidota kan gadonta ta taimakamata ta kwanta har lokacin deenah batadaina murkususu ba, duk tabi ta rirrike ammie... Dakyar ammie ta samu takira family doctor dinsu tayimasa bayani.. Cikin kankanin lokaci likitan yazo, direct dakin da deenah take ya shigo kamar yadda ammie ta basa umurni... 
Allurai yayiwa deenah da bata magunguna tasha... Ahankali tasoma jin ciwon na raguwa, kafin wani lokaci bacci mai nauyi yayi awon gaba da ita...
Bayan fitar likitan ne ammie ta dan kara gyara mata kwanciyarta ta kuma rufeta da blanket, zama tayi gefen ta tana duban fuskar deenah da baccinta yasoma nauyi, sauke ajiyar zuciya  kawai ammie keyi ita kanta a yan kwanakinnan ta fahimci yadda yanayin jikin deenah daya sauya, da girma da tadan karayimata, sauke ajiyar zuciya  takumayi, aranta tana fatan allah yabaiwa deenah lafiya... 
Fitowa tayi daga dakin ta baro ta tare da janyomata kofa.. 

Sai bayan sallar laasar deenah ta farka da hakan yayi daidai da shigowar ammie dan ta kuma duba jikinta.. 
Cikin kulawa ammie ta ida karasowa bakin gadon ta taimakawa deenah ta tashi zaune tare da tambayarta yadda takejin jikinnata... 

"Naji cikin yadainamin ciwo, wani abu dai nakejin yana zubomin kamar ina fitsari, nikuma bana fitsarin kwance.... "
Cike da damuwa ta idarwa yiwa ammie bayani... 

Murmishi ammie tayi mai sauti tasoma magana 'ba fitsari nane kikayi, nikam a islamiyya malam bai taɓa faɗamuku, idan mace tafara girma tana zubar da jinin al ada ba? "... 

Shiruu deenah tayi cikin son gane jawabin ammie kafin ta dubeta tace "ya fadamana, yace duk wata² akeyinsa, amma wai ba duka mata ba "... 

"Yawwa deenah, to shi ne kika farayi yanxu, kinga kenan kema kin girma "... 
Cewar ammie tana riko hannun deenah.. 
Deenah kuwa kara wa manyan idanunta girma tayi cikin mamaki ta dubi ammie tace "nima na girma kenan, ba zaa kuma cemin yarinya ba ko? ".. 
Wannan lokacin dariya ammie tayi tare da gyada mata kai alamar eh.. 
Kafin ta shiga toilet dakanta ta hadawa deenah ruwan dumi tayi wanka, bayan ta kammala ne ammie ta bata pad bayan ta gwada mata yadda ake amfani da ita... 
Bayan deenah ta fito ne doguwar riga marar nauyi ta kanti tasaka ta zauna abakin gado, sam batajin karfin jikinta tamkar anyimata dukan tsiya takeji.. 
Ko abinci ma gaza ci tayi dan batajin dadin komai abakinta... 
Kakkauran tea ammie ta hada mata shima half cup tasha ta ajiye.. 
Ammie kuwa batayi kasa aguiwaba haka tadinga yiwa deenah bayanu akan jinin al ada da mats alolinsa da yadda akeyin wankansa... Sosai takaramata haske akan sanin ta, takuma fadamata idan takumajin wani sauyi ta sanar da ita... 


***
IG ne zaune a main falo Shida yaransa, yayinda hankalinsu bakidaya na akan tv, shikuwa time by time yakan dubi agogon dake hannunsa yana furzan da zazzafar iska abakinsa... Kallo daya zakayimasa kassn cikin tsananin bacin rai yake, ta yadda kwayar idanunsa ta gaza boye hakan ta hanyar sauya launinta... Ya soma kosawa da rainin hanklin nafisa na fita duk lokacin da taga dama batare da izininsa ba, kyaleta da yayi hakan ba ya na nufin shi nusari, zai dauki mataki sosai akan dan baya ciki da guntun iskancin da ta bijiro dashi.. 
Shigowar su ita da drivernta yasanya IGP kallonsu lokaci daya ya watsar dasu, already ya sanarwa gate keeper idan sun dawo har driven su sameshi afalo... 
Sarai nafisa ta fahimci cikin yanayin da yake, basar dashi tayi ta cire siririn mayafin da ke kafadar ta takaraso falon tare da zama kan kujera cikin gajiyar da ta kwaso. 

Mikewa tsaye IG yayi ya nufi inda driver yake tsaye yana jiran umurnin megidannasa.. 
Hannu IG ya mika masa tare da furta "bani key din motar! "
Cikin tsorata da yanayinsa ya ciro key din daga aljihunsa ya mika masa... 
Da ido kawai IG ya kallesa, drivern ya fice daga falon da sauri..  
maido da dubansa yayi ga su iftahal yace suje dakinsu suyi kallon.. 
Bayan sunbar falon ne ya dubi nafisa da zuwa lokacin ta haɗe fuskanta dan ta fahimci masifa zaiyi mata.. 

"Daga ina kike? "... 
Ya jefo mata tambayar fuskarsa adaure.. 
Gyara zamanta tayi  tana dubansa amarairaice "honey tuhuma ta kakeyi... "
"Nace daga gidan ubanwa kike!!! "Cikin tsawa mai tattare da zallar bacin rai yake kuma tambayarta... 
Nafisa kuwa saida hantar cikinta takuma kaɗawa.. 
Cikin karfin hali tasoma magana "amma honey kasan bazanje inda bai kamata ba, ko kana tunanin zan wofintar da kainane "... 

Wani iri huci ya keyi ya koma ya zauna  yana kokarin saita kansa, "kada ki kuma fusata ni, and let me tell uh something, ni ba sakarai bane da zaki maidani hotuho kiyita yawo agari kamar tunkiya, sannan kuma babu damar na tambayeki inda kikaje kifara min kuka bla bla bla... Ki saurareni kiji bawai kinfi karfina bane yasa nake kyaleki, ina kokarin saita fushina ne dan banaso aiki da nadama, as from today ban yarda ko harabar gidannan ki kuma fita ba, if not that, zaki hadu da bacin raina, zaki kuma san asalin kalata.. Uh hear me!? 
Yayi maganar yana tsattsareta da jajayen idanunsa, nafisa da takuma daskarewa da jin kalamansa ta gyada masa kai  cikin karfin hali batare da ta dago da idanunta ta dubeshi ba... 

Tsaki yaja ya tashi ya haye upstairs ya nufi sashinsa... 
Sai alokacin ta sauke ajiyar zuciya, itakanta tasan tafara wuce makaɗi da rawa, tasan kuma halinsa sarai duk da ayanxu atsakiyar tafin hannunta yake amma bai lamuntar daukar raini, bai yadda ta rainasa ba, kuma bai yadda ta juyasaba yadda takesoba, aikinsu dai yayi tasiri amma wannan dabiar ajininsa take... 
Washe gari sai 11:00am yagama shirinsa cikin wani boyel ruwan kasa kasancewar ranar weekend ce... 
Yana bakin mirror ya gama saka wristwatch dinsa nafisa tashigo tare da sallama... 
Batare da ya dubi inda take ba ya amsa yana cigaba da shirinsa, ji yayi ta rungomosa ta baya tare da kwantar da kanta agadon bayansa.. Cikin salon bariki tasoma basa hakuri harda hawayenta, sosai takeso ya yarda da ita akan bazata kumayin abinda baya so ba... 
Saida ya gama kafe hular sa akansa kafin ya saka hannunsa ya juyo da ita yana dubanta... 
Awannn lokacin kawai ya tsinci kansa da jin tausayin ta, hannu yassnya ya tallabo face dinta "is ok na hakura dear, but uh have to take care".. 
Yayi maganar yana goge kwallar munafurcin da ta matso.. 
Fadawa jikinsa tayi ta rungumesa kafin ta dago kai ta mnna masa peck a wuya... 
Atare suka fito har babban falo tarakosa tare da bada sakon gaisuwarta agun ammie dan ta tabbatar can zai tafi, ssi kace daman ta damu da ammien... 
Ta window take kallonsa har driver ya bude masa mazaunin baya ya zauna sannan ya shiga yaja motar gate keeper ya bude musu gate suka fuce... 
Ajiyar zuciya tasauke tare da tabe baki, tasauke labulen takoma sashinta.... 

11:26am ya isa gidan... 
Bayan ya gama gaishe da ammie ne, take sanar masa deenah bata  lafiya.. 
Cikin kulawa ya ke duban ammie da son jin yadda yanayin jikin deenar yake, yake tambayar ammie "subhanallah meke damunta ammie, ankira doctor ya dubata? "
Tambayoyine yakeyiwa ammie masu dauke da kulawa da lamarin deenah.... 
Murmishi ammie tayi cikin nazartar yanayin dan nata tace "na kira likita yayimata duk abinda ya dace ajiya, amma taji sauki sosai, saidai rashin kuzari, ka tashi kaje ka dubata ".. 
Cikkn umurtarsa ta idar da zancen.. 
Kamar jira yake Tabasa umurni ya mike ya nufi inda zai sadasa da dakin ta. 

Tarar da ita yayi tana bacci, duk da baiga fuskanta ba dan ko ina najikinta tarufe da bargo... 
Karasowa yayi yakashe tvn dake aiki itakadai da dukkan alamu kallo ta gama baccin yadauketa... 
Bakin gadon ya karaso cikin nutsuwa yazauna yasaka hannu yadan yaye bargon ta yadda zai ga fuskarta, babu laifi tadanyi rama afuska dogon hancin ta ya kara fitowa sosai, kurmin zagayayyun manyan idanunta yadan fada.. Haka kawai yaga takara masa kyau ahaka... Shafa fuskanta yayi tare da dafa goshinta dan yaji ko zazzabine ke damunta tunda ammie bata fadamasa abinda ke damunta ba... 
Karaf deenah ta ware idanunta jin hannun mutum agoshinta, zumbur tayi ta mike zaune cikn tsoron ganinsa kamar daga sama, sai mazurai takeyi... 

Fuskarsa ba yabo ba fallasa ya dubeta atakaice ya tambeyeta "ya jikinnaki ".. 
"Naji sauki"
Ta basa amsa atsorace... 
Kallonta yakumayi ya dauke kansa na dan wani lokaci kafin yasake dubanta "meye haka, dallah saki jikinki dodo kikagani ne kikemin mazurai ".. 
Ya idar da maganar yana jifarta da harara dan ya fahimci kamar tana tsoronsa... 
Tsuke bakinta deenah tayi tagaza cewa komai sai kyafce²n idanu da takeyi da yasoma kawo ruwa, itadai har ga allah har yanxuy tsoronsa takeyi... 
"Fadamin meke damunki ".. 
Narai² tayi da idanu, sosai take tsoron yimasa karya, duk da ammie tace kada ta fadawa kowa, haka ta kwashe tafadamasa komai,.. 
Sosai ta basa dariya amma ya danne, kallonta yayi sama da kasa shikansa yaga  ta sauya masa Har tsawo ta karayimasa.. 
Cikin tsare gida yakuma cewa "yanxu marar ta daina ciwo ko kuwa har yanxu".. 

"Tabari "..
Ta basa amsa tana share kwallah... 
Gyara zamanta yayi sosai yana dubanta kafin yakira sunanta "madeenah namiki wani abu ne kike kuka? ".. 
Yayi tambayar yana tsareta da idanu.. 
Saurin girgiza kai tayi alamar  "a'ah".. 

"To kukan me kikeyi "... 
"Bakomai".. 
Ta basa amsa tana dago da kanta tanadubansa da shidin ita yake kallo cikin rawar murya tacigaba da cewa "amma kai ba dan mafiya bane ba ko? ""... 
Ta jefomasa tambayar, murmishi ya subucemasa shi har ya manta da wannan zancen, itakuwa abun na ranta, wannan dalilin ne yasa take tsoron sa kenan... 
Dubanta yayi sosai yakira sunanta itama ta dubeshi cikin ido "ni ba dan mafiya bane, kada ki kuma cemin haka, namiki baraza na ne rannan dan nasan halinki, ko namiki kama da mugu ne? "... 

Saurin girgiza kai tayi.. 

"To kidaina kallona amatsayin hakan "//
Ta kuma jinjina masa kai alamar to... 
"Kinci abinci ".. 
Ya tambayeta.
"Eh"

"Kadan ko dayawa, karkimin karya"... 

Cikin son gamsar dashi tabasa amsa da cewa "naci sosai "... 

"Good"
Ya fada tare da mikewa tsaye yana duba wristwatch din dake atsintsiyar hannunsa.. Kafin ya maido da dubansa gareta "allah yakara sauki "//
Bai tsaya jin amsarta ba ya fice.. 
Bayansa tabi da kallo har ya fice.. Sannan tarafka uban tagumi, sosai tayi mamakin yadda yake raina mata hankali har ya dasa mata tsoronsa a zuciyarta, koma miye itadai yanxu ta girma bazata kuma bari ya rainata ba... Dole ne ta nunamasa banbancin da da yanxu... 

Koda IGP ya fito daga dakin deenah sallama yayiwa ammie agaggauce dan akwai wasu documents dazai dauko a office... 
Ahanya yake dariyar deenah aduk lokacin da ya tuno abinda ta fadamasa, time by time yake girgiza kai yana murmishi shi kadai, shi yanxu yarinyar ma wlh dariya take basa, shikam yaushene madeenah zatabyi rayuwa irin ta mutane masu cikakken hankali da nutsuwa, bakidaya tsoronsa datakeji duk ya ida birkitamata kwanya... 

Sauke ajiyar zuciya yayi yana kallon gefen hanya... Abubuwa da dama yake tunawa game da deenah da rayuwarsu tabaya. 

***** 
Kwanaki, satittika, watanni duk sunja, cikin iko da qudurar ubangiji da kuma tallafin rayuwar da ya aramana, deenah sosai ta murmure ta kuma san yadda  zata dinga kula da kanta aduk lokacin da period dinta yayi, dan sosai ammie ta zage gun nunamata yadda zata dinga kula da kanta, sam bata kallon deenah amtsayin surukarta tamkar yarta take jinta... Zuwa wannan lokacin kuwa karatu ya gama shiga jikin deenah dan yanzu a harda tana da hizif 22 akanta, bangaren boko kuwa suna shirin zana jarabawar kammala karamar secondary ne  (J S C E).. sosai tamayar da hankalinta akan karatu, itakanta batada lokacin kanta dan ammie batayi kasa aguiwa ba, wurin koyawa deenah abinci na zamani dana gargajiya har da na larabawa, cikin ikon allah lokaci kankani ta iya wasu abubuwan, amma ta dan ci wahala musamman afannin girkin larabawa, sosai ammie ke bata horo ta fannin girke girke... 
Bangaren su babah ma kuwa da suka fahimci deenah na wajen ammie, bata gidan mijinta, bai ce komai ba dan koda ammie batayimasa bayani ba  yasan bazatayi abinda bashi bane, dole saitanada dalili ko manufarta nayin hakan, dan macece mai tsantsar hankali hangen nesa da nutsuwa, ga uwa uba wayewa da sanin abinda takeyi.. Hakan yasa ko kusa babah bai damuba, yakumayi matukar farinciki ganin sauyi atattare da yar tasa, dan duk lokaci bayan lokaci ammie takansa driver yakai deenah gida tagaishe da babah d kuma goggo asabe.. 
Itakanta madeenah tasan ta sauya rayuwarta ta da da yanxu akwai banbanci matuka, sosai tarage shirmen ta da shiririta da wauta, wayewa da gogewa takuma shigarta, ga nutsuwa, da halin miskilanci na rashin son magana da yasoma shigarta, saidai duk da haka inka taɓo ta zafi ne da ita, sau da dama idan aka shiga gonarta bata da hakuri kamar zawo, wannan dabiar da ta gaza bari abun ajininta yake, shi ne kuma abinda ya banbantata da marigayiya aunty jalila... 
Lokaci daya wani irin girma ya tasowa deenah gadan gadan, idan ka ganta tamkar zatayi shekaru sha bakwai, alokacin du² shekaru shabiyar take, amma duk inda  ya mace takai deenah ta taka matsayin, itakanta ammie har mamakin irin wannan girman da deenah tayi na lokaci daya takeyi, ga wani irin fitinannen kyau da girman yazo mata dashi, sigarta ta ya mace takuma bayyana sosai, duk da bata da kiba, amma batada rama  dan ko ina ajikinta acike yake, tsayinta  adai dai yake ya kuma bi da yanayin jikinta, dukiyar fulaninta kuwa yayi daidai  da kirjinta bul bul  (masha allah,) kugunta kuwa daidai yake da jikinta, tanada asalin kyan fuska da sai yanxu ya kuma bayyana, doguwae fuskanta dauke take da manyan zagayayyun idanunta farare tas, dauke da adon zara zaran gashin  idanunta da ya kuma kawatar da golden eyes dinta, sai siririn hancinta da zaizo har kusada  karamin bakinta mai dauke da natural pink color lips, tana da yalwar kashin gira  da gashin kanta msi laushi ba baki sidik... 
Ta kowane fanni deenah ta amsa  sunanta na ya mace ta fannin kyau da zubi da tsarin halittar da ubangiji yayi mata... 
Ammie kuwa, tana lura sosai da deenah, dan yadda take fita makaranta tana tsoron samarin da suke kokarin kaiwa deenah camfa amakaranta ko a  agida.. 
Musamman idan taje gaishe da babah idan ta fito waje, idan suka dawo gida saita sanarwa ammie wani ya biyota yana sonta , haka ko a skull dinsu malamai dayawa na kawomata camfa, xuwa yanxu abun yafara damun ammie duk da tasa ankuma sanarwa malaman deenah matar aurece tayi hakanne ta hanyar yimusu hannunka mai sanda, itakanta zuwa yanxu  tana ganin  lokaci yayi da zata sanarwa  da deenah matsayinta na kasancewarta matsayin matar aure, ta bari har su kammala zana jarabawarsu sannan ta fada mata takuma mayar da ita dakin mijinta dan tasan dole deenah taji zancen wani iri... Duk da dai ammie ta lura har yanxu hankalin gogannata bai karkato kan deenah ba, shi har yanxu yarinya yake daukarta, kuma bashida damuwa dan ya tabbatar babu abinda deenah zata nema tarasa awurin ammie, musamman fannin kulawa, to itama bangaren deenah ta fahimci karatu ne sosai aranta dan tana yawan sanarwa ammie cewa  burinta bai wuce ta zama cikakkiyar lauya ba, shiyasa ta mayar da hankalinta sosai kan karatu... 

Deenah ce akitchen tana hada miyar gyada da busasshen kifi na tuwon shinkafar  da suka kammala ita da mai aikin ammie,(abincin rana)  dan yanxu ammie sosai take sakarwa deenah ragamar girki ba dan komai ba saidan tasan kan ta afannin girki.. 
Shigowa kitchen din ammie tayi kitchen din tare da sallama.. 

Saida deenah ta zuba  yankakken alayyahun da ta wanke acikin tukunya kafin ta juyo ta amsa sallamar ammie cikin kulawa... 

"Kin kammala ne "... 
Ammie ta tambayeta tana karasowa gun take girkin dan kamshi daya cika kitchen din... 

"Aah na kusa kammalawa ammie,".. 
Cewar deenah tana murmishi tana duban  ammie.. Dan ta fahimci yadda ammie ke kokarin yaba girkin nata tunkafin ta dan dana, dan deenah akwai son ayaba mata, ta tsani a gwasaleta... 

"Dakyau, idan kin kammala ki sameni afalo zamuyi magana ".. 
Cewar ammie 
Deenah ta amsa da "to shknan ".. 
Ficewa ammie tayi, deenah kuwa rage wutar gas din tayi, ta wanke hannunta, fitowa tayi daga kitchen din tasanarwa da ladidi taje ta duba miyar... 
Afalo deenah ta tarar da ammie zaune tana duba wata jarida... 
karasowa tayi tazauna  akujerar dake fuskantar ammie.. 

Cikin kulawa ammie ta ajiye jaridar da take dubawa, ta dubi deenah tace "zo yata zauna nan akusa dani"... 
Ta nuna mata kujerar datake zaune akai zaman mutum biyu... 

Batare da bata lokaci ba deenah ta zauna ainda ammie ta nuna mata tana fuskantarta, tana tsimayin jin abinda ammie zata fadamata... 
Itama ammie sosai ta fuskanceta ta danyi gyaran murya taci gaba da cewa "abinda zan fadamiki nasan zai girgizaki mai yuwuwa bazaimiki dadi ba, amma ko miye ki dauka qaddarar ki ce ahaka, itakuma qaddara arubuce take babu wanda ya isa ya sauya ta, saidai bansani ba ko kinada karfin zuciyar jure abinda zai fito daga bakina,, dan zakiji maganar wata iri ne sakamakon boye miki hakan da akayi tsawon shekara daya harda rabi"... 

Deenah data matsu taji abinda ammie ke kokarin sanar mata, rike hannun ammie tayi da hannayenta tasoma magana "dan allah ammie ki sanarmin menene, bakiji zuciyata yadda ta fara dukan tara² ba "... 

Murmishi ammie tadanyi ta nutse da kallon deenah kafin ta numfasa tace "yanxu idan nace anyimiki aure baki  taɓa sani ba   ya zaki dauki zancen "... 
Ta karashe zancen tana kokarin nazartar yanayin deenah..... 



Mommyn boy &Nasreen

Murmishi ammie tadanyi ta nutse da kallon deenah kafin ta numfasa tace "yanxu idan nace anyimiki aure baki  taɓa sani ba   ya zaki dauki zancen "... 
Ta karashe zancen tana kokarin nazartar yanayin deenah..... 

           *****
Nabeela ce zaune akayataccen falon nafisa, sai faman raba idanu takeyi tana kara karewa kayataccen falon kallo, tun ashigowarta amain gate takuma tabbatarwa  lallai kawarta ta dace da aljannar duniya... Tundaga ss  dake tsaron gidan da lafiyar ta  zuwa kayataccen muhallin da take rayuwa, shi ne ya kuma jan hankalin nabila... 
Ahankali ta tashi tsaye ta shiga kaida kawowa afalon tana karemasa kallo, adaidai lokacin ne  ta yi tozali da katon enlergement na hoton IGP cikin uniform dinsa na yan sanda, yanayin fuskarsa mai dauke da kwarjini kadai zaka kallah kasan cike yake da zallar kishin aikinsa da kishin kasa, murmishi sosai nabeela tayi dan hoton yayi matukar kayatar da ita,karasawa tayi gun hoton  hannu ta sanya ta shafi katon hoton, cikin wani irin yanayi take sauke ajiyar zuciya... 
Hakan yayi dai dai da fitowar nafisa dauke da katon tire da ta cikosa da snacks da lemummuka da ruwa na tarbar kawartata ... 
Batare da ta kawo komai aranta ba ta dubi nabila da take  kokarin komawa ta zauna ganin fitowar  ata, murmishi nafisa tayi ta ajiye mata snacks din a kan table ta koma tazauna... 
Da idanu  nabila keta kallonta har  saida nafisa ta magantu "wannan kallo kuwa na lafiya ne , ko na sauya miki ne "... 
Ta idar da maganar cikin takama dan tabbatar da hakan... 

Sai alokacin nabila ta tsiyayi lemu a cup ta sha sannan ta kuma duban nafisa cikin gogewa da bariki tace "sauyi ma babbah ma kuwa, kawata dubi yadda kika koma wata hamshakiya da ke, kina cikin naira  sai abinda kike so kike yi, ga auren miji mai babban mikami, wow gaskiya keta dabance ".... 
Cikin son bugun cikin ta ta idar da maganar... 

Nafisa kuwa jin yadda nabila tashiga kodata ya sanyata sakin  dariya kafin ta tsagaita tace "ke ma yakamata kiyi hobbasa adinga bada labarinki, bawai kibayar da na wani ba ... "

Murmishi nabila tayi tadauke idanunta akan nafisa ta mayar dashi kan hoton IG... "Zanyi aure mana amma sai na samu miji irinnaki sak, mai ji da kyau, matsayi da kuma dukiya, dan nima ban cire rai da ga samun tagomashi irinnakiba "... 

Tayi maganar tana mayar da dubanta ga nafisa dan ta ga yanayinta... 
"Da jan aiki mai tattare da wahala agabanki kuwa  "... 
Cewar nafisa tana dariya... 
"Menene abun wahala,? Ai babu wahala tunda har kema kika samu duk da tsula tsiyarki da kikeyi , nikam bazan fitar da rai ba, keda kika yi aurenma, maimakon ki yi zamanki da samun jajirtaccen namiji irin wannan, ki rungumi abunki, amma duk da haka baki daina bariki da mazan waje ba... Gaskiya kawata ke hadamamma ce "... 
Ta idar da zancen cikin sigar zolaya dan bataso ta fahimci magana ce ta faɗa mata... 

Tabe baki nafeesa tayi tana murmishi kafin tace "ai ba'a canjawa tuwo suna,ahaka kuma nake tafiyar da tako na,  kuma har abada dan iska dan iskane... "

"Saidai idan bai tuba ba "..
Cewar nabila suka ssheqe da dariya tare da tafawa... 
Fira suka shigayi sosai, anan nabila take tambayarta kishiyarta dan  bataji motsinta ba, 
Fadamata komai nafisa tayi ta dora da cewa, "shikansa nasan ya manta da wannan yarinyar, may be ma tana can tana fama da siyar da doya abakin titi "... 
Ta karashe zancen suna dariya... 
Haka sukayi nutsu cikin firar deenah.. 
Sai yamma likis nabila tayiwa nafisa sallama dan ba da shirin kwana tazoba, zatabi jirgin karfe shadaya na safe zuwa abuja... Akwai wanda zasu hadu acan... 
Sosai nafisa tayiwa kawartata alkhairi da kudi masu yawa acikin envelop da zasukai dubu dari.. 
Ita kanta nabila saida tayi mamakin wannan uban kudin da ta bata wai tasakawa motarta mai... 
Har parking area ta rakota, saida ta shige motarta ta yimata key gatekeeper ya budemata gate ta fice sannan nafisa ta koma ciki...

Ahanya kuwa nabila na driving amma sake² takeyi akan nafisa, gaskiya tasamu hutu  IGP kuwa ya sakar mata bakin aljihu,boka kuwa ya rike musu kan maciji suna wasa da bindinsa , dole nafisa ta saki jiki ta tsula iskanci son ranta... 
Cije lebenta nabila tayi, lallai nafisa gogagga ce, da ita ce tasamu damar ta da  nutsuwa zatayi ta zauna agidan aurenta duk barikinta tunda komai ta nema ta samu.... "
Sauke ajiyar zuciya nabila tayi tana cigaba da murza sitiyarin motarta, zuwa yanxu ta tabbatar jin zafin nafisa ne da kishinta ke dawainiya da ita, zata koyawa nafisa hankali, sai ta nunamata banbanci karami da babbah abariki saita kwace abinda take tutiya da shi ahannunta.... 

Kwafa nabila taja afili tana murmishin da ita kadai tasan manufarsa... 

            ***
Murmishi mai sauti deenah ta shigayi kafin lokaci daya ta saki hannun ammie tare da saka  dariyar da ta zomata, ammie bata taɓa zolayarta ba amma yau meyasa takejin daɗin  zolayarta.. 
Saida deenah tadan tsagaita ta dubi ammie da itama dubanta take tana murmishi tace "nikam ammie ko afilm da labarai irinna marubuta bantaba jin haka ba, nadai fi jin hakan atatsuniya like how za a daura maka aure baka sani ba? "... 
Cewar deenah tana duban ammie tare da jiran amsar da zata bata... 
Gyara zama ammie takumayi tasake duban deenah.... "Ban taba miki wasa ko zolaya ba deenah, kinsan hakan, shiyasa tun farko nace bansan ya zaki dauki zancenba, hakika hakan yafaru kuma akanki, na fadamiki qaddarar mutum ba bu yadda bata zuwa... "
Cikin rudewa da gamsuwa da zancen ammie deenah ta dafa kirjinta muryarta na rawa "tace nikuma ammie, to waye mijin nawa, kuma waye yamin auren bansani ba "... 
Ta idar da maganar idanunta suka sauya kala lokaci daya  tab da kwallah cikin firgici da tsoron abinda zai fito bakin ammien...

Kokarin daidaita nutsuwar deenah ta shigayi kafin ta fadamata duk abinda yafaru har kawowa yanxu.... "Na fadamiki hakanne saboda naga lokacin sanar dake hakan yayi, kuma bai kamata kina zama agidannan ba amaimakon zama dakinki.. Dan haka  akwanakinnan zan mayar da ke dakinki "... 
Deenah kuwa daskarewa tayi, ɗib ta nemi kwallar da tafara zubarwa ta rasa, jin komai takeyi tamkar amafarki, saidai tanada tabbacin afarke take, jin kwalwarta tayi tana kokarin watsewa, lissafinta da tunaninta yafara dagulewa... 
Gaza furta komai tayi sai bakinta   dake rawa da alama akwai abinda takeson fada amma batasan me zata ce ba, lokaci daya wani irin gumi yafara tsattsafo mata... 
Cikin rashin mafita ta tashi tabar falon ta doshi dakinta batare da tacewa ammie komai ba ... 
Ammie ma mikewa tsaye tayi cikin damuwa tabi deenah da idanu, ta tabbatar daman rana irin wannan ko badaɗe zatazo,... 
Komawa tayi tazauna asanyaye, aranta tana adduar allah yatakaita komai yazo da sauki... 

Deenah kuwa tana shiga dakinta  cikin sanyin jiki ta isa bakin gadon ta tazauna ko ina najikinta rawa yakeyi, ga wani irin bugawa da zuciyarta keyi da karfi... 
Sai awannan lokacin tasamu nasarar fashewa da wani irin kuka mai  tsuma zuciyar mai 
sauraro.. Meyasa zaayimata haka, shin batada yancin da zaa aurar da ita kamar sauran yaya, sai ahaka zaa aurar da ita kamar bata da kowa .. Kuma ga mutumin da baya sonta kullum burinsa bai wuce yadda zai sakata bakinciki ya kuntata rayuwarta ba .... Mijin auntyn ta shi ne zai zama mijinta, zata haɗa miji da aunty jalila, auntynta da takeji tamkar itace ta haifeta, matar da ta gama yimata gata aduniya... Sam mijin aunty jalila bai taɓa burgeta takowace siga ba hasali ma haushinsa takeji sosai aranta, kuma ace da shine zatayi rayuwa ta har abada? "... 
Fashewa takumayi da wani irin marayan kuka cikin tausayawa rayuwarta take rairo kukanta... 
Batada yadda zatayi ta nunawa ammie rashin son boyayyen auren da akayimata, dan ammie bata cancanci ta saka mata da kiyayyar danta ba, duba da alkhairin da tayi mata arayuwa, amma harga allah anshiga hakkinta "... 
Wani sabon kukan ne ya kuma barkemata... Saida tayi mai isarta kafin ta daina, toilet tashiga ta wanke fuskarta da tayi jajir ta dawo gefen gado tazauna sai faman sauke ajiyar zuciya takeyi har lokacin bata daina juya zancen ba... 

Aranar wuni zungur a daki tayisa ko abinci bata iya ci ba, ta gaza danne zuciyarta da saita kanta, sam ko yunwar ma bataji... 
Ita kanta ammie abun sai ya dameta dan haka da yamma ta kira IGP ta fadamasa komai... 

IGP da fitowar sa kenan daga wanka yasoma waya da ammie, shiru yayi cikin wani irin yanayi yagaza furta komai, haka kawai ya ji ya damu sosai akan halin da deenah take ciki... 

Jin shiru ne ya saka ammie cewa "Abdulmajid kana jina... "
Ajiyar zuciya yasauke ya amsa da "eh ammie inajinki".. 
"Kazo ka baiwa deenah baki, dan yanxu tana bukatar rarrashi da ban baki, kaine kuma yafi kamata da yayi hakan, koba komai ku samu daidaito da fahimtar juna atsakaninku.... ".. 
Shiru yakumayi yana juya maganar ammie kafin ya ce "toh shknan ganinan".. 
Sallama sukayi kowa ya ajiye wayar... 
Furzan da zazzafar iskar bakinsa yayi, ba zaice zao gaza baiwa deenah hakuri ba to amma taya zata sauraresa dan ya tabbatar idan akwai wanda takejima haushi aduniyar nan bayansa ya ke...kuma dole ne ya aiwatar da  hakan... 

Agaggauce ya shirya cikin kananun kaya dasuke kuma bayyana kyau da haibarsa..... 
Deenah kuwa tana zaune kan sallaya tana adhkar na yamma tun bayan da ta gama sallar laasar bata tashi dags inda take ba.. Lokaci zuwa lokaci take sharce kwallar dake zubomata.. Har ta kammala adhkar din bata tashi daga inda take ba, wani irin ciwo kanta keyimata kamar zai rabe gida biyu, bakomai bane ya haifar mata da hakan illah kukan da tasha da yunwa dake ƙwaƙularta ta  bayan fage, azahiri kuwa sam batajin dadin komai bare jin yunwa... 
Ahankali aka turo kofar  tun kafin yayi sallama  kamshin tirarensa ya daki hancinta, sallama yayi ya ida shigowa dakin kai tsaye... 
Deenah kuwa tunda takallesa da kumburarrun idanunta ta watsar.. Lokaci daya zuciyarta tafara kokarin tsinkewa... 
Duk da acikin katon hijab take amma ya fahimci yadda tayi rama  lokaci daya, idanunta da fuskanta duk sun kumbura alamun taci kuka har ta godewa allah... 
Wannan lokacin tsintar kansa yayi da jim matsanancin tausayin deenah... 
Karasowa yayi ya zauna agefen carpet din da take zaune.... 
Zubamata idanu yayi ya fahimci ko kallonsa bata kaunar yi ga wasu irin kwallah da suka shiga zarya a kumatunta da yakejin su har cikin ransa tamkar tana ɗiga masa darma azuciyarsa... 

Hankacif ya ciro daga aljihunsa ya mika mata cikin tsare gida yace "karɓa share hawayenki magana zamuyi"... 
Kallonsa tayi ta watsar cikin jin haushinsa kamar bazata karbaba, amma yadda yayimata wani irin kwarjini ayau, kawai taji tasaka hannu ta karba tashiga goge hawayenta... 
Shikansa yayi mamaki yau deenah ko musu batayimasa ba, zura mata fararen idanunsa yayi har ta gama goge fuskanta... 
Cikin kwantar da murya yasoma magana "na sani ni mai tarin laifi ne agareki, ayanxu nasan babu wanda kike jiwa haushi sama dani, an aura miki wanda bakyaso, wanda ya fara manyata ki ka tashi agabansa yasan kuruciyarki... Kuma mijin auntynki, Ba a barki da zabinkiba anmiki shiga sharo ba shanu, nasan irin wannan tunanin ne ke kaikawo azuciyarki,  well bazan hanaki jin wannan radadin aranki ba.. Amma ki sani komai yana da dalili, kuma iyayenki da masoyan ki bazasu taɓa yin abinda zai cutar da keba, domin har kullum gobenki suke duba.... 
Tunda yafara magana har ya kai karshe deenah bata kulasa ba kuma batadago kai ta kallesa ba dan wani irin tukuki da daci dake tasoma ta akarkashin zuciyarta...  
Ya lura da yanayinta sosai, gyara zaman sa yayi ta yadda numfashinsu yake gauraya da na juna.. Cikin bugawar zuciya da ya tsinci kansa aciki yasanya hannayensa ya riko hannayenta duka acikin nasa... Ahankali yake murza lallausan hannayenta masu laushi da ɗumi... 

Dasauri deenah ta dago da jajayen idanunta tana dubansa..jin hnnunsa acikin nata, Saidai kamar an dinke mata baki ta gaza cemasa komai. 
Kallon da take masa yabasa damar kallon atsakiyar kwayar idanunta.. 
Saurin dauke idanunta tayi daga nasa, wani irin mugun kwarjini yayimata bazata juri kalon tsakiyar idanunsaba... 

Shikuwa cigaba da kallon fuskanta yayi yanacewa "now tell me, iyayenki da masoyanki zaki watsawa kasa a idanu, alhali suna da kyakkyawan zato akanki".. 

Cikin son kwantar mata da hankali da shigar da nutsuwa azuciyarta yayi maganar.. 

Deenah kuwa kara dauke kanta tayi gefe tana matsar kwallah tare da kokarin zame hannayenta daga cikin nasa dayake murza, zara zaran yatsunta ahankali... 
Sakin hannayenta yayi lokaci daya yana sauke ajiyar zuciya. 

Cikin hikima ya shiga bata baki, dan tabbas tana bukatar rarrashi a irin wannan yanayin, duk da taki sakin jikinta bare ta kallesa dul haka saida ya tabbatar taci abincin da tagaza ci, ta daiki sakin ranta kuma taki takansa, tilas ya kyaleta, dan bayason yawan naci, kuma bai isa ya hanata jin zafinsa aranta may be hakan zaisa ta huce... 

Sai bayan fitarsa ne ta fashe da kukan jin haushin kanta da yadda yake wani  bata umurni tana bi batare da musu ba, kamar wani ubanta, sam tagaza yayyabamasa maganganu masu zafi da tanada dominsa... Haka kawai ta tsinci kanta da yimasa biyayya, harda wani samun damar rikemata hannu... 
Tsaki takumaja cike da takaici ta shige toilet ta watsa ruwa ko make up batayi ba ta saka atamfa dinkin riga da skirt ta fito falo.. Babu laifi ta danji karfin jikinta da tayi wanka taci abinci...
Saidai har yanxu zuciyarta acunkushe take... Lissafinta daɗa dagulewa yake idan ta tuna ita wai ahaka matar aure ce, ga wanda sam bata son zama inuwa daya dashi... 
Babu laifi a yan kwanakinnan ta saki ranta, badan komai ba saidan bataso ammie ta fahimcu kiyayyar da takewa danta, sosai tasaki jikknta , amma ita kadai tasan radadin da takeji azuciyarta ammie kuwa bakaramin daɗi tajiba yadda taga deenah ta ware... 
Deenah kuwa batashiga tashin hankali ba saida taji ammie na sanarwa da babah a waya nan da kwanaki goma zata koma dakinta, shima din dan akwai shirin da zatayimata na komawar shiyasa taja kwanakin, abangaren babah kuwa yayi naam da yadda ammie ta tsara duk da batasanar da shi dalilin jan kwanakinba, ya kuma sanarmata da kafin lokacin yanason ganin madeena dan akwai nasihohin da ya kamata yayimata.... 
Har suka karashe wayar deenah na akan  matattakalar bene tana shirin safkowa ne zuwa falo taji da wanda ammie ke waya yasa ta dakata tana sauraronsu har suka gama wayar... 
Gaza ida karasowa falono tayi, jikinta amatukar sanyaye ta koma dakinta,kai dakawowa deenah tashigayi cin rashin mafita... Wani irin yanayi takejin kanta marar daɗi... 

Numfashi taja ta furzar ahankali taji zuciyarta na mata saukin radadi kafin ta dauko alkur'ani tafara muraji'a domin ta samawa kanta nutsuwa... 

Ranar jumaa ammie tasaka driver yakai deenah gida dan ta gaishe da baban kamar yadda ammien ta fadamata, batasan cewa taji duk abinda suke fada awaya ba.. 
Zaune take agefen tabarmar da babah yake goggo asabe na kan kujera yar zukunne..
Tun kafin babah ya magantu deenah ta fara kuka mai  sauti "bansan laifin da na muku ba zaku aurar dani ga wanda bana so kuma ba tare da ansanar da ni ba,.. "
Tsawa babah ya daka mata daya tilastata haɗiye sauran maganar tana cigaba da kuka kasa²,. 
"Rufamin baki! Saboda mun isa dake shiyasa muka aurar dake ga wanda muke so idan kuma zaki nuna mana bamu isa da keba to saiki bijiremana, ke madina tsaya kiji koma me zakiyi bakin alkalami ya riga da ya bushe faqat! ".. 
Cikin fada ya idar da zancen... 
Sake rushewa da kuka madina tayi... 
Goggo kuwa cike da tausayinta take girgiza kai kafin ta kwantar da murya cikin sigar rarrashi tasona yiwa babah magana "malam abita a sannu dan allah, dole madina taji wani iri a lamarin... "

Mayar da dubansa ga goggo babah yayi yace "kinsan halin deenah, rarrashi baya yimata sai anyi baran baran da ita, taurin kai ne da ita...... 
Gyara zama yayi ya mayar da dubansa ga deenah sa take kuka kamar ta siƙe "to barikiji aduniya bakida gata sama da mijinki, domin farantamin amatsayin mahaifinki dole ki danni zuciyarki ki bata baki ki koyi rayuwa da mijinki kiyi masa biyayya, ki saka masa nutsuwa azuciyarsa, daidai da second daya banyarda ki saba ma umurninsa ba, ki zama me yaye masa damuwa ta yadda zaiyi alfahari dake kamar yadda yayi da yayarki alokacin da take da rai, madeenah idan kikamin haka kin biyani, kin kuma saka min da kyakkyawa amatssyina na mahaifinki, madeenah zanyi alfahari dake domin kin fitar dani kunya.... Sabanin haka kuwa zan zare hannuna daga dukkan lamurranki wannan shine karshen zance... ".. 

Saurin  kallon babah tayi da jajayen idanunta da har lokacin suke ambaliyar hawaye, bai jira abinda zai fito daga bakinta ba ya tashi yayi alwala ya fice masallaci sakamakon kiran sallar la asar da aka farayi... Shikansa babah zuciyar adagule ya fice, idan ba haka yayiwa madeenah bazata shiga  taitayintaba, dan yasan halinta sosai... 

Babah na fita deenah tasaka kanta atsakankanin cinyoyinta tana cigaba da kukanta... 
Rarrashinta goggo ta dingayi tana bata baki... 
dago da kanta tayi tana duban goggo cikin disasshiyar murya tasoma magana "walh goggo bana sonsa yamin tsufa gashi da watar matarma , kuma ma mijin aunty jalila shi ne amatsayin mijina? ".. 
Dafa kafaɗarta goggo tayi cikin kwantar da murya ta ce "to ai addini bai haramta hakan ba madina, kuma da kike zancen yamiki tsufa, matan da suka fiki aji da kyau wayewa neman irin wannan namijin suke amma basu samu ba, wlh Abdulmajeed bashida makusa ta ko ina ya amsa sunansa na "ɗa namiji"... Ki godewa allah da ya baki namiji kamarsa, wasu ma fa neman mijin auren suke, hallau basu samu ba, bare ke da kika samu asanga wannan ai shi ne auren gata, shawarar da zan baki ki rungumi mijinki hannu bibiyu domin ko babu sa hannunmu ubangiji ya kaddaro ke matarsa da haka bakida yadda zakiyi dan haka ki kwantar da hankalinki babu ruwanki da wata matarsa... "
Share hawayenta deenah tayi cikin damuwa tasoma magana "ji nake kamar mafarki nakeyi, zuciyata da kwalwata sungaza jure nauyin wannan zancen".. 
Gyara zama sosai goggo tayi tace "m al amarin allah yake zuwa ta yadda bawa ba ya tsammani, nidai fatana kiyi biyayya kibi kuma duk abinda mahaifinki ya fadamiki dan sosai ransa ya baci ayau..... "
Da irin wadannan kalamanne goggo ta ke tausar deenah, har ta fara samun nutsuwa...babah kuwa tunda ya fice zuwa masallaci bai dawo ba  
Sai yamma likis driver yaxo ya dauketa... 
Har kofar gida Goggo ta rakata tana kumayimata nasiha... 
Tun alokacin da aka saka ranar komawarta, ammie ke kokarin sake gyara deenah.. Duk da ba maganin mata ta ke bata ba, markadaddun yayan itatuwa ne da madara sai kunun aya da yaji ridi da kwakwa da wasu sinadaran 'yayan itatuwa da suke kara lafiya da tsattsafo da niimar ya mace, haka fannin gyaran jiki ma saida ta saka aka sake mata wani sabo, deenah ta dawo sabuwar amarya dal, zuwa yanxu sosai takejin nauyin ammie duk da bawani son dan nata takeyi ba, amma ji takeyi ammie ta kara mata wani kwarjini da nauyin ta da takeji yadda ta dage tana gyara ta... 
IGP kuwa tun daga lokacin da ya fahimci deenah tsantsar jin haushinsa take, ya daina shiga sabgarta badan komai ba saidan ya bata dama ta huce, kar yawan bibiyarta da bata baki yakara saka mata wata kiyayyar tasa, so yake ya kashe wutar da ke ruruwa a zuciyarta da ruwan sanyi (ko ba komai kunsan gogan naku bai san raini😹) 
Amma ankare yake da ita sosai, da dik wani motsinta, dan kullum sai yazo gun ammie akallah kusan so biyu sabanin da da yake zuwa sau daya, badan komai ba saidan saboda deenah, ya tsani ya ganta acikin damuwa ko kadan, yana ganin kamar yagaza da alkawarin da ya daukarwa jalila na sadaukar da soyayyarta ga deenah, tabbas yayimata tanadin tsagwaron soyayya da zai nuna mata, koda kuwa zatacigaba da nunamasa zallar kiyayya, wannan alkawarine na soyayya kuma saiya cikawa jalila shi... 

Deemah kuwa da ta fahimci yadda IG yake wani rawar kafar zuwa gidan ta mayar da kanta yar kulle sam ta daina zama falo ta koma zaman daki, sosai tashiga wasan yar buya dashi... 
Shikansa ya fahimci hakan, bai damu ba dan yasan duk guje gujennata agidansa zata dawo, zaiga karshen guduwa.... 


                ***

IGP ne tsaye a dressing mirror yana shirya kansa cikin shirin fita aiki... 
Dan juyowa yayi ya dubi nafisa dake kan gadonsa lullube da bargo  tana kokarin sake komawa bacci, dauke idanunsa yayi ya cigaba da shirinsa kafin yace "baza ki tashi kiyi sallah ba kinsan rana tayi ko? ".. 
Ya idar da maganar batare da ya dubeta ba still yana shirinsa... 

Mika takumayi dan taji abinda yafada ta bude idanunta tare da murmishi mai dauke da zazzafar kaunarsa adan shagwaɓe tace "honey duk agajiye nake jikina yamin tsami sosai, ina bukatar na dan huta"... 
Ta idar da maganar tana kuma narkewa acikin bargo... 
Ta mirror ya kalleta ya watsar, har yanxu nafisa taki sabawa da yin sallah akn lokaci duk kokarin sa akan hakan, duk da ya lura abaya sallar ma bata dametaba, amma yadda ya tasanmata tsaye ne yasanya takeyi shima idan yana nan.... 

"Inaso na fadamiki, gobe in allah yakaimu deenah zata dawo dakinta'... 
Ya karashi maganar yana ɓalle bottle din rigarsa... 

Babu shiri na fisa ta wartsake tare da tashi zaune cikin darsuwar muguwar faduwar gaba "honey kana nufin daman baka rabu da yarinyar nan ba? "... 
Sai alokacin ya juyo ya kalleta sosai ya kafin yace "daman na fadamiki na rabu da ita? '"

Yayi maganar yana tsareta da idanunsa... 
Cikin saita kanta ta kakaro murmishi tare da fadar, "no allah ya kawota lafiya ".. 
Taja bakinta ta tsuke tana kallonsa har ya gama shirinsa ya fice... 
Sauke nannauyar ajiyar zuciya tayi, yarinya kamar manya tabi ta kanainayemata miji, haka tasha fama da yayarta, da dukkan alamu ahalinsu suna da nasaba da mayu, tazo ta zauna agidan, yanxu me zaa fara buga karamin wasan, babu boka bare malam, taagwaron makircinta kadai zaisa tabar gidan da kafafunta.... 
Murmishi takumayi dan bata dauki deemah amatsayin macen da zatayi kishi kafada da kafada da itaba... Ta dade tana juya zancen azuciyarta tare da mamakin jin maganar kamar saukar aradu, baccin da bata iya komawa ba kenan ta tashi... 


Aranar da deenah zata koma tun safe ammie tasameta adakinta ta dinga yimata nasiha akan zamantakewar da hakuri da juna ta haka ne zata ci ribar rayuwar aurenta domin hakuri shi ne matakin nasarar kowace rayuwa, ire iren wadannan nasihohinne ammie ta dinga yiwa deenah... 

Deenah kuwa kuka tasaka marar sauti tare da fadawa jikin ammie ta rungumeta tana cigaba da rairo kukanta marar sauti, sosai takejin kewar ammie, gashi zata shiga rayuwar da kejinta amatsayin bakuwa... 
Itakanta ammie jikinta yadanyimata sanyi, ta shaku da deenah sosai, to amma rayuwar ya mace ta gaji haka.. Cikin karfin hali ta cigaba da tausarta da bata baki... 

Ladidi ta saka ta cigaba da hadawa deenah kayanta... 

IG kuwa yana dawowa daga office ko gida bai wuceba yayo gidan ammie... 
Itakanta ammie abinda yasa bata sanya driver yakai deenah ba ta fison idan mijinta yazo ya wuce da ita hakan zaifi... 
IG kuwa sam baiyi tsamannin hakan ba.. 
Bayan sallar magrib Haka ammie ta sanya deenah ta yi wanka ta shirya cikin wani rantsattsen leshi ruwan makuba dinkin riga da skirt da ya zauna das ajikinta ya kuma fito da surar ds ubangiji yayi ma ta... 
Katon mayafi ta yafa akan ta suka fito falo sai wani irin niimtaccen kamshi jikinta ke fitarwa, shikansa IG da ya kalleta saida yasake kallonta, tayi masa wani irin masifar kyan da ko kusa baita ba sanin tana dashi.. Wannan ne karo nafarko daya karemata kallo sosai... 
Deenah kuwa soke kanta tayi kasa tana cigaba da matsar kwallah... 
har parking area suka fito tare da ammie tanata yimusu fatan alkhairi da adduo i,driver ya saka katon akwatin deenah a booth.. Ammie ta budewa deenah mazaunin baya ta shiga taxauna.. 
IG kuwa godiya yashiga yiwa ammie tare da adduar allah ya kara girma, albarka ta shiga sanyamusu har ya shige motar ya zauna... 

Ahanya kuwa shiru ya shiga tsakaninsu bakajin motsin komai sai iskar AC din dake zagaye ko ina acikin motar, sai sautin kukan deenah dake  fita kasa².. 
Har cikin ransa yakejin kukan na taɓasa, banza yayi da ita ya kyaleta.. Har suka isa gidan tana kuka.. Saida dtiver ya daidaita parking din motar acikin jerin gwanen rantsattsun motocin dake aparking area... 
"Zaka iya   tafiya".. 
Cewar IG cikin umurtar siragent sa'eed  

"Ok sir ".. 
Ya fada yana kokarin ficewa acikin motar... 
Bayan ya fita ne deenah itama tafara kokarin balle murfin motar dan ta fita Dan bakidaya kasancewarta taredashi yafara takurata, tagaza sakin jikinta , saidai gam taji kofar alamun arufe take... 
Sai alokacin ta juyo ta iya kallonsa da rinannun idanunta muryarta a dishe tafara magana "ka bude min zan fita".... 

.

Banza yayi da ita kamar baiji abinda ta fadaba  still yana duban kumbararrun idanunta, dauke kansa yayi ya sauke ajiyar zuciya  kafin ya maido da dubansa gareta sannan yace "ke bakya gajiya da kuka ne, wannan kukan ya isa haka domin yafara cutar da ni, sai wani faman cin magani kike kina share ni... "
Ya idar da maganar yanayimata kallon up and down, yadda ta shiga kyafce kyafcen idanu tana kokarin dakatar da hawayenta ya basa dariya amma ya danne... 
Matsowa yayi sosai daf da ita yayi ƙasa da murya yana duban fuskanta yace "ya fimiki ki koya wa zuciyarki yadda zaki so ni dan ban aureki dan na rabu dake ba... "
Ya idar da maganar yana murmishi idanunsa sula sauka  siririyar sarkar dake kwance lib a dokin wuyanta, still batace masa komai ba bare ta kallesa, zuciyarta  kuwa sai faman bugawa take sa karfin gaske... 
"Ke Kalle ni nan".. 
Cikin bata umurni yayi maganar... Batada zabin da ya wuce aiwatar da hakan.. Ahankali ta dubesa... 
"Yi murmishi ".. 
Kamar wata sakara tadan yi yaƙe da saida fararen hakoranta suka bayyana tanacigaba da share hawayenta, bata san meyasa ba bata iya masa musu ko gardama, haka kawai takejin nauyin hakan... 
Shima murmishin yayi yana kallon tsakiyar kwayar idanunta kafin yace "good "... 
Yafada tare da bude murafen motar ya fice itama ta fito... 
Sauri tashigayi harda harde kafafuwa tana kokarin shigewa ya dakatar da ita, bisa dole saidai su jera atare suyi tafiya.... Ta lura kamar da mugunta yake sakata wasu abubuwan, dan yasan basan hakan take ba daman duk abinda bataso shi alokacin yake sonsa.. Amma ya ta iya da fadan da yafi karfinta.. Haka suka jero har zuwa babban falo da babu kowa aciki.. Key din sashinta ya mikamata, batare da ta kallesa ba ta karba ta haye upstairs ta nufi sashinnata... 
Da idanu ya bita harsaida ta bace masa kafin yasauke ajiyar zuciya... Komai nata irin na jalilarsa  ne, hatta tafiyarsu da yanayinta amma ta fuskar halayya kowa da tasa, jalila sanyin hali da hakuri gareta, deenah kuwa zafi ce ga tsiwa da rashin hakuri.... 
Yana fatan allah yasanya masa soyayyar deenah azuciyarsa ta yadda zai tattaleta kamar kwai, yakuma koyamata zazzafar soyayyarta, ta gane banbancin yaro da tsoho... Kamar yadda take kiransa... 
Murmishi mai sauti yayi, baibi takan nafisa ba ya shige sashinsa.. 
Deenah kuwa tana shiga sashinta fes tagansa ko ina tas angyara komai, cikin jin haushin kanta da abubuwan da takeyi a yan kwanakinnan ta zauna abakin gado, jinta take takoma wata sakara da yake wani bata umurni tana bi yamayar da ita kamar danƙon jani talau... Tsaki taja afili aranta kuwa, ta rantse bazata kuma bashi wata ɓaraka dazai yimata wani abu makamancin wannan ba,... Tsaki takumaja akaro na ba adadi ta cire mayafinta ta tashi ta bude sib ta fara arranging kayan sakarwata... 

Sultan ne ta shigo dakin shida iftahal tare da sallama, iftahal da gudu ta karasa gun da deenah take ta rungumeta... "Oyoyo little mom, munyi kewarki sosai naji dadi da kika dawo... "
Rufe sib din deenah tayi, cikin farincikin ganinsu ta dago da kan iftahal itama tana dariya.. Tun kafin ta magantu.. 
Sultan ya ce "little mom wai meyasa kika  tafi ne, ni fa ko sanin tafiyarki banyi ba, wlh da bakinan sam gidan babu daɗi, aunty nafisa kuwa bata fira damu, wani lokacin ma idan muka fito falo muna kallo sai tace mu koma dakinmu muyi kada mu dameta "... 
Sororo deenah tayi cikin jin zafin abinda sultan ke fadamata take kallonsa har ya karashe zancen... 
"Ashe daman batada mutunci, ku da gidanku amma ta hanaku sakin jikinku, daman daga ganin wannan matar feƙaƙƙace shiyasa ni sam bataminba ".. 
Cewar deenah tana taɓe baki kafin ta dubi sultan tace "ya akayi kuka san na dawo"... 
. "abbie ne yafadamana".. 
Cewar iftahal.. 
Tabe baki takumayi, batace dasu komai ba ta cigaba da aikinta, tayata  sukayi har ta kammala sannan su ka shiga fira, sai da dare sosai sukayimata saida safe.. Iftahal kuwa cewa yayi gun little mom zata kwana.. Hakan yayiwa deenah dadi domin tsoron kwana itakadai takeyi awannan tanƙamemen dakin ... 

                      ***
Washe gari bayan sun gama karyawa ne, baki dayansu yace su sameshi a falonsa... Nafisa ce ta fara zuwa, deenah kuwa saida ta kwashi kusan mintuna biyar kafin ta shigo falon bakinta dauke da sallama, still sanye take acikin hijab dan ko da zasuyi break da hijab dinta take... 
Shugowarta saida kirjin nafisa yakuma bugawa wani irin kishi yakuma rufeta dan duk da deenah acikin hijab  take ta fahimci yadda yarinyar tayi fresh da wani irin kyau mai daukar hankali... Da murmishin datake kokarin danne abinda ke ranta tabi deenah da kallo harta karaso, gogan kuwa tunda ya dubeta ya watsar ya mayar da dubansa ga TV,ya lura  wani sabon salone deenah ke dauke da shi na zama da hijab agida kamar me takaba sai ya taka mata burki, yaji abinda take ma kuminiya acikin hijab din... 
Sumi² deemah takaraso ta zauna... 
Sai alokacin ya dubesu bakidayansu, batare da ɓata lokaci ba ya shiga yimusu nasiha akan zamantakewar su, anan ne ya tsara yadda kwanakin kowace zai kasance ya kumaji ta bakinsu... Deenah kuwa ba baka sai kunne, dan batasan me zata fada ba, nafisa ce zaƙaƙura gun magana har suka tsaida kowace kwanakin aikinta zai kasance kwana bibbiyu... 
Duban deenah yayi yace "hakan yamiki? ".. 

Daga masa kai tayi alamar eh.. 
Dubanta yakumayi cikin nazari, sam tagaza sakin jikinta, ya fahimci yadda nutsuwa ta shigeta sosai... 
Cigaba yayi da yimusu nasiha  da fatan allah yabasu zaman lafiya mai dorewa... 
Washe gari da yamma likis , nafisa ce ta shigo har falon deenah.. Karewa falon kallo tayi sosai, komai na falon iri dayane da nata babu abinda aka banbanta.  Cikin jin zafin hakan tanemi gu ta zauna adaya daga cikin kujerun dake falon, ta tabbatar deenah na bedroom dinta, dan haka kawai tazauna tana jiran  fitowar ta... 
Deenah data fito daga wanka tagama shiryawa cikin wani australian lace dinkin doguwar riga... Duk da simple make up tayi amma tayi kyau sosai... 
Fitowarta afalo kenan taci karo da nafisa zaune afalonnata.. Saida kirjinta ya buga ta lura da tun sanda ta dawo nafisa ke kokarin janyota jikinta da daɗa shisshigemata a lamurranta haka kawai taji ta gaza yarda da ita... 
Karasowa deenah tayi tana kakaro murmishi tare da yimata brk da shigowa... 
Nafisa kuwa wani irin abu ne ya soki zuciyarta tafahimci ta kowani fanni deenah tayimata zarrah kyau da kuma diri, hakan yakuma darsar mata da tsanar ta fiye da da, lallai masu iya magana sunyi gaskiya da suke cewa dariya baso bace.. 
Amsawa nafisa tayi ta na danne abinda ke bijiromata aranta ta dubi deenah dake kokarin zama akujerar dake facing dinta tace "na lura sam bakya sakin jikinki dani, bare  muyi fira, kin fison ki kadai ce ke kadai, ko baki daukeni amatsayin  yayarki bane"... 
Cikin kulawar da take nunawa ta karashe zancen.. 
Deenah kuwa murmishi tayi tadubi nafisa da itama kallonta takeyi tace "ba haka bane kawai wani lokacin ina karatune shiyasa"... 
Daga kafadarta nafisa tayi alamun babu komai kafin ta gyara zama ta dora da cewa "ni kam me yasake dawowa da ke gidannan, bayan kuma allah ya taimakeki kin kubuta, da ni ce na samu damarki wlh babu abinda zai maidoni, kuma ki sani daga halayen yallabai babu abinda ya sauya sai ma daɗa kamari da yayi dan yanxu harda duka yakeyi, dazan buɗe miki bayana zaki tausayaminne na koma tamkar jakarsa, ba auren soja kadai ake shan duka ba har da na dan sanda.... "

Tunda tafara magana deenah ke kallonta tagama fahimtar nafisa raina mata wayau takeyi, tsab takaranci makircinta, so take ta daɗa cusa mata tsoro sa kiyayyar IG, anxo gurin idan bata cusa mata hakanba, ita zata cusa mata bakincikin da zata kwana tana jinyar zuciyarta, zata nuna mata itama da nata makircin irin na ya mace ... 
Azahiri kuwa deenah gyada kai tayi tace "aunty nifa ba barmasa gidansa nayi ba, yasamamin wani gu ne wanda zan huta ya maidoni na dan wani lokaci, kuma yana matukar kulawa da ni , ssi naji kamar ya mayar dani acan yana bani lokacinsa da kulawarsa sosai may be kece kike shan wahalarsa, amma kuwa auren soyayya kukayi aunty"? 
Deenah tayi tambayar cikin son dagulamata lissafi dan ta lura yadda take wani kishin sa sai kace amfaɗa mata damuwa tayi da mijinnata tayi hakan kawai dan ta cusa mata haushi.... 
Nafisa kuwa gaza control din kanta tayi har saida fuskrta  tanuna abinda ke kasan zuciyarta lokaci daya yanayinta ya sauya... Deenah tana kallon yanayinta dariya tasoma kokarin subucemata ta danne tare da cewar "aunty lafiya dai yanayinki ya sauya haka? ".. 
"bakomai kawai kainane kemin ciwo ".. 
Ta bata amsa tana dan rike kanta tare da yamutsa fuska... 
"Eyyah bari na daukomiki magani".. 
Cewar madina tana kokarin tashi tare da kunshe dariyar mugunta abakinta... 
"No nagode zanje nasha sai na kwanta".. 
Cewar nafisa tana hararar deenah ta kasan ido tanaji kamar ta shaqeta ttashi tayi ta fice bata kuma bi takan deenah ba.. 
Deenah kamar jira takeyi ta fice ta saka dariyar mugunta harda rike ciki, ko ba komai ta caja mata lisssafi kuma zata samu lafiyarta da ga yimata shisshigi a al amurranta... 

Nafisa kuwa tana shiga falon ta kai da kawowa tashigayi zuciyarta fal da jin haushin IG wato ita ya rainama wayau ya maida ta sakarai ya dauki yarinya yakaita wani gun suna shan soyayya, furzan da zazzafar iska tayi tamai tattare da bakin kishi da ya turnokota, bai shata abanzaba ta rantse yau saita daga masa akwatin hankali tunda ranar girkintane ayau.... 


Da daddare deenah na kitchen tana hada fruitsalad da zata sha ta kwanta, dan sam batajin cin abincin daren ayau  sultan ne ya shigo kitchen din tare da sallama... 
Ta amsa masa, leda ce ya mika mata yace "abbie yace na kawomiki".. 
Jin sunan wanda ya ambata saida ya haifar mata da faduwar gaba, dakatawa tayi daga abinda take ta karba ledar ta ajiye akan table... Dubanta sultan ya cigaba dayi har saida ta tambayesa "lafiya ko wani abu ke damunsa? ".. 
Girgixa kai yayi ya ce "abbie ne ya fadamana cewar ke matarsa ce, little mum nayi mamakin cewa duk tsawon lokacinnan abbie ya aureki baki taba sani ba, haka nima bansaniba, bansan dalilin abbie na yin hakanba, saboda ni atunanina yadda ya daukemu yayansa haka ya dauke ki, amma banga laifinsa ba, mai yuwuwa yana so yayi madadi ne dake tunda yarasa ummie, "

Tunda sultan yafara magana deenah  tagaza dago dakanta ta dubesa, cikin wani irin yanayi marar dadi take sauraronsa, batasan  me zata ce masa ba domin ya fama mata inda kemata ciwo... 
Leqa fuskanta sultan yayi ya fahimci bata cikin yanayi mai dadi kiran sunanta yakuma yi cikin damuwa "little mom amma kina son abbie kuwa "
Yadda ya jefo mata tambayar yasa ta fara jin haushin tambayoyin da yake mata, ganin bazata kulashi bane ya sakashi ficewa daga kitchen din, ua fahimci batasan yawan surutu yanxu shiyasa ya rabu da ita.. 
 Bayan fitowar sultan ,ta sauke ajiyar zuciya, ta share kwallar da take boyo da ta zubo mata, daidaita nutsuwar ta yi  ta kammala hada fruitsalad din acikin plate dakinta ta wuce tare da ledar.. 
Ajiye plate din tayi akan table.. Ta mayar da hankalinta wurin duba  abinda ke cikin  ledar, katon kwalin wayane mai shegen kyau, still sai guntuwar takarda, bude kwalin wayar tayi, sosai wayar tayimata kyau da daukar hankali babbar waya ce mai shegen kyau da tsada sai daukar ido takeyi, ajiye wayar tayi ta warware guntuwar takardar tasoma karantawa _duk abinda kike bukata kisanar dani, akwai new sim card a wayar, then idan kinga dama ki sake karya layin either kiyi flooshing din wayar zakiga yadda zanyi dake, mai taurin kai kawai, *Ilove you*_
Taɓe baki tayi bayan tagama karantawa har da yimata wani gatse dan ya siya mata, ko kunya baijiba harda wani cemata _i love uh_ kamar wani yaro ita kam badan tana tsoron muguntarsa ba da tayi flooshing din taga idan zai kasheta... 
Ajiye wayar tayi taci gaba da shan fruits dinta... 

Nafisa kuwa adaren ranar sam IG ya gaza gane kanta, maganganu tashiga gaggayamasa son ranta, dashi kansa bai san abinda take tuhumarsa ba akai... Saida ya taka mata burki ta hanyar nuna mata  ainahin zafin sa kafin ta shiga hankalinta... 
Kuka ta sanya masa mai sauti tashiga yimasa korafin rashin adalcinda yayimata, fadamasa duk abinda deenah tasanar da ita tayi batare da ta bari ya fahimci deenah ce ta sanar da ita... 

Shikuwa tuni ya gano takan bajin zaren murmishi yayi marar sauti, har yanxu deenah halinta na nan ya tabbatar gonarta tashiga ita kuma ta rubtata.... 
Kallon ta yacigaba dayi yadda take rairo kukanta mai tattare da tsantsar bakinciki, shi abunma  takaici yabasa... Numfashi ya ja ya sauke ahankali, yasake dubanta "ke yanxu ko kunyar tuhumata hakan bakiyi ba, let me tell uh something, daga ke har deenah babu vwanda  nake da burin tauyewa hakki, inaso na kuma jan kunnenki da ki rike girmanki kisan irin firar da zaki dingayi da madina "... 
Ta kasan ido ta hararesa tafi ganin maganganun da yake amatsayin naɗe tabarmar kunya da yakeyi dan ta tutsiyesa.. 
Sharar hawaye ta cigaba da yi, xuwa wannan lokacin ya fara fusata da nafisa, ya fada mata bai aikata abinda take magana akai ba, bai san mai zai ce mata ta yarda dashi, ta kumayi kadan da yayimata rantsuwa tunda ba tsoron ta yakejiba, kai tsaye yakuma bata amsa da cewar yayiɗin duk abinda zatayo taje tayi... 
Yaja tsaki ya shige bedroom dinsa yabarta anan falo... 
Aranar takaici da kishi kamar nafisa zatayi bindiga ta ci kuka harta gode allah, tafi yarda da maganar deenah akan tasa, shikuwa borin kunya ne kawai yake... 


Washe gari da rana  deenah taji sallamar dr junaidiyya a babban falo, da ke itama fitowarta falon kenan... Dagudu ta karasa gun dr ta rungumeta cikin farincikin ganin ta, kai tsaye falonta suka wuce, ruwa ta dauko mata da lemu da cup ta ajiye agabanta.. Dr junaidiyya kuwa sai faman murmishi takeyi da jin dadin yadda ta iske deenah, bakidaya ta sauya, dalilin zuwanta gidan, ammie ke sanar da ita deenah takoma, ta zone da ta tabbatar da babu wani abu dake damun deenah..... 
Saida dr junaidiyya ta tsiyayi ruwan lemun a cup ta sha ta ajiye cup din tana duban deenah "Naji dadin yadda na sameki, kin saki jikinki sosai, hakan yana nuna kin amshi qaddarar da ubangiji ya rubutomiki hannu bibiyu".. 

Deenah kuwa azuciyarta cewa tayi "ya na iya da abinda yafi karfina, tunda iyayena sun nunamin ikonsu da bacin ransu akan lamarin dole na hakura badan naso ba "... 
Afili kuwa zubawa dr idanu tayi tana sauraren ta... 
"Ki rungumi mijinki, kiyi kokarin kwantar masa da hankali, kada ki zama irin matan da basu damu da kulawa da mazajensuba sune wadanda suka sakarwa masu aiki ragamar  mazajensu ta fuskar girka abinci da kuma gyara turakar miji, wannan hakki ne da ya wajaba kanki, sakarci ne kibar mai aiki tassn sirrin mijinki, allah sarki jalila alokacin da take raye bata taɓa barin sir yaci abincin masu aikinta ba, dakanta take girkamasa duk abinda yake bukata, bata taba gajiya ko kosawa gun hidimta masa ba amatsayinta na matarsa, wannan dalilin yasa, har abada jalila bazata taba gogewa a zuciyar yallaɓai ba... ".. 
Shiru dr junaidiyya tadanyi na wani lokaci kafin ta dubi deenah da itama dubanta takeyi cikin wani irin yanayi mai dauke da kewar yayarta.. Dafa kafadarta dr tayi, "dan allah kiyi kokari ki koyawa zuciyarki son yallaɓai, ki kiyaye fushinsa kiyi kokari ki gano abinda yakeso, ki sakasa farinciki da hakan, ta hakan zai debe masa kewar jalila da ya rasa, ku zauna lafiya keda abokiyar zamanki ki mutuntata ki bata girmanta, ku haɗa karfi da ƙarfe ku saka mijinku farinciki kinii madina "... 
Sauke nannauyar ajiyar zuciya deenah tayi, batajin hakan mai yuwuwa ne, sam har yanxu batajin soyayyarsa aranta, koda ma tayo hakan bai zama lallai yayi tasiri agunsaba tunda shima bai taɓa furta mata soba, zata dai aiwatar da hakan ta wata siga, koba komai ta sake cusawa nafisa haushi dan zuwa yanxu ta fahimci munafurci ke sakata tana shisshigemata da kuma maida ita sakarai da tayi... Indai tacigaba da yimata hakan sai ta saka zuciyarta ta yi bindiga alokaci daya... 
Jinjina kai deenah tayi alamun zatayi kokarin aiwatar da hakan.. Sosai dr taji dadin hakan ta sake duban deenah tace "idan akwai abinda baki gane ba, ko kina cikin damuwa ki sanar dani kai tsaye.. Kada ki banbantani da  jalila ki dauka tamkar ni ita ce kinji.... "
Gyada kai deenah tayi tana share kwallar da ta zubomata ta amsa da "to aunty nagode sosai... "

Har harabar gidan deenah ta rakata sannan ta dawo ciki... 

Washe gari itace ranar da deenah zata karbi aiki agun nafisa.. Duk da tana cikin fargabar wannan ranar hakan bai hanata shiga kitchen  fara gabatar da  girki itada sauran ma aikatan da suke tayata aiki  ba... Lokaci zuwa lokaci takan tsinci kanta cikin fargaba, tasan komai yanxu  dan ita ba jahila bace, tasan irin rayuwar dake cikin auratayya, itakadai ke sake sake azuciyarta, aranar dai duk abubuwan da aunty  junaidiyya ta fadamata saida ta aiwatar dasu, harda wasu sirrirukan, kafin sallar magrib daya daga cikkn ma aikatan gidan ta taimakamata suka shirya dining table din da manya manyan flask da suka zuba abincin aciki... 
Deenah kuwa bayan tayi sallar magrib ko hijab bata cire ba ta nufi sashin IG dan ta gyara masa turakarsa.. Tunda ga kayataccen falonsa take karewa ko ina kallo, komai tsab tsab, falon sai fitar da niimtaccen kamshin turarensa yake... Tabe baki tayi aranta tace "babu laifi yanada tsafta.. 
Bedroom dinsa ta wuce kai tsaye cikin wani irin yanayin fargaba dan wannan ne karo na farko da ta taba shigowa turakarsa... 
Gaskiya komai na dakin ya kayatar da ita... 
Bata bata lokaci ba tafara kakkabe ko ina ta shimfida sabon bedsheet akan gadon ta share kasan carpet din ta wanke toilet tas duk da babu abinda yayi ta tirare dakin da turaren wuta, lokaci daya dakin ya kara kyau da sakin niimtaccen kamshi.... 

Ita kuwa sai faman uban nishi takeyi ta fito daga sashinnasa...ji tayi baki daya tagaji.. Itakam bazata iya jurar wannan wahalar ba, tunda daman matarsa (nafisa) ba ta saba yimasa haka ba, may be ma ya kusheta ko kuma ya titsiyeta da tambayar abinda yakawota dakinsa...
