*_47_*

Tunda mommy tafara magana nafisa na maƙale da wayarta akunne  tanajinta har takai aya,  tsabar farinciki ya hana nafisa cewa komai sai murmishi take mai sauti kafin lokaci daya ta fashe da dariya, kamar mai sabon ciwon hauka, ji takeyi tamkar ta magance matsalarta ta gama, cikin farinciki tasoma magana "idan dai har wannan ne, na riga da na magance matsalata, domin abubuwan aiwatarwane masu sauki, mommy zan karawa malam kallah 100k domin kwalliya ta gama biyan kudin sabulu "... 
Murmishi mommy tayi har nafisa na iya jiyo sautinsa, ta fahimci yadda farinciki y lullube zuciyar yar tata lokaci daya... "To shknan, yanzu mun riga da munkawo karshen matsalar.. Sauran aiki na gareki.... "
Tattaunawa suka cigaba dayi kafin daga bisani suyi sallama, nafisa na ajiye waya ta tashi ta shiga kai ko komo adakin, duk abubuwan da mommy ta fadamata mai sauki ne, sai dai hanya daya ce zata samu nasarar samo gashin kan  deenah ta hanyar shigemata da nuna mata komai ya wuce ta haka kadai zata ci nasara akanta... 

Kwafa taja afili tare da bayyanar da murmishin mugunta  ta koma ta kwanta tana ta sakawa da warwarewa... 
Tundaga wannan ranar nafisa ta sauya, kokarin ta takuma janyo deenah ajikinta, deenah kuwa ta ankare da sabon sauyin da ta gani atattare da nafisa bata kawo komai arantaba dan atunaninta ta zubar da makaman yaƙin haukanta, dan abinda nafisa keyi ya wuce ta kirasa da kishi sai zallar hauka da sanin rashin ciwon kai da tabi ta dagawa kanta hankali, shiyasa sam  bata biyemata, kuma ba kawo komai aranta ba gameda sauyin da tagani agun nafisa... 

Deenah ce  zaune a kan daya daga cikin kujerun roba dake  a  garden, ta dora kafafunta akan medium table din dake gabanta tana waya da aunty junaidiyya, nafisa ce ta shigo garden din hannunta dauke da tire madaidaici da ke da robar ruwa da cup... Da murmishi a fuskanta takaraso inda deenah take ta janyo kujerar dake gefenta ta zauna ta dora tire din akan table tana kallon deenah da take kokarin kashe kiran bayan sunyi sallama... 

"Ashe kina nan,".. 

"Eh nadan gaji da zaman ne shi ne nafito nasha iska ".. 
Deenah ta bata amsa tana gyara zamanta tare da dubanta... 

"Yayi kyau".. 
Nafisa ta fada tana tsiyayo ruwam roban acikin cup  takai bakinta ta dansha sannan ta ajiye ta dubi deenah da hankalinta nakan wayar ta datke dannawa.. 
Cikin kwantar da murya nafisa tasoma magana "daman inaso na kuma baki hakuri akan abubuwan da suka faru abaya, inaso ki goge abun aranki domin kuskurene nayi, insha allah zamu zauna lafiya, daga bangarena wani abu baxai kuma tasowa ba kinji kanwata "... 
Murmishi deenah tayi tadago dakau  tana kallonta kafin tace "ban taba sakaki azuciyata ba na rike, nasan akan gaskiyarki kike domin kishi halitta ne ga kowa ce ya mace, sai dai kowace da irin yadda zata sarrafar  da nata "... 
Tayi shiru tana kallon nafisa dake kallonta cikin yanayin nadamar data shimfida afuskanta, deenah ta dora da cewa "nasan na miki laifi domin na aure miki mijinki, na ji labari cewa kin dauki tsayin lokaci kina son ya Aj, tun kafin ya auri aunty na, sai gani kwatsam namiki shiga sharo ba shanu.. Saidai yakamata mu dauki hakan amatsayin qaddara, da babu mai iya canja ta,  mu rungumi mijinmu mu sanya masa nutsuwa, domin acikin matansa ne kawai zai iya samun cikakkiyar nutsuwa amaimakon mu daga masa hankali".. 

Tunda deenah ta fara magana nafisa ta shiga gyada mata kai alamun gamsuwa da zancenta, azuciyarta kuwa kara kullatarta tayi, dagangan kenan deenah tayimta kutse a soyayyarta, tunda har ta shigo gonarta, ta dilmiya kanta amatsala, saita ɗai-ɗaita rayuwarta ta yadda ita dakanta sai ta tausayawa rayuwarta.... 
Afili kuwa murmishi tayiwa deenah tace "allah ya bamu zaman lafiya ".. 
Cikin nutsuwa deenah ta yi murmishi ta amsa da ameen... 
Fira nafisa tashiga jan deenah da ita wacce gaba dayanta, bugun cikinta na take dan taji yadda take zaune da IG,... 
To dayake deenar ta ku ta goge ba irin ta da bace, ayanxu tanada wayewar da tasan abinda take dan haka tasan irin firar da zatayi da kowa..... 

Cikin nasara nafisa ta samu nasarar kammala duk abinda mommy ta fada mata yanxu abu daya ne ya rage mata samo gashin deenah, aiki yafara tasiri, deenah kuwa awannan lokacin har ga allah tayi amanna da nafisa, bata kawo komai aranta ba... 

Deenah na kitchen suna kokarin hada abincin rana itada sauran ma aikatan gidan, kamar dai yadda tasaba aduk ranar aikinta, 
Nafisa ta shigo kitchen din, karemusu kallo tayi bakidayansu sun dukufa gun aiki tabe baki tayi  yadda taga deenah ta dage tana aiki ba mamaki saboda ta saba da wahala agida  shiyasa take shiga kitchen, abinda bazata taba iyawa ba kenan... 
Karasowa tayi inda deenah take tare da gyaran murya.. Hakan yasanya deenah juyowa ta kalleta tare da murmishi tayi mata barka da shigowa, ta mayar da hankalinta ga fere doyar da takeyi... 
Amsa wa nafisa tayi ta dora da cewa "kinga daga wurin wacce takemin saloon nake, naje ta wankemin kai, wai machine dinta  ya samu matsala, saidai kitso na samu, shine nakeso kifadamin inda kike zuwa nima na dinga zuwa can, kinga saimu dinga zuwa atare ko? ".. 
Ta karashi maganar da murmishi afuskanta Tana duban deenah
Ajiye fere doyar dake hannunta deenah tayi ta dubeta tace "shknan zan fadamiki, amma kwana uku da suka wuce na wanke kaina, saidai na baki address din  inda nake zuwa saikije"... 
Saurin katseta nafisa tayi da cewar 
"No zan iya jira har lokacin da zaki kuma komawa sai muje tare"

"Ok to badamuwa".. 
Deenah ta bata amsa tare da cigaba da aikinta... 

Ta wannan hanyar ce nafisa ta samu nasarar cikar burinta dan kuwa ranar da sukaje gun saloon din, ita aka farayiwa sannan deenah daga nanne kuma tasamu nasarar samun abinda takeso,.. 
Kiran mommy awaya tayi tasanar da ita tasamo gashin, washe  gari takanas mommy ta tado driver yazo ya karbi sakon.... 
Yanxu babu abinda ke damun nafisa, illah ta tsaya tasha kallon, dan karamin haukan da ta antaya deenah aciki... 

IG ne zaune afalonsa cikin nutsuwa yake kallon labarun da ake haskawa a NTA na karfe tara (9:00pm)... Ahankali yake kurban kofin coffee din dake hannunsa... 
iftahal ce ta shigo falon, yadda ya ganta yassn ba lafiya ba, tunkafin yafara tambayarta abinda ke faruwa tasoma magana cikin rashin nutsuwa "abbie, nashiga ɗakin aunty deenah akan ta taimakamin na ida assignment ɗina, shine  na tarar tana kwance akan gado sai rawar ɗari takeyi kamar tanajin sanyi, abbie batada lafiya fa "... 
Baibi takan iftahal ba dasauri ya fice da ga falonnasa iftahal ta bi bayansa...
Cikin rashin nutsuwa ya nufi sashin deenah.. A bedroom  ya tarar da ita kwance akan gado  tana bacci... 
Karasowa yayi har bakin gadon da take kwance yana sauke ajiyar zuciya tare da shafa fuskanta  cikin kulawa..., gashi kuma sam jikinta babu zafi. 
Maido da dubansa yayi ga iftahal, dake tsaye gefensa,  dan ta tabbatar masa da abinda tafadamasa... Marairaice fuska tayi "abbie cikin wani irin yanayi na isketa kamar me zazzabi sai kuma naga ta na dan zabura, na tambayeta meke faruwa, amma bata bani amsa ba, gashi kuma naga bacci takeyi yanxu.. "
Cikin gamsuwa da zancen ya ja dogon numfashi ya fesar ya kuma zubawa fuskar deenah idanu, iftahal yasa taje dakinsa ta dauko masa waya, cikin kankanin lokaci ta dawo da wayar ahannunta ta mikamasa... 
Kiran family dr dinsu yayi, ya sanar dashi bukatar ganinsa agaggauce... 
Cikin kankanin lokacin likitan ya iso, IG yayi masa jagora har zuwa dakin deenah da har yanxu bacci takeyi, ya kumayi masa bayanin abubuwan da takeyi.... 

Batare da bata lokaci ba likitan ya fara bincikar lafiyar deenah, saidai babu abinda bincikensa ta nuna masa alamu ya nuna lafiyarta kalau... 
Maida dubansa likitan yayi ga IG da yake jiran jin abinda likitan zai fada... 

"Sir gaskiya babu abinda bincke ya nuna min, zafin jikinta adaidai yake, bincike ya nuna lafiyarta kalau, may be pressure ne.. Gashi tana bacci kuma so pls kar atasheta har sai itace din ta tashi zata warware insha allah .... "
Tunda likitan yafara magana, idanun IG na akan deenah, amma hankalinsa na gun likita dake masa bayani... 
Sai alokacin ya dubesa "okey thank uh, uh can go "... 
Sallama sukayi likitan ya fice... 
Iftahal ma fita tayi tana yiwa deenah fatan samun lafiya.... 

Ta dauki lokaci mai tsawo tana bacci mai nauyi da shi kansa IG baccin ya basa mamaki... Dan tsayin lokaci ya gaza barin dakin yana jiran ta farka... 

Deenah kuwa  tamkar wacce aka tsikara ta farka daga baccin lokaci daya ta tashi zaune cike da mamakin irin baccin da tayi dan ko ina ajikknta yayimata tsami kuma batasan lokacin da baccin ya ɗauketaba.. 
IG kuwa saurin tallabo fuskanta yayi cikeda kulawa yasoma tambayarta lafiya... 
Gyara zama deenah tayi cikin gajiyat baccin da ta sha ta dubesa tana lumshe idanu,  ahankali tasoma magana "lafiyaklau nake, bansan ya akayi bacci ya daukeni ba, har na gaji da yinsa "
"Kin tabbatar lafiya kike? "
Yayi maganar ya na kuma  tallabo fuskanta idanunsa cikin nata .. 
Gyada masa kai tayi alamar "eh ".. 
Ajiyar zuciya yakuma saukewa tare da yiwa ubangiji hamdala sai alokacin yaji nutsuwa tazo masa... 
Tashi yayi ya nufi friedge din ta ya bude ya dauko robar ruwa.. 

Dan yamutsa fuska deenah tayi "bana jin karfin jikina, jinake jikina fa duk ya mutu hubby".. 
Murmishi sosai IG yayi ya dan harareta cikin wasa tare da mika mata robar ruwan ta karba ya zauna gefenta still yana cigaba da kallonta ya ce "may be baby na ne ke dan jijjiga ki, kafin kisaba ".. 
Yayi maganar yana daga mata gira... 
Noke kanta tayi tana yar dariya kasa kasa cikin yar shagwaba tace 'ba wani baby, yanxu haka yau ina period uh all know that"... 
Murmishi kawai yayi yana kallonta shikansa yasan hakan zolayarta kawai yakeyi, shiyasa bai damu da dr yayi mata awon cikiba yana fatan allah ya nuna masa lokacin da deenah zata dauki cikin dansa ko yarsa...  

Tundaga lokacin deenah bata kumajin komai ba.. Zamansu da nafisa kuwa, abun zai burge duk wanda ya kallah, ba deenah kadai ba hatta shikansa Ya samu nutsuwa yadda nafisa ta daina kokarin tada masa xaune tsaye agida,... 
Abangaren karatun deenah kuwa tuni result dinsu ya fito kuma yayi kyau sosai, har ma sun yi jamb sun fito da sakamako mai kyau... Zuwa yanxu IG admission ya samarwa sultan a university dake canada zai karanci enginearing, deenah kuwa cewa yayi bazai kaita abroad ba dan zata nesanta dashi ne shikuma bazai juri hakanba, dan haka kawai yasakata tayi applying a sir ahmadu bello university,.. Ynxu haka suna gab da fara rigitration... 

Deenah ce ta shigo sashin AJ hannunta dauke da flask da cup, ta hadomasa mint tea, tamkar yadda ya saba sha, awani lokacin kafin ya kwanta.... 
Jin motsin sa abayi,ya tabbatar mata da wanka yakeyi, Ajiyewa tayi atable  dake kusada gadonsa tare da zama agefen gado tana jiran fitowar sa, saida yadauki 5mins yana sakarwa jikinsa ruwa kafin ya fito daure da towel a kugunsa, sai wani dan karami dake hannunsa yana faman tsane ruwan da ke jikinsa, musamman acikin bakar sumar kansa.... 
Dubanta yayi yasakar mata murmishi "you're welcome my little, sannunki da kokari " yayi maganar tare da nufar wardrobe dinsa yana kokarin shirya kansa... 

Da idanu deenah ta bisa tana murmishi, cikin wani irin sonsa dake kuma bijiromata... 
"Hubby nifa yanxu na girma, niba little bace"... 
Tayi maganar cikin yar shagwaba... 
Ta mirror ya dubeta murmishi kawai yayi yacigaba da shirinsa kafin yace, sai ranar da allah ya nunamin  na miki ciki sannan zan tabbatar da hakan... 
Itama murmishi tayi  cigaba da hada masa mint tea din, har ya gama shirin sa kwanciya ya dawo ya zauna agefenta.... 
Mikamasa cup din tayi ya kurba still idanunsa akan ta wata irin wutar shaawarta ke bijiromasa, baya iya hakura da ita matukar kwananta ne... Bayan ya gama shan tea din, kashemusu hasken fitilar dake dakin yayi, ya kwanta kan gadon tare da janyota jikinsa, ya hada fuskokinsu  ta yadda numfashinsu yake haduwa, lokaci daya ya fara shinshinar wuyanta zuwa wasu sassan jikinta dake fitar da wani irin sanyayyen kamshi... Idanunsa alumshe ya gaza magana sai sauke ajiyar zuciya da yake... Hannunsa na yawo ako ina ajikinta... Deenah ma lumshe manyan idanunta tayi, tare da kara shigewa cikin faffadan kirjinsa tuni sakon da ya ke aikamata yafara tasiri ajikinta... Wata irin zabura deenah tayi cikin wani irin karfi da ya shiga jikinta tasoma kokarin raba jikinta da nasa, ko ina jikinta rawa yakeyi... 
Dasauri IG ya dawo hayyacinsa tare da kunna fitilar dake kan locker dan yaga meye kokarin faruwa da deenah .. 
Deenah kuwa tsalle tayi ta diro daga kan gadon tare da kwallah wata irin kara lokacin ta data kuma duban IG,cikin matsanancin tsoro da firgici tafara  kokarin barin dakin, cikin zafin nama da  IG ya fincikota jikinsa dan ya lura bata cikin nutsuwarta... 
Hadata yayi da jikinsa cikin tashin hankali yake tambayarta lafiya dakokarin sama mata nutsuwa ... 

Ita kuwa deenah rintse idanunta tayi, tana kuma kwallah kara "ka rabu dani karka cutar dani, banaso! , banaso!! "
Cikin kokarin kwatar kanta da take sonyi daga rikon da yayimata take kokarin kwace kanta tare da kaimasa duka da yakushi da dukkan karfinta amma tagaza.... 

Cikin tashin hankali IG ya saketa tare da sakawa kofar key dan ya lura so take ta gudu tabar dakin.. Da gudu takaraso bakin kofar tana jijjigata tamkar zata ɓalla ta, neman dauki takeyi da ceto... 
IG kuwa cikin tashin hankali na rasa kan Deenah da yayi ya kuma nufota, zuwa wannan lokacin kokarin kare kanta takeyi da jifarsa da duk abinda ta dauko, cikin lokaci  kankani ta harmutsa dakin, duk da jifarsa da takeyi da duk abinda ta rarumo hakan bai dakatar dashi daga kusantotaba.... 

Cikin nasara yasamu nasarar cafko ta da dukkan karfinsa ya riketa, dan ita kanta wani irin sabon karfine ajikknta... Kafadun ta ya rike cikin tashin hankali yake tambayarta abinda ke faruwa.... 
Deenah kuwa datayi kokarin fincikewa ta gaza kawai ta tafi luuuu!ta fada ajikinsa a sume... 
Cak IG ya dauketa ya dora kan gado robar ruwa ya dauko ya bude, ya cigaba da shafa mata ruwan afuska... Doguwar ajiyar zuciya deenah taja tasauke still bata bude idanunta ba lokaci daya bacci yayi awon gaba da ita, 
Adduoin neman tsari ya dinga tofa mata sosai... 
Cikin tsananin mamaki ya zubamata idanu yana kallonta.. Sauyin da ya gani atare da ita, yarasa gane shin tabin hankali ne yasamu deenah kokuwa shafar aljanu... 
Aranar AJ bai iya runtsawa ba, ya tasa deenah dake baccinta agaba yana kallo cikin wani irin yanayi, lokaci² yakan tofamata addu o i har aka kira sallar asuba... 
Bai tafi masallaci ba anan daki yayita, baikuma yi yunkurin tashin deenah ba.. 

Deenah kuwa saida gari yayo haske sosai sannan ta farka, tangaram kamar ba ita ba, tamkar babu abinda yafaru ajiyan... Zuwa lokacin AJ yafara zargin aljanu ne suka shigeta, domin duk abinda tayo ajiyan, alamun ta sun nuna bata ma san tayi ba... 
Tunda ga lokacin AJ yasanya ido sosai akan al amurran deenah, saidai sam haukan nata baya tashi sai ranar da ta karbi aiki, zuwa lokacin abun kara kamari yakeyi... 
Ita kanta deenah ta fuskan dabiunta wasu abubuwan sun canja, saurin daukar zafi da saurin fushi ne da ita, ga shi tafison kadaicewa ita kadai, batason kowa ya rabeta... Abubuwan da takeyi zai Nuna  maka batada cikakken hankali, ita kuwa aganinta komi tayi daidaine... 

Haka IG dakansa ya je gun babah yasanar masa yanayin da deenah take ciki... Babah kuwa yasha matukar mamaki dan a iya saninsa da madeenah bai taba jin tana da ciwon aljanu ba, bare wani abu makamancin hauka, shikansa IG kallo daya zaka yimasa kasan yana cikin matsananciyar damuwa akan halin da deenah take  ciki... 

Tantama babah yashigayi har saida yakira yadikko, dan yaji dagaske deenah nada wani boyayyen ciwon aljanu da sai yanxu ne ya bayyana.... Yadikko kuka tasaka dan ta rikice da bayanin da babah yayi mata, dan sam batasan deenah nada shigar iska atattare da itaba, sosai babah ya rarrasheta kafin ya katse kiran... 
IGP haka yakuma sanarwa da ammie dabiun  deenah dasuka sauya da irin abubuwan da takeyi... Ta shiga damuwa sosai domin alokacin saida taje gidan, kallon deenah kawai tayi taga babu cikakken hankali da nutsuwa atattare da ita... 
Malamai ta saka suka shiga yiwa deenah adduoi da rubutu, ta kowane fanni kokarin neman lafiyarta akeyi, haka babah, duk taimakon daya samo zaisa goggo asabe ta kawowa deenah bata barin gidan har saita tirsasa deenah tayi amfani da maganin agabanta, domin ita acewarta lafiyar ta kalau kawai adinga mai da ita mahaukaciya.... 
Nafisa ma tsantsar damuwarta tanuna akan halin da deenah take ciki, wani lokacin har kukan munafurci take sakawa agaban AJ.. 

AJ bai samun cikakkiyar nutsuwa matukar ba ranar nafisa bace, zuwa yanxu bisa dole ya hakura da deenah har zuwa lokacin da zata samu lafiya... 
Dan ko kadan bayason ganin abubuwan da takeyi sosai suke dagamasa hankali... 
Magunguna kuwa yi akeyi amma babu wani sauki sai wurin allah... Duk da haka basu karaya ba, itma auntu junaidiyya tana bada tata gudunmuwar sosai fatansu dai bai wuce deenah tasamu lafiya ba... 

Nafisa ce zaune afalonta cikin nutsuwa take chatt dinta lokaci zuwa lokaci takan tuntsire da dariya, achatt ne take sanar wa da nabila halin taskun da ta saka deenah, ta dora da cewar "na rantse da allah yanzu na fara ɗaiɗaita rayuwar deenah, gaba kadan sai ankoma daureta kamar goro... 

Dariya nabila tayi "lallai kinaji da shuumanci, to kidai bi  sannu kada reshe ya juye da mujiya"... 

"Har abada!  Nikadai keda ya AJ,  ba kuma ita kadaiba, wacce takuma kokarin shiga gonata zanyimata ko fiyeda abinda nayiwa Deenah ".. 

"Dakyau  na jinjina wa makircinki 😹 "... 
Cewar nabila tare da saka emoji akarshe maganar.. 
Chatt suka shigayi sosai, nabila kuwa ta samu makama akan nafisa.. Kafin ta kuma daidaita wani, ta lashi takobin ita saita ɗai ɗaita ta babu boka ba malam, saita shigar da nafisa duniyar hauka na tuggun da zata kullamata, ta hakane kadai itama zata samu abinda takeso, domin sunyi tarayya a abun so guda daya... 

Bayan wasu kwanaki bakidayansu suka shirya zuwa jumeta duba jikin innah, sakamakon rashin lafiyar da tayi... 
Wannan shi ne lokaci na farko da deenah ta taba zuwa jihar adamawa kuma ajumeta acikin dangin ya AJ, yanayin tsarin gidajen estate din da mutanen dake rayuwa acikinta da baki dayansu ahali daya ne, hakan bakaramin burge deenah yayi, lallai suna da ahali masu yawa da  mutunta juna, daga iya tarbar da deenah tasamu daga gun innah, deenah ta tabbatar da ya Aj ya gaji mutunci da dattako agun iyayensa lallai hausawa sunyi gaskiya dasuke cewa "MUTUNCI GADO NE..... "
haka duk sashin da taje sai an karramata, tarba ta mutunci da tarairaiya... 
Nafisa kam tunda tagaisheda hajiya innah ko 5mins batayiba ta nufi sashin hajjah.... Shima din ganin idon ya AJ ne yasanya ta dauki wannan lokacin acan... 

Deenah kuwa koda suka dawo sashin innah itada matar mujahid  niima da takeyimata jagora kowani sashi, abinci innah tasa aka kawowa deenah,  da sanyayyar fura da taji nono kindirmo.., dan tunda sukazo ruwa kawai tasha  suka kutsa cikin gida itada niima da lokaci daya allah ya hada jininsu... 

Koda suka shigo AJ tsiya yafara yimata akan ta daukemasa mata tana ta yawo da ita ko hutawa bata bari tayi ba... 
Dariya niima tasaka tare da cewa "tuba nake yallaɓai, kasan tunda yau ne farkon zuwanta gidannan dole ne taga yan uwa da dangi ".... 
Ta idar da maganar tana saka dariya... 
Dariya sukayi baki dayansu banda AJ da yayi murmishi kawai, hankalinsa na gun deenah, har innah ta bata umurni akan tazauna taci abinci ta huta... 
Ba musu deenah ta zauna, sosai ta kwashi tuwon  semo din miyat busasshiyar kubewa da man shanu asama,  ta dora da sanyayyar furar... 
AJ kuwa sai kallonta yake yana murmishi, yaji dadi sosai yadda deenah tasaki jikinta da danginsa, hakan ya kuma janyomata wani matsayi na daban a zuciyarsa... 

Innah kuwa sai tsiya take yiwa AJ wai ya saka yarinya agaba  da kallo ko kunyatta ba yaji... 
Sai alokacin yayi dariya, ya dubi innah yace "kinga kiji da kanki kawai, mutum da abunsa wazai masa shamaki".. 
Murmishi innah tayi cikin jindadi aranta tana fatan allah yakara hafe kawunansu baki daya... 

Bayan deenah tagama cin abincin sallar laasar tayi ta kuma hutawa, alokacin shima AJ yafita zuwa massllaci  , sashin hajjah, ta shiga dan ta gaishe su   da yake agaba da na innah yake, batasha wahala tagane sashinba, hajiya innah jamila kuwa suna umra ...
 Batare da jin komai ba tayi sallama ta kutsa kanta acikin falon... Hajjah ce da hajiy rumaisa sai mahaifiyar nafisa (, mommy) sai ita kanta nafisar... Shigowarta yasanya bakidaya falon yadauki shiru, da dukkan alamu suna tattauna maganar da ta shafesu ne... 

Karasowa deenah tayi cikin ladabi ta shiga gaishesu bayan tazauna akan carpet... 
Amsawa sukayi baki dayansu kafin hajjah ta dubi deenah tamayar da dubanta ga nafisa tace "wannan itace kishiyartaki? "... Bata jira amsar nafisaba tadora da cewar ", itace wacce aka liƙamasa  bayan yayarta tarassu? "...  Hajjah takuma tabe baki tace "banga laifinsu ba dan sunyi kokarin hada iri da mai dukiya da cikakken asali irin Abdulmajeed, kinsan zuriar gidannan su bangon sukari ne ajingina dasu kuma alasa,  su mayar da marar asali yakoma mai asali , marar gata yaxama mai gata "... 

Tunda hajjah ta fara magana deenah tafara fahimtar zamba cikin aminci take mata, zuciyarta tashiga bugawa da karfin gaske, ranta yasoma baci... 
Mommy ta karbe zancen da cewa "ance "kwarya tabi kwarya, idan tahau akushi ba rashin moruwa kadaiba har tsari bazata yiba, "..

Dago da kanta tayi tana dubansa still tana murmishi "bansan da kalmomin da zan gode maka ba,.... ".. 
Hannu ya sanya akan lips dinsa "sheeeet "
Ya katse ta, yadan dubeta sama da kasa yace "kawai kicemin kinasona, awannan lokacin da harrufan larabci zaki fada "... 
Dariya deenah ta saka tare da rufe fuskanta da tafukan hannunta ta fahimci zaulayarta yakeyi.. Dagudu ta fice daga falon cikin jin kunyarsa..  
Da murmishin da ya bayyana fafaren hakoransa yabita da kallo har ta fice... 
Acikin wannan lokacinne IG ya karbi budurcin deenah, ya gaza jurewa matuka,  daɗa haukatasa takeyi aduk lokacin da ya dubeta wani irin kyau take daɗa yimasa ga gogewa ta musamman, sannan kuma bawata kulawa yake samu ba aranar kwanan nafisa, sai idan itace take bukatarsa, zata ishesa da magiya har sai bukatarta, itakam sam batadamu da bukatarsaba, shikuwa gashi da zurfin shaawa, shikansa awannan lokacin gaza control din kansa har saida ya tabbatar ya raba deenah da budurcinta yakuma samawa kansa nutsuwa, ba iya deenah kadai ta wahala ba, shikansa ya sha matukar wahala kafin ya ratsata,, dan matse take sosai ta ko ina... Deenah kuwa da azaba ta isheta, ta suma ta farfado batasan adadi ba, ko ina jikinta asake yake ga  ciwo takeji acikin jikinta, a HQ dinta kuwa kamar anyi ɓarin barkono haka takeji taci kuka harta gode allah, babu wanda ya kwace ta ahannunsa.... 
Shikansa yasan ya wahalar da ita sosai musamman yadda yaji zazzabi mai zafi ya lullube ta... 
Kankameta yakumayi ya lumshe idanunsa wani irin farinciki da nutsuwa na shigarsa, yanajin wani irin sonta na zagaya ko ina acikin jiki bargo da jinin jikinsa.. Hakika deenah madadi ce mai tattare da alkhairi, bai taba tsamannin ubangiji zaiyi masa irin wannan tagomashin na basa madadin jalila bayan ya karbi jalila, hakika duk wanda ya dogara ga allah ya isar masa ga komai... Allah ya jikan jalila dan  itace silar sama masa nutsuwa ta har abada agidan duniya.... , idanunsa a lumshe yake adduar samawa jalila rahama agun mahaliccinsa, bata araye amma farincikin da ta barmasa kowane lokaci haɓaka yakeyi.... 
Bude idanunsa yayi akan deenah dake ta baccin wahala ya gyara mata gashinta da ya warwatsu a fuskanta ya manna mata peck a goshinta tare da sauke ajiyar zuciya mai tattare da nutsuwa.... 

Awannan ranar ko sallar asuba bai iya fita ba yana tare da deenah, tunda ya tashi da asuba yayi wanka yayi sallah shima yakoma kan gadon. 
sai misalin 7:06am ta farka, sosai ya taimakamata ta gyara jikinta, dan shi da kansa yayimata wanka, bata da yadda zatayi dan sam jikinta ba lakka tanaji ta na gani ya wanketa tas.. 
Saida yagama taimakamata sannan ya barta ta tsarkake jikinta... 
A yinin ranar yini tayi jikinta amace, har lokacin kuma hawaye take yi bata daina kuka ba, idanunta da fuskanta duk sun kumbara... 
Shikuwa tunda ya tafi office hankalinsa na gurinta, gashi yana ta kiran wayarta bata picking, duk bayan 5mins sai ya kira amma taƙi picking, yasan halinta sosai, yasan har yanxu har haushinsa takeji, koda zasu shiga kin rufe wayarsa yayi kamar yadda ya saba, dan kawai takirasa idan ta huce, amma yaji shiru, bayan fitowarsu daga meeting ya turamata text kamar haka _my little, can't uh pick my call?, nasan kirana akan idonki yake shigowa amma kike kyaleni, 2mins na baki kimaidomin da kirana, if not that wlh zan maimaita abinda yafaru jiya ayau, mai taurin kai kawai, ki tabbatar kin kara shan maganinki dan banason raki, loving uh with all my heart_..
Tunda ya turamata sakon ya fita harkarta yacigaba da harkokinsa na office.. 

Deenah tanajin shigowar sakon tasan shi ne, saida tayi kamar baxata duba ba kafin ta dauki wayar.. Tana gama karantawa atsorace tafara kokarin kiransa, 
Yana kallon kiran har ya tsinke bai daga ba, data damesa da kiran silent ya saka wayar yayi banza da ita ya kyale yana murmishi, ya tabbatar duk inda take yanxu tana nan a tsorace jin abinda ya fada... Sakon nafisa ne ya shigo awayarsa, saida ya gama duba wasu files sannan ya duba sakon, kalamaine masu nuna tsantsar soyayya da kulawa ta turo masa... 
Murmishi yayi na gefen kumati ya mayar mata da reply, ya tabbatar kudi take bukata ahannunsa shiyaasa tayimasa irin wannan text din, yagama nazartarta baki daya... 
Deenah kuwa haka ta dinga kai komo afalonta tana matsar kwallah  still tana kiran layin sa yaki picking 
Saida ya gaji ya turo mata da guntun sako "ya isa haka ai kinyi saving din kanki at the firt call "... 
Ganin sakon ya sama mata nutsuwa, sai alokacin tasamu ta zauna akan kujera ta na sauke ajiyar zuciya, tasan halinsa matukar ya fada to sai yayi din.... 

Wayar nafisa ce dake kan pillow tafara ruri, saida ta karashe shirin kwanciya bacci sannan ta dauko wayar akan gado tare da zama gefen gadon...
Kiran mommy ne har ya tsinke tana kokarin mayar da kiran ne kiran mommyn ya sake shigowa... Bata bata lokaci ba tadaga kiran dan tun safe take jiran kiran mommyn dan taji yadda sukayi da malam... 
Bayan sun gaisa ne mommy tasoma magana "zaayi miki aiki amma kayan aiki na hannunki"... 
Gyara zama nafisa tayi akan gadonnata tace "bangane ba mommy wane mataki ne aka daukarmin game da madeenah "... 
Murmishi mommy tace "kin cika garaje, tunda na kiraki ai kinsan bayani zanmiki, abu ne mai sauki amma idan zaki iya aiwatarwa... Zaki samo mataccen gashin kanta, da ruwan turaren da take amfani dashi ajikinta, sannan kuma xaki samu almajira yarinya karama da bata fara jinin al ada ba kiyi mata kyautar turmin atamfa, kuma ki tabbatar ke ce kika bata kyautar amma da hannun hagu, daga nan ki zuba ido aikin malam kallah zai fara aiki daga ranar basai ankuma kwana ba, aduk lokacin da takebe da Abdulmajeed musamman suka ssmu haduwar jiki zata ga ya rikide mata yakoma mata bakin kumurcin maciji dake kokarin hallaka rayiwarta, zata rikice ta fice da ga hayyacinta, zata dinga wasu abubuwa da zai nuna tabin hankali atattare da ita.....

Kukan deenah ya tsananta jin yadda babah ya yi shiru ya kyaleta batare da ya cemata komai ba, hakan yana nuna kenan basuda asalin kamar yadda ake goranta mata.... 
Wannan lokacin kuka maisauti ta kuma fashewa dashi, cikin tsinkewar zuciya take duban babah da zuwa yanxu zuciyarsa tasoma dagulewa... 
Labbanta ta soma motsawa alamun tana son tayi magana, amma bakincikin dake haƙilo a zuciyarta ya hanata magana... 
Shirun da babah yayimata shi kansa amsa ne ta kuma samu amsar haka, zata daina jin komai idan har anayimata gorin asali, zata danni zuciyarta akan damuwa da hakan, tunda ta tabbata ba asalin ne da suba.... 
Dasauri ta sunkuci jakarta tana goge hawayenta cikin zafin zuciya take kokarin barin gidan.... 
Sai alokacin babah yasamu damar yin magana ya katse deenah da cewa "dawo ki zauna"... 
Tsayawa tayi daga inda take, kafin tadawo ta zauna tana duban babah tana tsimayin  abinda zai fito daga bakinsa.....

IGP Abdulmajeed  ne zaune a office dinsa yana gudanar da ayyukan da ya saba... 
Wani jami'i ne ya shigo tare da sallama ya sarawa IG tare da kamewa, lokaci daya yasaki jikinsa ya soma magana cikin girmamawa "sir kanada bakuwa, tace ita kawar madam ce ".... 
Sai alokacin IGP ya dago  da kai yadubesa yadan ya mutsa fuska kafin yace "je kace mata tashigo"... 
"Ok sir! "
Yakuma ƙamewa sannan ya fice.... 
Cikin kankanin lokaci nabila ta turo kofar ta shigo,  sanye take cikin atamfa super wax dinkin riga da skirt daya gama bayyana duk wani lungu da sako na surar jikinta, ko arzikin mayafi babu sai katon gilashin da ya mamaye fuskanta, lokaci zuwa lokaci chewingum din dake bakinta yake bada sautin ƙas²... 
Tun kafin IGP ya bata umurnin zama ta zauna akan kujerar dake fuskantarsa tare da ajiye hand bag dinta akan table dinsa... 
Dubanta yashigayi fuskansa ba yabo ba fallasa domin tun ahigowarta ya gane ta... 
Nabila kuwa zame ƙaton gilashin dake manne afuskanta tayi tana duban IGP, cikin shuumammen murmishi ta ce "barka da warhaka yallabai"... 
Gyada mata kai yayi kawai ya katseta da cewar "meke tafe dakene "... 
Murmishi tayi na gefen kumatu kafin tace "ina tafe da abinda zai amfani rayuwarka, ya kuma cetoka kaida matarka da ke fama da ciwon aljanu kamar yadda kuke tunani"... 
Gyara zama yayi, ya kuma tsuke fuskarsa dan bayason wasa musamman akan abinda ya shafi iyalinsa, baiyi mamaki ba dan tasan lalurar dake tare da deenah hakan bai rasa nasaba da kasancewar nafisa kawarta.... Hannu ya dagamata alamar dakatar da ita kafin ya ce "pls go to the strength pont... "
"Yallabai kafin nace komai inaso kadaukarmin wani alkawari cewar duk bukatar da nema agurinka zaka min"... 
Kallon up and down ya dan yimata, kamar bazai kulata ba yace "indai naji zan iya, bukatar taki bata bi karfina ba, inajinki"...
Yayi  maganar yana dubanta.. 
Gyara zama nabila tayi ta soma cewa "matarka, kawata nafisa, maciyiyar amana ce, hatsabibiya kuma tantiriya, sululu kasau macijin sari ka noƙe, mai dawainiya da fuska biyu... Nafisa tana sonka amma tafison abinda ka mallaka, duk matsalar da kake ciki agidanka, nafisa ta haifar maka da ita, hatta lalurar dake damun matarka aikin nafisa, zagon kasa take maka, ba matar aure bace tana hulda da  mazan bariki  bayan kai, duk da nasan ka aureta ba ahayyacinka, wlh azeem asiri sukayi maka, itada kakarta da mahaifiyarta son zuciya ne dasu... Domin gazgata abinda na fadamaka, ka duba wannan chatt din "... 
Tayi maganar  tare da ajiyewa IG wayar ta agabansa, ta kasan idanunsa yake duban sakon, kafin ya furxan da iskar bakinsa ".. 
Dauke wayarta nabila tayi tana duban yanayinsa, cikin kankanin lokaci idanunsa sun sauya kala zuwa ja... Bakidaya kwalwarsa da tunaninsa sun daina aiki... haka kawai ya gaza yarda da maganganun nabila, hasalima kalamanta kona masa zuciya suka shigayi musamman yadda take aibata matarsa take alakanta ciwon deenah da ita, yasan nabila tana da gogewar da zata iya hadawa nafisa makirci mai sarkakiya, dan kallo daya zakayimata kasan kwayar idanunta a bude take...  
Bazai iya jimurin cigaba da sauraronta ba Zata kuma magana katseta "getout! "...
Batare da taji komai ba ta sake bude baki zatayi magana... 
Ya kuma daka mata wata uwar tsawar da ta gigita ta, "nace ki fita kibarmin office dina"! 
Ba shiri nabila ta dauki jakarta tafara neman hanyar fita... 
Sai da taje bakin kofar fita ta juyo takallesa ta re da murmishi "daman nayi tsammanin hakan saboda duk abinda zakayi ba ahayyacinka kake ba, har yanxu sihiri ne ke tasiri agangar jikinka shiyasa, shiyasa hujjojina basuyi tasiri agareka ba, amma ina mai baka shawara, dan kore wasiwasin da kalamai na suka haifarmaka kasaka malamai su dinga maka addua da rokon allah, ana sauke maka alkur'ani kowace rana, ananne hankalinka zai dawo jikinka zakayi tozali da ainahin gaskiyar dana fadamaka, saboda malamai sunce , kur'ani waraka ne ga mutane... Na barka lafiya "... 
Bata jira abinda zai fada ba ta fice... 
Wani irin numfashi mai tattare da huci ya fesar, duk laifinsa ne da ya bata dama har take kawomasa his family issue a office dinsa, idan har ta sake yunkurin yiwa daya daga cikin matansa kazafi ya sha alwashi sai ya koyamata darasin da har takoma ga allah bazata mantashiba... 

Tsaki ya ja, zuciyarsa adagule yaci gaba da aikinsa....

Cikkn nutsewa babah yakira sunan madina tare da dubanta baijira amsawarta ba yadauke kansa daga dubanta yaci gaba da cewa, ba dole saida dangi zaki iya rayuwa ba, kisani zaki iya rayuwa ake kadai kuma babu abinda zai sauya daga rayuwarki, ki dubi yaran dake rayuwa agidajen marayu basusan iyayensu ba basu san kowa daga nasuba, kuma basu fasa rayuwa ba, bare ke da  kikasan uwa da uba naki, kinga kuwa kinga ma samun asalinki, madinah duk abinda zakayi arayuwa ko ta wace fuska  sai mutane sun kalubalanceka wannan dabia ce ta dan adam.... "

Batare da ta samu gamsuwa da kalaman babah ba ta dauke fuskanta gefe tana runtse idanunta dasuke mata zafi, cije lip dinta na kasa tayi ta mayar da dubanta ga babah, cikkn disasshiyar murya ta soma magana "bamuda asalin kenan babah"... 
Saida zuciyar babah ta buga da karfin gaske kafin yayi saurin duban madina "kuna da cikakken asalinku, amma bana kaunar tunashi arayuwata, kuna da gata da wasu daga cikin yayan masu hannu da shuni basu kaiku gata ba... "
Yadda bana kaunar waiwayen asalinah, haka banason na tuna nidin waye, amma kisa aranki kinada cikakkiya kuma tsarkakkiyar nasaba daba kowane mahaluki, keda saar fitowada tsatso irinnakiba....ina fatan wannan jawabin zai samamiki nutsuwa, ina kuma rokanki da matukar irin wannan maganar ce zata kawoki gidana, karki sake zuwa banaso! "

Ya idar da maganar tare da mikewa tsaye ya zira takalminsa ya fice da ga gidan baki daya... 
Daskarewa deenah tayi a inda take tabi babah da kallo har ya fice... Bakidaya kwalwarta ta dakata daga aiki na wani lokaci, sam tagaza fahimtar kalaman babah.. Bakidaya jikknta ya sanyaya dan bata taba ganin babah cikin wani irin hali makamancin na yau ba... 

Tattaro yawun bakinta tayi da ya bushe, jikake kut... Ta dade ahaka kafin tadaidaita nutsuwarta, amma har lokacin tunaninta a yamutse yake... 
Sai gab da magrib driver yazo yadauketa.. Har lokacin babah bai shigo gidanba ta fahimci tamkar ma fushi yadauka da ita... 

Haka ta isa gida babu wani kuzari ajikinta, part dinta ta shige  kai tsaye tayimasa key, tafi bukatar ta kasance ita kadai dan tanaso tayi tunani akan sarkakken guntun tarihin ta da babsh yasoma bata... 
Kaida kawowa ta shiga yi, cikin samun mafita ta yi saurin dauko wayarta tasoma kiran layin yadikko domin ita kadai ce zata warware mata zare da abawa... 
Tasha wahala kafin tasamu layin yadikko kasancewar kauyennasu nada karancin network.. 
Yadikko nadaga kiran, deenah ta saka mata kuka tare da zama akan kujera... 

Cikin tashin hankali yadikko tace "yau ga iya shege, shagwabar ce ta motsa, shine zaki dagan hankali da kukanki... "
Batare da deenah ta daina kukanba ta shiga fadawa yadikko komai, har yadda sukayi da babah... 
Tunda deenah tafara mata zancen jikinta yayi masifar sanyi ta rasa me zata cewa deenah, tassn duk daren dadewa rana irin wannan zata zo... 
Sauke ajiyar zuciya yadikko tayi tace "karki damu madine, mahaifinki bazai taba fadamiki komaiba,... "

Cikin ƙaguwa deenah ta katse yadikko da cewa "Shknan yadikko tunda kinsan komai acikin satinnan zanzo kauyen tunda ke kinsan komai, dan allah karki boyemin kisanar dani "... 
"Shknan allah yakawoki lafiya, matukar hakan zai sama miki nutsuwa"... 
 Godiya tayiwa yadikko sukayi sallama, deenah ta ajiye wayar tare da sauke ajiyar zuciya, koba komai tadanji nutsuwa ta safko mata... 
Alwalar magrib ta dauro tagabatar da sallah, zama tayi akan sallaya tana lazimi har aka kira sallar issha, tatashi ta mayar da issha bayan ta kammala ne ta fice zuwa kitchen ta hada lipton tasha ta kwanta dan sam batajin yunwa, bakin cikin da nafisa da uwarta da kakarta suka kunsa mata ya cikamata ciki ta yadda idan ta tuno hakan take gaza cin abinci musamman ma acikin yan kwanakinnan, bayan da kuma kafin tafiyarsu jumeta, tana yawan fama da kasala, jiri, yawan jin tashin zuciya musamman da safe, ga yawan bacci da takeyi, bata damu da zargin kanta ko shigar cikine da ita, domin taga al adarta a wannan watan, shiyasa sam bata mayar da hankali ba... 

IGP Abdulmajeed, sai bayan sallar issha ya fito zuwa main falo, bayan yayi wanka ya sauya kaya zuwa jallabiya as usual, ganin kowa afalon baiga deenah ba kawai yaji yadan damu, sam baijin dadin kadaicin da ya auri madinah, gashi tayi masifar sauyamishi tun lokacin da suka dawo daga jumeta, sai wani Sharesa takeyi, ko magana idan yayimata atakaice take basa amsa bakidaya yarasa gane kanta... 
Har sukayi dinner suka kammala deenah bata fito ba zuwa lokacin ya gaza jurewa kawai yafara koran layinta, ya kira yafi akirga amma batayi picking ba.. 
Hakan yasa suna gama dinner kawai yanufi sashinta.. 

Da idanu nafisa ta bisa tana murmishi, ta tabbatar babu wani abu da zai kuma shiga tsakaninsa da madinah domin kuwa ta katange duk wata hanyar yuwuwar hakan, ta riga da ta haramta musu junansu, wannan shine ake cewa nan gani nan bari.... 

Yana shiga sashinta bai tarar da ita afalo ba, bedroom dinta ya wuce yaci saa kofar abude take.. Tarar da ita yayi kan gado tana bacci.... 
Karasowa yayi nutsuwa ya zauna agefenta yana kallon face dinta, cikin tsananin kewarta da wutar shaawarta dake ruruwa azuciyarsa yakai hannu ahankali yashafi fuskanta zuwa lips dinta... 
Sauke ajiyar zuciya ya yi... Yana matukar bukatar deenah, domin kokadan nafisa bata iya kashemasa kishirwar dake addabarsa ta deenah, itadin ta dabance irinsu ne keda wahalar samu acikin mata... 
Ahankali ya dinga kissing din ta, tunda ga goshinta zuwa lips dinta har zuwa dokin wuyanta.... 
Cikin sanyin muryarsa kasa² ya yi magana "Allah yabaki lafiya little "... 
Ya kashe mata hasken dakin ya fice tare da rufo mata kofar 

 

Bayan kwanaki biyu, bayan sallar issha deenah ta samesa afalonsa yana kallo, matuka yayi mamakin ganinta, dan ta dauki kwanaki masu yawa bata damu da zuwa inda yake ba, ita kanta deenah ayanxu bata iya tantance ranar aikinta da ba ranar taba domin nafisa itace kejan ragamar komai... 
Tunda ta shigo tareda sallama ya amsa ya kafeta da idanunsa, sanye take cikin hijab har kasa, hakan yatabbatar masa da sallar issha ta idar, har ta karaso ta zauna akujerar mazaunin mutum biyu da yake zauna akai... 

"Hubby "..... 
Takira sunan sa ahankali takuma gaza karashe abinda zata fada.. 
Mayar da hankalinsa yayi sosai akanta, "menene little"... 
Cikin sanyin murya ya tambayeta yana nazarin ta... 
Sunkuyar da kanta ta danyi tace "inaso zanje kauyen ringizau na gaishe da yadikko "... 

Girgiza kansa yayi "akwai wani abu dakike boyemin, nace kinada damuwa amma kinki yarda da hakan, kinki kuma fadamin meke damunki, deenah sokike kidinga wahalar dani ko? Kidinga bugani yadda kike so kamar tamaula, ko tausayin halin da nake ciki bakyayi? """. 
Ya karashe maganar yana kafeta da idanunsa... 
Sauke ajiyar zuciya tayi ta kuma dubansa "im sorry hubby, gaskiya ina cikin damuwa, kuma tana da nasaba da tafiyar nan da zanyi, dan allah karka tamvayeni komai, har idan lokacin da ni zanfadamaka ne yayi".. 
Tayi maganar ta na hade dukka hannayenta  alamun neman alfarma... 
Nannauyar ajiyar zuciya ya sauke ya dubeta yadauke idanunsa gefe... Baisan me zai kuma fada mata ba kawai yace "shknan, duk lokacin da kika saka rana zaki iya tafiya "... 
Ta lura da yanayin da ya shiga, taji sam bataji dadin hakanba, jiki asanyaye ta fito daga sashinnasa tare da yimsa sai da safe.... 
Tana fitowa daga falonsa tayi karo da nafisa da ke kokarin shiga sashinnasa... 

Kallon sama da kasa nafisa tayi mata, tare da jifar ta da wani irin wulakantaccen kallo ta raɓa deenah ta shige... 
Deenah kuwa runtse idanunta tayi, zuciyarta na mata turiri kafin ta bude idanubta tare da sakin murmishi mai ciwo tabar gun....  
Acikin yan kwanaki biyu deenah tagama shirin zuwa gun yadikko IG kuwa kudade masu yawa yabata yace takaiwa yadikko ta siyi goro , ranar safiyar alhamis da safe driver ya kaita, AJ bai tafi office ba saida yaga tafiyar su madinah sannan ya tafi.. A hanya ya kirata yace mata idan ta sauka takirasa dan ya tabbatar da lafiyarta.. Da to kawai ta amsa masa ta ajiye wayar.... 

Kauyen ringizau, deenah ce zaune adakin yadikko, bayan duk tagama gaisawa da mutanen gida, abokanta kuwa na yarinta dake gidajen aurensu da sukaji zuwan deenah haka sukayita zuwa suna ganinta tare da yimata sannu da zuwa cike suke da mamakin yadda deenah ta sauya ta yi kyau ta murje fatar jikinta kuwa sai wani irin kyalli da sheki takeyi musu kamar wata ba indiya, ga nutsuwa, kamar ba madinensu da suka sani ba , deenah kuwa bakaramin farincikin ganinsu tayi ba, haka tadinga yiwa kowa alkhairi, bayan kowa ya kama gabansa ne ta dubi yadikko da ta ajiyemata kwadon zogalen da tayimata da yaji albasa da kuli, dubansa kawai deenah tayi taji amai na shirin zo mata, tayi saurin kauda shi daga gabanta... 
Sororo yadikko tayi tana duban deenah dan ta tabbatar da abinda take zargi.. 
Katse shirunta deenah tayi tace "yadikko dan allah ki sanar dani, wlh ina tattare da matsuwa da fargabar sanin asalina "... 
Dan guntun murmishi yadikko tayi ta gyara zama ta dubi deenah tace "kidaina fargaba ki nutsu ki saurareni kikuma kwantar da hankali..... 

"Sarki ABDULJABAR, sarki na goma sha bakwai amasarautar AGADEZ, dake jamhuriyar niger, dane ga tsohon Sarki MUHAMMAD, wato mahaifina, domin ni kanwace ga sarki ABDULJABAR, da mu biyu ne tal awurin iyayenmu,  sarki muhammad kuwa ɗane ga sarki HASAAN, wato sarki nagoma sha biyar awancan karnin, zamu dauki tsayin lokaci idan nace zanzayyano miki salsalar wannan masaurata mai dumbin tarihi da albarka, atakaice dai sarki na goma sha bakwai sarki ABDULJABAR, ubane ga mahaifinki abdullahi... 

Masarautar AGADEZ, masarauta ce da allah yayiwa arziki, zaman lafiya da kwanciyar hankali, bawai iya kadai masarautarba, garin agadez, gari ne na zaman lafiya da kwanciyar hankali garine nakasuwanci da kuma noma, uwa uba kiwo, garine da ya tara mutane masu alkunya da kawaici, duk da aduk inda kaga alumma to dole ne kasamu nakirki da na banza amma awannan gari mafi yawansu da tawwane dake da dattako da sonjunansu, asalin buzayene na usul, da suke matukar riko da al adunsu... 

Sarki Abduljabar yanada mata uku, gimbiya sunaima  itace matarsa  ta farko  mahaifinta  shahrarren dan kasuwane anan garin agadex sai gimbiya raihana itakuma daga kasar sa udiyya ya aurota, zuwansa umrah ne suka hadu bayan dawowarsa kasarsa bada jimawa ba ya aurota ya kawota kasar niger itama mahaifinta attajirine ayankin garin riyadh kuma sananne mai babban mukamin gwamnati a garin riyadh, sai takarshen itace gimbiya murshida itakuma yar kasar nigeria ce yar asalin garin jigawa... 

Wadannan mataye nasa da ya auro daga mabanbanta kasashe, tsaye yake akansu dan ganin ya hade dukkan kawunansu dan shi mutum ne me kwadayin zaman lafiya, ko ba agidansaba, aduniyarma baki daya... Yayansa goma sha hudu.. , gimbiya   sunaima nada shidda, huɗu mata sai biyu maza, gimbiya murshida na da biyar maza hudu sai mace daya..  Ita kuwa gimbiya raihana ta dauki tsayin shekaru bata haihuba ta zubawa sarautar allah ido  ta kuma fawwalawa ubangiji lamurranta domin shine mai badawa da axurtawa bata damu da maganganun da ke yawo acikin gidanba na cewar ita din juyace, musamman da maganganun ke fitowa atsakankanin kishiyoyinta da saboda sudin suna haihuwa suke hade mata kai, bata taba damuwa da wannan tsegumin ba, domin tasan lamarin ubangiji, innama amruhu iza arada shai 'aan  anya kula lahu kun fa yakun! .. 
Duk lokacin da ubangiji yayi nufin ta samu nata rabon duk daran da dewa zata samu..., dan haka gangncine   da kuskure bawa yake fitar da rai daga samun rahamar ubangiji.. 
Sarki Abduljabar yana matukar damuwa da rashin haihuwa da gimbiya raihana bata samu ba, yana matukar kaunarta fiyeda matansa, saidai baitaba barin wata baraka da zasu gane hakan ba... 
Gimbiya raihana tanada matukar alkunya, kawaici, hakuri da nutsuwa, bata fiye sakawa ranta damuwa ba, wannan dalilinne yasanya ta samun matsayi na daban a zuciyar mai martaba sarki ABDULJABAR.... 
A rana tsaka kwatsam gimbiya raihana  ta samu ciki... Matukar farinciki agurin mai martaba baya boyuwa tamkar  alokacin ne zao samu rabo, cikin kankanin lokaci labari ya zagaye masarautar AGADEZ... 
gimbiya raihana kuwa taga tattali sa farinciki da mai martaba ya gaza boyewa afuskarsa dangane da ita... Wannan ne ya haifar da bakinciki atsakanin matansa da kananun maganganu akan rawar kafar da mai martaba yake nunawa  dangane da samun cikin da gimbiya raihana tayi, kamar wannan shi ne karo na farko da dazai samu ɗa ko ya, halin kishinmu na mata ya turnuko matansa da zazzafar kiyayyar gimbiya raihana da suke nunamata da ita da abinda take dauke dashi......... 
Ko kusa gimbiya raihana bata taba daga kai ta dubesa ba, haka rayuwarta take bata saka damuwa ko ta kullaci wani aranta, bata taba tankamusu ba daidai da rana daya... 
Haka tacigaba da rainon cikinta, har zuwa ranar da ubagiji ya qaddara fitowar abinda ke cikinta... Tasha wahala matuka kafin ta haifo santalelen namiji, mai kama da mai martaba sak tamkar yayi kaki ya tofar... Yaron kyakkyawa kallo daya zaka yimasa kasan ya hada ruwa biyu wato jinsin larabawa da kuma jinsin buzaye.... 
Gimbiya raihana kuwa tana haihuwa tace ga garinkunan ta amsa kiran mahaliccinta.... 
 
Maman boy...

_Alhmdllh nasamu sauqi na dawo typing, nagode da aduo i nku, kauna da kuma soyayyarku agareni🙏, allah yakaramana lafiya da zaman lafiya aƙasarmu baki daya._

*_49_*

Haƙiƙa Mutuwar gimbiya raihana ta  jijjiga  sarki ABDULJABBAR, ba iya shi kadai ba hatta masarautar agadez  ta jijjiga da mutuwar gimbiya raihana, bayan ankai gimbiya raihana makwancinta, mai martaba ya yiwa kyakkyawan jinjirin da ta bari huɗuba da suna ABDULLAHI ".... cikin tsantsar soyayya da tausayin abdullahi da yakeyi ya damkawa jakadiya huraira ragamar kular masa da yaronsa,naso ace nice na karbi rikon Abdullahi ahannuna, amma yadda na fahimci tsantsar so da maimartaba yake nuna masa  yasa nayi shiru  na hakura dan nasan zaiyi wahala mai martaba ya iya jure rashin abdullahi atattare dashi, rabasa da zanyi dashi kuwa tamkar na rabasa da farincikinsa ne duk da bashi kadai bane  dan saba, amma na zabi na hakura domin farincikin yayana mai martaba duk da cewar ni din bantaba samun haihuwa ba. 
 Jakadiya ta raini abdullahi cikin tsantsar gata da mulkin da ya taso acikinsa ta nuna wa abdullahi soyayya fiyeda yayan cikin ta, ta haɓaka gatansa na jinin sarauta... Kuma duk wannan abun tana yi ne duk dan samun gindin zama da neman suna agun mai martaba..., ta kuma samu nasarar hakan, dan mai martaba ya nutsu da yadda take kula da abdullahi, hakan yasa kullum daɗa yima ta alkhairi da kyaututtuka yakeyi. 
 
Haka dai abdullahi yataso cikin gata har yakai shekaru goma sha uku a duniya, awannan lokacin allah  yayimasa baiwa ta hankali da kuma nutsuwa da iya fito da zance dallah², akwai hakuri juriya da kuma kawar da kai, har wani lokacin mai martaba kan basa suna da "bawan allah"... 
Domin kaf acikin yayansa maza shidda babu mai hankali da nutsuwa irin ta abdullahi.. 
Wannan dalilin yasa yake daɗa jin kaunar sa azuciyarsa da kuma tausayinsa na rashin sanin mahaifiyarsa da baiyiba... 

Shekaru na ta ja,  lokacin hatta ran gadi idan sarki zai ita, yakan fita tare da abdullahi ne, ko kadan bayason yana nisa dashi, shikansa mai martaba baya iya boye so da tausayin sa da yakeyi azuciyarsa, yanajinsa shidin na dabanne,  kananun maganganu kuwa sun fara kai komo tsakanin fada da cikin gidan mai martaba na cewar "wai abdullahi shi ne zai gaje mai martaba bayan babu ransa... "
Haka kuwa takasance,dan  mai martaba da kansa ya fad'a a fada cewar "matukar baya araye to abdullahi shi ne magajin wannan masarauta, domin kobai fadaba kowa yasan irin nagarta hankali da nutsuwarsa,  kuma sam bai taba kwadayin zama akujerar mahaifinsa ba bayan babu ransa, sam ba itabace agabansa sabanin yan uwansa da tunda suka san miye mulki suke rigima da nuna zallar maitar son mulkin bayan bbu ran mai martaba, hakan yasa mai martaba yaga takowane fanni abdullahi shi yafi cancanta da mulkin, yakuma fadi hakanne dan mutanen fada su zama shaida koda bayan ransa idan fitina ta taso... Alokacin abdullahi bai wuce shekaru ashirin da takwas a duniya ba... 
Hankalin gimbiya sunaima  da murshida ya dugunxuma ainun, sun shiga tsananin tashin hankali da furucin da mai martaba yayi afada, musamman gimbiya murshida dake da maza har huɗu, kuma duk da kasancewar su magidanta basu daina fada yaka junansu akan kujerar mulkin mahaifinsu... 

Bakidayansu tsanar abdullahi da sukeji azuciyarsu ta kuma ninkuwa aransu, musamman yan uwansa da suke ganin agabansu aka haifesa kuma zaizo ya kwace musu masarauta, bakidayansu sun kullacesa, lokaci daya suka shiga neman hanyar da zasu tagayyara rayuwar bawan allah dabaisan hawa ba baisan sauka ba... Gimbiya murshida kuwa ta gitta ranta ga allah matukar tana aduniya abdullahi bazai taba hawa karagar mulkin mai martaba ba 

Abdullahi kuwa sam baiji daɗin yadda mai martaba ya zartar da wannan hukuncin lokaci daya ba... Yasan yadda yan uwansa suke fada atsakaninsu akan karagar mai martaba, hakan da mai martaba yayi zai saka suji babu dadi amatsayin su na magidanta dasuka mallaki hankulansu, kuma hakan  babban  kalubale ne akansa, shidai akullum fatansa allah yaja da ran mahaifinsu, yakuma tabbatar musu da alkhairi... 
acikin yan kwanaki kalilan mai martaba ya naɗa abdullahi amatsayin magajin garin agadez, ya kuma umurcesa da yaje kasar libiya, awani gari da ake kira Abughrain.. Yaje yaga yar amininsa da sukayi karatu tare amatsayin matar da ya zabamasa, dan zuwa lokacin shekarun abdullahi sun kai munzalin da yakamata ace yana da iyali domin kuwa yana da shekaru 30 alokacin... 

Masarauta fa ta rude kuma gidan sarki ya kama da wuta batare da saninsa ba, matansa da yayansa neman ganin bayan abdullahi sukeyi afujajan, haka suka shiga yimasa zagon kasa da duk wata hanya da zasu kawar dashi, cikin kariyar ubangiji allah ke kare bawansa abdullahi daga sherrinsu... Wanda suna samun yunkurin yimasa hakane ta hanyar jakadiya dake bankadomusu duk sirrinsa,sun samu hakanne daga gareta bayan sunyimata alkawarin baiwa daya daga cikin yayanta babban matsayi acikin masarautar matukar suka samu nasara, sannan ita kanta zata samu tagomashi,  yayinda shikuma abdullahi yake dubanta amtsayin uwa wacce ta tsuguna ta haifesa... 
Allah kuma bai taba basu nasara daidai da kwayar zarrah da zasu iya cutar da shi, har zuwa lokacin da aka naɗa sa amatsayin magajin gari.... 

Duk da ba a gidan sarauta nake ba alokacin dan aurene ya fitar da ni daga gidan sarauta, ina auren wani attajirin mai kudi kuma sananne anan Cikin garin agadez, nikuma kadaice matarsa har yabar duniya wato alhaji musa... 
Na fahimci yadda wutar qiyayya da hassada ke ruruwa agidan dan uwana na kuma saka idanu sosai, atsakanin matansa da yayansa, dan sanin abinda suke kokarin aikatawa abdullahi, domin kuwa nasan dole arina, wai ansaci zanen mahaukaciya, anan ne nafahimci jakadiya huraira zagon kasa takeyiwa abdullahi, dan na lura kullewa da shakuwa da tashiga tsakaninta da matan sarki... Na kumayi mamakin halinta na butulci da cin amanar mai martaba da akeyi da ita... Su kuwa dan sunyi  hakan ba abun mamaki bane domin babu waccw bansan halinta ba, haka yayan nasu musamman awannan yanayin na kwadaituwa da kujerar da yayana yake akai tun kafin yabar duniyar sunfara rigima akanta, to nasan komai zasu iya dan cikar burinsu.... 
Ganin haka yasa na baiwa dan uwana shawara akan yasaka ido sosai akan gidansa, domin yasan meke faruwa... Ya tambayeni dalilina na fadar hakan amma naku, na zabi na yimasa hannunka mai sanda, dan banaso na tada tarzoma a tsakanin iyalinsa... 
Da jakadiya ta fahimci yadda nasaka idanu a al amurransu, sai tasha jinin jikinta sosai, na kuma tabbatar ta sanar musu da cewar ankare nake dasu, shiyasa suka takawa kansu  burki... 

Bisa ga umurnin mai martaba, Abdullahi yaje garin abughrain na kasar libiya shida hadimansa da suka rakasa. zuwa gidan elhajj elyaqoub hashim attajirin dan kasuwa da yayi suna agarin abughrain, aminin mai martaba kenan da karatu ne ya hada amincinsu, kuma mahaifi ga nanah aeesh, da ita ce mai martaba ya zabawa abdullahi amatsayin mata, domin habaka amintaka dakuma abokantakarsu yasanya suke  son hada yayansu aure,.. Cikin hukuncin allah suka daidaita kansu, domin karo na farko da abdullahi yaji soyayyar wata ya mace lokacin da yayi  tozali da nanah aeesh, kyakkyawa ce sosai, ga alkunya da tsantsar ladabi da nutsuwa, hakan yasa lokaci daya ta shiga ransa... Haka itama nanah aeesh zabin da mahaifinta yayimata sam bata aibu atattare dashi ba, ta kumayi naam da hakan... 

Haka Abdullahi suka dawo kasar niger cikin farinciki da nutsuwar irin kyakkyawar tarbar da suka samu tunda ga kan iyayen nanah aeesh masu dattako, har xuwa ita kanta... Shikansa mai martaba yaji dadin hakan, dan lokaci daya suka daidaita kansu... Cikin kankanin lokaci abu na manya, aka daura auren, aka kawo amarya nanah aeesh daga kasar libya zuwa kasar niger... Bayan shagulgulan da aka gabatar da aka jima ba ayi irinsu ba a masarautar.... 
Haka aka kawo amarya dakinta anan cikin gidan sarautar, sashinta kuwa ya sha banban da sauran matan yayyensa domin nanah aeesh mahaifin ta mai kudin gaskene, babu ce kawai ke babu adakinta, dan ma duk wasu abubuwan bukata akwaisu acikin  gidan... 
Amarya kuwa saidai ace sam barka domin duk masoyin abdullahi zai tayasa murna da samun mace mai hankali da hakuri irin aeesh... 
 Awannan lokacin ne mai martaba ya harbu da cutar ajali... Ciwo lokaci kankani yakamasa ashe mai bukatar rai ne, bai dauki lokaci mai tsawo ba yana jinya allah yadauki rayuwarsa... 

Allahu akbar!!!  Abdullahi ya shiga tsananin tashin hankali marar misultuwa, yayi kukan rashin mahaifinsa sosai, hatta nikaina da nake cikin tashin hankalin rashin dan uwana tilo saida na tausayawa abdullahi... Ayinin ranar da aka rufe mai martaba gaza cin komai yayi bare kuma bacci.... Aeesh batayi kasa aguiwa ba wurin tausar mijinnata da kalamai masu sanya tsantsar nutsuwa.. Har saida ta tabbatar mijinta yafara samun nutsuwa... 
To nima hakan ta kasance ina cikin gidan, lokaci zuwa lokaci nakan shigo sashen abdullahi na duba halin da yake ciki,  sai da nayi kwanaki huɗu cikon na biyar.. Watarana da daddare na nufo zan shiga masaukina domin na kwanta nayi kicibus da jakadiya huraira..... 
Yanayin yadda ta dubeni cikib rashin gaskiya da shan jinin jikinta, tagaisheni ta raba ta wuce haka kawai naji ban yarda da itaba, na kuma gaza nutsuwa na je na kwanta har saina san wani abu game da ita, musamman danaga ta nufi sashin gimbiya sunaima... 
Bayan ta nabi batare da na bari tasan da hakanba har muka shiga sashin, saida tashiga falon sannan ta rufe kofar, nikuma alokacin nasamu damar tsayawa bajin kofar.. Domin ina tsoron mugun nufinsu akan abdullahi... 
Sai alokacin na fahimci, bakidayansu ne afalon gimbiya sunaima har da yaya yensu maza... 
Nashiga matukar tashin hankali na maganganun da kunnuwana suka jiyemin, lokaci daya gumi ya fara ketomin, jjkina yafara rawa.. 
Domin kuwa sun samu nasarar yiwa abdullahi kurciya sun kuma tabbatar saidai awayi gari aga babushi dagashi har iyalansa, domin yadda ɗa ke barin cikin mahaifiyarsa haka Abdullahi zaibar masarautar agadez har abada, daganan zasu tsayar da daya daga cikin yayansu ya zama shine magajin sarki, nikuma da zan kawomusu matsala zasu kasheni..... "

Yadda suka sheqe da dariya yasakani firgita na dawo hayyacina... Cikin tashin hankali da tsoro na baro sashin gimbiya sunaima... Kai tsaye sashin abdullahi na nufa, dan har sun rufe kofa sun kwanta na tashesu da bugun kofa... 
Koda Abdullahi ya bude kofar ya tsorata daganin yanayina... 
Cikin tashin hankali nasoma umurtarsa da ya debo kayansa mubar garin... Shikuwa kokarin tambayata yake abinda ke faruwa, amma banida nutsuwar  yimasa bayani, abinda nake ambata kawai shine "zasu cutar da kai, zasu kasheni! ".... 

Cikim rashin samun mafita daganin yadda na fice daga hayyacina, shima yasoma haɗa kayayyakinsu, shikansa hankalinsa atashe yake... 
Awannan dare mukayi bankwana da masarautar agadez ni da Abdullahi da kuma nanah aeesh dake dauke da tsohon ciki haihuwa ko yau ko gobe... 

Bakidaya ji nayi hatta kasar niger din na tsorata da ita, dan haka ba iya garin muka bar musuba, hatta kasar saida muka bar musu... 

Duk da akwai kudade ahannunmu amma munsha wahala kafin mu isa kasar nigeria dake makwabtaka da kasarmu niger, dan saida mukayi kwanaki biyu da yini daya amota kafin mu ketaro border mu shigo kasar nigeria, acikin garin katsina,  allah yakawomu, bamusan kowaba, hasalima bamu taba zuwa kasar ba acikin bulaguronmu muka tsinci kanmu awannan  kauyen na ringizau , nidai nasamu nutsuwa da salama domin ubangiji ya nisantamu da ga azzaluman bayinsa masu son zuciya, son abun duniya dakuma son kansu.. 
Taken da akewa garin katsina wannan take gaskiya ne... _katsina tadikko dakin kara, kunya garesu badai tsoro ba_
Wannan magana haka take domib munga hallaci agurin mutanen kauyen ringizau, amatsayinmu na baƙi da basu sanmuba bamu sansuba, baku ma suji tsoron taimakamana ba, musamman da suka fahimci muna bukatar taimakon, ga aeesh da tsohon ciki.. Haihuwa ko yau ko gobe... 
Gidan maigari muka samu masauki, aka bamu abinci muka ci muka sha muka kuma huta... Mai gari ya tambayemu mudin su waye... Bamu yarda mun fada masa komai da ya shafi rayuwarmu domin wani lokacin mu buzaye bamada yadda, dan gudun kar a cutar damu, musamman agari irin wannan damuke da banbancin al adu.. Mundai fadawa mai gari wani kadan daga labarinmu dan yasamu nutsuwa damu.. Kuma mu sanarmasa mudin yan gidan sarauta bane.... 
Kwanakinmu biyar a gidan mai gari, alokacin abdullahi yayi fafutuka yasamo mana gidan dazamu zauna anan cikin kauyen... Da yake akwai kudi ahannunsa, bayan ya canjasu zuwa kudin nigeria, sauran kuwa yafara fafutukar neman sanaar da zai rike mu... 
Tirkashi awannan lokacin duk abinda abdullahi ya taba baya albarka saidai ya tabarbare, cikun kankanin lokaci jarin da ya kafa sanaa dashi ya nemesa ya rasa tamkar ankona kudaden.... Gashi kuma ko kadan baya sa atado maganar da ta danganci garin agadez bare kuma masarautarta, hakan ya tabbatar min cewa burin su gimbiya ya cika dan asirin da sukayi masa ya fara tasiri ajikknsa, nagode allah da ina tare da abdullahi da hakan baisa ya nisance niba... 
Sosai abdullahi yashiga damuwa akan halin da yake ciki, duk da haka baidaina fafutukar neman hanyar da zai rufamana asiri ba... 
Bello dandan mai gari kuma aboki ga Abdullahi, shi ne ya sakasa acikin kasuwancinsa da ya ke acikin birnin katsina, duk karshen mako suke zuwa kauyen ringizau suga iyalansu sannan su koma gun sanaarsu... Sosai naji nutsuwa ta shige ni... Alokacin kuwa na auri limamin garin ringizau, yakaini gidansa amatsayin matarsa ta biyu, babban gidane sosai, maidauke da mutane daban daban, duk da haka ban daina kula da aeesh ba da keda tsohon ciki, akuma lokacinne abdullahi yayi yunkurin daukar aeesh su koma birni gun sanaarsa, na hanasa nace yabari har saita haihu, tunda can babu wanda tasani abirni... Haka Abdullahi ya hakura domin bai taba musu dani ba... Acikin  lokacinne Allah ya sauki aeesh lafiya ta sunkuto santaleliyar yarta mai kama da mahaifinta sak, kyakkyawar gaske, sosai abdullahi yayi farinciki da har yagaza boye hakan, yayiwa yarinya huɗuba da suna jalila... Bayan sunyi arbain ne, yadauketa su ka koma birni, haka dai rayuwa ta cigaba da tafiya, lokaci bayan lokaci, abdullahi kan ziyartoni yakuma yimani alkhairi, ahankali muka fara sabawa da kasar nigeria da kuma mutanen dake cikin ta... Aeesh kuwa tuni ta yaye jalila, yarinya mai burgewa da saurin shiga rai, ga wayau kawai ci da hakuri irinna iyayen ta, haka suka cigaba da rainon tilon yarsu cikin soyayya da kulawa,a lokacin kuwa ko batan wata aeesh bata kumayiba 
Har zuwa lokacin da jarabta takuma fadawa abdullahi, domin a shagon bello da yake taimakamasa wurin siyar da kayan masarufi, karshen shekara akanyu lissafi afitar da riba, akuma karo wasu kayan a shago, sai gashi a shekarar ba asamu konai ba, sai bakar asara da tashafi shagon, Abdullahi ya shiga cikin tsananin tashin hankali domin bello ya yarda dashi ta yadda hat yasakar masa ragamar shagon, ga kuma asarar da ta shafi shagon da shi kansa abdullahi bai iya cewa ga yafda akayi da kudi... 
Bello kuwa duk amincin dake tsakaninsa da abdullahi ya murzawa idonsa kwalli... Akan sam bai yadda ba sai abdullahi ya biyasa kudinsa, kuma yabar masa shagonsa , dakyar dai muka samu  bello ya hakura bayan na roki mahaifinsa mai gari da ya shiga cikin maganar, dan nasan abdullahi bazai taba cin amanarsaba... Tilas bello ya hakura amma yace babu shi babu abdullahi ya kuma koresa daga shagonsa.... Allah sarki haka Abdullahi ya rungumi qaddararsa , bai gajiya ba ya cigaba da fafutukar neman abunyi, abubuwa sukayi masa yawa ga matsalar iyalinsa ga matsalar mai gidan da suke zaune na haya dan har ya basu notice akan subar masa gidansa tunda sun gagara biyansa... Haka masifu suka sako abdullahi agaba.. Daga karshe dai ya barwa mai gida gidansa ya kuma nemi sanaar faci abakin titi yanayi... Muhalli kuwa, a inda yake zaune, gida ne da ba a ida gininsaba yayi ma gidan yan dabaru ya saka iyalinsa aciki... Rayuwar abdullahi takoma abun tausayi, da kyar yake iya samo abinda zai baiwa matarsa da yarsa tilo ga wani cikin da aeesh ta dauka har ya tsufa alokacin kuwa jalila nada shekaru goma sha hudu aduniya, a wannan yanayin ne ubangiji ya sauki aessh lafiya ta kuma samun karuwar ya mace, kyakkyawar yarinya mai tsananin kama da mahaifiyarta... Abdullahi kuwa saida na muka shiga fafutuka dakyar kafin asamu abun da za a yankawa yarinya na suna, ranar suna kuwa yarinya taci suna MADINATU..., 
numfashi yadikko tasauke batare da ta dubi deenah ba taci gaba da cewa "bakidaya haka mahaifinku yaci gaba da fadi tashi dan ganin bai bari kun tagayyara ba, jalila kuwa na matukar kaunatki kamar yadda kika sani, acikin halin da muke ciki, bata taba tausayawa kanta ba, akullum tausayin ki takeji da irin rayuwar da zaki taso aciki ta rashin galihu, akullum makomarki itace abun hangenta.. Akwai tsananin soyayya ta jini a tsakaninku har zuwa lokacin da ubangiji ya aiko da haske arayuwarmu bakidaya... Ina nufin shigowar Abdulmajeed a rayuwarmu, kuma shi ne bango majinginarmu, ubangjji ya shigo dashi rayuwarmu ne dan yazama fitila mai haskakamu, Allah sarki ahaka ciwon ajali ya kama aeesh, cuta mai son rai.. Bada jimawaba ubangiji ya karbi rayiwar aeesh... Alokacin baki wuce shkara daya da rabi.. Wannan dalilinne yasaka rikonki ya dawo hannuna, jalila kuwa da yake ansaka ranarta da Abdulmajeed ba abata lokaci ba aka daura aure.. Hakika iyayen Abdulmajeed, iyayene masu dattako da ganin kimar dan adam, basu taba nuna mana kyama ba amatsayinmu na talakawa da basu da cikakken asalin, suna kokarin girmamamu bisa yadda ubangiji ya karramamu amatsayinmu na yan adam "..... 
Sauke nannauyar ajiyar zuciya yadikko tayi akaro na biyu, ta dubi madina tace "wannan shi ne taƙaitaccen tarihin asalin rayuwarki, ina fatan yanxu kinsan asalinki kinkuma san wacece ke? "... 
Deenah kuwa fadawa tayi jikin yadikko ta rungumeta tare da  saka kuka mai tsuma zuciya, ta gaza cewa komai sai kuka da takeyi, still taki barin  jikin yadikko... Ita kanta yadikko hawayentake sakamakon fama tsohon mikin dake zuciyarta da  tayi.... Dago da deenah tayi daga jikinta ta tallabo fuskanta tasoma magana "madina duniyar nan cike take da salihan mutane da kuma azzalumai... Mum bar wata shariar dole sai akiyama zaa yita... ".. 
Sai alokacin deenah ta tsagaita da kukan tana duban yadikko  tasoma magana cikin disasshiyar murya "yanxu shknan bazamu taba komawa asalinmu ba, bazai yuwuba yadikko, "..  Deenah tafada tana girgiza kai...azzaluman mutane tun a duniya suke girbar abinda suka shuka,  Babu wanda ya isa ya yanke mana farinciki ta hanyar da tsemu daga  tsatsonmu, ba a taba canjawa tuwo suna,... "

Rufemata bakinta yadikko tayi da hannunta ta ciga dacewa "karki kuma wannan maganar, musamman agaban mahaifinki domin duk  irin son dayakemiki zai tsaneki kuma ya juya miki baya, dan ya tsani abinda abinda zai taso da maganar masarautarsu.. "
Ajiyar zuciya yadikko ta sauke tacigaba da cewa "dole sai munyi hoɓɓasa mun fara rabasa da sihirin da ke jikinsa sannan hankalinsa zai koma mahaifarsa, amma yin gaggawa baya haifar da komai sai zallar nadama "
Shiru deenah tayi tana cigaba da kwallar idanunta... Matuka ta tausayawa mahaifinta yadda ya dinga haduwa da jarabta iri², hakika yan uwan mahaifinta azzalumai ne batare da ysyimusu komai ba suka nisanta sa daga jin dadin  rayuwa sannan kuma suka harbosa uwa duniya duk da haka saida suka tagayyara rayuwarsa,suka mayar dashi daga mai cikakken yanci zuwa mara galihu, kawai saboda kwadayin mulki?! allah bazai taba barin azzalumai suyi tasiri ko nasara adoron kasa ba, akodayaushe ubangiji shike bayan mai gaskiya... Kuma tabbas allah zaiyiwa babah sakayya cikin kankanin lokaci... 

Bakidayansu asanyaye suka tashi  sukayi alwala suka gabatar da sallar laasar da aka soma kira.. 
Bayan sun kammala sallar ne deenah ta haye kan gadon yadikko ta kwanta tayi lamoo... Da dukkan alamu bacci ke kokarin dauketa, ga damuwar da ta cunkushemata zuciya... 
Yadikko dake zaune kan sallaya tana lazimi, ta zubawa fuskar deenah idanu tana murmishi.. 
Bayan ta kammala ne tayi gysran murya tare da cewa "ke kuwa madina tun wani lokaci kike fama da wannan yanayi bansani ba ".. 
Sai alokacin deenah ta bude idanunta dasuka soma nauyi ta dubi yadikko cikin muryar bacci tace "wane irin yanayi kuma yadikko "... 

"Na kasala da yawan baccinnan danaga ya sameki dan ni nasan bakida lalaci amma bakidaya kin canja".. 
Cewar yadikko tabaiwa deena amsarta... 
Lumshe idanu deenah tayi tadan waresu akan yadikko tace "kawai bacci nakeji ne kuma na gaji".. 
Yar dariya yadikko tayi "ja ira, ai nasan cikine ke wahalar dake shi ne bazaki fadaminba na kusasamun ɗa ko ƴa "... 
Ware idanunta deemah tayi sosai kan yadikko cikin yar shagwaba tasoma magana "dan allah kidaina jamin ciki yanxu nida zan fara karatu a kwanannan "... 
Rike haɓa yadikko tayi kafin tayi yar dariya "to aikuwa Abdulmajeed yamiki wayau, ko kiso ko karki so yaron ciki ne dake domin duk alamomin sa sungama bayyana ajikinki "... 

Juyawa yadikko  baya  deenah tayi ta gyara kwanciyarta ta cigaba da kokarin yin baccinta domin ita dai tana da tabbatacin cewa batada wani ciki... 
Cikkn farinciki yadikko ta girgiza kai, aranta tana fatan ubangiji ya sauki jikarta lafiya... 

da daddare kuwa deenah gaza  bacci tayi, tunani takeyi sosai akan dangin mahaifinta, da irin kalubalen rayuwa da iyayenta suka tsinci kansu aciki... Sai yanxu ta fahimci zancen aunty jalilah da take mata a ashekarun baya, tana nunamata IGP Abdulmajeed Ginshikine arayuwarsu ya cancanci ta girmamasa fiyeda yadda zata girmama jalilar, sai yanzu ta fahimci komai, sabanin da,  da hauka da kidahumanci ya hanata fahimtar aunty jalila... 
Runtse idanunta tayi siririyar kwallah ta zubo zuwa kumatunta... Tana fatan allah yajikan mahaifiyarta ya jikan auntynta jalilah da take matukar kewarta yasanya mutuwa hutuce gare su bakidaya... 

Washe gari bayan sallar azahar driver ya zo daukar deenah, da tunkafin zuwansa tagama shiryawa.. Sosai yadikko ke bata shawara akan tabi komai ahankali, domin komai yazo karshe da izinin ubangiji... Da sannu zaa warware komai... 
Har ƙofare (kofar gida) ya dikko ta rako deenah bayan tayi sallama da mutanen gida, yadikko  tana ta faman sanya mata albarka, har ta shige mota driver ya ja motar yadikko bata daina dagawa deenah hannu ba.. 

Deenah kuwa sai alokacin ta turawa IG sakon mai dauke da ban hakuri da kalamai masu sanyi har take sanar dashi cewa tana hanya... "
Ta sani matuka bata kyauta masa ba domin tunda ta baro gidan ta kashe wayarta dan bata tare da nutsuwar ta, sai yanxu da ta fara samun nutsuwa ta bude wauar ta turamasa da sakon... 
Sai bayan sallar laasar   suka iso katsina Suna   isa gida.. Kai tsaye sashinta ta wuce cikin matukar gajiya.. 
Wanka tayi ta dauro alwala tayi sallah, bakidaya jin jikinta takeyi duk ya mutu.. 
Deenah tadan damu yadda har lokacin bata reply na AJ, sannan taji sautin shigowar motarsa avikin harabar gidan bayan sallar magrib  hakan ya tabbatar mata da dawowarsa tasan   halin  kayanta dole sai tayi aikin rarrashi... 
Tana idar da sallar issha, kai tsaye ta wuce sashinsa... 
Afalo ta iskesa zaune yana kallon  TVC chennel... 
Tunda tayi sallama ya amsa yadan dauke kansa daga dubanta... 
Deenah kuwa akujerar da yake zaune ta karaso ta zauna tare da kwantar da kanta akan kirjinsa ta rungumesa, lumshe idanunta tayi tasoma basa hakuri cikin kwantar da murya... 
Sosai takejin yadda yake sauke ajiyar zuciya, hakan yasa ta dago dakanta tana duban eyeball dinsa danshima kallon ta yakeyi... 
Tashi tayi zaune tadan gyara zamanta har lokacin ya kafeta da idanunsa masu narkar da ita.. Ta kwayar  idanunsa kadai ta fahimci sakon da yake aikamata mai  nuni da  kin kyauta...! 
Kasa tayi da kanta tasoma magana "im sorry hubby, bazan boyemaka komai ba yanxu, kasan bana boyemaka komai, ko da naki fadamaka abinda ke damuna nima alokacin ina kokarin samun amsar dake kaikawo tsakanin kwalwata da zuciyata,.... 
Duban rashin fahimta yayi mata sai alokacin ya magantu da cewar "little wai meke damunki ne? ".. 
Yayi tambayar yana zubamata idanu domin shi har yanxu zargi yakeyi iskokan dake kanta ne ke sakata wasu abubuwan... 
Sauke ajiyar zuciya deenah tayi tadan dubesa cikin nazari kafin ta sunkuyar da kanta kasa, ta soma fadamasa abubuwan da ya faru zuwan su jumeta da irin abubuwan da su momy da hajiya rumaisa sukayimata, cikin rarrashi  da tausasa murya ta dora da  cewar "hubby ban fadamaka hakanba dan na hadaka da yan uwanka ba, dan girman allah kada kayi yunkurin cewa zaka dauki mataki, domin ni agganina hakan da sukayi yasa nasan mtsayina na kuma san nidin wacece.... 

IG da idanunsa suka soma sauya launi Zuwa ja dake nuna zallar bacin rai, ya dubi deenah yana neman karin haske akan kalamanta... 

Hakan yasa tacigaba da cewa, "naje gun yadikko ne dan ta fadamin miye asalinmu da ake mana gori, domin danaje gun babah bai fadamin komaiba sai zallar bacin rai dagani atare dashi... 
Bata boyewa AJ komai ba game da ita da kuma asalinta, domin ya cancanci yasan hakan tun ba yauba.... 
Zubamata idanu yayi tamkar ranar ya taba ganinta... Fizgar ajiyar zuciya yayi, ya tallabo fuskar deenah da dukkan hannayensa yana cigaba da kallonta... 
Lokaci daya ya rungumeta tsam akirjinsa tare da lumshe idanunsa yana fatan abinda yake zargi ya zama gaskiya, babu makawa ammie sai tayi farinciki da hakan 
, deenah kuwa sororo tayi   akirjinsa cikin mamakin yadda yanayinsa ya sauya lokaci daya, dagota yayi daga kirjinsa yana murmishi lokaci daya kwallah tafara zubomasa mai dauke da zallar farinciki... 
Cikin kwayar idanunsa deenah take kallonsa cikin mamakin sauyin yanayinsa lokaci daya, batasan dalilin sa nayin farinciki hakaba, dan bata tunanin labarin ahalinta da ta basa mai tattare da, bakin ciki son zuciya, da zalunci, sam bata tunanin hakan zai sakasa farinciki, kodai yana farinciki ne dan ita din ta fito daga gidan sarauta, to menene na alfahari da hakan bayan zaluncin da akayimisu... 
Irin wadannan tambayoyinne deenah ta shiga jerowa kanta azuciyarta.. Still  tana duban AJ daya gaza furta komai sai hamdala da ya shiga yi... 
Jin hannayensa tayi acikin nata yarike yana murza yatsunta a hankali,  cikin nutsuwa yake sauke ajiyar zuciya tattare da murmishi afuskarsa yakuma gaxa furta mata komai
Dubansa takumayi cikin daurewar kai , kafin Dauke idanunta  daga nasa tana tunani mai daure kwalwarta , dole akwai abinda yasakasa farinciki aciki labarinta. To amma menene ...
