Tun kafin ta fara kokarin tambayarsa yasake tallabo face dinta da dukkan hannayensa ya manna mata peck a goshinta tare da shafa fuskanta yana murmishi ya dubi kwayar idanunta yace "kije ki kwanta dare yayi..... "
Sai alokacin deenah tasamu nasarar katsesa cikin mamaki tasoma tambayarsa  "hubby duk me ya kawo wannan sauyin atattare da kai, sai wani farinciki kake yi, meke faruwa ne? ".. 
Murmishi AJ yakumayi na gefen kumatu ya kuma dubanta yace "ok bakyaso nayi farinciki musamman dajin wannan labarin? Dole nayi farinciki domin kuwa sai yanzu nagane ashe gimbiya nake aure "... 
Ya karashe maganar yana yar dariya kasa kasa.. 
Kukan shagwaba ta shiga yimasa tare da turo dan karamin bakin ta "harda wani zolaya hubby? "... 
Tsagaitawa ya danyi da  dariyar seriously ya dubeta yasoma magana "nayi farinciki amma ta wani fanni na tausayawa rayuwarku madinah, musamman babah ya hadu da kalubale na rayuwa ya sha wahala an cutar dashi da yawa , bazamu ɓata lokaci ba zan saka malamai su  shiga yiwa babah addua da saukar al qur ani mai girma, har allah yasa sihirin dake jikinsa yadaina tasiri, ya waiwayi asalinsa... "
Jinjina kai deenah  tashiga yi cikin farinciki da matukar kaunarsa take murmishi... 
"Kije ki kwanta, da safe zamuje gidan ammie dake "
Jinjina masa kai takumayi  ta furta "to".. 
Sannan tayimasa saidasafe ta fito, duk da dai batasan dalilin sa na zuwansu gun ammie ba amma taji ta gaza tambayarsa... 

Washe gari bayan sun gama breakfast, deenah  ta shirya tafito main parlour, fitowarta yayi daidai da fitowar AJ daga sashinsa cikin shigar manyan kaya na wani lallausan yadi mai ruwan makuba, saida ya karaso falo ya idar da kafe hula (zanna) akansa da ta shiga da kalar yadin dake jikinsa sai faman kamshi yakeyi yayi kyau sosai... 
Nafisa dake zaune afalo ganin suna shirin fita ne atare yasaka mikewa tsaye, cike da mamakin ganinsu, deenah kuwa tunda tashigo falon ta kalleta ta watsar,
Katse shirun da kallonsu datakeyi AJ yayi tare da fadar sai mun dawo... 
Wannan kalmar ta kuma daskarar da nafisa a tsaye, ta lura komai ya sukurkuce, tasirin  aikinta ya rage zafi tunda har IG zai iya kasantuwa da deenah a inuwa daya, to duk me yakawo hakan? Ina ma zasuje yanzu? ...
Rashin samun amsoshin tambayoyin da ta jerowa kanta ne ya saka ta zauna jiki a sanyaye cikin zafin zuciya tashiga nazari,.. 
Tashi tayi ta shige bedromm dinta da sauri, ta dauko wayarta tasoma kiran mommy... 
Saida ta kusan tsinkewa kafin mommy ta daga, ko gaishe da mommy batayi ba tashiga yimata korafi "mommy wani irin aikine wannan, wlh sam na daina ganin cigaba, sai ci baya, yanxu haka sun fita unguwa tare bansan gidan ubanda suka tafi ba, aiki nema yake ya lalace fa, shi kansa na rasa gane kansa a yan kwanakinnan"... 
Ta idar da maganar tanafashewa da kuka... 
Saida tayi mai isarta mommy bata iya rarrashinta ba har  saida   ta tsagaita dan kanta kafin mommy ta sauke ajiyar zuciya tace "jiya malam yakirani yake sanar dani aiki yana lalacewa domin tufka yakeyi ana yimasa warwara, ina nufin sun fahimci sihirine ajikin yarinyar dan haka suka tashi tsaye da addua da neman magani, sannan shi kansa Abdulmajeed, yanzu akwai garkuwa mai karfi atare dashi, hakan ya nuna kenan shima yatashi tsaye dan kare kansa... Idan kuwa har sun fahimci akwai sihiri a jikinsu kenan kinga kece abun zargi, babban tashin hankali na shi ne malam ya tabbatar min da cewa aikin dayayi mana na yarinyar nan tamkar ƙaiƙayi ne koma kan mashekiya duk ranar da ya warware kan.... "

"Kenan kaina zai dawo, ubanwa yace yamin na kaikayi koma kan mashekiya, duk tarin kudaden da nake turo wa abasa yazama na banza, na shiga uku na lala ce mommy"...  
Ta idar da maganar tare da rushewa da kuka mai tattare da zallar tashin hankali da dimaucewa... 

Itama momy cikin damuwa ta soma  rarrashin nafisa "kinga nutsu, ai bamu zamu taba zuba ido hakan ta faruba, wlh ko zan rasa raina sai na dakile faruwar hakan, nace malam kallah yakuma yi mana wani aikin mai zafi mukuma zamu sakarmasa bakin aljihu, yanxu nabasa kwanaki biyu ne domin hajjah bata lafiya yanxu haka ta samu bacci... Za a sake sabon aiki ( nace "karya fure take bata ya'ya) 
Sai alokacin yar nutsuwa ta shige ta, haka mommy tayita bata baki tare da kwantar mata da hankali har sukayi sallama... Har lokacin nafisa jin zuciyarta take a cunkushe..... 

cikin nutsuwa drivern IGP ke driving, cikin motar ya dauki shiru yayinda, shi da deenah kowa da abinda yake tunani a ransa... Maido da dubansa yayi gareta yadan tsura mata idanu yadda ta lumshe idanunta tamkar mai bacci yaga ta ƙara yimasa masifar kyau... Ahankali yasaka hannunsa acikin nata yana shafa lallausar fatar hannunta.. Matukar gaske yana cutuwa da rashin matarsa, duk da ya fahimci sauki na shigowa a al amurranta tunda yanxu takan sake dashi kuma wasu abubuwan duk ta rage yinsu... 
Deenah kuwa jin hannunsa acikin nasa yasa ta bude idanunta akansa, murmishi ya sakar mata, itama ta mayar masa da martani lokaci daya ta dora kanta akafadarsa tana cigaba da sauke sanyayyar ajiyar zuciya... 

IGP  da deenah ne zaune a falon suna jiran fitowar ammie... Bayan fitowar ta ne ta zauna, IGP yafara gaisheta kafin deenah, sosai ammie taji dadin ganin su musamman atare ta kuma fahimci yanayin jikin deenah sauki da sassauci na shigarta... 
Saidai sosai ammie ta fahimci, akwai wani abu dake tafe dasu.. Musamman AJ da ta fahimci da wani abu da yakeson ya sanar mata... 

Shiru ya dan biyo baya kafin IG yasoma magana cikin nutsuwa "ban manta ba ashekaru da yawa da suka wuce, lokaci zuwa lokaci ki kan yawaita bamu labarin, kanwar ummu (kanwar mahaifiyar ammie da yake ummu suke kiranta) da ta auri sarkin garin agadez na jamhuriyar niger, har ubangiji yayi mata rasuwa.... "

Cikin damuwa da tado mata tsohon mikin da yayi ta dubesa fuskarta dauke da damuwa tace "allah sarki har yau ummie  (ummu) bata daina zancen ummie raihana ba, akwai shakuwa sosai tsakaninsu tunkafin allah yayimata rasuwa, gashi kuma ko sau daya sarkin garinnan (agadez) bai taba barin dansa yazo gun dangin mahaifiyarsa ba saboda irin son  da yake masa gani yakeyi tamkar su ummu zasu rabasa dashi ne.. . Ni kaina bansan halin da abinda ta bari yake cikiba amma duk me ya kawo wannan zancen?  ".. 
Cikin damuwa ta ta soma tambayarsa .. 
Sauke ajiyar zuciya IG yayi ya dubi deenah da kanta ya daure kafin ya mayar da dubansa  sosai akan ammie a nutse yacigaba da magana tare da kiran sunan ammie "wannan dan nata da ta bari ba kowa bane illah mahaifinsu deenah "... 
Cikin matukar mamaki da yagaza boyuwa afuskar ammie ta dubi deenah da itama tsantsar mamakin ya hanata furta komai, kafin ta mayar da dubanta ga AJ, "ta yaya Abdulmajeed? Me kake kokarin sanar min "... 
 murmishi AJ yayi ya cigaba da duban ammie yana magana " duk abinda ubangijivya kaddaro to ya saka MUSABBABIN faruwarsa, ina nufin komai yana da dalili komai yana MUSABBABI... ni kaina nayi matukar farinciki da mamaki alokacin da deenah ta gama sanar dani su waye su anan ne na fahimci wannan dan kanwar ummu ba kowa bane illah mahaifinsu Deenah...., cikin nutsuwa AJ ya fada mata komai har zuwansu jumeta da cin fuskar da su hajjah sukayiwa deenah wanda shi ne silar bankaɗo boyayyen sirrin da basu sani ba. ... 
Zuwa lokacin ammie ta fara zubar hawayen farinciki... Cikin tsantsar soyayya ta ke duban deenah da itama hawayen take duk da mamakin kasantuwar hakan bai gama sakinta ba... 
Hakika duk inda naka yake zakajishi daban azuciyarka, bata tabajin jalila da deenah amatsayin surukanta ba, amatsayin yayanta take jinsu, ashe da ita da sudin jini dayane, 
Da hannu ammie ta yiwa deenah nuni da ta zo gareta... Kamar jira deenah takeyi, tafada jikin ammie ta na fitar da sautin kuka kasa², Rungumeta ammie tayi sosai ta shiga yiwa ubangiji hamdala... 
Kafin ta dubi AJ dake dubansu yana murmishi mai nuna zallar farinciki... 
Share hawayenta ammie tayi "kayi gaskiya Abdulmajeed komai nada musabbabi, gashi silar haka na gane dan uwana.... Allah nagodemaka... "
Ta idar da maganar tare da daukar wayarta tasoma laluben lambar ummu dan tayi mata albishir.. 
Dakatar da ita AJ yayi "kiyi hakuri ammie ba yanxu ba idan kika fadawa ummu na tabbatar bazata iya hakuriba dole sai tace zatazo nigeria , ga jikkn tsufa, sannan shikansa babah muna so mu saka malamai su fara yimasa addua har yadawo hayyacinsa kafin mu sanar dashi komai "... 
Shiru ammie tayi cikin gamsuwa da zancensa  kafin ta jinjina kai tare da ajiye wayar... 
Sun dade suna tattauna wa kafin su yiwa ammie sallama... 

Har suka shiga mota deenah na matsar kwallah, dubanta AJ yayi tare da murmishi lokaci daya ya dauke kansa tare da fadar "wai little kukannan na farinciki bazai kare ba, nifa yafara isa ta, kinsan bana son yan koke_koke ".. 
Yayi maganar yana maido da dubansa ga gareta tare da daga mata gira... 
Harararsa ta danyi cikin shagwaba ta soma magana "hubby tun jiya kasan da hakan baka sanar min ba sai yanxu "... 
Cike da zolaya yayimata kallon sama da kas yace "saboda kwanyar yara gareki, brain din ki baxai iya daukar zancen lokaci daya ba amma yanxu kin fahimta ba? "... 
Yayi maganar yana mata dariyar zolaya tare da kashe mata ido... 

"Hubby nikeda kwanyar yara? Nifa na girma ka daina mayar dani baya "... 
Still cikin shagwaba tayi maganar.. 
Dariya ta basa sosai "im sorry bazan kuma ba"
. 
Sai alokacin tayi murmishi tare da yar harararsa... 

"Zoki kwanta".. 
"Aina??"
Ta tambayesa cikin rashin fahimta... 

"Inda kika kwanta dazu, naji dadin hakan, na manta tsayin lokacin da naji dumin jikinki haka ".. 
Ya karashe zancen yanayin kasa da murya... 
Murmishi deenah tayi tana cigaba 
Da kallonsa yayimata wani irin kalar tausayi... 
"Please! ". 
Yayi maganar a marairaice... 
Wannan lokacin dariya ya bata... Cikin yar dariyarta ta kwantar da dukkan jikinta ajikknsa, hakan ya basa damar rungumeta tsam ajikinsa yana cigaba da sauke ajiyar zuciya... 

Bayan komawarsu gida deenah na shiga dakin ta soma yunkurin kiran yadikko dan ta sanar da ita babban abun mamakin da ya bayyana ayau... 
Cikin sa'a ta samu yadikko batare da samun tangardar network ba.. Bayan sun gama gaisawane deenah ta fada mata komai, sosai yadikko tayi mamakin jin  haka, ta kuma sanarwa da deenah cewa  bada jimawa ba zata shigo cikin garin... 

Cikin dare kuwa AJ gaza bacci yayi, ya tsinci kansa da tuna abubuwan da suka faru a ranar, farinciki yake sosai idan ya tuna banda alakar auratayya deenah yar uwarsa ce kuma kanwarsa ta jini, yanajin dadin kasantuwar hakan sosai, yana yiwa deenah wani irin so ne da shi kansa bazai iya misultashi ba... 
Yana akwance ringingine  ya Lumshe idanunsa  yasoma  biting lips dinsa yana fatan allah ya ida baiwa deenah lafiya, ya nuna maya yadda yayi matukar kewarta dan kuwa zuwa yanxu hakurinsa na rashinta yasoma zuwa karshe, ita kadaice ke da ruwa mai yaye masa k'ishin sa, ya tabbatar da deenah daban take...
Tunanin maganganun da nabila ta fadamasa ne a office ya kuma fado masa, yagaza goge maganganun a kwalwarsa... Kusan kowane lokaci sai yayi tunanin maganganun ta, vayason ya cutar da nafisa ta hanyar zarginta da abunda bata aikataba, dan baya tunanin nafisa zata iya cutar da madinah, bare kuma hulɗa da mazan bariki ya fiso yana aiki da hujja.. Komai kenan zaicigab da addua, allah ya baiwa deenah lafiya, allah ya bayyana masa sirrin da ke boye idan akwai shi... 
Furzan sa iskar bakinsa yayi ya tashi zaune tare da rike kansa cikin wani irin yanayi marar daɗi... 
Duban agogon dake manne abangon dakin yayi data nuna 2:37am tashi yayi ya shige toilet ya dauro alwala.. Ya cigaba da jera nafilfilu, da addu oin kariya da ga sherrin masu sherri... 
Bashi ne yassmu bacci ba sai bayan sallar asuba, alokacinne ya samu bacci ya daukesa... 
Tundaga wannan lokacin ta kowane fanni IG ya sanya  malamai addua da sauke alkurani duk sati, haka itama ammie anata bangaren, duk ranar jumaa sai malamai sunyi saukar alkurani da addu oi musamman na karya sihiri... 
Babah kuwa tun ranar da deenah tabar gidannan, ya shiga cikin wani irin yanayi marar daɗi, tambayar da deenah tayi masa ta fama masa tsohon mikinsa ya dawo da sabon rauni da zugi a zuciyarsa, ya kuma jin ya tsani jin koda sunan garin agadez, bare yayi alfahari da shidin dan cikin masarautar ta ne... Hatta goggo asabe ta shiga cikin hankali ganin yadda babah ya sauya lokaci da ya.. babu yadda batayi da shiba akan ya sanar mata abinda ke damunsa amma yaki saidai kawai yacemata babu komai... Tasan ruwa  baya   tsami banxa koma miye zata cigaba da yimasa addua, allah ya warware masa matsalolinsa ko wadanne irine domin  matsala ita ke haifar da damuwa... 

Cikin hukunci da buwayar ubangiji abubuwa nata warwarewa haƙiƙa duk wanda ya yi tawakkali da allah to ya isar masa da komai, daman kuma karshen alewa kasa kuma karshen tika_tika tik!! 
Ta kowane bangare ganin sauki da warwarewar al amurra sukeyi.. 
Deenah kuwa haka kawai takejin jikinta tamkar wacce tayi ciwo ta warke, wani irin lafiya takeji ajikinta da nutsuwar zuciya dake shigarta saɓanin da... 

Babah kuwa tun zuwan yadikko gidansa tayi tsinke, anan take fadamasa zuwan deenah akauue ta kuma sanar da ita abinda yagaxa fada mata domin ta haka ne zata samu nutsuwa... 
Tunda yadikko ta fara magana babah yayi shiru bai ce komai ba hakan yasa yadikko tausasa murya ta soma magana "tsayin shekaru talatin da biyu kenan rabonka da mahaifarka, abdullahi bakada makankara da asalinka kuma ba a sauya asali... Kamar yadda ba a sauya kaddara, haka baka isa ka sauya kasancewarka jinin sarautar agadez ba saboda wasu tsiratun bata garin cikinta, yakamata ka waiwayi asalinka kodan saboda ka nemawa zuriarka mutunci.... "

Ajiyar zuciya babah ya sauke shikansa yana jin yana  yawan tunanin kasarsa da son komawa asalinsa, to amma bayason komawarsa ta haifar da wani abun ayanxu, duba da masu son kansu da mulkinne akan karagar, amma baida zabi da ya wuce bin maganar yadikko, .. 
Duban yadikko yayi ya sauke ajiyar zuciya kafin yace "shknan, ina fatan hakan yazama alkhairi amma bazan zauna atare dasu ba.... "
Murmishi yadikko tayi domin ko iya hakan ya wadatar taji dadin hakan sosai, tashiga sanyawa babah albarka... 
Ammie kuwa jin  zuwan yadikko ne agidansu deenah ta saka drivernta ya kawota,... 
Sosai yadikko taji dadin zuwan ammie domin kuwa tazo adaidai lokacin da ya dace... 
Bayan sun gama gaisawa da babah goggo asabe ta debo mata ruwa mai sanyi ta ajiye agaban ammie suka kara gaisawa.. Tana kokarin tashi ne ta basu wuri yadikko ta tsayar da ita akan ta zauna domin itama tana da hakkin sanin komai .. 
Yadikko da ammie suka shiga yimasa bayanin da ya sakasa matukar  mamaki... Duban ammie yayi da take zubda kwallah mai tattare da tausayin dan uwannata kafin ya dubi yadikko lokaci da ya ya mayar da kanshi kasa yana ambaton sunan allah cikin nutsuwa... 
Lokaci daya yar siririyar kwallah ta samu nasarar zubomasa ya saka hannunsa ya share. 
Goggo aaabe kuwa da yanxu tasan abinda ke faruwa tashiga cikin matsanancin mamaki da al ajabi har hakan yagaza boyuwa a fuskarta... 
ammie kuwa kuka take marar sauti tana kuma kara godewa allah, babah ma lokaci bayan lokaci yake goge kwallah... Hatta goggo asabe saida ta share nata kwallar.. 
Yadikko kadai mai sauran karfin hali ta fara magana "yanxu ba kuka ne abunyiba, domin ubangiji ya warware mana matsalolinmu lokaci daya... Wannan mu daukesa amatsayin jarabta da kuma qaddara da bawa ba ya iya tsere musu... Zulunci baya taba dorewa haka duk nisan dare gari zai waye..... ".. 
Jinjina kai babah ya shigayi alamun gamsuwa da zancen ta... Anan ne kuma suka fitar da lokaci na koma wa kasarsu da suka bari... 

Ammie kuwa bayan komawarta gida taso kiran ummu ta sanar da ita, to amma tasani farinciki bazai barta tazauna ba har sai tace zata zo nigeria, ta fison idan suka dawo daga niger ta sanar mata komai daganan kuma zasu zo saudiyyar tare da babah yaga sauran yan uwan mahaifiyarsa. 
Bayan sallar issha takira AJ tasanarmasa komai... Hamdala ya shigayi cikin tsananin farinciki yadda komai ke warware wa salin alin... 
Bayan ya gama wayar ne ya sanarwa da deenah har lokacin da aka tsaida na zuwa agadez... 
Dan farinciki kuka ta sanya tare da fadawa jikinsa tana cigaba da kuka... 
Hannayensa ya saka yasake manna ta acikin faffadan kirjinsa, yagaza furta komai sai sanyayyar ajiyar zuciya da yake saukewa mai dauke da zallar nutsuwa... Azuciyarsa kuwa hamdala ya shiga yiwa ubangiji... 

deenah ce zaune amain falo suna kallo ita da sultan da rabin hankalinsa na akan wayar sa da yake dubawa... 

Iftahal ce ta shigo falon tare da sallama suka amsa mata ta nemi guu ta zauna ta dubi deenah tace "aunty wai meke faruwa ne da aunty nafisa? "... 

"Me kika gani ne? "
Deenah ta tambayeta... 
Na  fita zan ba gate keeper ya siyomin recharge card na biyo takan windonta naji sautin kukanta, kuka fa take sosai tamkar wani ke dukanta, kuma na lura a kwanakinnan tafison zama asashinta ita kadai kamar mayya, sam bata walwala.... "
Tabe baki deenah tayi, kafin tayi magana sultan ya dubi iftahal cikin tsare gida irin na mahaifinsa da ya dauko ya soma magana "to meye ta shafeki da abinda takewa kuka, kukan jin dadi ne takeyi tunda babu wanda yayimata laifi, ko hadiye zuciyarta zatayi babu abinda ya shafeki, ki mayar da hankali akan abinda ya shafeki... "
Shiru iftahal tayi tare da jan bakinta ta tsuke... 
IGP ne ya shigo falon dauke da murmishi a fuskarsa, wani irin kallo ya jefi deenah da shi mai saukar da kasala.. Saurin dauke kanta tayi, ta lura sam bayaji kunya cikin yayansa yadinga nuna mata maitar soyayyarsa... 
Shima zama yayi adaya daga cikin  kujerun da ke falon suka shiga fira wacce baki dayanta akan tafiyar karatun sultan canada ne... Suna cikin firar ne babu zato deenah taji wani amai ya tafo mata yunkura wa tayi zata tashi da sauro sakamakon yunkurin aman da tasomayi tana kokarin tashi ne jiri ya kwasheta, zata fadi akasa IGP yayo saurin tarato tafada ajikinsa tacigaba da zazzaga aman tamkar zata zazzagar da hanjinta... 
Bakidayansu sun rude... Cikin tashin hankali AJ ya soma kiran docternsu musamman yadda yaga deenah tayi ligi².. 
Cak ya dagata bai zame da ita ko ina ba sai bedroom dinta, atoilet dinta ya wuce ya wanke mata jikinta ya taimakamata ta sauya kaya ta kwantar da ita sai faman sannu yakeyimata... 
Shikansa saida ya sauya kayan jikinsa  dan bakidaya sun baci da amanta... 

Koda likita yazo bai bata lokaci ba ya fara duba deenah da bincika lafiyarta, duk wasu gwaje² da aune aunensa sun nuna masa deenah nada shigar ciki na tsayin sati goma sha uku... 
Haka kuma ya sanarwa da AJ... 
Cikin tsananin farinciki da mamaki yake duban deenah kafin ya mayar da dubansa ga likitan farincikknsa ya gaza boyuwa tamkar wannan shine karo na farko da zai samu baby.. Hamdala ya shiga yiwa ubangiji... 
Sultan da iftahal  dmkallon juna sukayi tare da murmishi mai tattare da jin dadin abinda likitan ya fada... 

AJ kuwa har compound suka fito tare da likitan, anan ne ya ke sanar masa deenah tana period saboda ko watan da ya wuce taga period dinta daman hakan yana faruwa ne? "... 
Cikin kwantar masa da hankali likitan ya nuna masa wannan ba damuwa bane domin mata da yawa sunayin hakan saidai idan abinda ke cikin ya kara girma ana kula dashi yadda yakamata... 
Sallama sukayi da likitan ya shige motarsa bayan ya samu gamsuwa da jawaban da yayi masa.. 

Koda ya dawo dakin deenah bai tarar da su sultan ba.. Cikin nutsuwa ya karaso bakin gadon ya zauna agefenta yana kallin fuskarta yadda take bacci a nutse, bakaramin kyau take karamasaba idan yana kallonta awannan yanayin... 
Hannunsa ya sa akan damammen cikin ta da bazaka taba cewa akwai dan mutum acikiba... 
A hankali ya shafi cikin har zuwa kasan mararta... Lokaci daya ya sumbaci cikin, sannan ya dago dakansa ya manna lips dinsa akan nata yabata hot peck... 
Lullubeta ya danyi da blanket ya fito tare da rufo mata kofar zuciyarsa fal da farinciki da nutsuwa... 
Tundaga lokacin yashiga tarairayar deenah kamar kwai, maganar karatunta ma cewa yayi babu wannan maganar, ba zata wahalar masa da kanta da babynsa ba ta bari sai next uper year lokacin baby yafara wayau.. 
Tasan halinsa saran i idan yayi magana baya sauyawa, tilas ta hakura, ta cigaba da laulayin cikinta da zuwa yanxu tafarajin motsin abinda ke cikinta.. 
Koda lokacin tafiyarsu agadez yakaraso , sam IG yaki yarda ayi tafiyar da deenah wai karamin ciki ne da ita babu inda zataje.. Wannan lokacin kuka ta saka masa sosai ta nuna masa zallar yarintarta, saida ammie ta taka masa burki ta nuna masa sam bai isa ba sannan ya barta, shima badan yasoba... 

Ranat laraba da misalin karfe biyu da rabi, jirginsu ya daga zuwa kasar niger.....

*_51_*

Tunda jirginsu ya dira kasar niger babah yakejin wani irin yanayi, baki daya komai ya sauyamasa, sabanin sanin da yayiwa kasar shekaru 32 da suka wuce, komai a zamanance yake tafiya... 
Yadikko kuwa bata kuma cika da mamaki ba saida suka shigo garin  agadez musamman acikin babbar masarautar, komai ya canja, lallai komai tafiya yake yi da zamani... 
Deenah kuwa sosai tsarin masarautar agadez ta kayatar da ita,  gogg asabe  kuwa da wannan lokacinne ta taba shigowa kasar niger bata ita cewa komai ba kallon yanayin kasar da mutanen ciknta take. babah kam sai alokacin yakejin komai na dawo masa sabo musamman rasuwar mahaifin sa marigayi mai martaba, shikadai yasan halin da zuciyarsa take ciki.. Har suka shiga ciki n masarautar... Tirkashi.. Anan fa kallo yakoma sama, domin duk inda su babah da yadikko suka saka kafa sai an tsaya andubesu duba mai tattare da tsananin mamakin ganinsu, yayinda wasu ke kokarin shaidasu, wasu kuwa sungama shaida fuskokinsu.. Tun kafin isowarsu fada, tuni labaru yazo kunnen sarki sufyaan, babban ɗa ga marigayi sarki abduljabbar wanda shine ya gaje kujerar.... 
Kallo daya zaka yiwa sarki ka fahimci tsantsar tashin hankali da fuskarsa ta gaza boyewa ..  Har zuwa lokacin da su babah suka shigo cikin fadar... Anan fa fada tadauki shiru, kowa mazurai yakeyi da raba idanu... Waziri kuwa hamdala ya shiga yiwa ubangiji na dawowar yarima andullahi.. Cikin karfin hali  sarki sufyan ya share gumin da ta tsatssafomasa ya na duban su banah da suka tsaya  abakin kofar fada, suna kokarin karasowa gun sarkin, sarki  sufyaan ya rigasu isowa gunsu... Cikin wani irin yanayi mai tattare da damuwa sarki ya dubi babah tare da dauke kansa lokaci daya, ya dubi deenah da babu tantama yasan ya ce ga Abdullahi kafin ya sauke dubansa ga yadikko da itama dubansa take tagaza furta komai, haka goggo asabe sai kallon ikon allah take..  cikin tsananin jin kunyarsu sarki ya shiga yimusu sannu da zuwa, abun mamaki sarki da kansa yayi musu jagora zuwa tsakiyar fadar..., anan fa aka fara gaishe dssu babah cikin tsantsar girmamawar da akeyiwa jinin sarauta... 
Cikin sanyin jiki  maimartaba ya koma ya zauna akan karagar sa ya dubi daya daga cikin hadimansa da ke fada ya saka akiramasa jakadiya... 
Waziri ne ya magantu cikin girmanawa "allah yabaka nasara, ka manta jakadiya   na halin rashin lafiya domin kuwa zuwa  yanzu batasan waye akanta ba... 
Jinjina kai sarki yayi ya cigaba da umurtar waziri da a basu masauki mai kyau na alfarma akuma shigar da akwatunansu  aciki.. 
Kakkyawan masauki aka kai su babah, lokaci daya aka hadamusu da hadimai, abinci kuwa na alfarma iri iri aka shiga gabatar musu dashi... 
Sosai yadikko  da goggo suka ci abincin deenah kuwa fruits kawsi tasha dan matukar taci abinci musamman mai nauyi sai tayi amai... 
Baba kuwa kadan yaci shima dan bayason yadikko ta damu. 
Acikin gida kuw, kawo yanxu kowa ya gama sanin dawowarsu yadikko da yarima abdullahi...  
 Gimbiya sunaima da murshida kuwa duk da bakar gabar da ta shiga tsakaninsu hakan bai hanasu jajantawa junansu ba game da dawowar yarima abdullahi sun shiga matsanancin tashin hankali basu kadaiba hatta masu mara musu baya.. Domin sunsan dawowarsu daidai take da ɗaiɗaituwar su da kuma tozarci da jin kunya akan abinda suka aikata, sosai suke kokarin samawa kansu mafita.. 
Daddare kuwa mai martaba yasaka waziri yayi masa kiran babah... Ba tare da bata lokaci ba babah yabi bayan waziri har zuwa babban falon mai martaba da ya gaji da haduwa... 
Karasowa babah yayi tare da sallama, waziri kuwa cikin girmamawa yayi wa mai martaba saida safe ya wuce... 
Sarki sufyan dake zaune kan kujera ya amsa sallamar tare da mikewa tsaye yana tarbar kanen nasa cikin murmishi da sakin fuska...  
Akujerar dake fuskantar mai martaba babah ya zauna kamar yadda sarki ya umurcesa... 
Shiru yadan biyo baya, kafin sarki yayi gyaran murya yasoma magana cikin jin kunya da damuwa.... "Nasani daman komin dadewa rana irin wannan zata zo, daxaka waiwayi  ahalinka, domin ko ina dai akaje gaskiya gaskiya ce bata taɓa canjawa... "
Shiru mai martaba yayi still yagaza  hada ido da babah cikin raunanniyar murya yacigaba  da magana  "mun cutar da kai abdullahi saboda bukatar mu ta duniya mun aikata don zuciya munyi burus da maganar marigayi muka cutar da rayuwarka, yanxu dubeni dai acikin sarautar, amma jina nake kamar ina cikin masifa da bala i, ni kadai nasan bala in da nake ji, bayan da aka wayi gari babu kai babu yadikko, munyi abinda mukeso kuma abinda zuciyoyinmu suka ayyana mana, mun dauka tafiyarka maslaha ce da zata kawo mana nasarar cimma karagar mahaifinmu, ashe abun ba haka bane daga nan wata sabuwar rigimar ta barke, musu da gardama ya shiga tsakanin iyayenmu mata kowace tace dole daga cikin yayanta zaa fitar da sarki, cikin kankanin lokacin masarauta ta harmutse batare da ta samu mai jagorantar taba... Ankai gwauro ankai mari, fitina na ci gaba da wanzuwa acikin gidakan nadin sarauta, har saida hukumar zartarwa ta fada tashigo cikin maganar gida... Inda ananne aka tsayar da ni nine zan gaji kujerar amatsayina na babban da acikin maza agun mai martaba, to tamkar dai fetur aka kara zubawa wutar gidan , dan gimbiya Goggo sunaima tsalle tayi ta dire akan bata yarda ba hakan kuma bazai faruba domin itace babbah acikin yayanta zaa fitar da sarki duk da akwai manyansu, saidai bata samu nasara ba domin hukumar fada ta zartar da hukunci, wannan bakincikinne ya sanya suka shiga nunawa junansu matsananciyar gaba da kiyayya kuma suka shiga aikawa kawunan su da  bakin  nufi ta karkashin kasa... ".. 
Shiru mai martaba yayi na dan wani lokaci  cikin wani irin yanayi ya nisa   ya cigaba da magana "hakika mun zubar da martabar wannan masarauta da iyayenmu da kakanninmu suka jima suna tattalinta, kaicho munji kunya... "
Ya karashe maganar yana kuka tamkar wani yaro... 
Cikkn wani irin yanayi babah yasoma rarrashinsa "kadaina kuka mai martaba, har yanxu kana da damar da zaka dawo da kimar masarautar nan"... 


"Abdullahi me zanyi idan banyi kuka ba? ".. 
Cikkn kukan mai martaba ya katse babah yadora da cewar "sakamakon abinda muka shuka ne muke girbewa, yanxu ayanayin da nake ciki duk dare bana iya bacci saidai nayi kwanan zaune, idan na kwanta kuwa haka zaayita firgita ni,  haka zan kama ihu kowa najina kamar wani zautacce , bazan samu bacci ba sai gari yayi haske, malamaina sun tabbatar min sihiri ne aka jefeni dashi, bana tantamar cewa wannan aikin na yan uwa nane, ga mahaifiyata cutar ɗimuwa ta shafeta, jakadiya kuka sai ankwantar sai antayar duk lalurar uzirinta daga kwance takeyinsa, bakidaya kowa ya kosa da ita agidannan, gimbiya  
 Goggo sunaima ma, wani lokacin wasu abubuwa takeyi tamkar  dai zararriya wani lokacin har sai an rufeta a daki ko kuma ankira likita ya danna mata allura kafin asamu lafiyarta, wannan kuma bakomai  bane face sherri da muke jifar junanmu dashi, baki daya son zuciya ya saka mun illata rayuwarmu da goben yayanmu.. 

Babah kuwa sai jin mai martaba sufyan yayi kasa yarike kafafunsa yana neman afuwarsa... Dasauri baba yayi saurin dagosa yana girgiza masa kai "haba yaya wannan bai kamata ba, kaifa yayane kuma sarkin wannan gari kuma kake rike kafafuna dan neman gafara, wlh ban taba kullatarkuba, fatana baiwuce ubangiji ya ganar daku gaskiya ba.... "
Maimartaba yana kuka babah ya yamar dashi kan kujera ya zaunar dashi... 
Batare da ya damu da share hawayensa ba yaci gaba da magana "fiye da hakanma zanyi abdullahi dan neman yafiyarka, ita gaskiya daya ce kuma ubangiji jan bayyana ta dan kawo karshen azzalumai da zalunci, akullum gaskiyardai ita ce saman karya mai gaskiya shike nasara akowane lokaci ban san haka ba, saida nashiga makarantar wahala na dauki darasin dole na duniya.... "

"Wlh na yafemaka yaya, da duk yan uwana ina fatan kadaina tuna abubuwan da suka wuce "... 
Cewar babah.. 
Cikkn murmishi tare da kuka mai martaba ya share hawayensa lokaci daya ya rungume babah yana cigaba da hawaye yanayimasa godiya... 
Shikansa babah zuwa lokacin hawayen yake zubarwa... 
Dagosa mai martaba yayi yana sharemasa hawayen ya dubesa yace "gobe in allah yakaimu da safe  akwai babban taro da zaayi  a fada, taro ne na kowa da kowa wanda nine na shirya hakan ina fatan zaku halarta... 
Cikin murmishi babah ya jinjina masa kai tare da fadar "insha allah ranka shi dade... "

"Shknan kaje ka kwanta na tabbatar akwai gajiya har yanxu a tattare da kai, idan kaje ka gaishemin da yadikko ina tsananin jin kunyarta, sannan ka gaishemin da iyalanka da yarinyarmu (deenah)... 
Murmishi sosai babah yayi ya amsa da "insha allah zan isar da sakon ka mai martaba, nabarka lafiya"... 
Cikin girmamawa yayi masa sallama.. 
Sarki sufyaan kuwa bin bayan babah yayi da kallo cikin burgewa har ya fice, duk cikin su, shikadai ne keda tsarkakkiyar zuciya ya fisu nagarta takowane fanni, wannan shine abinda mahaifinsu keson nuna musu amma suka saka son zuciya sukayi facali da maganganunsa... 

Tunda babah ya fita yadikko bata rintsa ba hankalinta na gunsa batasan kuma menene zaifaruba da kiran da sarki sufyaan ya yi masa... Shima babah koda yadawo saida ya shigo dakin da su yadikko suke duk sunyi bacci banda ita da take jiran dawowar babah, saida yayi mata bayanin yadda sukayi da sarki sufyaan  dan ta samu nutsuwa  yakuma shaidamata akwai taro afada da mai martaba ya sanar masa kafin yayi mata saida safe sannan ta kwanta da tunani fal azuciyarta... 

Da safe kuwa tun wurin karfe 9:00am fada ta amsa kiran mai martaba ta cika ta tumbatsa da dan mutum..jiran fitowar mai martaba kawai akeyi.. 
Sai 9:30am sarki sufyaan yashigo fada shida hadimansa... 
Babah kuwa wuri na musamman aka tanadar masa shida iyalansa, kallo² ne ya shiga tsakanin su gimbiya sunaima da dasu yadikko , bakuma su jin zasu iya tanka wa su yadikko dan ji da isa da sarauta, itama yadikko watsar da su tayi, ta kuma nunawa babah da bata yarda ya gaishe da kowa ce ba.. 

Gyaran murya sarki yayi  da ya sanya fadar  ya kuma daukar shiru cikin nutsuwa ya mike tsaye yasoma magana "kamar yadda kowa yasani da yawa yawan tarihin sarakuna kan bar mulkine alokacin da  waadin kwanakinsu aduniya ya kare.. To amma wannan karin abun ya sha banban ga abinda kowa yasani gameda hakan, a yau zan ajiye sarauta, sarauta zata koma a inda take inda ya kamata ta zauna.... "
Kalaman sarki yasaka kowa shiga matsanancin mamaki da tunani iri iri, gimbiya murshida kuwa jin kalaman dan nata yasaka ta cikib tashin hankali.. 
A hankali mai martaba ya nufo gun da su babah suke ya riko hannun babah da shikansa cikin rashin fahimta yake dubansa... 
Dubansa mai martaba yayi yakira sunansa "Abdullahi, da yanxu kaine sarkin wannan masarauta domin irinka wannan masarautat ke bukata.. 

Cikin mamaki babah yake duban mai martaba "me kake fada haka ranka shi dade, wlh banxo kasar nan dan rabaka da mulkinka ba kuma ni bana shaawat mulki ko can, nakuma dawo kasar nan saboda... "

A'ah abdullahi "... 
Mai martaba ya katsesa ya cigaba da cewa "irinku ne masarauta ke bukata alamun adalin sarki shi ne  wanda ya na gudu mulki na binsa bawai wanda yake gudu yanabin mulki ba, idan kuma kaki amincewa zan kasance sarkin da yaji kunya acikin masarautar  nan..... "

"Zancen banza zancen wofi me kake fada ne haka? ".. 
Cewar mahaifiyar sarki sufyaan cikin tashin hankali... 
Karasowa sarki yayi gabanta yadanyi kasa da muryarsa "ummah kinsan komai, son zuciyar ya isa haka, hukuncin da na yanke bazai taba canjawa ba... "
Baijira abinda zata fada ba ya juya ya koma gun babah xake tsaye cikin mamaki... 
Bakin ciki ya hana gimbiya murshida zama fadar kawai ta fice itada mukarrabanta... 
 Sarki kuwa cigaba da magana da babah yayi "karka manta burin mahaifin mu da wasiyyarsa akan cewa kai ne magajinsa, ka cika masa wannan burin, kuma ka dubi yadda tsufa ya kamani shekaruna sunja abdullahi "... 
Shiru babah yayi kafin ya dubi yadikko ta gyada masa kai cikin murmishi... 
Shiru yayi mai nuna amincewa da yasaka mai martaba rungumesa cikin farinciki... 
Bakidaya fada ta dauki kabbara... 
Deenah kuwa kukan farinciki ta saka alokacin da taga ana nada mahaifinta amatsayin sarki... 
Mutane da yawa zuciyarsu cike take da farinciki, yadikko fa goggo asabe kukan farinciki suka shigayi... 

Bayan kwanaki biyu da na da babah sarki, aka nemi gimbiya murshida aka rasa tayi batan dabo bat, alokacin ne allah yayiwa jakadiya huraira rasuwa daga doguwar jinyar da takeyi... 
Allah sarko duk d cuta da cin amana da tayiwa babah saida yaji mutiwarta, yakuma saka aka mutunta gawarta aka kaita makwancinta da fatan ubangiji ya gafarta mata zunubanta... 
Gimbiya sunaima  ma ganin yadda yayanta sukayimata bore suka koma bayan gaskiua suka nemi afuwar sarki abdullahi yasanya itama tazubar da makaman yakinta ta mika wuya, cikin jin kunya mai tattare da kaskanci ta nemi afuwar babah kuma ya yafe mata, duk gangar jikin da ke da zuciya mai kyau kullum cikin samun nasara arayuwa take! 
Bayan wasu  kwanaki, mai martaba ya umurci gimbiya deenah da tayi gaggawar komawa dakin mijinta domin  ta  debi kusan mako biyu a niger... 
Babu yadda deenah ta iya ta yi sallama da si yadikko da sauran yan uwanta.. Da zata tafi kuwa da hadimai shidda aka hada ta dasu... 
Cikin farinciki  da nutsuwa ta dawo  kasar nigeria.. 
Koda ta dawo, sosai IG ya nuna mata yadda ya yi kewarta, matukar gaske ya cutu na rashin ganinta da yayi... Shafa cikinta yayi wai yayi kewar babynsa.. Dariya kawai tayimasa ta fahimci tashen samartaka da baiyi ba ada shi ke dibar gogan nata yanxu ... 
Bayan sun nutsu ne take basa labarin abinda ya faru.. 

Fito da manyan idanunsa yayi cikin mamaki ya ke fadar "yanxu kenan babah shi ne sarki? "... 
Jinjina masa kai tayi alamar eh tana murmishi... 
Ajiyar zuciya ya sauke, hakika qudirar ubangiji ta fi karfin hankali da tunanin mutum.. 
Hamdala yayi ya cigaba da murmishi yana dubanta ya danyi kasa da murya cikin zolaya ya furta 'nikuma na zama angon gimbiya kenan? "... Yayi maganar yana kashe mata ido
Harararsa tadanyi lokaci daya tasaka masa dariya dan shima dariyar yakeyi kafin ya tsagaita ya ce "kenan shiyasa naga wasu mata na kai kawo a main falo masu kama da buzaye da kuma kayan yan agadez, hadimanmu ne ko? "... 
Cikn farinciki suka kuma saka dariya ta fada jikinsa still tana dariya ta fahimci matuka gogannata ya koma gwanin zolaya, ta gama fahimtar yadda yake jin dadin zolayarta.. 
ammie ma ta yi farinciki da mamakin labarin da deenah  ta sanar mata.. Ta kuma sake godewa allah komai yayi farko daman zaiyi karshe.. Kuma dukkan tsanani yana tare da sauki, kuma kamar hakane ubangiji kan sauya rayuwar bawansa ta inda baiyi tsammani ba... 

Bangaren nafisa tun ranar da suka gama waya da mommy ta shiga tsantsar tashin hankali musamman akan furucin malam kallah da yace aikin kaikayi ne koma kan mashekiya, gashi duk iyayen kudin da suke tura masa yagaza tabuka musu komai, ga rashin lafiyar hajjah jikin kullum daɗa tsanani yakeyi... Bata kuma tsorataba saida taje jumeta taga yanayin jikin hajjah tana kwance amma komai sai anyimata... Bakidaya abun duniya ya hade musu ya damesu, musamman mommy ga damuwar nafisa da kullum sai ta kirata tana mata kuka musamman babban tashin hankalinta da taji deenah nada ciki,  ga rashin lafiyar hajjah komai ya cakwalkwalemusu, mommy tarasa inda zata tsoma rayuwarta taji sanyi... 
Nafisa kuwa har lokacin bata daina turawa malam kallah mahaukatan kudade ba, duk abinda ta mallaka yasoma karewa, dan kawai tsoron kada abinda tayiwa deenah yadawo kanta, ga cikin da deenah ke dauke dashi da take fatan ya lalace dan hardashi tayiwa malam kallah bayani ... 
Data koma tambyar AJ kudade, da yaga yawan tambayar kudin  yayi yawa yasoma tuhumarta  abinda take da kudaden,.. Da ta rasa me zata fada masa kawai ta sanya masa kuka wai jarinta ne yayi kasa shi ne take son habaka sa, ya rasa gane kanta kwata kwata duk ta wani fige  tayi firi firi da ita kamar wata firgitatta... 
Bai bi takanta ba ya tura ma ta half million a account dinta... Kuma tundaga lokacin nafisa ta daina tambayarsa kudi... Harkokinta da take awaje kawai yake kawomata kudi.. Batada wata matsala illah deenah dake gabanta, dan yanxu abun nata tunxura yake duk lokacin da tafiya ta kwana ta kama IGP itama ka ma gabana takeyi sai ta tabbatar da ranar dawowarsa kafin ta dawo, zuwa yanxu hatta ma aikatan gidan sunfara zargin madam nafisa akan tafiye tafiyen datakeyi  sai dai kowa yaja bakinsa ya tsuke babu damar magantuwa... 

Yau kam da yamma likis motarta ta danno kai acikin harabar gidan... Ba ta damu da ma aikatan gidan dake faman jero mata sannu ba ta kutsa kanta zuwa main falo a matukar gajiye.... 
Sabbin fuskar da ta gani ajiya kagin ta fita yau ma sune ta sake gani suna kai ka kawo afalon... Yanayinsu da shigarsu basu yimata kama da na yan kasar nan ba... 

Monica ce tashigo falon dauke da tire a hannunta na fruits ta ajiye akan table.. Cikin yamutsa fuska nafisa ke tambayarta wadannan su din su waye... 
Cikin girmamawa monica ta soma magana "hadiman ranta shi dade ne, sune masu hidimta mata, kinsan yanayin al adun gidan sarauta... "

"Wacece kuma ranta shi dade? ".. 
Again cikin yamutsa fuska da rashin fahinta nafisa tayi tambayar.   

"Madam ina nufin madam deenah ashe ita din gimbiya ce "... 
Cikin son tsegumi monica tayi maganar.. 

Katseta nafisa tayi "ke dallah dakatamin, ni saar wasankice da zaki na fadamin wadannan banzayen maganganun "... 
Cin jin zafi  nafisa keyimata fada dan ta fara zargin monica zolayarta take mai yuwuwa kishinta ne akan madina da ta gani yasa take zolayarta... 

Deenah da ke upstairs tana kokarin safkowa cikin murmishi ta magantu dan duk ta gama jin abinda suke fada.. 

"Aunty baki  yarda da  abinda ta fadamiki ba? Amma ni idan na fadamiki zaki yarda ko? "
Deenah ta karaso falon tana murmushi tare da kallon tsakiyat kwayar idanun nafisa da itama dubanta take cikin bugawar zuciya da razana... 
Ajiyar zuciya deenah ta sauke ta zauna monica kuwa sumi² tabar falon... "To ya kikaga hukuncin allah? Wato ke tushiya uwar asali.. Ba ke din bace da iyayenki ku ke min gorin asali ba?  Hmm!  Na fa damu alokacin matuka amma ayanxu kullum sai na shimuku albarka domin ta silarkune na san kaina, akokarin ku na cutar dani sai gashi kun taimakeni, bazan miki alfahari ba amma yanxu ina da asali mai tsada da kudi baya siyensa, inada asalin da na wuce kananu irinku sumin gori... Ya abdulmajeed kuwa da kike takamar dan uwankine to ni nafiki kysanci dashi.. Idan baki samu gamsuwa ba kya iya neman karin bayani agunsa, Bazan cika ki da surutuba domin karin bayanin sanin asalina  kinemi jaridar waziriya hausa, sun wallafa nadin sarautarahaifina wato sarkin agadez a shafinsu... Domin da yawa yawan yan jaridun nigeria sun je sun ganewa idanunsu, karki dauka ina fadamiki hakanne cikin izza da takama, aah inaso ki fahimci kuskurenki kin fahimceni... "
Deenah kuwa bata jira me zata fadaba ta soma shan yayan itatuwanta tare da mayar da hankakinta akan TV bata kuma bin takan nafisa ba... 
Nafisa kuwa mazurai ta shigayi zuciyarta tashiga bugawa da karfin gaske... Bata iya fadar komai ba ta wuce sashinta da sauri... 
Da yar dariya deenah ta rakata ta tabbatar yau idan tana da hawan jini sai ya tashi... 
A ranar nafisa gaza rintsawa tayi kalaman deenah ke mata kai ka  kawo a kwanyarta... haka da ta tuntubi IG da maganar gameda gaskiyar abinda deenah ta fada mata... 
Cikin kokarin gamsar da ita ya tabbatar mata da hakan ya kuma fadamata yadda akayi ya gane deenah jininsa ne... 
Ya kuma duban yanayinta sosai tare da kiran sunanta "sau tari idan kayi kokarin cutar da wani babu gaira babu dalili sai cutuwar ta zame masa alkhairi ".. 
Cikin hannunka mai sanda ya fadamata magana... 
Gaza cewa komai nafisa tayi kawai tasaka kuka tare da barin falonnasa, tayi iya bakin kokarinta ta danne bakincinta amma tagaza... 
Kuka tashigayi mai sauti tamkar anyima ta bushara da mutuwar mommy, ta rasa ina zata saka rayuwarta kawai ta kira nabila ra zayyana mata komai... Ita kanta nabila sai da hankalinta ya tashi jin wacece deenah, domin taga nafin sarautar mahaifinta a rahama Tv Acikin labarai, koba komai taji dadi abubuwa na daɗa dagulewa nafisa, babban burinta bai wuce samun kafa guda daya da zata tadawa nafisa bomb.. 
Afili kuwa rarrashinta tashigayi ta kuma sanarmata asatin zata shigo kt su kuma tattaunawa... 

Koda nafisa ta ajiye wayar, kai tsaye locker ta nufa tabude ta dauko wasu kwayoyi ta afa abakinta tabi da ruwa.. Sannan tabi da wani syrup  tasha ta haye gado ta kwanta tana sauke ajiyar zuciya, sam batayi yunkurin fadawa su mommy wannan maganar ba dan tasan babu wani hoɓɓasa da zasu iya yi ta fahimci karfinsu ya riga ya kare ayanzu... 
Tunani kala² tashigayi har bacci yayi awon gaba da ita.. Haka take kasancewa matukar ta tsinci kanta a cikin matsananciyar damuwa, yan afe afen kwayoyinta takeyo ta kora da syrup ta hau bacci idan ta farka sai taji ta garas, tun agida tasaba da hakan.... 
Cikin yan kwanaki biyu tadanji nutsuwa ta kuma daina fitowa a main falo dan taki jinin ta fito tayi arba da deenah ko hadimanta wani irin bakinciki takeji nayi mata hakilo, musamman yadda yarinya karama (deenah) tasamu bakin gaigaya mata maganganu.. Sam ta tsani zaman gidan dan ta ko ina bata da power, idan har tana son farinciki saidai idan gantalinta ta fice... 

IGP Abdulmajeed ne zaune a office dinsa yana kokarin rufe wayarsa sakamakon meeting din dazasu shiga sako ya shigo ta wayarsa a gaggauce yashiga dubawa domin ya dauka deenah ce.... Saidai bakuwar number ce yagani. 
Cikin faduwar gaba ya Karawa eyeball dinsa girma  sakamakon ganin abinda rikitaccen sakon  da shigo wayarsa ya kunsa....

_"matarka kuma ƙawata na aminci hotel Room 41,tana can tana zubar da kimar igiyar aurenka da ke kanta, wannan lokacin ban damu da   ka yarda dani ko akasin hakaba,   amma  kaje ka ganewa idanunka zahirin gaskiya"!!!_
Cije lebensa IGP yayi tamkar zai hudasa ya runtse idanunsa tare da furzan da zazzafar iska abakinsa ya sake karanta text message din da zuwa yanxu ya fahimci daga wurin nabila sakon ya fito.. 
Dauke idanunsa yayi dasuka soma sauya kala  daga screen din wayar tasa, zai dauki mummunan mataki akan nabila idan bata daina shiga sabgar gidansa da ta ahalinsa ba ta fara kaisa bango, xai ladabtar da ita ta yadda zata gane ita din karamar yar bariki ce... 
Yana kokarin kiran number da ta turo masa sako ne, ta kirasa bai bata lokaci ba ya daga kiran tare da kangawa akunnensa, shiru yayi yana sauraron ta yana jiran jin sabon makircin da kazafin da zata kuma yiwa nafisa... Da hakan zai zama silar tarwatsewar farincikin rayuwarta... 
Cikin nutsuwa da farincikin cimma nasara nabila tasoma magana "yallabai baka da sauran lokaci na tabbatar kaga sako na, wallahil azeem matarka karuwa ce,kaje ka ganewa idanunka, idan kaga akasin haka duk hukuncin da ka dauka akaina na yarda, nasan kai kanka kafara zargin nafisa amma kana kokarin kawar da hakan domin baka da hujjar hakan  ,  kuma bakaso kana cigaba da zarginta saboda martabar aure ... "
Cikin wani irin yanayi ya datse kiran, barin office din yayi da sauri koda ya fito compound karbar key yayi daga hannun driver dakansa ya fixgi motar ya fice daga division din... 

Kai tsaye aminci hotel din ya nufa , da yake shidin sananne ne  kuma yana sanye da kayan  aikinsa ko da yaje reception bai bata lokacin gabatar da kansa ba ya soma tambayar ma aikacin da ke reception din spare   key din daki mai lamba arba in dadaya... 
Cikin girmamawa ya dauko ya mika masa tare  da rakasa har dakin ya nuna masa yakoma kan aikinsa..
Cikin wani irin yanayi da faduwar gaba ya bude kofar dakin ta re da kutsa kansa yana fatan abinda yake zato kar ya zama gaskiya.... 
Gaza motsawa yayi daga inda yake lokaci daya idanunsa ya kada yayi jajir jiyoyin dake boye akansa suka fito radau, lokaci daya jikinsa ya fara tsuma sakamakon mugun ganin da idanunsa sukayi kuma zahirin gaskiyar abinda nabila ta fadamasa, matarsa ta sunnah tare da wani kato a hotel akan gado daya suna fasikanci da mutumin da zai yi saar mahaifinta... 
Runtse idanunsa yayi da karfin gaske yana kokarin saita nutsuwarsa... 

Nafisa kuwa da shigowar IGP bagatatan kamar anjefosa ya sa ka su dawowa hayyacinsu... Cikin yanayin tsantsar tsorata da fargaba ta kwallah kara tare da dora hannu aka, cikin rudewa ta yi saurin janyo bargo dan kokarin boye tsiraicinta... Shikuwa abokin badalar  da suke wa laƙa bi da alhajin dala maxurai ya shigayi ya janyo boxer dinsa yana kokarin sakawa
IGP kuwa batare da yasake bude idanunsa akansu  ba ya juya yabar dakin cikin wani irin yanayi da yakejin tsanar nafisa da kyamarta, tsanar da bai taɓa yiwa wani mahaluki irinta ba.... 
Koda ya shiga mota cikin zafin da zuciyar sa ke masa ya fizgi motar yabar hotel din... 
Sam aranar bai samu damar halatar meeting dinba musamman yadda yake jin zuciyarsa da tunaninsa na dagulewa, abinda idanunsa suka gani ya kasa gogesa a zuciyarsada majigin idanunsa... 
Da daddare kuwa ko abinci bai iya ciba baisan wani irin mataki zai dauka wa nafisa ba, abinda ya sani kawai ya tsaneta ya kuma tsani ganinta, deenah kuwa ganin yanayin da ya shigo gidanne da yadda ya wuce sashinsa kai tsaye   ta tabbatar da damuwar dake damunsa dan ba hakan yasaba shigowa gidan ba... Bata bi ta kansa ba har shige ta barshi ne ya huta ya dan sauka daga yanayin yake kafin ta ji abinda ke damunsa, dan tasan matukar ransa abace yake, bazata taba bi dashi yadda ya kamata tafahimce sa sosai..
Har misalin karfe tara na dare nafisa bata shigo gidanba... 
IGP kuwa yana fitowada ga toilet bayan ya sakarwa kansa ruwa, wayars a ta fara vibrating, ganin bakuwar number da yayi, yayi kamar bazai daga ba dan yasan nabila ce kuma bayason jin wani abu da ya danganci nafisa... 
Daga kiran yayi, tare da yin shiru dan sauraren abinda zata kuma fada masa... 
Nabila kuwa jin yadaga wayar yasanya ta fara magana " yallaɓai  yanxu ka tabbatar da abinda na fada maka   ko? Sam bana nufin yiwa nafisa kazafi, zuwa yanxu kasancewa karuwa kake aure marar  kuma lasisi... "
Shiru nabila tadanyi tare da kumayin kasa muryarta "ina fatan baka manta alkawarin da ka daukarmin ba... "
Sai alokacin IGP yaja tsaki lokaci daya ya katse wayar  ya ajiye tare da zama gefen gado ya rike kansa yana fitar da wani irin numfashi.. 
Hakan yayi daidai da shigowar deenah a dakin tare da sallama, ganinsa da takumayi a irin yanayin da ya shigo ya sanyayamata jiki.. Cikin jiki ta karaso inda yake... 
Dubanta yayi da rinanmun idanunsa ya sauke ajiyar Zuciya yana kokarin  daidaita nutsuwar sa tare da miko mata hannu alamun ta zauna akusa dashi... 
Bata mika masa hannun nata ba tazauna ta na fuskantarsa cikin tsananin damuwa ta soma magana "hubby me ke damunka ne? "... 

"Banajin dadi ne, saboda na dan gaji yau a office ".. 
Yayi maganar yana fuskantarta... 
Kallon kwayar idanunsa tayi tare da girgiza kai ta kira sunansa "hubby ba haka kake kasancewa ba idan ka dawo daga office a gajiye me ke damunka "... 
Tsura mata idanunsa yadanyi yana nazarinta kafin ya dauke dubansa akanta "lafiya kalau nake little just is a matter, pls karki kuma tambayata wani abu "... 
Tadanji zafin yadda ya bata amsa, shi kansa baida zaɓi da wuce yabata wannan ta yadda zai dakatar da tambayar da takemasa, bazai iya tonawa nafisa asiri ba... Zai barta ne kawai da halinta amma ya haramtawa kansa ita... 
"Shknan ka huta lafiya saida safe".. 
Ta fada tare da mikewa tsaye tana kokarin barin dakin cikin jin zafi... 
Shima mikewa yayi tare da kamo hannunta lokaci daya ya rungumota jikinsa tare da rike waist dinta... Kafin hannunsa ya sauka akan cikinta da zuwa yanzu ya fara girma... Ahankali yakira sunanta asaitin kunnenta "little kar kiji haushi na pls, bana cikin yanayi mai dadi ne... ".. 
"Naji, ka kyale na tafi na kwanta ".. 
Tayi maganar atakaice.. 
"Baki huce ba ko? To ba inda zaki tafi "... 
Sai alokacin ta dago da kanta ta dubesa ahankali ta soma magana "na fahimceka hubby, akwai damuwar da bai kamata ka tilasta sai ka jita, akwai damuwar kuma da ya kamata abayyana, akwai kuma damuwar da lokaci ke bayyana ta, ko ma miye ina fatan ubangiji ya saukakamaka ita komai ya zo maka da sauki "... 
Murmishi ne yafara wanzuwa afuskarsa still yana kallon cikin idanunta matukar gaske deenah ta fahimcesa tamkar yadda jalila ke fahimtarsa alokacin da take raye aduk lokacin da ya shiga damuwa, hakika bakomai ne ya kai ka sani ba musamman agun da kasan bazaa boye maka komai ba dan komai na da sirri arayuwa... 
Hot peck ya manna mata agoshinta lokaci daya ya rungumeta yana amsa adduarta wata irin zazzafar soyayyar ta na kuma ratsa sa... 
Cikin sanyin jiki deenah ta zame jikinta tana dubansa tare da mirmishi sannan tayi masa saida safe ta rufo masa kofar... 
Da murmishi yabi bayanta yana sauke ajiyar zuciya... 

Nafisa kam tun bayan fitar IG daga dakin, ta fice daga hayyacinta kuka takeyi sosai harta saka kayanta agaggauce ta shirya ta fice cikin dimaucewa ko takan alhajin dala bata biba da yake kokarin tsayar da ita wai ta tsaya tayi wanka... Harta shiga mota ta fizgeta tafice daga hotel din kuka takeyi bakidaya ta dimauce tunaninta duk ya dagule.. Itakam ta shiga uku da ganin wannan ranar ta tozarci agareta ta tabbatarwa kanta koda itace autar mata IGP Abdulmajeed ya gama zaman aure da ita, hakan na nufin tonuwar asirinta.. Ina zataje da wannan abun kunya.. Tana tukin ta na kuka mai sauti... 
Bata yi kuskuren tunkarar gidan sa ba dan tasan komai zai iya faruwa tasan halinsa da zafin zuciyarsa duk da sautari yana kokari sosai wurin saita kansa... Dan bayason yin aiki da nadama... 
Cikin rashim makama ta nufi gidan ammie... Allah ne kawai ya kawota gidan lafiya dan tanayi parkin din motarta ta fito tana kuka har ta shigo cikkn falon ammie bata daina kukan ba... 
Ammie dake zaune afalo tana kallo cikin tsananin tashin hankalin ganin nafisa ta mike tsaye tana dubanta aranta tana karanto innalillahi wa inna ilaihir rajiun domin daga dukkan alamunta sun nuna ba lafiya take ba.. 
Nafisa kuwa cikin kuka mai tsuma zuciya ta karaso cikin falon tare da zaman dirshan akasa tana rairowa ammie kuka cikin rashin nitsuwa, ta shiga tashin hankali dan batasan irin hukuncin dazata fuskanta agun ammie ko ya AJ ba... Abinda ta sani kawai ta gama zubar fa kimarta a idanun ya Aj,...... 
Shi dama mutunci madara ne idan kuma ya zube baya taba kwasuwa, kuma ba a ɓari akwashe lafiya kamar yadda tsalle daya ake afada rijiya sai ayi dubu ba a fito ba.. 
Ammie kuwa tagama karaya da lamarin hankalinta yasoma tashi duban agogon dake manne abangon falon tayi data nuna karfe 9:30pm ta samu guu ta zauna tana tambayar nafisa abinda ke faruwa... 
Bakidaya tagaza yimata bayani dan batasan mezata fada mata, shin zata ce mata danta ne ya kamata ahotel tana fasikanci? Me zata fadawa ammie... 
Jin yadda nafisa ta gagara yimata bayani ne sai kuka ya sanya ammie dauko wayarta tasoma kiran layin AJ, bugu daya zuwa biyu ya daga kiran, kai tsaye cemasa tayi yazo ya sameta agida "
Ta fada tare da kashe wayar.. 
Sosai AJ yayi mamakin kiran gaggawar da ammie tayimasa yana fatan dai lafiya bai kawo komai a ransa ba ya zira jallabiyarsa ya dauki key din mota ya fito... 
Sosai ammie ke rarrashin nafisa da har lokacin bata daina kukan ba lokaci zuwa lokaci ammie ke sauke ajiyar zuciya... Bakidaya ta gaza fahimtar yanayin nafisa, abinda kawai tasani tana cikin tashin hankalin da ta gaza mata bayanin abinda ke faruwa.. 
Cikin mintuna 28 ya iso gidan... 
Yana shigowa falon nafisa ta yi kasa da kanta tana kuma tsananta rairo kukanta mai dauke da zallar nadama da jin kunya... 
Shikansa yana ganinta zuciyarsa ta fara tafasa da jin zafinta da wata irin wutar kiyayyarta dake ruruwa aziciyarsa ya tsane ta ya tsani ganinta, sautari yakan tuhumi kansa da tambayoyin me yasa ya auri nafisa, shi kansa bazai ce ga dalilin da ya sa ya auretaba, dan sam ba ta dace dashi ba domin hadinsa da ita tamkar hadin ganbixa ne... 
Dauke rinannun  idanunsa yayi daga kallonta, sai yanxu ya fahimci dalilin kiran da ammie takeyimasa... 
Tun kafin ya zauna ammie ta soma tambayarsa abinda ke faruwa dan hankalinta yasoma tashi... 
Saida ya zauna akasan carpet din ya dubi ammie da rinannnun idanunsa, kafin ya dan dubi nafisa da kanta ke kasa kunya ta hanata dago da kanta ta dubesa still sharar  hawaye takeyi har lokacin.. 
Maido da dubansa yayi ga ammie yasoma magana "na dauka ta miki bayanin komai.. "
"Babu abinda tafadamin sai kuka takeyi wai meke faruwa ne Abdulmajeed? ".. 
Duban nafisa ya kuma yi cikin yanayin tsanarta da kallon kyamata ya soma magana "zuwa da kikayi anan bashi ne zai samo miki maslaha ba, domin bazan taba kuskuren cigaba da kazamar mace irinki ba "... 
Rushewa da kuka nafisa ta kumayi sai alokacin tasamu bakin magana, "ammie ki saka baki kada ya ce zai sake  ni, wlh  ina sonsa, bansan me zan fadawa iyayena ba idan ya sakeni... Bazan iya zama awannan duniyar  ba, nayi masa laifi, dan allah kitayani basa hakuri ya yafe min "... 
Gyara zama ammie tayi cikin rashin fahimta ammie ta dubesa ta dubeta kafin ta tambayeta abinda tayi masa..... 
Shiru nafisa tayi cikin kunci da kunya da suka rufeta ta gaza magana... 
Cikin zafin zuciya AJ ya daka mata tsawar da har saida ta tsorata "kiyi mata bayani da bakinki!! "
Cikin rawar jiki da uban gumin da ya fara tsattsafo mata ta fara in- i-ina, batasan ta ina zata fara bayani ba kuma bata taba kawo wa ranta ya AJ zai iya titseyeta da tambaya agaban ammie ba ta yadda reshe zai juye da mujiya haka ba.. Cikin tashin hankalin da bata taba tsintar kanta acikiba tasoma fadawa ammie komai, karo na farko da ta kunyatar dakanta... Fashewa tayi da kuka tadora da cewa "nasan nayi kuskure na amsa laifi, wlh bazan kuma ba tsautsayine da qaddara, dan allah ammie kibasa hakuri nasan zai hakuri... "

Ba iya IGP kadai ba hatta ammie zuciyarta ta fara suya da jin bayanin da nafisa tayi mata ta kuma bata kunya, duban AJ ammie tayi ta yadda zata iya karanto tsantsar bacin rai da tunkushewar da zuciyarsa take ciki... Sauke ajiyar zuciya tayi ta dubi nafisa da ke tsammanin fitowar wata kalma daga bakinta... Girgiza kak ammie tasomayi cikin rashin jin dadi tasoma  magana "kinyi kuskure nafisa, kuma koda wasa kada ki kira hakan amatsayin qaddara fa ce son zuciyarki, kin zubar da ƙima da darajar aurenki, da darajar ahalinki... Babu abinda zan iya cewa sai abinda Abdulmajeed ya fada, domin wannan cin amana ne da rashin sanin darajar  aure,  duk hukuncin da ya yanke akanki bazan tilasta sa ko akasin hakaba.. ".. 
Aza hannu nafisa tayi aka "nashiga uku na lalace asiri na ya tonu"... 
Ta karashe maganar da kuka mai zallar nadama... 

"Bazan taba tonamiki asiri ba, amma bazan iya cigaba da zama dake amatsayin matar aure na ba, na tsaneki ta yadda bana kaunar koda kallon fuskarki, wlh bazan ce ga dalilin da ya saka na aureki ba... Kin cutar dani.... ". 
Cikin wani irin yanayi ya idar da maganar yana jifanta da wani irin kallo... 
Dauke dubansa yayi daga gareta yasm cigaba da cewa "na datse igiyoyin aurena duka akanki dan bazan taba sake zama dake ba bana bukatar sake ganin koda mekama da ke ne agidana ... "
Kuka takuma sakawa  mai sauti still hannayenta na akanta.. Cikin wani irin mummunan yanayi take rairo kukanta bata taba kawowa rayuwa zatayi mata irin wannan mummunan juyin ba..
 Gashi ta rasa ya AJ har abada, deenah tayi nasara itakuma tafadi.... 
Sam ammie bataji dadin yadda IG ya yanke hukuncin datse igiyoyin auren duka ba... 
To amma yanayin da yake ciki na gubar da nafisa ta shayar da zyciyarsa zai iya yin komai dan huce haushinsa.. 
Tunda kuma ya fadin hakan ya yiwa ammie saida safe ya fice, ita kanta bata taba ganinsa ayanayi irin na yau, tabbas abun da ciwo dan kuwa bakowane namiji zai ga matarsa turmi da tabaryaba, bai dauki wani mummunan mataki bayan saki ba matukar yana sonta, hakan kadai ya isa ya nunawa  nafisa batada sauran matsayi agunsa... 
Nafisa ta ga zallar tashin hankalin da bata tsba tsintar kanta acikiba, bata taba tunanin zata tsinci kanta cikin jin kunya da nadama ba... Tayi kuka har ta gode allah.. Ita kanta ammie da ta gaji da rarrashinta tashi tayo ta shige ciki ta barta anan... 
Dole anan nafisa ta kwana sai da safe tu nufi jumeta dan ko ammie batasan tafiyarta ba saidai ta fito taga babu ita hakan ya nuna mata cewar sammako tayiwa garin adamawa(jumeta)... 
Bakidaya abu bai yiwa ammie dadi ba, batason abinda zai tada hatsaniya ko kadan bare abinda zai kawo cikas musamman acikin zuriar mai nagge estate.. Abinda ta yi gudu kenan tun farko... 

Matukar gaske mommy tashiga cikin tsananin tashin hankalin ganin nafisa, musamman cikin irin yanayin data ganta kamar korarriya  bakikidaya fuskarta ta kumbure dan kuka idanunta sun kankace, dakyar  ta iya sanar da mommy IG ya saketa ... Cikin tashin hankali mommy tashiga tambayarta, saidai sam bata samu amsarta a bakin nafisa sai kuka da takeyi.. Haka mahaifinta ya tasa ta gaba da tambaya akan ta fadamasu dalilin daya sa Abdulmajeed ya saketa har saki uku arana tsaka haka... Yayi juyin duniya taƙi fadamasa ji takeyi zuciyarta tamkar zata yi bindiga dan abinda ya AJ yayimata tamkar kisan mummuƙe ne, ya ƙi fadar komai yabarta ta tonawa kanta da kanta asiri.. Mommy kuwa da tarasa bakin magana saboda takaici  alokacin kuka tasaka  Sosai, bakidaya tashiga tashin hankali ga rashin lafiyar hajjah sauki sai gurin allah dan zuWa yanxu bata gane waye akan ta komai sai anyi mata , likitoci kuwa sun yi iya kokarinsu dan fano cutar da ke damun hajjah amma sun gaza, hakan yasa suka dukufa da neman maganin gargajiya, ga nafisa kuma aurennata ya mutu... Kuka mommy take sosai bisa dole ita asiri aka musu idan ba haka ba ta yaya lokaci daya irin wadannan wadannan masifu suke fado musu akai akai dole akwai wanda ke jifarsu... 
Sosai daddyn nafisa ya kwatsi mommy akan irin maganganun da takeyi tamkar ba musulma ba sam batassn qaddara ba, bare tawakkali... 
Su baffah aminu kuwa da suka ga nafisa ta dawo gida dun dauka jikin hajjah tazo dubawa to saida sukaga har anyi kwanaki uku ba tada niyyar komawa gidanta, anan baffah aliyu ya soma fada wai tazo ta koma dakinta tunda taga jikin hajjar.. Anan ne fa mommy tasanar dashi..... 
Bashi kadai ba hatta  sauran yan uwansa hankalinsu ya tashi da jin cewa Abdulmajeed ya sako nafisa.. 
Koda suka tasa ta gaba harda mommy da daddynta da  tambayar abinda ya faru har ya saketa haka kuka ta saka musu.. Baffah aliyu da ransa yasoma baci da koke koken nafisa, fada yashiga yi mata sosai, saida baffah aminu ya dakatar dashi sannan ya kira Abdulmajeed awaya, ya tuntubesa akan zancen.. Ba musu AJ ya amsa ya saketa da baffah aminu ya tambayesa dalili bai sanar masa cikin hikima yacemasa ya tambayeta domin itace ta janyo koma miye ya faru... 
Sallama sukayi a daddafe kafin baffah aminu ya mayar da dubansa ga nafisa daketa sharbar kwallah cikin tsare gida yasoma magana "karki kuskura ki mayardamu kananun yara dole da akwai mummunan abun da kika aikata Abdulmajeed ya yanke wannan hukuncin akanki, nasan yana zuciya da zafin rai amma yana aiki da tunani da hankali bai taba bari zuciyarsa taja ragamar tunaninsa ba, komai da tunani yake yinsa... ".. 
Cikin jin zafin yadda take musu wasa da hankali baffah aliyu ya cabe zancen da cewa "nafisa baki wuce na baki kashi ba wallahi, shin zakimin bayani koko sainada kaki "... 
Sanin halin baffah aliyu ba wasa ya sanya ta fashe da kuka tayi kasa da kanta batasan ta ina zata fara karantomusu wannan kazamin labari, itakam ta shiga uku ta tabbatar baffah aliyu bazai barta da rayuwar ta ba... 
Tsawa daddynta ya dako mata da ta saka ta zabura cikin kuka ta soma magana, batada zabi da yawuce fada musu komai ta hanyar tonawa kanta asiri... 
Jin abinda nafisa tafada yasaka duk wanda ke falon daukar salati cikin tsantsar tashin hankali baffah aminu saboda takaici da tashin hankali hularsa ya cire yana fifita dan wani irin zafi ne ya taso masa, mommy kuwa kuka takuma sakawa tana sallallami, haka daddynta ambaton sunan allah ya shigayi dan gane da irin abun kunyar da nafisa ta aikata da aurenta sannan hakan cin mutuncin dokokin ubangiji ne da wulakanta al adarsu ta fulanin usul... 
Yadda mommy ke kuka ya sanya nafisa itama cigaba da kukan, kafin ta ankara baffah aliyu ya fara kai mata duka ta ko ina.. Babu shiri nafisa tasoma yunkurin kwatar kanta ta hanyat neman waje tsira... 
Aguje ta fito daga falonsu zuwa bannan harabar gidan still baffah aliyu bai daina bin ta da duka ba.. 
Ganin haka yasanya baki daya su mommy daddy baffah aminu suka rankayo suka biyo bayansa... 

Aza hannu aka mommy tayi ya kurma ihu wai baffah aliyu zai kashe mata ya... 
Kafin wani lokaci tuni harabar gidan ta soma cika da jamaar gidan, dakyar dai su baffah aminu suka kwace nafisa da  tayi ligi² ahannun baffah aliyu... Ya gaza magana sai hawaye da yake faman zubarwa na bakinciki, nsfisa ta cutar dasu ta kuma gurbata musu tsarkakkiyar zuriarsu... Anan fa fadan yakoma gun mommy domin acewarsa &ik abinda nafisa tayi da sa hannun mommy da hajjah ne, gashi yanxu ta gurbata su.. Abu dai sam babu dadin ji dan ko ina acikin gida sun san auren nafisa ya mutu... 
Haka baffah aminu yayita tausarsa tare da basa baki... Dun kumayi alkawarin cewa sin zame hannayensu acikin dum wasu harkoki dasuka shafi nafisar... 
Ita kanta hajiya innah sai alokacin tasan abinda yafaru hankalinta ya tashi sosai... 
haka tasaka mujaheed ya kira mata, AJ tayimasa fada sosai akan bai kyautaba duk yadda ta baci nafisa yar uwarsa ce bai kamata yayi mata kamata yayi ya zauna da ita ahaka ya kuma rufa mata asiri... 
AJ kuwa cewa hajiya innah shi baxai taba zama da abinda bayasoba, koda ace yana sonta tayi hakan bazai taba  cigaba fa zama da wacce bata da mutunci bare tassn mutuncin aurensa.. 
Shikansa mujaheed yaji dadin hakan, dan ko shi ne irin matakin da AJ ya dauka irinsa zai dauka... Dan shi ko alokacin da Aj ya auri nafisa sam baiga dacewarsuba bare yanxu da komai ya dagule, shikansa baffah hafiz sam baiji dadiba baikuma ji dadin yadda baffah aliyu ya fito atsakiyar gida yana dukan nafisa ba, dul da abun da ciwo amma yafiso abu komai ahankali duk da abinda ake gudun yafaru.. 
Bangaren mommy kuwa tana kuka tana masifa wai baffah aliyu xai kashemata ya dan bashi ne ya haifetaba... 
Shikuwa mahaifin nafisa da kansa yadauki zafi akan lamarin barin gidan yayi ya dauki motarsa ya fice bakidaya kunyar abinda nafisa ta aikata yakeji... 
Haka mommy ta taimakawa nafisa tayi wanka da ruwan dumi.. Ko ina jikinta ciwo yake mata dan dukan da baffah aliyu yayimata bana wasa bane har saida ya fasamata lebenta da goshinta... Cikin kankanin lokaci zazzabi mai zafi ya rufeta... 
Kafin wani lokaci tuni kananun maganganu sun fara yawo acikin estate din, abunka da gidan yawa kowa tofa albarkacin bakinsa yake akan mutuwar auren nafisa.... 
Bangaren deenah kuwa, tana biye da duk halin da AJ yake ciki musamman da ta fahimci yana cikin damuwa sam ta gaza samun  sukuni har saida taga ya fara warewa... 
Suna a dining table suna dinner ita da shi babu kowa gidan sai su kadai, sultan tuni yayi joining a university dake canada yasoma karatunsa iftahal kuwa bata samun isasshen lokacin kanta kullum tana dakinta tana karatu sskamakon jarabawar kammala secondry da take kusantosu... 
Cikin nutsuwarsa yake cin hadaddiyar jallof din taliyar da deenah ta girka masa, yanayin yadda yake cin abincin kadai zai nuna maka yadda abincin yayi dadi... 
Deenah na murmishi take dubansa bayan ta tsiyayo masa ginger juice mai sanyi a cup  ta ajiye masa agabansa... 
Ta dan lumshe fararen idanunta still fuskarta dauke da murmishi take dubansa kafin tace "nasamu nutsuwa sosai"... 
"Ta me? ".. 
Yayi mata tambayar tare da mayar  da dukkan hankalinsa akanta.. 
Juya golden eyes dinta tayi kafin ta daidaita dubanta akansa tace "yadda na ga kadawo da walwalarka sabanin kwanakin baya da bana iya jurar ganinka acikin wani yanayi "... 
Dubanta yayi da kayataccen murmishi shimfide a face dinsa kafin ya mayar da hankalinsa akan abincin sa... Saida ya gama cin abincin ya kora da lemun kafin ya dubeta a tsanake tare da kiran sunanta "kinsan me? ".. 
Ta girgiza masa kai tana kafesa da idanunta... 

Saida yadan numfasa kafin yacigaba da cewa "na rabu da nafisa ina nufin na saketa, na fadamiki hakanne, saboda zaman tare dan kisani "... 
Gyara zama deenah tayi tana karawa dukkan eyeballs dinta girma akansa cikin mamakin abinda ya fada tasoma tambayarsa "hubby meke faruwa haka me yayi zafi? "... 
Cikin rashin jindadin abinda ya aikata take tambayarsa... 
Tsuramata idanu yayi yana dubanta yana murmishi kafin lokaci daya yadauke idanunsa akanta yasoma cin abincinsa dan baisan amsar dazai bata ba... 

Deenah kuwa nazari tashigayi sai yanxu tagane damuwarsa kenan ta nada nasaba da rabuwarsa da nafeesa? Idan har zai damu haka to akanme zai rabu da ita tunda ya na sonta kenan?.. 
Anan take na matan ya motsa (kishi)  duk da batasan dalilin da yasaka ya saketaba.. Hakanan ta damu kanta da damuwarsa ashe akan matarsa ne har ita kanta tadaga hankalinta.. 
Afili  kuwa tabe baki tayi ta gaza hakuri har saida tayi magana "akanme zaka sanar dani kenan na saka ka maidota bayan kayi kuskuren sakinta, tunda har kanajin sonta a zuciyarta meyasa ka saketa, infact nothing cocern me in, tsakaninka da matarka ne ".. 
Sosai ta so ta basa dariya dan ya fahimci kumallon ne ya motsa.. Da yake yan miskilancinne akansa kawai ya dubeta yana jifarta da murmishi yacigaba da cin abincinsa... 
Deenah kuwa yadda yayi mata ne ya sanya ta kuma kuluwa, ba ta kuma bi takansa na ta mike tsaye tana kokarin barin dining area din ya riko hannunta yana dariya tare da leko fuskarta.....
