_"matarka kuma ƙawata na aminci hotel Room 41,tana can tana zubar da kimar igiyar aurenka da ke kanta, wannan lokacin ban damu da   ka yarda dani ko akasin hakaba,   amma  kaje ka ganewa idanunka zahirin gaskiya"!!!_
Cije lebensa IGP yayi tamkar zai hudasa ya runtse idanunsa tare da furzan da zazzafar iska abakinsa ya sake karanta text message din da zuwa yanxu ya fahimci daga wurin nabila sakon ya fito.. 
Dauke idanunsa yayi dasuka soma sauya kala  daga screen din wayar tasa, zai dauki mummunan mataki akan nabila idan bata daina shiga sabgar gidansa da ta ahalinsa ba ta fara kaisa bango, xai ladabtar da ita ta yadda zata gane ita din karamar yar bariki ce... 
Yana kokarin kiran number da ta turo masa sako ne, ta kirasa bai bata lokaci ba ya daga kiran tare da kangawa akunnensa, shiru yayi yana sauraron ta yana jiran jin sabon makircin da kazafin da zata kuma yiwa nafisa... Da hakan zai zama silar tarwatsewar farincikin rayuwarta... 
Cikin nutsuwa da farincikin cimma nasara nabila tasoma magana "yallabai baka da sauran lokaci na tabbatar kaga sako na, wallahil azeem matarka karuwa ce,kaje ka ganewa idanunka, idan kaga akasin haka duk hukuncin da ka dauka akaina na yarda, nasan kai kanka kafara zargin nafisa amma kana kokarin kawar da hakan domin baka da hujjar hakan  ,  kuma bakaso kana cigaba da zarginta saboda martabar aure ... "
Cikin wani irin yanayi ya datse kiran, barin office din yayi da sauri koda ya fito compound karbar key yayi daga hannun driver dakansa ya fixgi motar ya fice daga division din... 

Kai tsaye aminci hotel din ya nufa , da yake shidin sananne ne  kuma yana sanye da kayan  aikinsa ko da yaje reception bai bata lokacin gabatar da kansa ba ya soma tambayar ma aikacin da ke reception din spare   key din daki mai lamba arba in dadaya... 
Cikin girmamawa ya dauko ya mika masa tare  da rakasa har dakin ya nuna masa yakoma kan aikinsa..
Cikin wani irin yanayi da faduwar gaba ya bude kofar dakin ta re da kutsa kansa yana fatan abinda yake zato kar ya zama gaskiya.... 
Gaza motsawa yayi daga inda yake lokaci daya idanunsa ya kada yayi jajir jiyoyin dake boye akansa suka fito radau, lokaci daya jikinsa ya fara tsuma sakamakon mugun ganin da idanunsa sukayi kuma zahirin gaskiyar abinda nabila ta fadamasa, matarsa ta sunnah tare da wani kato a hotel akan gado daya suna fasikanci da mutumin da zai yi saar mahaifinta... 
Runtse idanunsa yayi da karfin gaske yana kokarin saita nutsuwarsa... 

Nafisa kuwa da shigowar IGP bagatatan kamar anjefosa ya sa ka su dawowa hayyacinsu... Cikin yanayin tsantsar tsorata da fargaba ta kwallah kara tare da dora hannu aka, cikin rudewa ta yi saurin janyo bargo dan kokarin boye tsiraicinta... Shikuwa abokin badalar  da suke wa laƙa bi da alhajin dala maxurai ya shigayi ya janyo boxer dinsa yana kokarin sakawa
IGP kuwa batare da yasake bude idanunsa akansu  ba ya juya yabar dakin cikin wani irin yanayi da yakejin tsanar nafisa da kyamarta, tsanar da bai taɓa yiwa wani mahaluki irinta ba.... 
Koda ya shiga mota cikin zafin da zuciyar sa ke masa ya fizgi motar yabar hotel din... 
Sam aranar bai samu damar halatar meeting dinba musamman yadda yake jin zuciyarsa da tunaninsa na dagulewa, abinda idanunsa suka gani ya kasa gogesa a zuciyarsada majigin idanunsa... 
Da daddare kuwa ko abinci bai iya ciba baisan wani irin mataki zai dauka wa nafisa ba, abinda ya sani kawai ya tsaneta ya kuma tsani ganinta, deenah kuwa ganin yanayin da ya shigo gidanne da yadda ya wuce sashinsa kai tsaye   ta tabbatar da damuwar dake damunsa dan ba hakan yasaba shigowa gidan ba... Bata bi ta kansa ba har shige ta barshi ne ya huta ya dan sauka daga yanayin yake kafin ta ji abinda ke damunsa, dan tasan matukar ransa abace yake, bazata taba bi dashi yadda ya kamata tafahimce sa sosai..
Har misalin karfe tara na dare nafisa bata shigo gidanba... 
IGP kuwa yana fitowada ga toilet bayan ya sakarwa kansa ruwa, wayars a ta fara vibrating, ganin bakuwar number da yayi, yayi kamar bazai daga ba dan yasan nabila ce kuma bayason jin wani abu da ya danganci nafisa... 
Daga kiran yayi, tare da yin shiru dan sauraren abinda zata kuma fada masa... 
Nabila kuwa jin yadaga wayar yasanya ta fara magana " yallaɓai  yanxu ka tabbatar da abinda na fada maka   ko? Sam bana nufin yiwa nafisa kazafi, zuwa yanxu kasancewa karuwa kake aure marar  kuma lasisi... "
Shiru nabila tadanyi tare da kumayin kasa muryarta "ina fatan baka manta alkawarin da ka daukarmin ba... "
Sai alokacin IGP yaja tsaki lokaci daya ya katse wayar  ya ajiye tare da zama gefen gado ya rike kansa yana fitar da wani irin numfashi.. 
Hakan yayi daidai da shigowar deenah a dakin tare da sallama, ganinsa da takumayi a irin yanayin da ya shigo ya sanyayamata jiki.. Cikin jiki ta karaso inda yake... 
Dubanta yayi da rinanmun idanunsa ya sauke ajiyar Zuciya yana kokarin  daidaita nutsuwar sa tare da miko mata hannu alamun ta zauna akusa dashi... 
Bata mika masa hannun nata ba tazauna ta na fuskantarsa cikin tsananin damuwa ta soma magana "hubby me ke damunka ne? "... 

"Banajin dadi ne, saboda na dan gaji yau a office ".. 
Yayi maganar yana fuskantarta... 
Kallon kwayar idanunsa tayi tare da girgiza kai ta kira sunansa "hubby ba haka kake kasancewa ba idan ka dawo daga office a gajiye me ke damunka "... 
Tsura mata idanunsa yadanyi yana nazarinta kafin ya dauke dubansa akanta "lafiya kalau nake little just is a matter, pls karki kuma tambayata wani abu "... 
Tadanji zafin yadda ya bata amsa, shi kansa baida zaɓi da wuce yabata wannan ta yadda zai dakatar da tambayar da takemasa, bazai iya tonawa nafisa asiri ba... Zai barta ne kawai da halinta amma ya haramtawa kansa ita... 
"Shknan ka huta lafiya saida safe".. 
Ta fada tare da mikewa tsaye tana kokarin barin dakin cikin jin zafi... 
Shima mikewa yayi tare da kamo hannunta lokaci daya ya rungumota jikinsa tare da rike waist dinta... Kafin hannunsa ya sauka akan cikinta da zuwa yanzu ya fara girma... Ahankali yakira sunanta asaitin kunnenta "little kar kiji haushi na pls, bana cikin yanayi mai dadi ne... ".. 
"Naji, ka kyale na tafi na kwanta ".. 
Tayi maganar atakaice.. 
"Baki huce ba ko? To ba inda zaki tafi "... 
Sai alokacin ta dago da kanta ta dubesa ahankali ta soma magana "na fahimceka hubby, akwai damuwar da bai kamata ka tilasta sai ka jita, akwai damuwar kuma da ya kamata abayyana, akwai kuma damuwar da lokaci ke bayyana ta, ko ma miye ina fatan ubangiji ya saukakamaka ita komai ya zo maka da sauki "... 
Murmishi ne yafara wanzuwa afuskarsa still yana kallon cikin idanunta matukar gaske deenah ta fahimcesa tamkar yadda jalila ke fahimtarsa alokacin da take raye aduk lokacin da ya shiga damuwa, hakika bakomai ne ya kai ka sani ba musamman agun da kasan bazaa boye maka komai ba dan komai na da sirri arayuwa... 
Hot peck ya manna mata agoshinta lokaci daya ya rungumeta yana amsa adduarta wata irin zazzafar soyayyar ta na kuma ratsa sa... 
Cikin sanyin jiki deenah ta zame jikinta tana dubansa tare da mirmishi sannan tayi masa saida safe ta rufo masa kofar... 
Da murmishi yabi bayanta yana sauke ajiyar zuciya... 

Nafisa kam tun bayan fitar IG daga dakin, ta fice daga hayyacinta kuka takeyi sosai harta saka kayanta agaggauce ta shirya ta fice cikin dimaucewa ko takan alhajin dala bata biba da yake kokarin tsayar da ita wai ta tsaya tayi wanka... Harta shiga mota ta fizgeta tafice daga hotel din kuka takeyi bakidaya ta dimauce tunaninta duk ya dagule.. Itakam ta shiga uku da ganin wannan ranar ta tozarci agareta ta tabbatarwa kanta koda itace autar mata IGP Abdulmajeed ya gama zaman aure da ita, hakan na nufin tonuwar asirinta.. Ina zataje da wannan abun kunya.. Tana tukin ta na kuka mai sauti... 
Bata yi kuskuren tunkarar gidan sa ba dan tasan komai zai iya faruwa tasan halinsa da zafin zuciyarsa duk da sautari yana kokari sosai wurin saita kansa... Dan bayason yin aiki da nadama... 
Cikin rashim makama ta nufi gidan ammie... Allah ne kawai ya kawota gidan lafiya dan tanayi parkin din motarta ta fito tana kuka har ta shigo cikkn falon ammie bata daina kukan ba... 
Ammie dake zaune afalo tana kallo cikin tsananin tashin hankalin ganin nafisa ta mike tsaye tana dubanta aranta tana karanto innalillahi wa inna ilaihir rajiun domin daga dukkan alamunta sun nuna ba lafiya take ba.. 
Nafisa kuwa cikin kuka mai tsuma zuciya ta karaso cikin falon tare da zaman dirshan akasa tana rairowa ammie kuka cikin rashin nitsuwa, ta shiga tashin hankali dan batasan irin hukuncin dazata fuskanta agun ammie ko ya AJ ba... Abinda ta sani kawai ta gama zubar fa kimarta a idanun ya Aj,...... 
Shi dama mutunci madara ne idan kuma ya zube baya taba kwasuwa, kuma ba a ɓari akwashe lafiya kamar yadda tsalle daya ake afada rijiya sai ayi dubu ba a fito ba.. 
Ammie kuwa tagama karaya da lamarin hankalinta yasoma tashi duban agogon dake manne abangon falon tayi data nuna karfe 9:30pm ta samu guu ta zauna tana tambayar nafisa abinda ke faruwa... 
Bakidaya tagaza yimata bayani dan batasan mezata fada mata, shin zata ce mata danta ne ya kamata ahotel tana fasikanci? Me zata fadawa ammie... 
Jin yadda nafisa ta gagara yimata bayani ne sai kuka ya sanya ammie dauko wayarta tasoma kiran layin AJ, bugu daya zuwa biyu ya daga kiran, kai tsaye cemasa tayi yazo ya sameta agida "
Ta fada tare da kashe wayar.. 
Sosai AJ yayi mamakin kiran gaggawar da ammie tayimasa yana fatan dai lafiya bai kawo komai a ransa ba ya zira jallabiyarsa ya dauki key din mota ya fito... 
Sosai ammie ke rarrashin nafisa da har lokacin bata daina kukan ba lokaci zuwa lokaci ammie ke sauke ajiyar zuciya... Bakidaya ta gaza fahimtar yanayin nafisa, abinda kawai tasani tana cikin tashin hankalin da ta gaza mata bayanin abinda ke faruwa.. 
Cikin mintuna 28 ya iso gidan... 
Yana shigowa falon nafisa ta yi kasa da kanta tana kuma tsananta rairo kukanta mai dauke da zallar nadama da jin kunya... 
Shikansa yana ganinta zuciyarsa ta fara tafasa da jin zafinta da wata irin wutar kiyayyarta dake ruruwa aziciyarsa ya tsane ta ya tsani ganinta, sautari yakan tuhumi kansa da tambayoyin me yasa ya auri nafisa, shi kansa bazai ce ga dalilin da ya sa ya auretaba, dan sam ba ta dace dashi ba domin hadinsa da ita tamkar hadin ganbixa ne... 
Dauke rinannun  idanunsa yayi daga kallonta, sai yanxu ya fahimci dalilin kiran da ammie takeyimasa... 
Tun kafin ya zauna ammie ta soma tambayarsa abinda ke faruwa dan hankalinta yasoma tashi... 
Saida ya zauna akasan carpet din ya dubi ammie da rinannnun idanunsa, kafin ya dan dubi nafisa da kanta ke kasa kunya ta hanata dago da kanta ta dubesa still sharar  hawaye takeyi har lokacin.. 
Maido da dubansa yayi ga ammie yasoma magana "na dauka ta miki bayanin komai.. "
"Babu abinda tafadamin sai kuka takeyi wai meke faruwa ne Abdulmajeed? ".. 
Duban nafisa ya kuma yi cikin yanayin tsanarta da kallon kyamata ya soma magana "zuwa da kikayi anan bashi ne zai samo miki maslaha ba, domin bazan taba kuskuren cigaba da kazamar mace irinki ba "... 
Rushewa da kuka nafisa ta kumayi sai alokacin tasamu bakin magana, "ammie ki saka baki kada ya ce zai sake  ni, wlh  ina sonsa, bansan me zan fadawa iyayena ba idan ya sakeni... Bazan iya zama awannan duniyar  ba, nayi masa laifi, dan allah kitayani basa hakuri ya yafe min "... 
Gyara zama ammie tayi cikin rashin fahimta ammie ta dubesa ta dubeta kafin ta tambayeta abinda tayi masa..... 
Shiru nafisa tayi cikin kunci da kunya da suka rufeta ta gaza magana... 
Cikin zafin zuciya AJ ya daka mata tsawar da har saida ta tsorata "kiyi mata bayani da bakinki!! "
Cikin rawar jiki da uban gumin da ya fara tsattsafo mata ta fara in- i-ina, batasan ta ina zata fara bayani ba kuma bata taba kawo wa ranta ya AJ zai iya titseyeta da tambaya agaban ammie ba ta yadda reshe zai juye da mujiya haka ba.. Cikin tashin hankalin da bata taba tsintar kanta acikiba tasoma fadawa ammie komai, karo na farko da ta kunyatar dakanta... Fashewa tayi da kuka tadora da cewa "nasan nayi kuskure na amsa laifi, wlh bazan kuma ba tsautsayine da qaddara, dan allah ammie kibasa hakuri nasan zai hakuri... "

Ba iya IGP kadai ba hatta ammie zuciyarta ta fara suya da jin bayanin da nafisa tayi mata ta kuma bata kunya, duban AJ ammie tayi ta yadda zata iya karanto tsantsar bacin rai da tunkushewar da zuciyarsa take ciki... Sauke ajiyar zuciya tayi ta dubi nafisa da ke tsammanin fitowar wata kalma daga bakinta... Girgiza kak ammie tasomayi cikin rashin jin dadi tasoma  magana "kinyi kuskure nafisa, kuma koda wasa kada ki kira hakan amatsayin qaddara fa ce son zuciyarki, kin zubar da ƙima da darajar aurenki, da darajar ahalinki... Babu abinda zan iya cewa sai abinda Abdulmajeed ya fada, domin wannan cin amana ne da rashin sanin darajar  aure,  duk hukuncin da ya yanke akanki bazan tilasta sa ko akasin hakaba.. ".. 
Aza hannu nafisa tayi aka "nashiga uku na lalace asiri na ya tonu"... 
Ta karashe maganar da kuka mai zallar nadama... 

"Bazan taba tonamiki asiri ba, amma bazan iya cigaba da zama dake amatsayin matar aure na ba, na tsaneki ta yadda bana kaunar koda kallon fuskarki, wlh bazan ce ga dalilin da ya saka na aureki ba... Kin cutar dani.... ". 
Cikin wani irin yanayi ya idar da maganar yana jifanta da wani irin kallo... 
Dauke dubansa yayi daga gareta yasm cigaba da cewa "na datse igiyoyin aurena duka akanki dan bazan taba sake zama dake ba bana bukatar sake ganin koda mekama da ke ne agidana ... "
Kuka takuma sakawa  mai sauti still hannayenta na akanta.. Cikin wani irin mummunan yanayi take rairo kukanta bata taba kawowa rayuwa zatayi mata irin wannan mummunan juyin ba..
 Gashi ta rasa ya AJ har abada, deenah tayi nasara itakuma tafadi.... 
Sam ammie bataji dadin yadda IG ya yanke hukuncin datse igiyoyin auren duka ba... 
To amma yanayin da yake ciki na gubar da nafisa ta shayar da zyciyarsa zai iya yin komai dan huce haushinsa.. 
Tunda kuma ya fadin hakan ya yiwa ammie saida safe ya fice, ita kanta bata taba ganinsa ayanayi irin na yau, tabbas abun da ciwo dan kuwa bakowane namiji zai ga matarsa turmi da tabaryaba, bai dauki wani mummunan mataki bayan saki ba matukar yana sonta, hakan kadai ya isa ya nunawa  nafisa batada sauran matsayi agunsa... 
Nafisa ta ga zallar tashin hankalin da bata tsba tsintar kanta acikiba, bata taba tunanin zata tsinci kanta cikin jin kunya da nadama ba... Tayi kuka har ta gode allah.. Ita kanta ammie da ta gaji da rarrashinta tashi tayo ta shige ciki ta barta anan... 
Dole anan nafisa ta kwana sai da safe tu nufi jumeta dan ko ammie batasan tafiyarta ba saidai ta fito taga babu ita hakan ya nuna mata cewar sammako tayiwa garin adamawa(jumeta)... 
Bakidaya abu bai yiwa ammie dadi ba, batason abinda zai tada hatsaniya ko kadan bare abinda zai kawo cikas musamman acikin zuriar mai nagge estate.. Abinda ta yi gudu kenan tun farko... 

Matukar gaske mommy tashiga cikin tsananin tashin hankalin ganin nafisa, musamman cikin irin yanayin data ganta kamar korarriya  bakikidaya fuskarta ta kumbure dan kuka idanunta sun kankace, dakyar  ta iya sanar da mommy IG ya saketa ... Cikin tashin hankali mommy tashiga tambayarta, saidai sam bata samu amsarta a bakin nafisa sai kuka da takeyi.. Haka mahaifinta ya tasa ta gaba da tambaya akan ta fadamasu dalilin daya sa Abdulmajeed ya saketa har saki uku arana tsaka haka... Yayi juyin duniya taƙi fadamasa ji takeyi zuciyarta tamkar zata yi bindiga dan abinda ya AJ yayimata tamkar kisan mummuƙe ne, ya ƙi fadar komai yabarta ta tonawa kanta da kanta asiri.. Mommy kuwa da tarasa bakin magana saboda takaici  alokacin kuka tasaka  Sosai, bakidaya tashiga tashin hankali ga rashin lafiyar hajjah sauki sai gurin allah dan zuWa yanxu bata gane waye akan ta komai sai anyi mata , likitoci kuwa sun yi iya kokarinsu dan fano cutar da ke damun hajjah amma sun gaza, hakan yasa suka dukufa da neman maganin gargajiya, ga nafisa kuma aurennata ya mutu... Kuka mommy take sosai bisa dole ita asiri aka musu idan ba haka ba ta yaya lokaci daya irin wadannan wadannan masifu suke fado musu akai akai dole akwai wanda ke jifarsu... 
Sosai daddyn nafisa ya kwatsi mommy akan irin maganganun da takeyi tamkar ba musulma ba sam batassn qaddara ba, bare tawakkali... 
Su baffah aminu kuwa da suka ga nafisa ta dawo gida dun dauka jikin hajjah tazo dubawa to saida sukaga har anyi kwanaki uku ba tada niyyar komawa gidanta, anan baffah aliyu ya soma fada wai tazo ta koma dakinta tunda taga jikin hajjar.. Anan ne fa mommy tasanar dashi..... 
Bashi kadai ba hatta  sauran yan uwansa hankalinsu ya tashi da jin cewa Abdulmajeed ya sako nafisa.. 
Koda suka tasa ta gaba harda mommy da daddynta da  tambayar abinda ya faru har ya saketa haka kuka ta saka musu.. Baffah aliyu da ransa yasoma baci da koke koken nafisa, fada yashiga yi mata sosai, saida baffah aminu ya dakatar dashi sannan ya kira Abdulmajeed awaya, ya tuntubesa akan zancen.. Ba musu AJ ya amsa ya saketa da baffah aminu ya tambayesa dalili bai sanar masa cikin hikima yacemasa ya tambayeta domin itace ta janyo koma miye ya faru... 
Sallama sukayi a daddafe kafin baffah aminu ya mayar da dubansa ga nafisa daketa sharbar kwallah cikin tsare gida yasoma magana "karki kuskura ki mayardamu kananun yara dole da akwai mummunan abun da kika aikata Abdulmajeed ya yanke wannan hukuncin akanki, nasan yana zuciya da zafin rai amma yana aiki da tunani da hankali bai taba bari zuciyarsa taja ragamar tunaninsa ba, komai da tunani yake yinsa... ".. 
Cikin jin zafin yadda take musu wasa da hankali baffah aliyu ya cabe zancen da cewa "nafisa baki wuce na baki kashi ba wallahi, shin zakimin bayani koko sainada kaki "... 
Sanin halin baffah aliyu ba wasa ya sanya ta fashe da kuka tayi kasa da kanta batasan ta ina zata fara karantomusu wannan kazamin labari, itakam ta shiga uku ta tabbatar baffah aliyu bazai barta da rayuwar ta ba... 
Tsawa daddynta ya dako mata da ta saka ta zabura cikin kuka ta soma magana, batada zabi da yawuce fada musu komai ta hanyar tonawa kanta asiri... 
Jin abinda nafisa tafada yasaka duk wanda ke falon daukar salati cikin tsantsar tashin hankali baffah aminu saboda takaici da tashin hankali hularsa ya cire yana fifita dan wani irin zafi ne ya taso masa, mommy kuwa kuka takuma sakawa tana sallallami, haka daddynta ambaton sunan allah ya shigayi dan gane da irin abun kunyar da nafisa ta aikata da aurenta sannan hakan cin mutuncin dokokin ubangiji ne da wulakanta al adarsu ta fulanin usul... 
Yadda mommy ke kuka ya sanya nafisa itama cigaba da kukan, kafin ta ankara baffah aliyu ya fara kai mata duka ta ko ina.. Babu shiri nafisa tasoma yunkurin kwatar kanta ta hanyat neman waje tsira... 
Aguje ta fito daga falonsu zuwa bannan harabar gidan still baffah aliyu bai daina bin ta da duka ba.. 
Ganin haka yasanya baki daya su mommy daddy baffah aminu suka rankayo suka biyo bayansa... 

Aza hannu aka mommy tayi ya kurma ihu wai baffah aliyu zai kashe mata ya... 
Kafin wani lokaci tuni harabar gidan ta soma cika da jamaar gidan, dakyar dai su baffah aminu suka kwace nafisa da  tayi ligi² ahannun baffah aliyu... Ya gaza magana sai hawaye da yake faman zubarwa na bakinciki, nsfisa ta cutar dasu ta kuma gurbata musu tsarkakkiyar zuriarsu... Anan fa fadan yakoma gun mommy domin acewarsa &ik abinda nafisa tayi da sa hannun mommy da hajjah ne, gashi yanxu ta gurbata su.. Abu dai sam babu dadin ji dan ko ina acikin gida sun san auren nafisa ya mutu... 
Haka baffah aminu yayita tausarsa tare da basa baki... Dun kumayi alkawarin cewa sin zame hannayensu acikin dum wasu harkoki dasuka shafi nafisar... 
Ita kanta hajiya innah sai alokacin tasan abinda yafaru hankalinta ya tashi sosai... 
haka tasaka mujaheed ya kira mata, AJ tayimasa fada sosai akan bai kyautaba duk yadda ta baci nafisa yar uwarsa ce bai kamata yayi mata kamata yayi ya zauna da ita ahaka ya kuma rufa mata asiri... 
AJ kuwa cewa hajiya innah shi baxai taba zama da abinda bayasoba, koda ace yana sonta tayi hakan bazai taba  cigaba fa zama da wacce bata da mutunci bare tassn mutuncin aurensa.. 
Shikansa mujaheed yaji dadin hakan, dan ko shi ne irin matakin da AJ ya dauka irinsa zai dauka... Dan shi ko alokacin da Aj ya auri nafisa sam baiga dacewarsuba bare yanxu da komai ya dagule, shikansa baffah hafiz sam baiji dadiba baikuma ji dadin yadda baffah aliyu ya fito atsakiyar gida yana dukan nafisa ba, dul da abun da ciwo amma yafiso abu komai ahankali duk da abinda ake gudun yafaru.. 
Bangaren mommy kuwa tana kuka tana masifa wai baffah aliyu xai kashemata ya dan bashi ne ya haifetaba... 
Shikuwa mahaifin nafisa da kansa yadauki zafi akan lamarin barin gidan yayi ya dauki motarsa ya fice bakidaya kunyar abinda nafisa ta aikata yakeji... 
Haka mommy ta taimakawa nafisa tayi wanka da ruwan dumi.. Ko ina jikinta ciwo yake mata dan dukan da baffah aliyu yayimata bana wasa bane har saida ya fasamata lebenta da goshinta... Cikin kankanin lokaci zazzabi mai zafi ya rufeta... 
Kafin wani lokaci tuni kananun maganganu sun fara yawo acikin estate din, abunka da gidan yawa kowa tofa albarkacin bakinsa yake akan mutuwar auren nafisa.... 
Bangaren deenah kuwa, tana biye da duk halin da AJ yake ciki musamman da ta fahimci yana cikin damuwa sam ta gaza samun  sukuni har saida taga ya fara warewa... 
Suna a dining table suna dinner ita da shi babu kowa gidan sai su kadai, sultan tuni yayi joining a university dake canada yasoma karatunsa iftahal kuwa bata samun isasshen lokacin kanta kullum tana dakinta tana karatu sskamakon jarabawar kammala secondry da take kusantosu... 
Cikin nutsuwarsa yake cin hadaddiyar jallof din taliyar da deenah ta girka masa, yanayin yadda yake cin abincin kadai zai nuna maka yadda abincin yayi dadi... 
Deenah na murmishi take dubansa bayan ta tsiyayo masa ginger juice mai sanyi a cup  ta ajiye masa agabansa... 
Ta dan lumshe fararen idanunta still fuskarta dauke da murmishi take dubansa kafin tace "nasamu nutsuwa sosai"... 
"Ta me? ".. 
Yayi mata tambayar tare da mayar  da dukkan hankalinsa akanta.. 
Juya golden eyes dinta tayi kafin ta daidaita dubanta akansa tace "yadda na ga kadawo da walwalarka sabanin kwanakin baya da bana iya jurar ganinka acikin wani yanayi "... 
Dubanta yayi da kayataccen murmishi shimfide a face dinsa kafin ya mayar da hankalinsa akan abincin sa... Saida ya gama cin abincin ya kora da lemun kafin ya dubeta a tsanake tare da kiran sunanta "kinsan me? ".. 
Ta girgiza masa kai tana kafesa da idanunta... 

Saida yadan numfasa kafin yacigaba da cewa "na rabu da nafisa ina nufin na saketa, na fadamiki hakanne, saboda zaman tare dan kisani "... 
Gyara zama deenah tayi tana karawa dukkan eyeballs dinta girma akansa cikin mamakin abinda ya fada tasoma tambayarsa "hubby meke faruwa haka me yayi zafi? "... 
Cikin rashin jindadin abinda ya aikata take tambayarsa... 
Tsuramata idanu yayi yana dubanta yana murmishi kafin lokaci daya yadauke idanunsa akanta yasoma cin abincinsa dan baisan amsar dazai bata ba... 

Deenah kuwa nazari tashigayi sai yanxu tagane damuwarsa kenan ta nada nasaba da rabuwarsa da nafeesa? Idan har zai damu haka to akanme zai rabu da ita tunda ya na sonta kenan?.. 
Anan take na matan ya motsa (kishi)  duk da batasan dalilin da yasaka ya saketaba.. Hakanan ta damu kanta da damuwarsa ashe akan matarsa ne har ita kanta tadaga hankalinta.. 
Afili  kuwa tabe baki tayi ta gaza hakuri har saida tayi magana "akanme zaka sanar dani kenan na saka ka maidota bayan kayi kuskuren sakinta, tunda har kanajin sonta a zuciyarta meyasa ka saketa, infact nothing cocern me in, tsakaninka da matarka ne ".. 
Sosai ta so ta basa dariya dan ya fahimci kumallon ne ya motsa.. Da yake yan miskilancinne akansa kawai ya dubeta yana jifarta da murmishi yacigaba da cin abincinsa... 
Deenah kuwa yadda yayi mata ne ya sanya ta kuma kuluwa, ba ta kuma bi takansa na ta mike tsaye tana kokarin barin dining area din ya riko hannunta yana dariya tare da leko fuskarta.....

"Little wai fushi kikayi? , ni ne fa gentle man dinki"... 
Cikin yar tsokana yayi maganar yana dan murza yatsun hannunta sosai ya fahimci yadda tagaza boye kishinsa da take... 
Yadda tayi shiru da shi ne tare da dauke kanta gefe ta na kokarin saita kanta  ne yasanya sa sake murmishi.. Ya lura wannan cikin da rigima da saurin fushi ya zomata... Mikewa yayi tsaye still hannunsa acikin nata kafin ya kamo dayan hannun yarike yana kuma daidaita dubansa akanta... Ahankali ya kira sunanta yacigaba da magana "bana son aikata abinda zanyi nadama shiyasa sai nayi zirfin tunani kafim In aiwatar da kowane irin hukunci ne.. Ban rabu da nafisa dan nayi nadamar rabuwa da ita ba, face hakan Shi ne maslaha agareni.... "
Shiru yayi yana duban yanayinta... 
Deenah kuwa kasa tayi da kanta bata damu da tambayarsa laifin da nafisa tayimasa ba ta rabu da ita haka lokaci daya amma tabbas taji babu daɗi aranta domin ko babu alakar aure nafisa yar uwarsa ce bata tunanin ta cancanci wannan hukuncin da ya yanke mata... 
Hannayensa taji a kugunta ya rike   ya matso da ita ajikinsa sosai ya shiga kallon golden eyeballs dinta kafin yace "saki fuskanki mana,yar yarinya da ke kawai mai wahalar da babban mutum irina "... 
Ya idar da maganar yana lakace siririn hancinta, yadda yayi maganar cikin zolaya yasakata yin dariya tare da shagwaba.. Lokaci daya ya rungomota jikibsa yana mata dariya... 

Haka rayuwa tacigaba da tafiya da deenah agidan aurenta tamkar dai bata taba rayuwa da nafisa agidanba, sosai ta mayar da hankalinta gun tattalin farincikin mijinta, tsantsar kulawa da soyayya bata wasa ta wannan fannin... Aunty junaidiyya kuwa sosai take taimaka mata da wasu shawarwarin musamman akan abinda ya shigemata duhu, sam batajin kunya ko dar gun nunawa IGP tsagwaron soyayyar da take masa tamkar yadda shima yake mata nacin so.. Shi kansa har mamaki yakeyi yadda yarinya karama kamar deenah ke kokarin sauyasa, ta mayar da shi tamkar rakumi da akala, babu boka ba malam sai tsantsar biyayya ladabi da hakuri da kissoshi irinnamu na mata (kazalika kowace mace allah yabata hatimin da zata juya mijinta babu boka babu malam wanda yin hakan ma lada zai saka tasamu, saidai da yawanmu bamusan wannan sirrin na biyauya ga miji ke kawo hakanmu da yawa yawanmu rashin hakuri na cutar da mata agidajen aurensu).. 

Cikin deenah kuwa sosai yake samun kulawa agun IGP, ko office yake sai ya kira ya tambayi lafiyar babynsa, gaba daya ya biyemata shikansa idan yana tare da ita jin kansa yake kamar wani yaro.. 
Sosai cikin deenah ya bayyana, cikin  ya kara bayyanar da kyan sigarta ko ina jikinta ya kara ciccikowa, takara yin fresh sosai, ita yanzu fatanta bai wuce allah yasauketa lafiya ta fara zuwa makaranta ba dan gabadaya hankalinta yadauku zuwa karatunta... 
Bangaren ammie kuwa bayan wani lokaci kadan ta shirya kaiwa dan uwanta (Babah) ziyara da kuma tayasa murna akan nauyin jan ragamar al umma da ubangiji ya dora masa..daganan Zara nemi izininsa idan da halo zasu wuce kasar saudiyya tare Domin yaga dangin mahaifiyarsa da bai taba sani ba..   Da yake IGP ABDULMAJEED yana cikin hutune na aikinsa ya sanya tafiyar ta kasance da shi da kuma deenah harda iftahal, shikansa yana so yaje ya gaishe da baban, deenah fafur taki hakura akan sai ta bisu dan sosai take kewar babah, yadikko da kuma Goggo asabe... 
Bakidaya haka suka rankaya zuwa kasar niger har garin agafez zuwa masarautarta... 
Matukar gaske mai martaba (babah) yayi farincikin ziyarar dasu ammie sukayi masa.. Hatta yadikko da gimbiya Goggo asabe sunyi matukar farinciki yadda suka gansu... Musamman deenah dake dawainiya da tsohon cikinta... 
Tarba ta musamman akayimusu domin su din na musamman ne, matukar gaske masarautar agadez da yanayin gidan sarautar ya kayatar da IGP Abdulmajeed da kuma ammie.. Komai tafiya yake kan tsari... 
Sai zuwa dare ammie tasamu ganawa da mai martaba tun bayan gaisawar da sukayi. Shikansa IG sai alokacin ya kuma sake zuwa ya gaishe da babah, cikin farinciki da kaunarsa babah ya amsa masa lokaci dsya yashiga sanyawa rayuwar aurrnsu albarka.. 
Shikansa babah farincikin sa ya gaza boyuwa, har IG ya gice daga falon yabarsu dansu zanta shi da ammike... 
 Cikin soyayya ta yan uwantaka suka shiga tattaunawa tare da ammie anan ne take fadamasa abinda  ta shirya, sosai babah yaji dadin hakan dan daman wannan zancen yana kai komo aransa... 
Duban ammie yayi cikin nutsuwa yasoma magana "hakan yayi, allah ya tabbatar da alkhairi ina ga kasantuwar gabatowar watan ramadhan zamu tsaya muyi umrah acan sai mu dawo".. 
Cikin gamsuwa da zancen babah take gyada kai tana murmishi... 
Shima murmishin yayi ya kuma cewa "allah yasakamiki da mafificin alkhairi akan namijin kokarin da kike yi, alal hakika kefin jaruma ce "... 
Murmishi sosai ammie tayi tare da yimasa godiya, sun dan taba fira kafin suyi sallama tabar falon mai martaba kasantuwar yadda dare ya fara... 
Deenah kuwa sosai suka shiga fira da yadikko da kuma goggo asabe, su kansu sunyi matukar kewarta, saida dare ya fara ne yadikko ta ce "taje gun mijinta tabarshi shi kadai amasaukinsu, da yake sashi guda aka fidda musu... 
Washe gari haka deenah ta dinga shiga kowane sashi ta na gaidar da yan uwanta da ke gidan.. Ko wani  sashi ta shiga da irin alkhairin da take musu kafin ta fito... 
Sosai kowa yaji dadin ganinta, musamman yadda take kyautatamusu... Wannan na daga cikin irin kyawawan dabiun mahaifinta.. Lallai barewa bazatayi gudu ba danta ya rarrafa ba...  
Kwanansu uku a masarautar, suka dunguma zuwa kasar sa udiyya harda mai martaba sarki abdullahi da iyalansa har yadikko ba abari b... 
Tafiya ce da ta tabbata cikkn farincikk da annashuwa da zukatan kowanensu ke ciki har suka isa kasa mai tsarki, har cikin birnin riyadh... 
Tsantsar farincikin ganin ammie da kuma babah da ahalinsa agun ummuh sam baya misultuwa gareta... Kukan farinciki dattijuwar ta saka tare da rungume babah.. Tana jin ajikinta tamkar yar uwarta ce ta rungume.. Hakab kadai ya nunawa babah tsantsar soyayyar dake tsakanin mahaifiyarsa da kuma ummuh.... 
Deenah ma cikkn hawayen farinciki take duban dattijuwar bakarabiyar matar da zatakai shekaru tamanin da biyar suwa casa in a duniya.  Amma har lokacin kyawunta bai gusheba...
Bayan sun huta ne kuma aka gabatat musu da abincin dare a kayataccen dining table din dake dining area acikin rantsattsen falon ummuh, wanda masu yimata aiki ne suka gabatar da komai... Kallo daya zaka yiwa dattijuwa ka fahimci yadda hutu ya zauna daram ajikinta musamman idan ka dubi irin kayataccen gidan da take zaune acikinsa...  

Sosai suka ci abncin, sunaci suna taba fira da ummuh cikkn harshen larabci, dan sam babu hausa abakinta... 
Kallon yadda wannan zuriar ke fira cikin soyayya da kulawa kadai zakayi su burge ka.... 
Awannan ranar dai ummuh kwanan farinciki tayi yadda zuriarta suka baibayeta ana fira cikin aminci... 
Acikin yan kwanakin da sukayi agatin riyadh, saida ummuh ta saka ammie ta gwadawa babah duk wasu dangin mahaifiyarsa da bai sansu ba... Duk wanda kuma yaga babah sai ya tuno da rasuwar mahaifiyarsa domin duk kamanninta ne babah ya dauka. 

Deenah kuwa da ummuh shakuwa sosai ta shiga tsakaninsu  sosai ummuh takejin deenah aranta, sai nan nan take da  ita . musamman yadda cikinta ya tsufa... AJ kuwa baki daya ummuh tagama takurasa domin ta hanasa koda kebewa da matarsa ne, matuka yake cutuwa da yadda ummuh ta shiga tsakaninsa da matarsa sai wani ririta ta ke wai ciki ne da ita.. 
Ammie kuwa na ankare da su saidai tayimusu murmishi... Dan tasan ummuh ayadda take ganin deenah da tsohon ciki bazata taba bari taje masaukin mijinta ba,.. 
Kwanci tashi asarar mai bacci har sukayi azumi a kasar saudiyya inda ananne sukayi umrar su, bayan kwanaki biyu suka juyo zuwa kasarsu, sosai ummuh taji rashin dadin komawarsu... Batada yadda zatayo tilas ta hakura... Saida suka koma kasar niger suka raka babah, kwanakinsu biyu acan  suka biyo jirgin nigeria... 

Koda suka dawo kuwa duk yadda IGP yakai ga yin hakuri ya kyale deenah ta huta gaza bakura yayi dan matukar gaske ya cutu na rashinta, aranar dai saida ya nuna mata yadda yayi matsananciyar kewarta... Ita ma ta shaida allah daya ne domin koda wasa IGP bai raga mata, saida ya tabbatar ya sauke tarin gajiyarsa akanta kafin ya samu nitsuwa... 
Zuwa lokacin kuwa duk wani tarin siyayyar kaya anyiwa unborn baby.. Tsadaddun kayane himili guda da duk wani abinda ake bukata na shirin haihuwa anyi,.. Babu abinda ya rage kuma suke fata da ya wuce allah yasauke deenah lafiya domin n haihuwarta ya tsaya... 

A jumeta kuwa nafisa ta dauyarinyan kwanaki tana jinyar dukan da baffah aminu yayimata, ko ina jikinta ciwo yakemata bakidaya ta sauya tayi rama sosai bata da aiki sai kuka kusan kowane lokaci tana daki, zuwa yanxu ita kanta bata sakewa dan hatta dadynta fushi yake da ita sosai, babu inda zata raɓa taji dadi sai gun mommy baki daya duniyar da rayuwar tayi mata baki kirin batajin dadin komai, batada ikon sakewa agidan saboda kunyat abinda kowa yasan ta aikata.. Kullum cikin killace kanta take adaki idan tayi kukan tagaji ta watsa kwayoyi fa syrup tayi kwanciyarta, kuma hakan baya sama mata wata nutsuwa domin da wani sabon bacin ran zata tashi, musamman idan ta tuna cewa deenah ta kwace mata ya Aj daga hannunta kuma tana shirin haifamasa abinda ke cikinta da zuwa yanzu AJ yadauki son duniya ya dora aciki.. 
Idan ta tuna hakan ji takeyi zuciyarta tamkar zatayi bindiga... 
Hajiya rumaisa ma da taxo jajantawa mommy abinda yafaru tayi mamakin yadda nafisa ta sauya, cikin damuwa mommy ke sanar mata deenah ce ta shiga ta fita ta raba nafisa da mijinta dan haka bazasu taba zubamata idanu ba su kyaleta, anan ne take fadawa goggon tata (hajiya rumaisa) madina ma jinin sarautar agadez ce bakidaya ta zame musu karfen kafa yarinya karama nema take ta burkita musu lissafi... 
Sosai hajiya rumaisa tayi mamakin abinda mommy ta fadamata... Lallai yazama dole su sake shiryawa yarinyar domin bokanta yafi nasu bokan, kodan yadda su mommy suka kunyata aciki da wajen msi nagge estate.. 
Haka dai hajiya rumaisa tayita yiwa mommy famfo tana bi.. Dan sosai mommy kejin haushin sakin wulakancin da AJ yayiwa nafisa tamkar babu alakar yan uwantaka atsakaninsu... 
Hajjah kuwa kwance take sai abinda akayimata bata iya magana saidai tanajin komai.. Ta kuma san abinda ke faruwa acikin  gidan, kowane lokaci cikin hawayen bakincikin abinda yafaru takeyi hatta bacci bata iyayi.. Aikuwa hakan yayi sanadiyyar ta so da hawan jininta  sosai su baffah aminu su baffah aminu suka shiga tashin hankali dan likita ya tabbatarmusu matukar bata daina saka damuwa azuciyarta komai zai iya faruwa da ita har ya kaiga rasa rayuwarta.. 
Haka su baffah suka dinga bata baki da hakuri akan ta cire damuwa aranta tabarsu da tashin hankalin da nafisa ta ingizasu aciki.. 
Ita kuwa hajjah yadda takeji ayanxu gwanda ta mutu da wannan rayuwar da take ciki.. Ga ciwo ga kuma ciwon bakin ciki dake addabar zuciyarta... 

Nabila kuwa saidaga baya ta ji,  nafisa takira ta take sanar mata mutuwar aurenta... Jajanta mata nabila tayi ta nuna mata tamkar batasan abinda ya faru ba, zatasha mamaki ne idan tasanar mata ita ce silar rushewar duk wani tanadinta... 
Washe garin ranar tazo duba jikin nafisar...
nafisa na adakinta shigowar nabila yasaka ta fashewa da kuka har nabila ta karaso tana dubanta cikin wani irin yanayi batare da tacemata komai ba tanemi guu ta zauna agefen gadon da take tana cigaba da kallonta tace "na shigo ne mommy ke cemin kina daki, sam yanxu kindaina sakin jikinki acikin mutane tunda wannan abun yafaru"cewar nabila tana duban nafisa dake kuka tana sharbar hawaye .. 
Goge fuskarta nafisa tayi tasoma magana "nabila dubi yadda rayuwa ta maidani cikin halin kaskanci da jin kunya, wlh bana iya fitowa koda acikin sashin mune babu inda nake raba naji sanyi araina, gun mommy kawai nake samun sassauci hatta kannena basa ganina da wani mutunci.. Ban taba tsammanin zan wayi gari acikin wannan balain ba ban taba tunanin akwai ranar da zatazo da zan rasa ya Aj haka ba.... 
Kuka yakuma cin karfnta dubanta kawai nabila keyi batare da ta cemata komai ba kafin ta tabe baki ta dauke dubanta daga gareta tasoma magana, "koma miye ya faru bai kamata ki zargi kowa ba sai kanki, shi avu mai mahimmanci rikesa ake da mahimmanci, amma ke ko ahakan saida kika nuna halinki na akuyanci kinga kuwa ke kika tozartar dakanki, wlh nafisa da ni ce a matsayinko nabar rayuwar bariki kamar yadda nabar cikin tsohuwa ta... 

Tsayawa da kukanta nafisa tayi da jemammun idanunta take duban nabila yadda take zaro zance mai tattare da cin fuska "me kike nufi ne "
Daidaita dubanta nabila  tayi  akanta tasoma magana "akwanakin baya kince bazan taba samun namiji irin mijinki ba wai dan ina yar iska ba?, ni kuma na tabbatar miki da cewa kece ke kazamin iskanci ni nawa barikin me tsabta nakeyi, shiyasa na ganar da ke kuskurenki"... 
"Ban fahimce kiba? "!! 
Cikin kaduwa nafisa ta tambayi nabila tare da karawa idanunta girma... 

"Bazan boye miki ba domin banyi dan inajin tsoronkiba nayi nd dan biyawa zuciyata bukatarta halin da kika tsinci kanki ni na ingizaki alokacin da na kiraki kika tabbatar kina hotel tare da alhajin naira nikuma alokacin na turaea IG adress din hotel din da kike, dan ku sani na dade inamiki zagon kasa agun mijinki da yasan kalar matar da ya ke aure amma abun mamaki zuciyarsa a tsakarke take bai taba zarginki ba... Tunda ga lokacin na shiga neman hanyar dazan rabaku kuma gashi na samu.. 

Wata irin shaka nafisa takaiwa nabila da dukkan karfinta ko ina jikinta rawa yake yi idanunta sun sake kadawa sun yi jajir "me nayi maki kike bibiyata da sharri meyasa kika rabani da farinciki na? Ina amana da aminta dake tsakaninmu ke ba mutum bace! Ki fadamin ubanmi nayi maki kika kassara ni "... 
Cikin tsawa mai  tattare da tsawa nafisa tayo maganar tare da tsananta rikon wuyan nabila... 
Nabila da taji shaka kuwa itama tasaka dukkan karfinta ta fincike hannun nafisa daga wuyanta "dallah sakeni man, duk abinda zakiyi bazaki taba huce haushin abinda nayimaki ba har abada, kinsan meyasa nayimaki hakan saboda munyi tarayya da son abu daya wato IGP ABDULMAJEED tsohon mijinki domin nima allah ya jarrabeni da sonsa, ta hakan da nayi kadai zan iya mallakarsa, dan haka karkiga laifina nima ciwon so ne ya sanya nayimiki hakan, kuma da kike fadar aminci dake tsakaninmu, kokece halin da nake ciki haka zakimin dan bakidayanmu muna dawainiya ne da son zuciya... Saidai ki mutu wlh amma sai na auresa dan shima yamin alkawarin hakan matukar abinda yake zargi akanki gaskiya ne "... 
Kukan kura nafisa tayi ta cacumo nabila cikin bakin ciki da kunar zuciya ta hada ta da bango, suka shiga kokawa da neman illata junansu... 
Hakan yayi daidai da shigowar mommy aguje dan atsakar gida take juyo tashin hankalin dasuke yi... 
Kansu tayo tasoma yunkurin rabasu amma ta gaza hakan yasa ta fice aguje tana kururuwar neman agaji... 
Saida akayi dakyar aka raba tsakanin nafisa da nabila da kowanensu yaji jiki... Nafisa kuwa kuka takeyi mai raunata zuciya ta zauna gefen gado tamkar zata siƙe.. 
Nabila kuwa gyalenta tadauka da jakarta ta fice tana ta faman haki da nishi.... 
Ko ahakan tasan ta kassara farincikin nafisa har abada har ma da rayuwarta... 
Nafisa kuwa tashiga tsananin tashin hankalin rana zafi inuwa kuna, jin zuciyarta take tamkar zata fashe, hakika nabila tassmu nasarar cin amanarta amma itama tasaurari yadda rayuwa zata juya mata, domin da ita da nabilar dik kanwar ja ce... Ta kullaci nabila sosai har takejin tamkar ta kauda ita daga doron kasa, dan tayi amfani da amincin dake tsakaninsu ta azabatar da zuciyar ta da ruwan guba... 
Tayi mata TSARTUWAr dafi da har ta koma ga allah tana jinyar sa a zuciyarta..... 
                  ***
Deenah kuwa zuwa wannan lokacin IGP ya hanata yin komai agidan, ko kwakkwaran motsi bayason tanayi, kowane lokaci yana tare da ita tundaga lokacin da cikinta ya shiga wata na tara, ko office yake hankalinsa na gareta.. Akallah zaiyimata kira kusan biyar arana, dan yaji lafiyarta da ta baby.. Musamman yanxu da tafara nakudar tsaitsaye... 
Sosai dr junaidiyya keyimasa dariya da zolaya.. Duk ya wani bi ya damu , domin ita da abinda ke cikin ta (deenah)   suna cikin koshin lafiya saidai fatan allah ya sauketa lafiya, ita dakanta ta hana deenah zama domin mai ciki anaso tana motsa jikinta yadda ya kamata, musamman ita dake fama da naƙudar tsaitsaye..

Ranar wata alhamis deenah tana bacci azabar ciwo ta tasheta... Ahankali ta tashi zaune tare da raba jikinta da nasa, dan tunda cikinta ya tsufa dakinsa take kwana, sam bai yarda ta kwana ita kadaiba... 
Ziro kafafunta tayi daga kan gado tana ambaton sunan allah sakamakon yadda kafafunta da bayanta da ya rike kam, ga marar ta da tasoma yimata ciwo... 
Gaza motsawa tayi daga inda take ta durkushe abakin gado tana murmukusu tana ambaton sunan allah... Batare da ta bari ta tashesa ba.. Ta dauku tsayin mintuna ashirin ahaka kafin ubangiji ya rabata da abinda ke cikinta cikin kudura da buwayarsa,... Kukan da santaleliyar jaririyarta ta kwallah ya tashi AJ daga baccinsa, dasauri ya tashi zaune tare da kunna fitilar dakin lokaci daya haske ya gauraye dakin... Cikin mamaki da al ajabi yake du an bakuwar duniyar (baby gal), da deenah da ke kwance cikkn jinin haihuwa ko ina jikinta ya saki idanunta alumshe take sauke numfarfashi mai tattare da gajiya da kasala... 
Cikkn tsananin farinciki ya sauko daga kan gadon yayo kan deenah ya dagota, dan ya tabbatar afarke take ko kuwa asume... 
Bude gajiyayyun idanunta deenah  tayi akansa tana kasalallen murmushi.. Cikin tsananin farinciki da hamdala ya rungumeta ajikinsa tsam.. 
Kafin lokaci daya ya kira dr junaidiyya ya sanar da ita, dan tace ko wani lokaci deenah taji alamun  haihuwa  asanar da ita... 
Anan yake sanar mata deenah ta haihu yan zu haka tana akwance.. 
Da yake alokacin anfara kiran sallar asuba tun kafin gari ya yi haske driver yakawo dr junaidiyya gidan, alokacin tuni ya taimakawa deenah ta gyara jikinta da kuma taimakon hadiman ta da ke gidan aka gyara gun da kuma kyakkyawar baby gal me kama da mahaifiyarta sak, saida kamanninta yafi bayyana dana jalila... AJ kuwa kallo daya yayiwa yarinya yaga tamkar jalila ce tayi kaki ta ajiye.. Matsananciyar kama babyn take da jalila... Atake yayi mata huduba da suna *JALILA* cikin soyayya da matukar kaunar yarinyar yake dubanta... Dake ta faman wutsil acikkn lallausan towel hakan zai tabbatar maka da babyn cikin koshin lafiya take... 
Ita kanta deenah sosai taga kamannin yarinyar dana aunty jalila... Tayi farinciki sosai yadda ya Aj yayimata huduba da sunan aunty jalila tana fatan ubangiji ya albarkaci rayuwarta yasanya ta dauko kyawawan dabiun aunty jalila, musamman hakuri, kawaici da juriya, ita kuwa aunty jalila ubangiji ya jikanta ya haskaka kabarinta... 

Share hawayen ta deenah tayi na kewar yar uwarta da takeyi... 
Shikansa AJ yana cikkn wani irin yanayi na jin kewar tsohuwar matarsa... 
Cikin nutsuwa ya saka hannunsa ya gogemata hawayen yana dubanta tare da murmishi "little kuka kuma? Bazaki godewa allah ba akan niimar da yayi mana, hakika ubangiji ya karbi jalila sannan kuma yasake bamu wata jalilar... 
Yayi maganar yana duban santaleliyar yar tasa dake rungume ahannunsa lokaci daya ya manna mata peck a lips din babyn..ita kuwa baby tashiga neman mamanta tare da saka hannunta abaki ta soma tsotsa, lokaci daya tafara kuka.. 
Mikamata ita yayi ta karba ta fara bata mama.. Bakidayansu sun zubawa yarinyar idanu cikin soyayyar da suke mata suna dubanta.. 
Koda dr tazo duba deenah ta shigayi, sam batasamu wata matsala ba lafiya lau allah yasauketa... Saidai baki dayansu suka nufi asibiti dan asake duba lafiyarta da ta baby... 
Ko yini basu yiba suka dawo gida sai ruwa da aka karawa deenah dan taji karfin jikinta... 
Baby da mai jego kuwa duk wanda yagani sai sam barka... Kuma duk wanda yasan fuskar jalila ya dubi fuskar baby to zaice jalilar ce dan babu inda ta baro jalila... 
Ammie kuwa haka ta dinga  hamdala agurin ubangiji alokacin da ta ga kishiyar tata (baby)  masha allah yarinya kyakkyawa da ita..... 
Tun kafin wasu kwanaki da haihuwar tuni mutan jumeta sunji haka maryam da yanzu suka dawo garin abuja da zama tun kwanaki uku da haihuwar ta shigo kt, ita ke kula da deenah sosai da duk wani abinda take bukata... Deemah kuwa jego ya karbeta sosai tayi kyau takara cikowa fatar jikinta takumayin laushi da sheki.. Ta  kowani fanni tana samun kulawa, ammie ma sosai take nunawa deenah kulawa... 
IGP kuwa akwatuna set uku yayiwa deenah na jego, kowane akwati an shakesa da tsadaddun suturu na gani na fada... Abu dai kowa sai sam barka... 
Mutanen jumeta kuwa tun saura kwanaki  biyar suka diro kt dan ayi suna  dasu, haka mutan kasar larabawa (saudi) sun samu zuwa, yadikko kuwa sai daren suna ta shigo nigeria goggo asabe kuwa bata samu shigowa ba dan batajin dadin jikknta sakamakon shigar jinjinrin cikin dake manne ajikinta... Itada  mai martaba (babah) kuwa sai sinkin  kyauttukansu suka aikowa baby da  deenah.  
Tun kafin suna gida ya cika ya tumbatsa da yan uwa da kuma abokan arziki... Sosai baby da maijego suka samu tarin kyautattuka daga danginta dama mutanen jumeta abun sai sam barka masha allah... Hajiya innah kuwa  saidai ta aikowa da yar tata (baby) da kyaututtuka da ita kanta mai jego... Sosai aka gwangwaje mai jego da baby da kyaututtuka na alfarma da ban mamaki... 

Ranar safiyar suna kuwa  aka yankawa baby manya mqnyan raguna guda biyu da dan maraqi (namijin saniya) ta amsa sunanta JALILA anayimata alkunya da mima, anyi shagali sosai, amaryar jego deenah sai kyalkyali take da sheki duk inda ta gitta sai ankalleta.. Shikansa uban tafiyar yaga takarayimasa masifar kyau  Sosai itada babynta.. Sosai taro ya amsa sunansa dan taro ne na manyan masu hannu da shuni da suka amsa sunansu... anci ansha ankumayi shagali da hotuna sosai , haka maroka da mawaka  sunxo sun baje basirar su ankuma yimusu batin kudi... 
Abu dai sai sam barka dan taro ya kayatar har ya tashi lafiya amaryar jego deenah kuwa sai kyalli take tayi... 
Mutanen jumeta kuwa sun shiga mamakin sunan jalila da yarinya ta amshe, saidai ba abun mamaki bane dan irin son da AJ ya nunawa jalilar alokacin da take raye, hakan ya nuna lallai har yanxu soyayyarta na wanxuwa a zuciyarsa bakuma zai taba mantawa da jalila arayuwarsa ba, domin ta raini farincikinsa har zuwa numfashinsa na karshe itadin cikamakon farincikinsa ne..... 
Ta wani bangare kuwa mutanen jumetan na taya nafisa takaicin irin yadda ta cutar da kanta ta datsewa rayuwarta jin dadin da basa tunanin zata samu gurbinsa koda agaba ne... 
Haka dai kananun maganganu keta kai kawo a tsakaninsu yayinda dayawa zagin hajjah da mommy suke domin su ne suka janyomusu abun kunyar da yarsu ta aikata axuriar su... 
Yadikko kuwa kwanan ta hudu takoma agadez, sukuwa mutanen saudi kwana uku sukayi suka koma.. Bakidaya dai taro yatashi lafiya sai sambarka... 
Aka bar mai jego da babynta mima... 
Wani irin so IG keyiwa mima, musamman saboda sunan da taci... 
Matukar yana gida to yana tare da mima yana mata wasa, ba deenah kadai ba hatta iftahal dariya takewa abbienta, irin yadda yake nunawa mima so.. 
Ta kowane fanni mima na samun soyayya, sultan kuwa sam baisan mima ba dan yana makaranta sai a whats app iftahal ta tura masa picturrs din suna da na baby mima little sis dinsu.. 
Yayi farinciki sosai tare da mamakin yadda babyn tayi kama sosai da ummiensu (jalila)... 
Haka dai rayiwa mai farinciki da kwanciyar hankalo ta cigaba da wanxuwa a cikin gidan, shikansa IGP yanajin matukar nutsuwa na shigarsa ayanxu musamman idan yana cikin iyalansa... 
Sai da deenah ta gama wanka ne kafin ta fara yimasa zancen cigaba da karatunta... 
Sam ya nuna bai amince ba wai bazata yo nesa dashi ba kuma yadaina ganin mima ba... 
Haka ya dinga kawomata dalilai marasa makama dan kawai bayason suyi nesa dashi... 
Kuka ta sanya masa sosai, bai kulata ba alokacin ya kyaleta dan ya lura har yau kuruciya na dawainiya da deenah... 
Deenah kuwa ganin yaki kulata ne ya kuma hasala ta na kuka tasanarwa aunty junaidiyya... 
Da kyar dai da rarrashi junaidiyya ta shawo kan IGP akan amincewarsa da alkawarin karatun da ya daukarwa deenah bayan tahaihu ta buga masa misalai da matan da suke karatun kuma suna rainon yayansu... 
Saida tayi dakyar kafin ya amince shima bisa sharadin cewa saidai ta tsaya a universty ta katsinan tayi karatun bai yarda tayi nesa dashi ba.. 
Sosai deenah tayi farinciki, dan batada gurin da ya wuce yin zurfin karatu dan ganin ta zama cikakkiyat lawyer da al amurran da suka shafi matsaltsalin mata. 

Alokacin da iftahal zasuyi jamb kuwa tare suka kuma sauyawa da deenah cikin hukuncin ubangiji suka samu nasara.. Kafin wani lokaci tayi applying a alqalam university da ke nan katsina, iftahal kuma a hassan usman polytechnic (HUK)  tayi applying.. 
Batare da bata lokaci ba akayi approving.. Tare da samun admission dinsu cikin kankanin lokaci IGP yayimusu rigistration suka shiga karatunsu gadan².. 
Sosai deenah takejin dadin karattunta, duk da yadda take himmantuwa da karatunta bata wasa da lokacin mijinta da kuma na gidanta da mima da tayi wayau sosai abunta.... 
Babu abinda yasauya daga kulawar da takewa mijinta.. Suna mugun kulafucin junansu sosai.. 
Bayan watanni uku.. 
Karfe biyar da rabi IGP abdulmajeed ya fito daga office dinsa.. 
Kai tsaye gun inda aka tanada dan ajiye motoci ya nufa.. Dasauri drivernsa ya bude masa murfin motar zaman baya ya shiga.. 
Hakan yayi daidai da shigowar mota kirar 406 a division din nasu... 
Yadda motar ta shigo ne da gudu ta dauki hankalin jamian tsaron dake compound din  haar matukin motar ya daidaita parking motarsa a kusa da Rantsattsiyar motar IGP...... 
Cikkn hanzari aka bude murfin motar nabila ce tafito a motar, kai tsaye motar IGP da ta lura itace a gefen da tayi parking ta nufa, domin shi kansa tun shigowar motar yake son ganin mai irin wannan tukin, ganin nabila da yayi ya dan daure fuskarsa yana dubanta, shi ya manta da wata aba nabila, wani irin kallo yayimata ya dauke kansa tare da baiwa drivern command ya tada motar su tafi... 
Da sauri nabila takara so inda yake "sir kaganni amma zaka tafi kuma fa kasan zuwannan nakane... Ina bukatar lokacinka yallabai... 
Kamar bazai kulata ba ya dubeta sama da kasa ya watsar ya cigaba da cewa "ina jinki, banason bata lokaci "... 
Tasani ko ta fi me? Bazai taba fitowa dan ya saurareta ba amma ko ahakan ba konai tunda harzai iya sauraronta... 
Dubansa tayi tare da gyara tsayiwarta tasoma magana tana murmishi "na dauki lokaci mai tsayi bana kasar nan, amma duk da haka zuciyata na tare da kai aduk inda nake, nasamu labarin rabuwa da nafisa da kayi... Kuma nasan kana sane da alkwarin daka daukar min a kwanakin baya cewar matukar abinda na fadamaka game da nafisa gaskiya ne zaka min duk abinda na bukata....

"Bakomai bane bukatata shi ne cigaba da rayuwa atare dakai, ina nufin na maye gurbin tsohuwar matarka kuma kawata da ka saki.. Domin nima allah ya jarabceni da sonka "... 
Cikkn jin zafin kalamanta ya katseta dacewa "ke dallah dakata "... 
Yayi maganar yana jifanta da kallon sama da kasa kafin yacigaba da cewa  "bana kuskure acikin kuskure, da zan saki nafisa dan na aureki to da ban saketa ba saboda dake da ita duk kanwar ja ce, itadinma aurena da ita qaddara ce da bana iya kubuce mata, idan kuwa na bari hakan yacigaba da faruwa to ya zama ganganci da rashin sanin ciwon kai.... 
Cikin wani irin rauni da fuskarta tabayyana ta katsesa da cewa "ban damu da zaman da kai da nafisa ba, amma karka manta cewa kamin alkawarin biyamin kowace  irin bukata ta idan har ka tabbatar da zarginka akan nafisa .. "
. "kina yaudarar kanki idan har kina tunanin zan iya aurenki ".. 
Yayi maganar yana kuma hade fuskansa kafin ya kuma dubanta yace "idan har aure kike bukatar yi kifada ko nawa zan iya baki daga 1M to 50M, zan dauki nauyin komai kuma zanbiyaki ladar aikinki ko nawa kika bukata, matukar kin nutsu kuma kin mayar da hankalinki akan auren, zan taimakamiki takowane fanni...".. 
Shiru nabila tayi bata da zabi da yawuce hakan tunda tasan kamar yadda yafada bazai kara maimata kuskuren da yayi ba ya aureta.. Yadda ya ambaci mahaukatan kudaden dazai bata ya kwadaitar da ita hakan domin ko ta nuna bata yarda ba ɓatan- ɓata kan-kan zatayi bataga tsuntsu bataga shaho... 

Yadda tayi shiru ne ya sanya cigaba da magana "kituromin account number zanmiki transfer, beside kar na sake ganin kafafunki sun biyo ni awannan division din, dan nan ba wurin shashanci bane, idan kuma kika saka kunnen kashi inada hanyoyin da zanyi maganinki, as from today babu wata alakar da zatasa kina bibiyata okey? ".. 
Yayi maganar yana tsareta da manyan idanunsa cikin son warning din ta.. 

Rausaya kai nabila tayi tana murmishi "nagode yallabai ni daga yau ma bazaka sake ganina ba, tunda har karshen yarjejeniyar mu ta zo na amince ".. 
Ta idar da maganar cikin farinciki.. IGP kuwa bai sake bi takanta ba ya umurci driver da ya ja motar su fice..

*_JUMETA_*
jikin hajjah ya tsananta sosai sauki kuwa sai na musulunci.. Kwararrun likitoci kuwa sun yi iya yinsu sun zubawa sarautar allah idanu, har yanxu basu gano takamaiman cutar dake damun hajja ba... Hatta yayan ta sun zubawa ikon allah ido.. Sub tsorata kwarai yadda ciwon nata babu wani sauki dayake shiga ga jininta da ke kuma hawa duk lokaci da akayi mata BP, ga jiki na tsufa... 
Da mommy da kuma momy ikilima suke kwana gun hajjah suna jinyar ta.... 
Suna tsaka da wannan tashin hankalin na ciwon hajjah, zubaida yar hajiya rumaisa ta shigo estate din cikin hankali tana kuka kai tsaye sashin su hajjah ta wuce... Yanayin da ta shigo ya sanya baki daya hankalinsu ya kuma tashi.. Cikin tashin hankali ta ke sanar musu  hajiya rumai sa na gida tana wasu abubuwa kamar mai shafin iskokai bakidaya takoma tababbiya neman hallaka kanta takeyi... Da zu ma da gudu ta fito daga daki tayi tsalle zata fada rijiya inda allah ya taimaka jikanta kamal na kusa ya rirriketa duk  ta jiwa kanta ciwo duk ta yayyage kanta, anyimata karatun andauka iskokai ne amma abun yaki sauki, yanzu haka andaure tana daki ankuma rufeta.... Lafiyaklau suka kwanta da ita suka wayi gari suka ganta acikin wannan mummunan halin... 
Kuka mommy  Tasaka sukam sun shiga uku wannan wane irin bala ine ke bibiyarsu, mommy ikilima kuwa ambaton  sunan allah tashigayi cikin matsananciyar damuwa.. Anan takira baffah ibrahim dan ta sanar dasu.. Ashe har sunji nan yake sanar mata yanzu haka sunkaita asibitin mahaukata ko acan dinma saida aka kuma daureta dan haukan dake kwalwarta neman rayuwarta yake domin so take ta kashe kanta... 
Sun shiga tsananin tashin hankali sosai.
Tun kafin wani lokaci magana ta karade ciki da wajen gida hajiya rumaisa ta haukace... Babban abun tashin hankalinma yadda hajiya rumaisa ke tonawa kawunansu asiri da duk wani kulli dasukayi na karkarshin kasa ita da hajjah... Ikon allah lallai allah baya barin hakkin wani kan wani, ashe abinda suke kullawa  ne ya dawo musu, dama haka karshen azzalumi yake karewa cijin tozarci da jin kunya.... 
Ko a asibitin mahaukata ma sai da su baffah suka nemi alfarma suka zo da wani babban malami a sake yiwa goggo rumaisa roƙiyya domin sun fahimci akwai iska dake cikin lamarin, ikon allah, ananne aka tabbatar da shedanun aljannu ne akanta kuma sunyi magana cewa "malaminsu (hajjah da hajiya rumaisa) shine ya turosu sakamakon aikin da yayimusu basu biyashi ba, itakuwa hajiya rumaisa har saida ta damfaresa, hajjah kuwa ya sakar mata cutar da aka gaza gane kanta.. 
Sosai su baffah hafizu, da su ibrahim suka shiga cikin tashin hankali lallau su hajjah sun cuci kansu sun cuci yayansu da zuriarsu sun kuma janyo ruwan dafa kansu.. Wannan son zuciya da daukar alhaki haka har ina zasu dashi, duk da haka basu hakura sa nema musu magani ba domin duk yadda ta lakace naka nakane, babu abinda zakayi da abinda ya zame maka dole...

Ana cikin wannan yanayi ubangiji ya karbi rayuwar hajjah, ciwo ya warke ciwo mai son rayuwa, mutuwar da ta dagawa dukkan  yayanta hankali, da sauran mutanen gidan da suke tausayin makomar hajjah duk da rahama ta allah ce, mommy kuwa tashiga matsanancin tashin hankali dan alokacin hawan jininta ya taso  fadi daga tsaye, kuma wani sabon tashin hankalin yakuma wanzuwa aka kwasa akayi asibiti... Tana a gadon asibitin akayiwa hajjah sutura aka kaita gidanta na gaskiya, rayuwar duniya babu tabbas kuma babu dawwama, kana naka allah na nashi ta allah itace karko, allah kabamu ikon aikata alkhairi da aiki nagari dazai bimu har makwancinmu.... 
Bakidaya zuriar me nagge sun shiga halin jimami na rasuwar hajjah... 
Sosai hajiya innah ta ji mutuwar hajjah tana fatan ubangiji ya yafemata laifukanta yakuma ji kanta, itakuwa mommy da hajiya rumaisa allah yabasu lafiya... Tun aranar baffah hamza ya kira ammie yasanar musu... 
Nafisa kuwa tayi kuka  ta yadda har aka fara tunanin mutuwar hajjah da rashin lafiyar mommy ya zauta kwalwarta.. Ta killace kanta ba ci ba sha sai tsantsar kuka... 
Mommy ikilima kuwa tayi ban bakin har ta gaji ta kyaleta... 
Suka cigaba da tarbar baki dan amsa gaisuwa, IGP ma da ammie ta sanar masa washe gari ya hado kan iyalansa sai jumeta... 
Dakin innah suka sauka suka fara yimata gaisuwa daganan IG ya wuce yayiwa hajiya jamila (goggo) gaisuwa ya kuma wuce zuwa dakin hajjah inda su mommy ikilima suke karbar gaisuwa ysyimusu gaisuwa kafin yafito harabar gidan inda ananne maza ke amsar gaisuwa.. 
Deenah kuwa saida suka huta itada iftahal kafin suyiwa su mommy ikilima gaisuwa... 
Taso yiwa nafisa gaisuwa to saidai bata ganta acikin mutane ba shiyasa tayi shiru dan batadon tambayar inda nafisar take ya haifar da kananun maganganu... 
Sai bayan sallar laasar kafin driver yakaisu asibitin da aka kwantar da mommy... Abun ya tsananta domin basu samu ganin mommy sakamakon irin halin da take cikine likitoci suka hana kowa shiga ganinta sai nurses din dake kula da lafiyarta dan har lokacin batasan waye akanta ba... 
Haka suka dawo cike da alhinin halin da mommy take ciki tare da fatan allah ya tashi kafadunta... 
Bayan dawowarsu ne niima ke baiwa deenah labarin abubuwan da suka faru tundaga mutuwar auren nafisa har kawo yanxu... 
Matuka deenah tashiga tsananin mamakin yadda nafisa ke mata zagon kasada abubuwan son zuciyarsu  da suke aikatawa itada iyayenta.., kaga mutum kawai amma kowa da irin zuciyarsa, allah ka tsarkakemana zukatanmu.  Amma duk da haka deenah tayi masifar tausayawa mommy da nafisa da kuma Goggo rumaisa a irin halin da suka tsinci kansu... Lallai sherri dan aike ne duk nisan inda ka aikesa watarana gunka zai dawo... Allah dai yabamu ikon aikata alkhairi (ameen ) 
Saida akayi adduar uku kafin su baro jumeta da yake suna hutun makaranta ne... 
Bayan kwanaki uku mutane duk sun kama gabansu gidan ya rage jamaa sai wadanda ba a rasa ba... 
Da misalin karfe 9:30am mommy  ikilima ta nufi dakin nafisa dan ta kai mata karin kumallonta, bata tarar da ita adakinba, ta kuma tabbatar da bata bayi domin bayin dakinta yasamu matsala na waje kuma akwai wadanda ke shiga acikinsa har yanxu... 
Cikin mamaki mommy ikilima tashiga duban dakin tana nazari, ita dai sam bataga fitowar nafisa a falo ba bare ta ga fitarta to ina taje ne...? 
Ta ajiye flask din da sunkin biredi a kan locker dake gefen gadonta... 
Karaf idanunta ya sauka kan farar takardar dake ninke a kan lokar sai biro dake gefenta... 
Kuma yadda akayi ajiyar takardar zai nuna sako ne aka bari...
Dasauri mommy ikilima tadauki takardar ta warware tasoma karantawa... 
_bazan iya cigaba da wanzuwa acikin wannan rayuwar ba mai tattare da kunci zafi da tsantsar jin kunya ba... Nabarku ahalina bawai dan bana sonku ba, saidan na samu sa ida azuciyata a irin halin son zuciya dana tsinci kaina aciki, na fahimci rayiwa bakomai ake nema ake samu ba a duniya, kuma duk abinda allah baiyiba dan adam bai isa ya qaddaro faruwarsa ba, sai yanxu duniya tabani wannan darasin da rashin saninsa ya kaini shiga wannan halin, mommy da hajjah sun nunamin gata da har suka saka inajin babu abinda zan nema aduniyar nan narasa aduniya ashe bahaka bane, gashi na rasa kima ta mutuncina tamkar yadda suka rasa nasu.... Kada kuyi yunkurin nemana domin bazaku ganniba nayi nesa daku ta yadda zanyimuku wuyar gani domin ko na zauna awulakance da kaskanci zan rayu daku nikuma zuciyata bazata iya jurar haka ba daga karshe ina yiwa mommy fatan samun lafiya ina fatan allah ya sake hada fuskokinmu aranar sakamako.... ._
Koda mommy ikilima ta karashe karanta wasikar kuka tasaka tana sallallami cikin tashin hankali ta fice ta nufi sashin baffag ibrahim tana sanar masa da mahaifin nafisar... 
Baki daya sallallami suka shigayi mahaifinta kuwa ambaton sunan allah ya cigaba da yi cikin wani irin yanayi na bacin rai... 
Baffah ibrahim kuwa gaza cewa komai yayi sai juya takardar da yake ahannunsa yake  ya na jinjina kai, har yanxu duniya bata baiwa nafisa  babban darasi ba.. Domin da tadauki darasi acikinta  da batayi yunkurin shiga duniya ba domin hakan ba maslaha bane illah sake aikata wani kukskuren... Mahaifinta kuwa lokaci daya yazame hannunsa daga al amurranta kamar yadda tace tashiga duniyar, to ya barta da duniyar taje zata koyamata darussa da dama, shikam  yakosa da lamarin nafisa...  
"RAYUWA kenan alokacin da ta kuntatawa wasu alokacin zata yalwatawa wasu haka duniyar take juyin juyi kuma rawar yan mata idan ta gaba ta taka sai takoma  baya..  Babu makarantar dake saurin bada  darasi irin duniya, allah yasa muyi kyakkyawan karshe.. "

               ***
ɓangaren deenah kuwa ta mayar da hankalinta sosai gun karatunta dan yanzu haka shekara daya da rabi taragemata ta idar da karatunta, mima kuwa tayi wayau sosai, shekarunta biyu kacal amma tamkar tayi shekaru  uku, kyakkyawar yarinya sai wayau da saurin shiga rai... Duk wanda yaga mima sai ta shiga ransa sosai..
Deenah kam cikin kankanin lokaci tazama babban mace acikin manyan mata, sosai sunanta ya karade wasu daga cikin yankunan kauyukan jihar katsina ta hanyar tallafawa mata da yara da sana oin dazasu dogara kansu agidajen aurensu ta wanni fannin gwana ce agun baiwa mata shawarwari game da harkokin cigaban rayuwarsu akarkashin sabuwar tsayaytar kunggiyar da ta kafa wato gidauniyar tallafawa mata da kananun yara "JALILA FOUNDATION".. sosai tasamu goyon baya agun IGP dari bisa dari ya nuna jindadinsa akan hobbasan da tayi na taimakawa mata, sau tari takansamu gayyata daga kafafen yada labarai atattauna da ita game da matsalolin mata da hanyoyin magancesu.. Allah ya dafa mata a al amurranta domin ta silar hakam  samu daukaka, ubangiji ya dubi tsarkakkiyar niyyarta ta taimakawa mata yakuma dafa mata a al amurranta... 
Cikin kankanin lokaci tazama vabbar mace duk da karancin shekarun ta domin alokacin bata wuce 28yrs ba... Amma da dama mata kan bata girma irin na dattijuwar mace har ma sukanyimata kirari da suna "uwa ma bada mama " hutu da jindadin ya zauna sosai ajikin deenah... Musamman yadda take gudanar da rayuwarta atsanake shi ne zai burge mai kallo.... 
IGP ma yana bada gudunmuwarsa sosai ta fannin bata shawarwari da duk abinda yashigemata duhu... 

Sultaan kuwa tuni yayi graduation ya baro  kasar canada inda yagama karantar business administration ya kuma fito da kyakkyawan sakamako  yadawo kasar  sa nigeria... 
Ganin yadda ya fito da good redult ne yasanya IGP gaggauta gina  masa masana antar sarrafa robobi... Ya mallakamasa industry din halak malak domin shima ya tsayu da kansa... Da zuwa yanzu masana antar tasoma  bunkasa da sabbin ma aikata jajirtattu.. 
Sultaan baisan da wane baki zai yiwa mahaifinsa  godiya ba.. Saidai yayi fatan ubangiji yakara girma ya ja da nisan kwana... 
Haka rayuwar taci gaba da tafiyar musu cikin jin dadi da nutsuwa da yalwataccen zaman lafiya... 
Kwanci tashi dai da ahakan muke karar da kwaninki mu,  tuni deenah ta kammala karatunta na law iftahal kuma ta kammala da mass communication ta zama cikakkyar journlist takuma soma aiki a wata sabuwar radio station da aka bude mai zaman kanta... 
Deenah kuwa saida ta dauki tsayin  watanni biyar kafin upper dinta da tayi applying ta fito tasoma aikinta na law, BARR kuma GIMBIYA MADINA ABDULLAHI KENAN.. 

IGP kuwa ganin yadda iftahal ta mayar da hankalinta sosai akan aikinta ya sanya da kansa ya kirata a main falo dinsa anan yake umurtarta da ta gaggauta fito da miji acikin manemanta ya aurar da ita, domin ta kammala karatunta ga aiki ta samu aurenr ya rage mata, tunda akwai tarin manemanta dasuke jiran samun izini daga garesa.. Sosai ya nuna mata baxai dauki hakanba yadda sam babu auren agabanta, domin yana taka tsantsan da duk abinda zai taba martabar gidansa da ta ahalinsa, bayada wasa akan iyalansa baya kuma son ana shigomasa da wargi acikin al amurransa... 
Iftahal kuwa batada zabi da ya wuce bin umurnin abbanta itadin bawai auren ke bataso bane kawai tana tsoron mazan zamaninnan duk da kuwa ta shaida irin soyayyar da shahid (ɗa ne ga wani tsohon dan majalissar jihar katsina)  ke mata ta gsskiya ce, yana masifar sonta sosai.. 
Kamar yadda IGP ya bata kwanaki uku da ta fitar da miji, tun kafin kwanaki ukun tasanarwa shahid.. Yayi matukar farinciki sosai, akuma satin yaturo da manyansa akayi magana cikin girma irinna manyan mutane aka tsaida ranar biki sati shida, iyayen shahid kuwa basubar gidan IG ba saida suka ajiye sadakinsu naira dubu dari uku.... 
Tundaga lokacin  da aka saka rana deenah suka shiga hidimar biko gadan gadan.. Itace ta zama tamkat uwa domib wuka da nama duka ahannunta suke... 
Kwanci tashi har biki yazo aka kai amarya kankararren gidanta dake nan layout... Biki kam yayi biki dan bikine akayi abirnin katsina na yayan manya... 
Abubuwa dai nata tafiya yadda yadace da kuma yadda ubangiji ke tsara al amurransa.. rayuwa tayiwa barr deenah da zuriarta dadi da nutsuwa.. IGP kuwa girma ya fara cimmasa dan yanzu haka ya haura shekaru hamsin da hudu 54yrs, aduniya amma idan  kagansa tamkar dan shekaru 33, dan hutu nutsuwa dake ratsa sa, saidai  alamun manyanta da fuskarsa tafara nunawa wanda yakarawa fuskarsa tsantsar kwarjini da haiba... 
Alokacin kuwa yara uku deenah ta haifa, bayan mima, muhammad, hammad,da kuma noor.. Mima kuwa Tuni ansaka ta tsadaddiyar makaranta ta police chlideren school dake nan katsina.. Tana matakin primary 3 ne yayinda muhammad ke primary 1,hammad kuwa nursery sai noor da bata wuce shekaru  uku da ba ajima da yayeta ba.... 
Yara ne kyawawa ga wayau da saurin shiga rai ga duk wanda ya gansu... Zuria kuwa saidai ayi sambarka da godiya gun ubangiji da ya kara wanzar da lafiya  da zama lafiya a tsakaninsu.... 
Haka rayuwa tacigaba da gudana, alokacinne kuma ubangiji ya karbi rayuwar hajiya innah sakamakon yar gajeruwar rashin lafiyar da tasha, allahu akbar mutuwa kenan da bata sanar da lokacin zuwanta saidai aganta akowani lokaci.. 
Mutuwar hajiya innah ba iya jikokinta kadai da Yayanta ta girgiza ba, dik wani wanda ke mai nagge estate yaji mutuwar dattijuwar kowa yabob innah yakeyi, anshaideta dosai da hakuri juriya da kawar da kai.. Tun lokacin da hajjah na da rai sosai tayi hakuri da ita da kuma hajiya goggo (jamila)  da zuwa yanxu ita kadai ta rage acikin matan marigayi mai nagge itama sosai ta tsufa... 
Haka IGP  da iyalansa suka zo  jumeta akayi jana izar hajiya innah dashi, matukar gaske yaji mutuwar kakartasa, to saidsi babu yadda ya iya da ya wuce hakuri domin kullu nafsin za ikatul maout..  
Saida akayi adduar uku kafin su baro jumeta... 

BAYAN WATANNI SHIDDA... 

Da misalin karfe 10:00 na dare IGP ya gama kallon labarai, toilet ya wuce ya watsa ruwa yayi shirin kwanciya.. Har zuwa 11:00pm baiji shigowar deenah ba, hakan yasa ya janyo  system  dinsa ya shiga amfani da ita kamar yadda yasaba,ya dauki awanni biyu da ya kaisa karfe shabiyu yana aikin office dinsa.. 
Sai kuma alokacin deenah ta shigo bedroom din nasa dauke da sallama abakinta, bak daya hannayenta na goye a bayanta da alama akwai wani abinda take boyemasa..  
Bai dubetaba ya amsa sallamar yana murmishi still idanunsa akan system dinsa, saida ta karaso ne tazauna agefensa sannan ya mayar da dubansa akanta... 
yasoma magana "bakidaya  kids sun kwace minke, yanxu  wuyar gani kike min madam, amma daina kulawar da ni, zan dauki  mataki ne yarinya zan dauko shakaf yar sha hudu "... 
Ya idar da maganar cikin zolaya... 
Shagwabe fuska deenah tayi duk da zancen nasa ya bata dariya tasoma magana "hubby ka fa tsufa kabarwa yara, wannan ai su sultan"... 
Shima dariya sosai yayi  yana duban kwayar  idanunta shikansa ya fada ne kawai amma baya iya rayuwa da wata ya mace matukar ba deenah... 
Kwantar da kanta tayi akafadarsa ta saka hannunta ta rike damtsen hannunsa cikin sigar rarrashi tasoma magana "im sorry hubby, kasan noor da rigima idan ba bacci tayi ba bata bari na na motsa ko nan da can ".. 
Batare da ya dubeta ba yayi murmishi na gefen kumatu yace "kane ko kanwa noor ke bukata, dan haka yanzu ki zama ready,.... 
Yar dariya mai sauti deenah tayi kafin tace "allah ba yanxu ba hubby insha allah sai noor ta kara wayau na huta da rigimarta.... "
Tayi maganat tare raba jikknta da nasa tana dubansa kafin ta umurcesa ya rufe idanunsa... 
Murmishi yayi sosai na gefen kumatu yana dubanta, ya fahimci har yanzu deenah yarinta na ruftarta amma ko meye bari ya rufe yaga suprise din da zata basa... 
Rufe udanunsa yayi yana jiran jin abinda zata yimasa, saida ta manna masa peck a liips dinsa.. 
Kafin ta umurcesa ya bude idanunsa... 
Yana budewa ya karawa eyeball din sa girma cikin suprising dinsa da tayi.. yake duban hadaddiyar silver wristwatch din dake acikin dan karamin kayataccen box da ta bude.. 

Tun kafin ya magantu ta soma magana "ina tayaka murnar zagayowar ranar haihuwarka, allah ya albarkaci shekarunka da wadanda ka riska dama wadanda baka kaiga cimmawa ba, happy 55yrs dear ".. 
Ta idar da maganar cikin narkakken murmishi tana kashe masa dayan idanta... Tare da mikamasa gift dinsa... 
Tsantsar farinciki ya gaza boyuwa a fuskar IG, tabbas deenah ta yi suprising dinsa ta kuma shammacesa sosai, rungumeta kawai  yayi ya manna tsam akirjinsa yagaza furta komai sai sauke ajiyat zuciya da yake yana murmishi mai nuna zallar farinciki... Dagota yayi yana manna mata peck agoshinta ya sake mayat da ita ajikinsa yana sauke numfashi "thank uh dear, kin shammaceni, Im so spechless... 
Cikin farinciki ya ke maganar... 
Deenah kuwa murmishi tashigayi tana sake cusa kanta acikin kirjinsa... 
Sauke ajiyar zuciya ya kumayi, yacigaba da hamdala da tahmidi ga ubangiji yasoma magana "ban taba tunanin zan cigaba da farinciki ko dariya ba tun ranar da na rasa jalila, aahe jalila tabar duniyar ne amma still ina tare da ita, ta barmin farincikin da zai cigaba da bina har  lokacin da kasa zata rufe idanuna... Kece cikamakon farincikina madeenah, JALILA, tayimin abinda bazan taba biyanta da shi ba, saidai nayi fatan ubangiji ya haskaka kabarinta ya sanya mata nutsuwar kwanciya a makwancinta, yar aljannah kenan... 
Ya idar da maganar yana lumshe idanunsa tare da kara rike deenah tsam dake ajikinsa... 

Dumin hawayen deenah da  yaji ajikinsa  sakasa saurin dagota daga jikinsa, kuka take marar sauti tare da murmishi... 
Cikin tashin hankalin ganin hawaye a idanunta yake duban fuskanta tunkafin ya magantu ta saka hannu ta share hawayen tasoma magana "hubby naga soyayya iri² a duniyar nan  ta jini, amma ban taba ganin irin soyayyar da yaya ke yiwa kanwaba misalin wacce aunty jalila ta nunamin na jima banga tsantsar so da sadaukarwa irib tataba, ta nunamin so, bata barni nayi maraicin uwa ba, ta raineni tamkar ita ta haifeni daga karahe ta gatantani takuma auramin mijinta, kuma duk dan tattalin gobena ta yi hakan, wannan wani irin so ne hubby "... 
Share hawayen dasuka kuma zubomata tayi tacigaba da magana cikin jin kewar aunty jalila "allah yajikanki auntynah, allah ya jaddada rahamarsa gareki.. Addua itace kadai soyayyar da zamu nuna miki ayanzu "... 
Cikin kuka ta idar da zancen tare da fadawa jikinsa tana cigaba da rairo kukanta, shikansa jikinsa yayimasa sanyi sosai.. Ya tsinci kansa cikin wani irin yanayi... 

Cikin son kwantarwa deenah sa hankali ya dagota yana murmishi ya amsa da ameen dear... Dame kika zomin? "
Yayi maganar yana jifanta da wani irin kasalallen kallo... 
Guntun murmishi ya bayyana afuskarta tayi kasa da kanta tana goge idanunta sarai ta fahimci abinda yake nufi . 
Leqen fuskarta yacigaba da yi yana murmishi jin yadda yaga wani sabon salo wai jin kunyarsa take ya soma magana "good, yaba kyauta tukuici, nima zanbaki zazzafan tukuicina adarennan ".. 
Ya idar da maganar yana kashe mata ido... 
Cikin yar shagwabar ta datake fizgar nutsuwarsa ta dubesa tare da karawa eyeball dinta girma .. Cikin kissa irin ta hallitar ya mace  tafara yimasa dan kukan shagwaba batare da ta furta komai ba... 

Baki daya ta ida rikita sa da salonta... Cikin rashin jure bakon yanayin da yake fizgarsa ya kuma mannota jikinsa cikin yin kasa da murya yace "fadamin wani abu?"/. 
Me zan fada ma? "
Ta tambayesa idanunta alumshe tana murmishi... 

Saitin kunnenta yadan hura mata iska yace "ki cemin kina sona da harrufan larabci"... 

Dariya deenah ta shigayi sosai cikin jin kunya dan ta fahimci zolaya ce yakemata na abubuwan da suka faru ashekarun baya da ya ki manta shiriritarta... 
Shima dariyar yashigayimata, kafin su shiga kokarin fara farantawa junansu rai Ta hanyar raya darensu da ibadar aure......
